// ==UserScript==
// @name         AE Suite
// @namespace    ae.tools.suite
// @version      1.0.0
// @description  Consolidated AstroEmpires tool suite: fleet monitor, tech parse, auto-launch, bookmarks, predictor, empire menu, layout overseer. Modules toggleable via Tampermonkey menu. Single shared AEShared core.
// @match        https://*.astroempires.com/*
// @match        *://*.astroempires.com/*
// @run-at       document-idle
// @grant        GM_setValue
// @grant        GM_getValue
// @grant        GM_deleteValue
// @grant        GM_listValues
// @grant        GM_setClipboard
// @grant        GM_addStyle
// @grant        GM_registerMenuCommand
// ==/UserScript==

/* ONE script, ONE GM store (private, cross-subdomain), ONE AEShared core.
 * Migration order: (1) install patched exporter versions of the OLD Harvester /
 * Quick Bookmarks / Fleet Monitor and load AE once per server (dumps their GM
 * data to localStorage), (2) install this suite (shim imports the dumps into
 * suite GM and rebuilds the overseer master), (3) remove ALL old scripts.
 * NOTE: empire-menu previously ran at document-end; suite runs at document-idle
 * (later). Flagged; verify menu injection still lands on slow pages. */

/* =============================================================================
 * AE SHARED — Visual theme + fixed-region tiling foundation
 * Version: 0.3.0
 * Server-agnostic. Self-contained. No external dependencies, no build step.
 *
 * PURPOSE
 *   Single source of truth for how every AstroEmpires in-game tool panel looks
 *   (theme) and where it appears (fixed-region, content-edge-aware tiling).
 *
 * HOW TO USE (baked-in model)
 *   Paste this whole file near the TOP of each userscript. Then:
 *     const ui = AEShared.createPanel({ scriptId, title, subtitle });
 *     ui.body.innerHTML = '...'; ui.setStatus('ok','idle');
 *   createPanel() also starts the live-override watcher automatically.
 *
 * OVERRIDE MODEL (probe-verified channel: localStorage)
 *   The storage probe confirmed localStorage is shared across separate
 *   userscripts on the same origin (GM storage is isolated per script and
 *   cannot bridge scripts; it stays each script's private store). An optional
 *   overseer writes per-script layout and theme OVERRIDES into one namespaced
 *   localStorage key; every script reads them on load and re-applies live when
 *   the stored revision changes. Empty store => baked-in defaults (standalone).
 *   ⚠ localStorage on this origin is also readable by the game page; layout/
 *     theme prefs are low-sensitivity and the key is hard-namespaced.
 *
 * TILING MODEL (fixed region per script, now overrideable)
 *   AEShared.REGIONS gives each script ONE default corner. Placement is
 *   content-edge aware (panels pushed into the gutter beside centered game
 *   content; warns if too narrow). A full-width top banner is reserved via
 *   layout.bannerHeight. An override may set a different corner, explicit
 *   px offsets (free placement), and/or width.
 *
 *   ONE UNVERIFIED SEAM remains: getContentBox() auto-detects the centered
 *   content wrapper; set layout.contentSelector + layout.bannerHeight to lock.
 * ========================================================================== */

const AEShared = (function () {
  'use strict';

  const VERSION = '0.3.1';

  // ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  // DESIGN TOKENS (default values). Override targets use these exact var names.
  // ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  const TOKENS = {
    '--ae-bg':          '#0c1018',
    '--ae-surface':     '#121a2b',
    '--ae-surface-2':   '#0f1626',
    '--ae-raised':      '#1a2540',
    '--ae-border':      '#243049',
    '--ae-border-soft': '#1c2740',

    '--ae-text':        '#d6deea',
    '--ae-text-muted':  '#9fb0c8',
    '--ae-text-dim':    '#6b7a92',

    '--ae-primary':     '#3fb6c4',
    '--ae-primary-ink': '#06171b',
    '--ae-secondary':   '#f0b541',
    '--ae-success':     '#41d99a',
    '--ae-warn':        '#f0b541',
    '--ae-danger':      '#f0655a',
    '--ae-on-danger':   '#ffffff',   /* text on a danger-coloured background (e.g. IN-PROGRESS banner) */

    '--ae-whitelist':   '#41d99a',
    '--ae-blacklist':   '#f0655a',

    '--ae-safe':        '#a371f7',   /* safe-drop accent (from AutoLaunch v2.5+ migration) */
    '--ae-orange':      '#ff7a00',   /* departed / outbound accent (promoted from reports-monitor local token) */

    '--ae-radius':      '8px',
    '--ae-radius-sm':   '5px',
    '--ae-gap':         '8px',
    '--ae-shadow':      '0 8px 28px rgba(0,0,0,.55)',

    '--ae-font':        "-apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, 'Segoe UI', Roboto, Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif",
    '--ae-font-mono':   "ui-monospace, 'SF Mono', Menlo, Consolas, 'Liberation Mono', monospace"
  };

  // ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  // COMPONENT CSS. Flat single-class selectors. Injected into a shadow root.
  // ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  const BASE_CSS = `
:host { all: initial; }
* { box-sizing: border-box; }

.ae-panel {
  font-family: var(--ae-font); color: var(--ae-text); background: var(--ae-bg);
  border: 1px solid var(--ae-border); border-radius: var(--ae-radius);
  box-shadow: var(--ae-shadow); overflow: hidden; font-size: 12px; line-height: 1.45;
}
.ae-panel__bar {
  display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 8px; padding: 8px 10px;
  background: var(--ae-surface); border-bottom: 1px solid var(--ae-border);
  cursor: default; user-select: none;
}
.ae-panel__bar--drag { cursor: move; }
.ae-panel__sweep { height: 2px; width: 100%; background: linear-gradient(90deg, var(--ae-primary), transparent 70%); opacity: .8; }
@media (prefers-reduced-motion: no-preference) {
  .ae-panel__sweep { background: linear-gradient(90deg, transparent, var(--ae-primary), transparent); background-size: 220% 100%; animation: ae-sweep 6s linear infinite; }
  @keyframes ae-sweep { from { background-position: 200% 0; } to { background-position: -20% 0; } }
}
.ae-panel__dot { width: 9px; height: 9px; border-radius: 50%; background: var(--ae-text-dim); flex: 0 0 auto; }
.ae-panel__dot.is-ok   { background: var(--ae-success); box-shadow: 0 0 6px var(--ae-success); }
.ae-panel__dot.is-warn { background: var(--ae-warn);    box-shadow: 0 0 6px var(--ae-warn); }
.ae-panel__dot.is-bad  { background: var(--ae-danger);  box-shadow: 0 0 6px var(--ae-danger); }
.ae-panel__titles { display: flex; flex-direction: column; line-height: 1.15; min-width: 0; }
.ae-panel__title { font-weight: 600; font-size: 12px; letter-spacing: .04em; white-space: nowrap; overflow: hidden; text-overflow: ellipsis; }
.ae-panel__sub { font-size: 10px; color: var(--ae-text-dim); font-family: var(--ae-font-mono); }
.ae-panel__status { margin-left: auto; font-family: var(--ae-font-mono); font-size: 10px; color: var(--ae-text-muted); }
.ae-panel__body { padding: 10px; max-height: 70vh; overflow: auto; }

.ae-tabs { display: flex; gap: 2px; padding: 6px 8px 0; background: var(--ae-surface); border-bottom: 1px solid var(--ae-border); }
.ae-tab { font: inherit; font-size: 11px; color: var(--ae-text-muted); background: transparent; border: 0; border-bottom: 2px solid transparent; padding: 6px 9px; cursor: pointer; border-radius: var(--ae-radius-sm) var(--ae-radius-sm) 0 0; }
.ae-tab:hover { color: var(--ae-text); background: var(--ae-raised); }
.ae-tab[aria-selected="true"] { color: var(--ae-primary); border-bottom-color: var(--ae-primary); }

.ae-btn { font: inherit; font-size: 11px; color: var(--ae-text); background: var(--ae-raised); border: 1px solid var(--ae-border); padding: 5px 10px; border-radius: var(--ae-radius-sm); cursor: pointer; }
.ae-btn:hover { border-color: var(--ae-primary); }
.ae-btn:active { transform: translateY(1px); }
.ae-btn--primary { background: var(--ae-primary); color: var(--ae-primary-ink); border-color: var(--ae-primary); font-weight: 600; }
.ae-btn--primary:hover { filter: brightness(1.08); }
.ae-btn--ghost { background: transparent; }
.ae-btn:focus-visible { outline: 2px solid var(--ae-primary); outline-offset: 1px; }

.ae-input, .ae-select { font: inherit; font-size: 11px; color: var(--ae-text); background: var(--ae-surface-2); border: 1px solid var(--ae-border); padding: 5px 8px; border-radius: var(--ae-radius-sm); width: 100%; }
.ae-input:focus, .ae-select:focus { outline: none; border-color: var(--ae-primary); }
.ae-input--mono { font-family: var(--ae-font-mono); }

.ae-table { width: 100%; border-collapse: collapse; font-family: var(--ae-font-mono); font-size: 11px; font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums; }
.ae-table th { text-align: left; color: var(--ae-text-dim); font-weight: 600; padding: 4px 6px; border-bottom: 1px solid var(--ae-border); }
.ae-table td { padding: 4px 6px; border-bottom: 1px solid var(--ae-border-soft); }
.ae-table tr:hover td { background: var(--ae-surface); }
.ae-num { font-family: var(--ae-font-mono); font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums; }

.ae-kv { display: flex; justify-content: space-between; gap: 10px; padding: 3px 0; border-bottom: 1px solid var(--ae-border-soft); }
.ae-kv span { color: var(--ae-text-muted); }
.ae-kv b { font-family: var(--ae-font-mono); font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums; }

.ae-badge { display: inline-block; font-family: var(--ae-font-mono); font-size: 10px; padding: 1px 6px; border-radius: 999px; border: 1px solid var(--ae-border); color: var(--ae-text-muted); }
.ae-badge--ok   { color: var(--ae-success); border-color: var(--ae-success); }
.ae-badge--warn { color: var(--ae-warn);    border-color: var(--ae-warn); }
.ae-badge--bad  { color: var(--ae-danger);  border-color: var(--ae-danger); }
.ae-wl { color: var(--ae-whitelist); }
.ae-bl { color: var(--ae-blacklist); }

.ae-banner { padding: 6px 10px; font-family: var(--ae-font-mono); font-size: 11px; background: var(--ae-surface); border-bottom: 1px solid var(--ae-border); }
.ae-banner--alert { background: color-mix(in srgb, var(--ae-danger) 18%, var(--ae-bg)); color: var(--ae-danger); border-bottom-color: var(--ae-danger); }

/* Standard collapse/expand control — rounded square, + (expand) / − (collapse).
   The single idiom for every panel; modules must not roll their own caret/text. */
.ae-panel__bar > .ae-toggle:last-child, .ae-panel__bar > .ae-bar-right { margin-left: auto; }
.ae-bar-right { display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 6px; margin-left: auto; }
.ae-toggle {
  flex: 0 0 auto; width: 18px; height: 18px; padding: 0; margin: 0;
  display: inline-flex; align-items: center; justify-content: center;
  font: 600 13px/1 var(--ae-font-mono); color: var(--ae-text-muted);
  background: var(--ae-raised); border: 1px solid var(--ae-border);
  border-radius: 5px; cursor: pointer; user-select: none;
}
.ae-toggle:hover { color: var(--ae-primary); border-color: var(--ae-primary); }
.ae-toggle:active { transform: translateY(1px); }
.ae-toggle:focus-visible { outline: 2px solid var(--ae-primary); outline-offset: 1px; }

/* Modal overlay (master settings). Covers the panel it is mounted in. */
.ae-overlay {
  position: fixed; inset: 0; background: rgba(4, 8, 14, .72);
  display: flex; align-items: flex-start; justify-content: center;
  padding: 40px 12px; overflow: auto; z-index: 10;
}
.ae-modal {
  width: 100%; max-width: 520px; background: var(--ae-bg);
  border: 1px solid var(--ae-border); border-radius: var(--ae-radius);
  box-shadow: var(--ae-shadow); overflow: hidden;
}
.ae-modal__bar {
  display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 8px; padding: 10px 12px;
  background: var(--ae-surface); border-bottom: 1px solid var(--ae-border);
  font-weight: 600; font-size: 12px; letter-spacing: .04em;
}
.ae-modal__body { padding: 12px; max-height: 70vh; overflow: auto; }
.ae-modal__foot {
  display: flex; gap: 6px; padding: 10px 12px;
  border-top: 1px solid var(--ae-border); background: var(--ae-surface-2);
}
.ae-field { display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 8px; padding: 5px 0; }
.ae-field label { flex: 1; font-size: 11px; color: var(--ae-text-muted); }
.ae-field input[type="color"] {
  width: 34px; height: 22px; padding: 0; background: none;
  border: 1px solid var(--ae-border); border-radius: 4px; cursor: pointer;
}
.ae-sw { display: inline-flex; align-items: center; gap: 6px; font-size: 11px; }
.ae-sec { margin-bottom: 12px; }
.ae-sec__h {
  font-size: 10px; text-transform: uppercase; letter-spacing: .08em;
  color: var(--ae-text-dim); padding-bottom: 4px; margin-bottom: 6px;
  border-bottom: 1px solid var(--ae-border);
}

.ae-muted { color: var(--ae-text-dim); }
.ae-hr { height: 1px; background: var(--ae-border); border: 0; margin: 8px 0; }
`;

  // ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  // PERSISTENT OVERRIDE STORE — localStorage (probe-verified cross-script).
  // Shape: { layout: { [scriptId]: {anchor?,top?,bottom?,left?,right?,width?,maxHeight?} },
  //          theme:  { '--ae-*': value, ... },
  //          rev:    <number bumped on every write> }
  // ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  const STORE_KEY = 'ae::overseer::v1';

  function readStore() {
    try { const raw = localStorage.getItem(STORE_KEY); return raw ? (JSON.parse(raw) || {}) : {}; }
    catch (e) { return {}; }
  }
  function writeStore(obj) {
    try { localStorage.setItem(STORE_KEY, JSON.stringify(obj || {})); return true; }
    catch (e) { console.warn('[AEShared] writeStore failed', e); return false; }
  }

  // Theme token overrides (reads the verified channel; {} => baked-in defaults).
  function getOverrides() {
    const s = readStore();
    return (s && s.theme) ? s.theme : {};
  }
  // Per-script layout override (null => use baked-in REGIONS default).
  function getLayoutOverrides(scriptId) {
    const s = readStore();
    const m = (s && s.layout) ? s.layout : {};
    return scriptId ? (m[scriptId] || null) : m;
  }

  // Single source of truth for store mutations (used by the overseer).
  const store = {
    read: readStore,
    write: writeStore,
    replaceLayout(scriptId, region) {
      const s = readStore(); s.layout = s.layout || {};
      s.layout[scriptId] = Object.assign({}, region);
      s.rev = (s.rev || 0) + 1; writeStore(s); return s;
    },
    clearLayout(scriptId) {
      const s = readStore(); if (s.layout) delete s.layout[scriptId];
      s.rev = (s.rev || 0) + 1; writeStore(s); return s;
    },
    setTheme(tokenObj) {
      const s = readStore(); s.theme = Object.assign({}, s.theme, tokenObj || {});
      s.rev = (s.rev || 0) + 1; writeStore(s); return s;
    },
    clearAll() { const s = { rev: (readStore().rev || 0) + 1 }; writeStore(s); return s; }
  };

  function buildTokenBlock(overrides) {
    const merged = Object.assign({}, TOKENS, overrides || {});
    return ':host {\n' + Object.keys(merged).map(k => `  ${k}: ${merged[k]};`).join('\n') + '\n}\n';
  }
  function styleText(overrides) { return buildTokenBlock(overrides) + BASE_CSS; }

  /* Token block for contexts a shadow root's :host cannot reach — e.g. a popup
     window written via document.write, or a panel injected into the game's light
     DOM. Keeps those surfaces on the SAME palette as every shadow panel, so the
     theme (and any overseer override) stays consistent everywhere. */
  function cssVarsFor(selector, overrides) {
    const merged = Object.assign({}, TOKENS, overrides || getOverrides());
    return (selector || ':root') + ' {\n' +
      Object.keys(merged).map(k => '  ' + k + ': ' + merged[k] + ';').join('\n') + '\n}\n';
  }
  /* Raw resolved value of a single token (for JS that must set a colour inline
     in the light DOM, where CSS vars are unavailable). */
  function token(name, overrides) {
    const merged = Object.assign({}, TOKENS, overrides || getOverrides());
    return merged[name] || null;
  }

  function adopt(shadowRoot, overrides) {
    if (!shadowRoot) throw new Error('AEShared.adopt: shadowRoot required');
    const ov = overrides || getOverrides();
    let el = shadowRoot.querySelector('style[data-ae-theme]');
    if (!el) { el = document.createElement('style'); el.setAttribute('data-ae-theme', VERSION); shadowRoot.appendChild(el); }
    el.textContent = styleText(ov);
    return el;
  }

  // ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  // LAYOUT CONFIG + FIXED-REGION REGISTRY
  // ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  const layout = {
    z: 2147482000,
    bannerHeight: 30,            // ⚠ estimate; set to actual full-width banner height
    edgeGap: 8,
    gutterGap: 10,
    contentSelector: null,       // lock once confirmed; null = auto-detect
    contentFallbackWidth: 1000
  };

  /* ZONE GRID — 5 slots per gutter, numbered top->bottom: left-1..left-5,
     right-1..right-5. A zone is resolved to pixels at RENDER time, so unlike the
     old explicit-px mid slots it stays fully viewport-responsive on resize.
     left-1 is RESERVED for the Layout Overseer: its panel must stay clickable
     during Arrange mode, so no module may occupy the slot it sits in. */
  const ZONE_SLOTS_DEFAULT = 5;
  const ZONE_SLOTS_MIN = 2, ZONE_SLOTS_MAX = 8;
  const OVERSEER_ZONE = 'left-1';
  const RESERVED_ZONES = [OVERSEER_ZONE];

  /* Slot count is user-configurable (Overseer → Settings) and lives in the shared
     store, so every module resolves zones against the SAME grid. */
  function getSlots() {
    const s = readStore();
    const n = parseInt(s && s.slots, 10);
    if (!n || isNaN(n)) return ZONE_SLOTS_DEFAULT;
    return Math.max(ZONE_SLOTS_MIN, Math.min(ZONE_SLOTS_MAX, n));
  }
  function setSlots(n) {
    const st = readStore();
    st.slots = Math.max(ZONE_SLOTS_MIN, Math.min(ZONE_SLOTS_MAX, parseInt(n, 10) || ZONE_SLOTS_DEFAULT));
    st.rev = (st.rev || 0) + 1; st.ts = Date.now();
    writeStore(st);
    return st.slots;
  }

  function zoneList() {
    const out = [];
    const n = getSlots();
    ['left', 'right'].forEach(side => {
      for (let i = 1; i <= n; i++) out.push(side + '-' + i);
    });
    return out;
  }
  /* Zones a module may be placed in (overseer's slot excluded). */
  function assignableZones() {
    return zoneList().filter(z => RESERVED_ZONES.indexOf(z) === -1);
  }
  function parseZone(z) {
    const m = /^(left|right)-(\d+)$/.exec(String(z || ''));
    if (!m) return null;
    const slot = parseInt(m[2], 10);
    // A saved zone may exceed a reduced slot count — clamp so the panel stays on
    // screen instead of resolving to a slot that no longer exists.
    return { side: m[1], slot: Math.max(1, Math.min(getSlots(), slot)) };
  }

  const REGIONS = {
    'overseer':         { zone: 'left-1',  width: 460, label: 'Layout Overseer (reserved slot)' },
    'report-scanner':   { zone: 'left-2',  width: 400, label: 'Report Scanner' },
    'bookmarks2':       { zone: 'left-3',  width: 280, label: 'Auto-Launch + Destinations' },
    'intercept-solver': { zone: 'left-4',  width: 400, label: 'Intercept Solver (incl. fleet monitor)' },
    'tech-harvester':   { zone: 'right-1', width: 300, label: 'Tech Harvester' },
    'bookmarks':        { zone: 'right-2', width: 280, label: 'Quick Bookmarks' },
    'prebattle-predictor': { zone: 'right-3', width: 260, label: 'Pre-Battle Predictor' },
    'credit-optimizer': { zone: 'right-5', width: 340, label: 'Credit Allocation Optimizer' },
    'messages-parser':  { zone: 'left-5',  width: 320, label: 'messages.aspx parser' },
    'map-intel':        { zone: 'right-4', width: 300, label: 'Base Intel (rival base DB)' },
    // right-5 nominally belongs to credit-optimizer, but that module is not in the
    // suite build; if it ever ships, reassign one of them.
    'scout-killer':     { zone: 'right-5', width: 320, label: 'Scout Killer (autoscout predictor)' },
    'fleet-scanner':    { zone: 'left-5',  width: 300, label: 'Fleet Scanner (exact compositions)' },
    // legacy id kept so pre-split overrides still resolve (see reports-monitor split)
    'reports-monitor':  { zone: 'left-2',  width: 400, label: 'Fleet Monitor (legacy, pre-split)' },
    '__fallback__':     { zone: 'right-4', width: 300, label: 'unregistered' }
  };

  // CONTENT-BOX DETECTION — UNVERIFIED SEAM (auto-detect + fallback).
  function getContentBox() {
    const vw = window.innerWidth || document.documentElement.clientWidth;
    if (layout.contentSelector) {
      const el = document.querySelector(layout.contentSelector);
      if (el) { const r = el.getBoundingClientRect(); if (r.width > 0) return { left: r.left, right: r.right, vw, source: 'override' }; }
    }
    let best = null;
    document.querySelectorAll('table, div').forEach(el => {
      const r = el.getBoundingClientRect();
      if (r.width < vw * 0.35 || r.width > vw * 0.98) return;
      if (Math.abs(r.left - (vw - r.right)) > Math.max(40, vw * 0.08)) return;
      if (!best || r.width > best.width) best = { left: r.left, right: r.right, width: r.width };
    });
    if (best) return { left: best.left, right: best.right, vw, source: 'auto' };
    const w = Math.min(layout.contentFallbackWidth, vw);
    const left = (vw - w) / 2;
    return { left, right: left + w, vw, source: 'fallback' };
  }

  // Pure-ish placement math. Returns CSS props + a resolved absolute rect (for
  // ghost previews) + which gutter (if any) is too narrow. overrideRegion:
  // undefined => read the store; pass an object to preview an unsaved edit.
  function computeRect(scriptId, overrideRegion) {
    const base = REGIONS[scriptId] || REGIONS.__fallback__;
    const ov = (overrideRegion !== undefined) ? overrideRegion : getLayoutOverrides(scriptId);
    const r = Object.assign({}, base, ov || {});
    const box = getContentBox();
    const vw = box.vw;
    const vh = window.innerHeight || document.documentElement.clientHeight || 800;
    const w = r.width;
    // Stacking: `priority` (0..99, higher = on top) decides which panel wins when
    // two overlap. Set per module in Overseer → Settings.
    const prio = Math.max(0, Math.min(99, parseInt(r.priority, 10) || 0));
    const css = { position: 'fixed', width: w, zIndex: (r.z != null ? r.z : layout.z + prio), priority: prio };
    let warnSide = null;

    const z = parseZone(r.zone);
    const hasExplicit = ['top', 'bottom', 'left', 'right'].some(k => typeof r[k] === 'number');
    if (z && !hasExplicit) {
      // Resolve the zone now: slot height tracks the viewport, so the layout
      // reflows correctly on resize instead of being frozen at snap-time px.
      const usable = Math.max(vh - (layout.bannerHeight + layout.edgeGap) - layout.edgeGap, 120);
      const slotH = usable / getSlots();
      css.top = Math.round(layout.bannerHeight + layout.edgeGap + (z.slot - 1) * slotH);
      if (z.side === 'left') {
        css.left = layout.edgeGap;
        if (layout.edgeGap + w > box.left - layout.gutterGap) warnSide = 'left';
      } else {
        css.right = layout.edgeGap;            // same offset as every right-hand element
        if (vw - layout.edgeGap - w < box.right + layout.gutterGap) warnSide = 'right';
      }
      css.slotHeight = Math.round(slotH);
    } else if (hasExplicit) {
      if (typeof r.top === 'number')    css.top = r.top;
      if (typeof r.bottom === 'number') css.bottom = r.bottom;
      if (typeof r.left === 'number')   css.left = r.left;
      if (typeof r.right === 'number')  css.right = r.right;
    } else {
      const a = r.anchor || 'top-right';
      if (a.includes('top'))    css.top = layout.bannerHeight + layout.edgeGap;
      if (a.includes('bottom')) css.bottom = layout.edgeGap;
      if (a.includes('left'))  { css.left = layout.edgeGap;  if (layout.edgeGap + w > box.left - layout.gutterGap) warnSide = 'left'; }
      if (a.includes('right')) { css.right = layout.edgeGap; if (vw - layout.edgeGap - w < box.right + layout.gutterGap) warnSide = 'right'; }
    }
    if (typeof r.maxHeight === 'number') css.maxHeight = r.maxHeight;

    const previewH = (typeof r.maxHeight === 'number') ? r.maxHeight : 160;
    const left = (css.left != null) ? css.left : (vw - (css.right || 0) - w);
    const top  = (css.top  != null) ? css.top  : (vh - (css.bottom || 0) - previewH);
    return { css, rect: { left, top, width: w, height: previewH }, region: r, warnSide, box, vw, vh };
  }

  // Tracked panels (for resize + live-override re-tiling).
  const _panels = [];
  let _resizeTimer = null;

  function placeHost(hostEl, scriptId, shadow) {
    if (!REGIONS[scriptId]) console.warn(`[AEShared] No region for "${scriptId}". Using fallback. Add an entry to AEShared.REGIONS.`);
    hostEl.setAttribute('data-ae-script', scriptId); // lets the overseer detect which scripts are actually loaded
    const { css, warnSide, box } = computeRect(scriptId);
    const s = hostEl.style;
    s.position = 'fixed';
    s.zIndex = String(css.zIndex);
    s.width = css.width + 'px';
    s.top = s.bottom = s.left = s.right = 'auto';
    if (css.top    != null) s.top    = css.top + 'px';
    if (css.bottom != null) s.bottom = css.bottom + 'px';
    if (css.left   != null) s.left   = css.left + 'px';
    if (css.right  != null) s.right  = css.right + 'px';
    if (css.maxHeight != null) {
      const body = (shadow || (hostEl.shadowRoot)) && (shadow || hostEl.shadowRoot).querySelector('.ae-panel__body');
      if (body) body.style.maxHeight = css.maxHeight + 'px';
    }
    if (warnSide === 'left')  console.warn(`[AEShared] left gutter too narrow for "${scriptId}" (content starts at ${Math.round(box.left)}px).`);
    if (warnSide === 'right') console.warn(`[AEShared] right gutter too narrow for "${scriptId}" (content ends at ${Math.round(box.right)}px).`);

    let entry = _panels.find(p => p.host === hostEl);
    if (!entry) { entry = { host: hostEl, scriptId }; _panels.push(entry); }
    if (isMasterHidden()) hostEl.style.display = 'none';   // suite is master-hidden
    if (shadow) entry.shadow = shadow;
    entry.desiredTop = css.top;              // where the zone wants it (downward default)
    watchOverflow(entry);
    fitVertically(entry);
    return css;
  }

  /* GROW UPWARD WHEN THERE IS NO ROOM BELOW.
     Default is downward from the slot's top edge. But a tall panel low on the
     screen (or one that just expanded) would run off the bottom, so if its height
     will not fit below its anchor we flip it to bottom-anchored — it then grows
     upward from the bottom edge instead. Purely presentational: the stored zone
     is unchanged, so nothing is "moved" behind the user's back. */
  function fitVertically(entry) {
    const host = entry.host;
    if (!host || typeof entry.desiredTop !== 'number') return;
    const vh = window.innerHeight || document.documentElement.clientHeight || 800;
    const h = host.offsetHeight || 0;
    if (!h) return;
    const bottomLimit = vh - layout.edgeGap;
    const s = host.style;
    if (entry.desiredTop + h > bottomLimit) {
      // no room below -> pin to the bottom and let it extend upward
      const bottom = Math.max(layout.edgeGap, layout.edgeGap);
      s.top = 'auto';
      s.bottom = bottom + 'px';
      s.maxHeight = (vh - layout.bannerHeight - 2 * layout.edgeGap) + 'px';
      entry.flipped = true;
    } else if (entry.flipped) {
      // room again -> restore the default downward anchor
      s.bottom = 'auto';
      s.top = entry.desiredTop + 'px';
      s.maxHeight = '';
      entry.flipped = false;
    }
  }

  /* Re-check on every size change: collapsing/expanding a panel changes its height,
     which is exactly when the flip is needed. */
  function watchOverflow(entry) {
    if (entry._ro || typeof ResizeObserver === 'undefined') return;
    try {
      entry._ro = new ResizeObserver(() => fitVertically(entry));
      entry._ro.observe(entry.host);
    } catch (e) { /* older engines: resize/rev handlers still re-fit */ }
  }

  // ── MASTER HIDE: one switch conceals every suite panel and pauses all activity ──
  // Flag lives in the overseer store (same rev-watched key), so it propagates to every
  // tab live. Modules subscribe via the 'ae-master-hidden' window event (detail.hidden)
  // and must stop timers/sessions/loads while engaged.
  function isMasterHidden() { return !!readStore().masterHidden; }
  function setMasterHidden(on) {
    const st = readStore();
    st.masterHidden = !!on;
    st.rev = (st.rev || 0) + 1;
    writeStore(st);
    _applyMasterHidden();      // same-tab: storage events do not fire for our own writes
  }
  let _chip = null;
  function _applyMasterHidden() {
    const hidden = isMasterHidden();
    _panels.forEach(p => { p.host.style.display = hidden ? 'none' : ''; });
    // restore chip: a tiny low-profile dot, the only thing left on screen
    if (hidden && !_chip && typeof document !== 'undefined') {
      _chip = document.createElement('div');
      _chip.setAttribute('data-ae-script', 'master-chip');
      _chip.textContent = 'AE';
      _chip.title = 'AE suite hidden \u2014 click to restore';
      _chip.style.cssText = 'position:fixed;right:4px;bottom:4px;z-index:2147483399;font:9px/1 monospace;'
        + 'padding:3px 5px;border-radius:8px;background:rgba(20,28,34,.75);color:#7aa;cursor:pointer;opacity:.55;';
      _chip.addEventListener('mouseenter', () => { _chip.style.opacity = '1'; });
      _chip.addEventListener('mouseleave', () => { _chip.style.opacity = '.55'; });
      _chip.addEventListener('click', () => setMasterHidden(false));
      document.documentElement.appendChild(_chip);
    }
    if (!hidden && _chip) { _chip.remove(); _chip = null; }
    try { window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('ae-master-hidden', { detail: { hidden } })); } catch (e) { /* */ }
  }

  // Live re-application when the stored revision changes (overseer edits).
  let _lastRev;
  let _watching = false;
  function _applyAll() {
    _panels.forEach(p => { placeHost(p.host, p.scriptId, p.shadow); if (p.shadow) adopt(p.shadow); });
    _applyMasterHidden();
  }
  function refit() { _panels.forEach(fitVertically); }
  function startLiveUpdates() {
    if (_watching || typeof window === 'undefined') return;
    _watching = true;
    _lastRev = readStore().rev;
    _applyMasterHidden();     // persisted state applies on every page load
    const check = () => { const rev = readStore().rev; if (rev !== _lastRev) { _lastRev = rev; _applyAll(); } };
    window.addEventListener('storage', (e) => { if (!e || e.key === STORE_KEY) check(); });
    setInterval(check, 1500);
  }

  if (typeof window !== 'undefined') {
    window.addEventListener('resize', () => {
      clearTimeout(_resizeTimer);
      _resizeTimer = setTimeout(() => _panels.forEach(p => placeHost(p.host, p.scriptId, p.shadow)), 150);
    });
  }

  // ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  // CONVENIENCE: themed + positioned panel shell in one call.
  // ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  /* Build the standard collapse control. DOM-constructed (no innerHTML) so it is
     safe under AE's Trusted-Types CSP. Returns the button; call .set(bool) to
     sync it if collapse state changes elsewhere. */
  function makeToggle(opts) {
    opts = opts || {};
    const btn = document.createElement('button');
    btn.className = 'ae-toggle';
    btn.type = 'button';
    let collapsed = !!opts.collapsed;
    function paint() {
      btn.textContent = collapsed ? '+' : '\u2212';           // + / − (true minus)
      btn.title = collapsed ? 'Expand' : 'Collapse';
      btn.setAttribute('aria-expanded', String(!collapsed));
    }
    btn.set = function (c) { collapsed = !!c; paint(); };
    btn.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
      e.stopPropagation();
      collapsed = !collapsed; paint();
      if (typeof opts.onToggle === 'function') opts.onToggle(collapsed);
    });
    paint();
    return btn;
  }

  function createPanel(opts) {
    opts = opts || {};
    const scriptId = opts.scriptId || '__fallback__';
    const host = document.createElement('div');
    host.setAttribute('data-ae-script', scriptId);
    const shadow = host.attachShadow({ mode: 'open' });
    adopt(shadow, opts.overrides);

    const region = REGIONS[scriptId] || REGIONS.__fallback__;
    const dragClass = (region && region.drag) ? ' ae-panel__bar--drag' : '';

    const root = document.createElement('div');
    root.className = 'ae-panel';
    root.innerHTML = `
      <div class="ae-panel__bar${dragClass}">
        <span class="ae-panel__dot"></span>
        <span class="ae-panel__titles">
          <span class="ae-panel__title"></span>
          <span class="ae-panel__sub"></span>
        </span>
        <span class="ae-panel__status"></span>
      </div>
      <div class="ae-panel__sweep"></div>
      <div class="ae-panel__body"></div>`;
    shadow.appendChild(root);

    root.querySelector('.ae-panel__title').textContent = opts.title || scriptId;
    root.querySelector('.ae-panel__sub').textContent = opts.subtitle || '';

    document.documentElement.appendChild(host);
    placeHost(host, scriptId, shadow);
    startLiveUpdates();

    const dot = root.querySelector('.ae-panel__dot');
    const statusEl = root.querySelector('.ae-panel__status');
    function setStatus(state, text) {
      dot.classList.remove('is-ok', 'is-warn', 'is-bad');
      if (state === 'ok')   dot.classList.add('is-ok');
      if (state === 'warn') dot.classList.add('is-warn');
      if (state === 'bad')  dot.classList.add('is-bad');
      if (text != null) statusEl.textContent = text;
    }

    return {
      host, shadow, root,
      bar: root.querySelector('.ae-panel__bar'),
      body: root.querySelector('.ae-panel__body'),
      dot, statusEl, setStatus, region
    };
  }

  return {
    VERSION, TOKENS, BASE_CSS,
    getOverrides, getLayoutOverrides, store,
    buildTokenBlock, styleText, cssVarsFor, token, adopt,
    layout, REGIONS, getContentBox, computeRect, placeHost, startLiveUpdates, createPanel,
    makeToggle, isMasterHidden, setMasterHidden,
    OVERSEER_ZONE, RESERVED_ZONES, zoneList, assignableZones, parseZone,
    ZONE_SLOTS_DEFAULT, ZONE_SLOTS_MIN, ZONE_SLOTS_MAX, getSlots, setSlots,
    fitVertically, refit
  };
})();

/* =============================================================================
 * AE DATA — shared data layer for the AE Suite
 * Version: 0.1.0
 *
 * PURPOSE
 *   Deduplicate information that multiple modules independently collected about
 *   the SAME entity, without losing any field or changing any panel's UI.
 *
 * TWO ENTITY AXES (deliberately NOT merged with each other)
 *   1. LOCATION (coord)  -> AELocations : one unified catalog, superset fields.
 *      Previously duplicated: Quick Bookmarks (`ae_bm::<server>`, GM) and
 *      Auto-Launch (`ae_bookmarks_v1`, localStorage) each kept their own list
 *      of named coords with zero sharing.
 *   2. PLAYER (name)     -> AEPlayers   : READ-ONLY cross-reference only.
 *      Tech Harvester's intel DB and the Monitor's whitelist/blacklist are NOT
 *      the same kind of record (rich intel vs. tag membership) and are NOT
 *      merged. AEPlayers only joins them for display.
 *
 * DESIGN RULES (per Aran)
 *   - APPEND ONLY. Fields are unioned, never dropped. A module deleting its
 *     entry only removes ITS source tag; the record survives while any other
 *     module still claims it.
 *   - Panels stay visually separate: each module reads a filtered VIEW of the
 *     catalog (its own source), so existing UX is unchanged.
 *   - Legacy keys are READ during migration and then LEFT IN PLACE as backup.
 *     Nothing is deleted.
 *
 * CONTRACT-CRITICAL
 *   AELocations.viewAutolaunch() MUST return exactly {name, coord, safe} objects
 *   — Auto-Launch's destination picker and the `ae_drop_plan` hand-off to the
 *   Pre-Battle Predictor depend on that shape. Covered by harness assertions.
 * ========================================================================== */

const AEData = (function () {
  'use strict';

  const VERSION = '0.1.0';

  // Server identity: modules disagree historically.
  //   bookmarks / bookmarks2 / tech-harvester : subdomain  ('borealis')
  //   reports-monitor                          : full host  ('borealis.astroempires.com')
  // Both are derived here so cross-reads work regardless of origin convention.
  const SERVER = (location.hostname.split('.')[0] || 'unknown').toLowerCase();
  const SERVER_FULL = location.hostname.toLowerCase();

  const LOC_KEY = 'ae::locations::v1';

  // Legacy sources (read for migration; never written, never deleted)
  const LEGACY_BM_GM = `ae_bm::${SERVER}`;      // Quick Bookmarks   (GM storage)
  const LEGACY_AL_LS = 'ae_bookmarks_v1';       // Auto-Launch       (localStorage, {server: [...]})

  const SRC_BOOKMARKS  = 'bookmarks';
  const SRC_AUTOLAUNCH = 'autolaunch';

  /* ---------- storage primitives (GM when present, localStorage fallback) ---- */
  function gmGet(k, d) {
    try { if (typeof GM_getValue === 'function') return GM_getValue(k, d); } catch (e) {}
    try { const v = localStorage.getItem(k); return v === null ? d : v; } catch (e) { return d; }
  }
  function gmSet(k, v) {
    try { if (typeof GM_setValue === 'function') { GM_setValue(k, v); return true; } } catch (e) {}
    try { localStorage.setItem(k, v); return true; } catch (e) { return false; }
  }
  function lsGet(k, d) {
    try { const v = localStorage.getItem(k); return v === null ? d : v; } catch (e) { return d; }
  }

  const norm = c => (c == null ? null : String(c).trim().toUpperCase()) || null;
  const uid = () => 'l' + Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 9);

  /* ---------- catalog read/write -------------------------------------------
     Shape: { <server>: { records: [ record ] }, _rev: n }
     record = {
       id, coord|null, name, kind, baseId, path, note, addedAt,   // bookmarks-origin
       safe,                                                       // autolaunch-origin
       sources: { bookmarks?: true, autolaunch?: true },
       lastSeen
     }
     Dedupe identity: coord (uppercased) when present, else id.
  -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */
  function readAll() {
    try { return JSON.parse(gmGet(LOC_KEY, '{}')) || {}; } catch (e) { return {}; }
  }
  function writeAll(all) {
    all._rev = (all._rev || 0) + 1;
    return gmSet(LOC_KEY, JSON.stringify(all));
  }
  function records(all) {
    const a = all || readAll();
    return (a[SERVER] && Array.isArray(a[SERVER].records)) ? a[SERVER].records : [];
  }
  function identity(rec) { return rec.coord ? 'C:' + rec.coord : 'I:' + rec.id; }

  /* Merge one incoming record into the list under a given source tag.
     Union semantics: existing scalar fields are only filled if absent, EXCEPT
     fields the calling source owns (passed in `owned`), which it may update. */
  function upsert(list, incoming, source, owned) {
    incoming.coord = norm(incoming.coord);
    const key = incoming.coord ? 'C:' + incoming.coord : null;
    let rec = key ? list.find(r => identity(r) === key)
                  : list.find(r => r.id && r.id === incoming.id);
    if (!rec) {
      rec = { id: incoming.id || uid(), coord: incoming.coord, sources: {} };
      list.push(rec);
    }
    Object.keys(incoming).forEach(f => {
      if (f === 'id' || f === 'sources' || f === 'name') return;   // `name` handled per-source below
      const v = incoming[f];
      if (v === undefined || v === null || v === '') return;
      const isOwned = owned && owned.indexOf(f) !== -1;
      if (isOwned || rec[f] === undefined || rec[f] === null || rec[f] === '') rec[f] = v;
    });
    // PER-SOURCE LABELS: the two modules legitimately hold different names for
    // the same coord (e.g. 'Front' vs 'Front line'). Collapsing them into one
    // field destroys a label, so each source keeps its own. `name` remains as a
    // display fallback for any consumer that just wants "a" label.
    if (incoming.name) {
      rec.names = rec.names || {};
      rec.names[source] = incoming.name;
      if (!rec.name) rec.name = incoming.name;
    }
    rec.sources[source] = true;
    rec.lastSeen = Date.now();
    return rec;
  }

  /* SHARED-LIST semantics.
     Both panels now show the SAME coords (a coord added in either is visible in
     both), so a delete in one panel must actually delete — leaving the record
     alive in the other panel would be confusing, since the other panel shows it.

     But each panel only *sees* part of the catalog, so a save may only delete
     within ITS OWN VIEW SCOPE:
       - Quick Bookmarks sees everything          -> scope = all records
       - Destinations sees only launchable coords -> scope = records with a coord
     Without the scope, saving from Destinations would wipe the non-coord (base /
     link) bookmarks it cannot even display.

     Fields are still UNIONed and per-source labels preserved: a panel never
     clobbers a field it does not own. */
  function replaceScoped(source, list, owned, inScope) {
    const all = readAll();
    all[SERVER] = all[SERVER] || { records: [] };
    const recs = all[SERVER].records;

    const kept = {};
    list.forEach(item => { kept[identity(upsert(recs, Object.assign({}, item), source, owned))] = true; });

    all[SERVER].records = recs.filter(r => {
      if (!inScope(r)) return true;                 // invisible to this panel -> untouched
      if (kept[identity(r)]) return true;           // still in the saved list
      return false;                                 // visibly deleted by the user
    });
    writeAll(all);
    return all[SERVER].records;
  }

  /* ---------- views (each module sees only its own list; UX unchanged) ------ */
  /* Quick Bookmarks sees the WHOLE catalog — including coords first added in
     Destinations. Its own per-source label is used when it has one. */
  function viewBookmarks() {
    return records()
      .map(r => ({
        id: r.id, kind: r.kind || (r.coord ? 'coord' : 'link'), coord: r.coord || null,
        baseId: r.baseId || null, path: r.path || '',
        name: (r.names && r.names.bookmarks) || r.name || '',
        note: r.note || '', addedAt: r.addedAt || r.lastSeen || Date.now()
      }));
  }
  function saveBookmarks(list) {
    // owns the bookmark-specific fields; never clobbers Destinations' `safe`
    return replaceScoped(SRC_BOOKMARKS, list,
      ['kind', 'baseId', 'path', 'note', 'addedAt'],
      () => true);                       // sees everything -> may delete anything
  }

  /* Destinations sees every LAUNCHABLE coord (base/link bookmarks have no coord
     and cannot be a move target, so they are excluded).
     CONTRACT: exactly {name, coord, safe} — the destination picker and the
     ae_drop_plan hand-off depend on this shape. */
  const launchable = r => !!r.coord;
  function viewAutolaunch() {
    return records()
      .filter(launchable)
      .map(r => ({
        name: (r.names && r.names.autolaunch) || r.name || r.coord || '',
        coord: r.coord || '', safe: !!r.safe
      }));
  }
  function saveAutolaunch(list) {
    // owns only `safe` (+ its own label); scoped so it cannot delete the
    // non-coord bookmarks it never displays
    return replaceScoped(SRC_AUTOLAUNCH, list, ['safe'], launchable);
  }

  /* Full catalog (both sources unioned) — for future consumers / overseer. */
  function allLocations() { return records().slice(); }

  /* ---------- one-time migration (union-merge; legacy keys left intact) ----- */
  function migrateLocations() {
    const all = readAll();
    if (all[SERVER] && all[SERVER].migrated) return { skipped: true };
    all[SERVER] = all[SERVER] || { records: [] };
    const recs = all[SERVER].records;
    let fromBm = 0, fromAl = 0;

    try {
      const legacy = JSON.parse(gmGet(LEGACY_BM_GM, '[]')) || [];
      legacy.forEach(b => {
        upsert(recs, {
          id: b.id, coord: b.coord, kind: b.kind, baseId: b.baseId, path: b.path,
          name: b.name, note: b.note, addedAt: b.addedAt
        }, SRC_BOOKMARKS, null);
        fromBm++;
      });
    } catch (e) { console.warn('[AEData] bookmarks migration failed', e); }

    try {
      const blob = JSON.parse(lsGet(LEGACY_AL_LS, '{}')) || {};
      (blob[SERVER] || []).forEach(b => {
        upsert(recs, { coord: b.coord, name: b.name, safe: !!b.safe }, SRC_AUTOLAUNCH, null);
        fromAl++;
      });
    } catch (e) { console.warn('[AEData] autolaunch migration failed', e); }

    all[SERVER].migrated = VERSION;
    writeAll(all);
    const merged = recs.filter(r => r.sources[SRC_BOOKMARKS] && r.sources[SRC_AUTOLAUNCH]).length;
    console.log(`[AEData] locations migrated: ${fromBm} bookmarks + ${fromAl} autolaunch -> ` +
                `${recs.length} records (${merged} shared coords deduped). Legacy keys kept as backup.`);
    return { fromBm, fromAl, total: recs.length, merged };
  }

  /* ---------- PLAYER cross-reference (READ-ONLY; no merging, no writes) ----- */
  const TECH_KEY = `aeTechHarvester::${SERVER}`;
  const WL_KEY   = `${SERVER_FULL}::aeplus_rep_whitelist`;
  const BL_KEY   = `${SERVER_FULL}::aeplus_rep_blacklist`;

  function tags(key) {
    return String(gmGet(key, '') || '').split(',').map(s => s.trim().toUpperCase()).filter(Boolean);
  }
  function techDB() {
    try { return (JSON.parse(gmGet(TECH_KEY, '{}')) || {}).players || {}; } catch (e) { return {}; }
  }

  /* Join by player name. Returns null-safe summary; never mutates either store. */
  function lookupPlayer(name) {
    const n = String(name || '').trim();
    if (!n) return null;
    const up = n.toUpperCase();
    const wl = tags(WL_KEY), bl = tags(BL_KEY);
    const db = techDB();
    let intel = null;
    for (const id in db) {
      const p = db[id];
      if (p && String(p.name || '').trim().toUpperCase() === up) { intel = p; break; }
    }
    return {
      name: n,
      whitelisted: wl.indexOf(up) !== -1,
      blacklisted: bl.indexOf(up) !== -1,
      known: !!intel,
      techs: intel ? (intel.techs || {}) : null,
      guild: intel ? (intel.guild || null) : null,
      lastSeen: intel ? (intel.lastSeen || null) : null
    };
  }

  return {
    VERSION, SERVER, SERVER_FULL, LOC_KEY,
    // locations
    viewBookmarks, saveBookmarks, viewAutolaunch, saveAutolaunch,
    allLocations, migrateLocations,
    // players (read-only)
    lookupPlayer,
    // internals (harness)
    _readAll: readAll, _writeAll: writeAll, _records: records, _upsert: upsert
  };
})();

/* =============================================================================
 * AE MAP INTEL — rival base database
 * Version: 0.1.0 (PARSER SEAM UNIMPLEMENTED — see parseBasesTable)
 *
 * WHAT THIS IS
 *   A searchable index of every base seen on the map: owner, guild, level,
 *   economy, coord, fleet presence. Built from /map.aspx pages.
 *
 * ACQUISITION STRATEGY (per Aran: "lazy + opt-in throttled crawl")
 *   1. LAZY (always on, zero anomalous traffic): whenever the user *visits* a
 *      map page during normal play, harvest it. The DB grows from ordinary use.
 *   2. CRAWL (opt-in, user-triggered): a throttled, jittered, RESUMABLE walk of
 *      a coord range. Never runs on its own. Pauses on tab-hide, stops on error.
 *      This is the loudest thing in the suite — see DETECTABILITY below.
 *
 * DETECTABILITY (stated user concern — do not silently weaken these)
 *   Sequential authenticated fetches are the most automation-like signal the
 *   suite produces. Mitigations, all deliberate:
 *     - jittered delay between fetches (±30%), never a fixed cadence
 *     - a hard per-session fetch cap
 *     - resumable: stop any time, continue later; no need for one long burst
 *     - a page the user is already on is parsed for free (lazy path)
 *   Defaults are conservative. Raising them is the user's call, not ours.
 *
 * STORAGE  `ae::bases::v1`  (GM, private, shared across the suite, per server)
 *   { <server>: { bases: { <coord>: BaseRecord }, meta: {...} } }
 *   BaseRecord grows by UNION — new fields append, existing ones are never
 *   dropped (same rule as AEData locations).
 * ========================================================================== */

const AEMapIntel = (function () {
  'use strict';

  const VERSION = '0.1.0';
  const STORE_KEY = 'ae::bases::v1';
  const SERVER = (location.hostname.split('.')[0] || 'unknown').toLowerCase();

  /* Crawl pacing is USER-DEFINED (Aran's call), with the SAME jitter model the
     report scanner already uses — ±30% plus a sub-second smear, so the cadence
     is never machine-regular no matter what delay is chosen. Defaults are a
     conservative starting point, not a ceiling. */
  const CRAWL_DEFAULTS = {
    baseDelaySec: 3,        // mean delay between fetches (user-adjustable)
    floorSec: 0.6,          // min average with jitter (user-adjustable, hard min 0.6)
    jitterPct: 0.30,        // ± jitter fraction (user-adjustable)
    tradePlunder: false,    // opt-in: fetch each base's trade sub-page for TR plunder
    maxPerSession: 250,     // hard cap per run; resumable, so not a coverage limit
    stopOnErrors: 3         // consecutive failures abort the run
  };
  const HARD_FLOOR = 0.6;   // never below this regardless of settings (server courtesy)
  function getCrawlCfg() {
    const { blk } = serverBlock();
    const c = (blk.meta && blk.meta.crawl) || {};
    const floor = Math.max(HARD_FLOOR, parseFloat(c.floorSec) || CRAWL_DEFAULTS.floorSec);
    return {
      baseDelaySec: Math.max(floor, parseFloat(c.baseDelaySec) || CRAWL_DEFAULTS.baseDelaySec),
      floorSec: floor,
      jitterPct: (c.jitterPct != null && !isNaN(parseFloat(c.jitterPct))) ? Math.max(0, Math.min(0.9, parseFloat(c.jitterPct))) : CRAWL_DEFAULTS.jitterPct,
      tradePlunder: !!c.tradePlunder,
      maxPerSession: Math.max(1, Math.min(2000, parseInt(c.maxPerSession, 10) || CRAWL_DEFAULTS.maxPerSession)),
      stopOnErrors: CRAWL_DEFAULTS.stopOnErrors
    };
  }
  function setCrawlCfg(patch) {
    const { all, blk } = serverBlock();
    blk.meta = blk.meta || {};
    blk.meta.crawl = Object.assign({}, blk.meta.crawl, patch || {});
    writeAll(all);
    return getCrawlCfg();
  }

  /* ---------- storage ------------------------------------------------------ */
  function gmGet(k, d) {
    try { if (typeof GM_getValue === 'function') return GM_getValue(k, d); } catch (e) {}
    try { const v = localStorage.getItem(k); return v === null ? d : v; } catch (e) { return d; }
  }
  function gmSet(k, v) {
    try { if (typeof GM_setValue === 'function') { GM_setValue(k, v); return true; } } catch (e) {}
    try { localStorage.setItem(k, v); return true; } catch (e) { return false; }
  }
  function readAll() { try { return JSON.parse(gmGet(STORE_KEY, '{}')) || {}; } catch (e) { return {}; } }
  function writeAll(a) { return gmSet(STORE_KEY, JSON.stringify(a || {})); }
  function serverBlock() {
    const all = readAll();
    all[SERVER] = all[SERVER] || { bases: {}, meta: { created: Date.now() } };
    return { all, blk: all[SERVER] };
  }

  const normCoord = c => (c == null ? null : String(c).trim().toUpperCase()) || null;

  /* Union-merge a parsed base into the index. Fields APPEND: a later scan that
     lacks a field (e.g. economy not visible) must not erase a value an earlier
     scan captured. `seen` history lets us show staleness and detect changes. */
  /* Delete all stored bases whose coord falls within a scope string (same grammar as
     scans: 'B35', 'B35:45-B35:47', 'B34:43:56', a single astro, or a comma list).
     Returns { deleted, coords } — deleted count + the coords removed. This is permanent
     (the whole point of the button); the DB tab routes here via the command channel. */
  function deleteBasesInRange(scopeText) {
    const matcher = scopeMatcher(scopeText);
    if (!matcher) return { deleted: 0, coords: [] };
    const { all, blk } = serverBlock();
    const removed = [];
    Object.keys(blk.bases).forEach(coord => {
      if (matcher.test(coord)) { delete blk.bases[coord]; removed.push(coord); }
    });
    if (removed.length) { writeAll(all); bumpRev(); }
    return { deleted: removed.length, coords: removed };
  }

  function upsertBase(rec) {
    const coord = normCoord(rec.coord);
    if (!coord) return null;
    const { all, blk } = serverBlock();
    const prev = blk.bases[coord] || { coord, firstSeen: Date.now(), seen: 0 };
    const next = Object.assign({}, prev);

    Object.keys(rec).forEach(k => {
      const v = rec[k];
      if (v === undefined || v === null || v === '') return;   // never overwrite with nothing
      next[k] = v;
    });

    // ownership change is intel in itself — keep a short trail
    if (prev.player && rec.player && prev.player !== rec.player) {
      next.ownerHistory = (prev.ownerHistory || []).concat([{ from: prev.player, to: rec.player, ts: Date.now() }]).slice(-5);
    }
    next.coord = coord;
    next.lastSeen = Date.now();
    next.seen = (prev.seen || 0) + 1;
    blk.bases[coord] = next;
    writeAll(all);
    recordJumpGate(next);   // lazy JG-level capture for the scout killer's speed boost
    bumpRev();
    return next;
  }

  /* Jump Gate registry: a shared, per-region record of the JG level + owning guild seen on
     base pages, keyed by galaxy:region. Filled lazily by upsertBase whenever a base carrying
     a Jump Gate structure is viewed; the scout killer reads it to boost a kill fleet that
     departs a friendly (same-guild) JG region by (1 + 0.7 x level). Updated in place when the
     level or guild changes ("fed back if it changed"). */
  const JG_REGISTRY_KEY = 'ae::jgregistry::' + SERVER;
  const SELF_GUILD_KEY = 'ae::selfguild::' + SERVER;
  function regionKey(coord) { const cp = coordParts(coord); return cp ? (cp.galaxy + ':' + String(cp.region).padStart(2, '0')) : null; }
  function getJgRegistry() { try { return JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(JG_REGISTRY_KEY)) || {}; } catch (e) { return {}; } }
  function recordJumpGate(rec) {
    try {
      const lvl = rec && rec.structures && rec.structures['Jump Gate'];
      if (!(lvl > 0) || !rec.coord) return;
      const rk = regionKey(rec.coord); if (!rk) return;
      const reg = getJgRegistry();
      const cur = reg[rk];
      const guild = rec.guild || (cur && cur.guild) || null;
      // keep the highest level seen in the region; refresh guild/coord/time on any change
      if (!cur || lvl > cur.level || cur.coord === rec.coord || guild !== cur.guild) {
        const level = cur && cur.coord !== rec.coord ? Math.max(lvl, cur.level || 0) : lvl;
        reg[rk] = { level: level, guild: guild, coord: rec.coord, at: Date.now() };
        localStorage.setItem(JG_REGISTRY_KEY, JSON.stringify(reg));
      }
    } catch (e) {}
  }
  /* Friendly (same-guild) JG level for the region a coord sits in. guild = the user's guild;
     when omitted, returns any JG in the region. 0 = none. */
  function friendlyJgLevel(coord, guild) {
    const rk = regionKey(coord); if (!rk) return 0;
    const e = getJgRegistry()[rk];
    if (!e) return 0;
    if (guild && e.guild && e.guild.toUpperCase() !== String(guild).toUpperCase()) return 0;
    return e.level || 0;
  }
  function getSelfGuild() { try { return localStorage.getItem(SELF_GUILD_KEY) || null; } catch (e) { return null; } }
  function setSelfGuild(tag) { try { if (tag) localStorage.setItem(SELF_GUILD_KEY, String(tag).replace(/[\[\]]/g, '').toUpperCase()); } catch (e) {} }

  /* Cross-window change signal: any base write bumps a localStorage counter so an
     open database tab (a separate document) can notice and refresh. localStorage is
     shared across same-origin windows; GM storage is not reliably event-driven. */
  const MIRROR_KEY = 'ae::bases::mirror::' + SERVER;
  function bumpRev() {
    try {
      const k = 'ae::bases::rev::' + SERVER;
      const v = (parseInt(localStorage.getItem(k), 10) || 0) + 1;
      localStorage.setItem(k, String(v));
      // Mirror the base data to localStorage too. GM storage is per-script and a
      // separate DB tab can't read it; the mirror lets that tab poll + self-refresh
      // without any postMessage handshake.
      localStorage.setItem(MIRROR_KEY, JSON.stringify(allBases()));
    } catch (e) {}
  }
  function currentRev() {
    try { return parseInt(localStorage.getItem('ae::bases::rev::' + SERVER), 10) || 0; } catch (e) { return 0; }
  }

  function allBases() {
    const { blk } = serverBlock();
    return Object.keys(blk.bases).map(k => blk.bases[k]);
  }
  function getBase(coord) {
    const { blk } = serverBlock();
    return blk.bases[normCoord(coord)] || null;
  }
  function stats() {
    const b = allBases();
    const players = new Set(), guilds = new Set();
    let oldest = Infinity, newest = 0;
    b.forEach(x => {
      if (x.player) players.add(x.player);
      if (x.guild) guilds.add(x.guild);
      if (x.lastSeen < oldest) oldest = x.lastSeen;
      if (x.lastSeen > newest) newest = x.lastSeen;
    });
    return { bases: b.length, players: players.size, guilds: guilds.size,
             oldest: isFinite(oldest) ? oldest : null, newest: newest || null };
  }

  /* ---------- cell value parsing -------------------------------------------
     The map page prints defence/structure cells as a RATIO, and the numerator can
     be FRACTIONAL (e.g. "5/5", "10/20", "0.31/10", "0.16/5") — confirmed as literal
     page content, not a derived estimate. So we keep the raw text alongside the
     parsed pair and never round: `0.31` is what the game says, and silently
     turning it into 0 or 1 would be inventing data. */
  function parseRatio(text) {
    const t = String(text == null ? '' : text).trim();
    if (!t || t === '-') return null;
    const m = /^([\d.]+)\s*\/\s*([\d.]+)$/.exec(t);
    if (m) {
      const val = parseFloat(m[1]), max = parseFloat(m[2]);
      if (isNaN(val) || isNaN(max)) return { raw: t };
      return { raw: t, val, max, pct: max ? val / max : null };
    }
    const n = parseFloat(t.replace(/,/g, ''));
    return isNaN(n) ? { raw: t } : { raw: t, val: n };
  }

  /* Plain integers that may carry thousands separators (fleet sums, economy). */
  function parseNum(text) {
    if (text == null) return null;
    const n = parseFloat(String(text).replace(/[,\s]/g, ''));
    return isNaN(n) ? null : n;
  }

  /* "[OOPS] Sean Lee" -> { guild: 'OOPS', player: 'Sean Lee' } */
  function parsePlayerCell(text) {
    const t = String(text || '').trim();
    const m = /^\[([^\]]+)\]\s*(.*)$/.exec(t);
    return m ? { guild: m[1].trim(), player: m[2].trim() } : { guild: null, player: t };
  }

  /* ---------- query / search ----------------------------------------------
     Backs the searchable table (and, later, hover intel + threat board). */
  function search(q) {
    const opts = q || {};
    let rows = allBases();
    if (opts.player) {
      const p = String(opts.player).toUpperCase();
      rows = rows.filter(r => String(r.player || '').toUpperCase().indexOf(p) !== -1);
    }
    if (opts.guild) {
      const g = String(opts.guild).toUpperCase();
      rows = rows.filter(r => String(r.guild || '').toUpperCase().indexOf(g) !== -1);
    }
    if (opts.coordPrefix) {
      const c = normCoord(opts.coordPrefix);
      rows = rows.filter(r => r.coord.indexOf(c) === 0);
    }
    if (typeof opts.minLevel === 'number') rows = rows.filter(r => (r.level || 0) >= opts.minLevel);
    if (typeof opts.minEco === 'number')   rows = rows.filter(r => (r.economy || 0) >= opts.minEco);
    if (typeof opts.minFleet === 'number') rows = rows.filter(r => (r.fleetSize || 0) >= opts.minFleet);
    if (opts.staleDays) {
      const cut = Date.now() - opts.staleDays * 864e5;
      rows = rows.filter(r => r.lastSeen >= cut);
    }
    const key = opts.sort || 'economy';
    const dir = opts.desc === false ? 1 : -1;
    rows.sort((a, b) => {
      const x = a[key], y = b[key];
      if (typeof x === 'string' || typeof y === 'string') {
        return String(x || '').localeCompare(String(y || '')) * -dir;
      }
      return ((x || 0) - (y || 0)) * dir;
    });
    return rows;
  }

  function toCSV(rows) {
    const cols = ['coord', 'player', 'guild', 'baseName', 'level', 'economy', 'fleetSize', 'lastSeen', 'seen'];
    const esc = v => {
      const s = (v == null) ? '' : String(v);
      return /[",\n]/.test(s) ? '"' + s.replace(/"/g, '""') + '"' : s;
    };
    const head = cols.join(',');
    const body = rows.map(r => cols.map(c => esc(c === 'lastSeen' && r[c] ? new Date(r[c]).toISOString() : r[c])).join(',')).join('\n');
    return head + '\n' + body;
  }

  /* =========================================================================
   * ⚠ PARSER SEAM — NOT IMPLEMENTED. THIS IS THE ONLY UNVERIFIED PIECE.
   *
   * The /map.aspx bases table is NOT documented anywhere in the project's dev
   * context (which covers only the player's OWN empire/base pages), and the
   * existing reports-monitor parser deliberately scopes to `table#map_fleets`
   * and never touches the bases listing. Writing this from guesswork would be
   * unverified logic shipped as fact — so it throws until a real page sample
   * pins the table id/class, the column order, and the link hrefs that carry
   * player/base IDs.
   *
   * CONTRACT once implemented — return an array of:
   *   { coord, baseName, baseId, player, playerId, guild, level, economy,
   *     fleetSize, planetType }
   * Any field may be absent (map visibility varies); upsertBase() unions, so a
   * missing field never erases a previously captured one.
   * ========================================================================= */
  // Verified against real /map.aspx samples (base, empty+debris, system, galaxy).
  // A map astro page carries at most ONE base, in table#map_base:
  //   Base{base.aspx?base=ID} | Owner{profile?player=ID} | Occupier{...} | Eco "cur / max"
  // Astro type/terrain/area live in table.astro; structures/defenses (visible for
  // ANY base, confirmed on a rival) in table#base_resume-structures.
  function txt(el) { return el ? el.textContent.replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim() : ''; }
  // Match by scanning hrefs (a CSS [href*="x?y"] selector chokes on the '?').
  /* Find a base.aspx?base=ID link anywhere on a page. The map astro page links to the
     base's own page; the live DOM may place that link outside the #map_base table my
     fixture used, so scan all anchors as a fallback. Returns the id or null. */
  function findBaseLink(doc) {
    const anchors = [...doc.querySelectorAll('a[href*="base.aspx"], a[href*="base="]')];
    for (const a of anchors) {
      const m = /base=(\d+)/.exec(a.getAttribute('href') || '');
      if (m) return m[1];
    }
    return null;
  }

  function idFrom(el, pageParam) {
    if (!el) return null;
    const anchors = [...el.querySelectorAll('a[href]')];
    for (const a of anchors) {
      const m = new RegExp(pageParam.replace('.', '\\.') + '=(\\d+)').exec(a.getAttribute('href') || '');
      if (m) return m[1];
    }
    return null;
  }

  function parseAstroHeader(doc) {
    const a = doc.querySelector('table.astro, table.base');
    if (!a) return { planetType: null, terrain: null, area: null };
    // The header is "Label: value" pairs separated by <br>; read each field as
    // exactly up to the next label so a greedy match cannot swallow the rest.
    const t = a.textContent.replace(/\s+/g, ' ');
    const field = (label) => {
      const re = new RegExp(label + ':\\s*(.*?)\\s*(?:Astro Type|Terrain|Area|Solar Energy|Fertility|Resources|Metal|Gas|Crystals)\\s*:?|' + label + ':\\s*(.*)$');
      const m = re.exec(t);
      const v = m ? (m[1] != null ? m[1] : m[2]) : null;
      return v ? v.trim() : null;
    };
    const areaM = /Area:\s*(\d+)/.exec(t);
    return {
      planetType: field('Astro Type'),
      terrain: field('Terrain'),
      area: areaM ? +areaM[1] : null
    };
  }

  /* Base-page header (base.aspx only): Base Name | Location | Area | Energy |
     Population | Trade Routes. Values are "used/max" ratios; Trade Routes (TR) is
     detail-scan data — it exists ONLY on the base page, so it is collected only
     when a detail scan visits base.aspx. */
  function parseBaseHeader(doc) {
    // Live page: table.base_top, header row shows the ACTUAL base name (not the label
    // "Base Name"), then Location | Area | Energy | Population | Trade Routes. Match by
    // class first, then fall back to the Trade Routes + Population label pair.
    let tbl = doc.querySelector('table.base_top');
    if (!tbl) {
      const tables = [...doc.querySelectorAll('table')];
      tbl = tables.find(t => /Trade Routes/i.test(t.textContent) && /Population/i.test(t.textContent));
    }
    if (!tbl) return null;
    const rows = [...tbl.querySelectorAll('tr')];
    // The data row is the one carrying the location coord link.
    const dataRow = rows.find(r => r.querySelector('a[href*="loc="]'));
    if (!dataRow) return null;
    const c = [...dataRow.cells];
    const ratio = i => (c[i] ? parseRatio(txt(c[i])) : null);
    // Columns: 0 name | 1 Location(coord) | 2 Area | 3 Energy | 4 Population | 5 Trade Routes
    return {
      baseName: c[0] ? txt(c[0]) : null,
      area: ratio(2), energy: ratio(3), population: ratio(4),
      tradeRoutes: ratio(5)
    };
  }

  /* Base-page fleet table (table#map_fleets): Fleet | Player "[Guild] Name"{player=ID}
     | Arrival | Size. MULTIPLE players' fleets are listed, and one player can appear on
     several rows, so sizes are SUMMED per player. Given the base owner's id + guild, the
     total splits three ways (Aran's spec):
       ownerFleet      = sum of the owner's own fleets at the base
       guildFleet      = sum of NON-owner fleets whose guild == the owner's guild
       otherFleet      = sum of everyone else (different guild or unguilded)
     Sizes carry thousands separators ("565,900") -> parsed with parseNum. */
  function parseFleets(doc, ownerId, ownerGuild) {
    // Base page uses #base_fleets; map astro page uses #map_fleets. The id'd element
    // may be a wrapper whose real rows live in an inner sorttable, so gather fleet
    // rows from within whichever container exists (a row = >=4 cells + a player link).
    const container = doc.querySelector('#base_fleets, #map_fleets');
    if (!container) return null;
    let rows = [...container.querySelectorAll('tr')].filter(r => r.cells.length >= 4 &&
      r.querySelector('a[href*="player="]'));
    // Fallback: some layouts put the fleet rows in a sibling sorttable, not inside
    // the id'd wrapper. If the wrapper yielded nothing, scan nearby sorttables.
    if (!rows.length) {
      const alt = [...doc.querySelectorAll('table.sorttable, table.tbllisting1')]
        .find(t => /player=/.test(t.innerHTML) && /Size/i.test(t.textContent));
      if (alt) rows = [...alt.querySelectorAll('tr')].filter(r => r.cells.length >= 4 &&
        r.querySelector('a[href*="player="]'));
    }
    if (!rows.length) return null;
    let owner = 0, guild = 0, other = 0, ownerN = 0, guildN = 0, otherN = 0;
    const og = ownerGuild ? String(ownerGuild).toUpperCase() : null;
    rows.forEach(r => {
      const c = [...r.cells];
      const pid = idFrom(c[1], 'player');
      const who = parsePlayerCell(txt(c[1]));
      const g = who.guild ? who.guild.toUpperCase() : null;
      const size = parseNum(txt(c[3])) || 0;
      if (ownerId && pid === String(ownerId)) { owner += size; ownerN++; }
      else if (og && g === og)                { guild += size; guildN++; }
      else                                    { other += size; otherN++; }
    });
    return {
      ownerFleet: owner, guildFleet: guild, otherFleet: other,
      ownerFleets: ownerN, guildFleets: guildN, otherFleets: otherN,
      totalFleet: owner + guild + other
    };
  }

  /* Economy/capacity block (#base_processing-capacities) — base owner, owner income,
     economy, construction, production, research. Adapted from a working reference
     collector; these are core base fields the earlier version was missing entirely. */
  function parseCapacities(doc) {
    const box = doc.querySelector('#base_processing-capacities');
    if (!box) return null;
    const table = box.querySelector('table.layout.listing3') || box;
    const out = { owner: null, ownerIncome: null, economy: null, construction: null, production: null, research: null };
    [...table.querySelectorAll('tr')].forEach(tr => {
      const tds = tr.querySelectorAll('td');
      if (!tds.length) return;
      const label = txt(tds[0]).toLowerCase();
      const value = tds[1] ? txt(tds[1]) : '';
      const n = () => parseNum(value);
      if (label === 'base owner') { const a = tr.querySelector('a[href*="profile.aspx?player="]'); out.owner = a ? txt(a) : value; }
      else if (label === 'economy') out.economy = n();
      else if (label === 'owner income') out.ownerIncome = n();
      else if (label === 'construction') out.construction = n();
      else if (label === 'production') out.production = n();
      else if (label === 'research') out.research = n();
    });
    return out;
  }

  /* AE stamps each base page with how STALE the intel is ("recorded data from X
     ago"). This is intel about the intel — a months-old reading is far weaker than
     a fresh one. Distinct from our own scan timestamp (lastSeen). */
  /* Trade-route plunder from the base's trade sub-page (base.aspx?base=ID&view=trade).
     TWO layouts exist:
       - OWN base:  Base|Player|Economy|Distance|Status|Income|Cancel  (sorttable,
         distance in sorttable_customkey), header in <thead>.
       - NON-OWNER (exposed): Linked to|Player|Distance|Status  (plain table, distance
         is plain text, header is a tr.listing-header inside tbody, no sorttable).
     TRs are only visible when the base is UNFOGGED (Intel Age blank). A fogged base has
     no #base_trade table -> returns null (caller leaves trPlunder untouched).
     Plunder per route = 2 * distance; returns { plunder, routes } summed, or null. */
  function parseTradePlunder(doc) {
    const box = doc.querySelector('#base_trade');
    if (!box) return null;
    const table = box.querySelector('table.sorttable') || box.querySelector('table.listing') || box.querySelector('table table') || box.querySelector('table');
    if (!table) return null;
    // Locate the Distance column from whichever row holds the headers (thead th OR the
    // first row of th's inside the table).
    let headerCells = [...table.querySelectorAll('thead th')];
    if (!headerCells.length) {
      const firstThRow = table.querySelector('tr'); // header row is the first tr of th's
      if (firstThRow && firstThRow.querySelector('th')) headerCells = [...firstThRow.querySelectorAll('th')];
    }
    const heads = headerCells.map(th => txt(th).toLowerCase());
    let distCol = heads.indexOf('distance');
    if (distCol < 0) return null;   // no Distance column -> can't compute
    let plunder = 0, routes = 0;
    // data rows = tr's that have td's (skip the header th row)
    [...table.querySelectorAll('tr')].forEach(tr => {
      const tds = tr.querySelectorAll('td');
      if (tds.length <= distCol) return;
      const cell = tds[distCol];
      const key = cell.getAttribute('sorttable_customkey');
      const dist = parseNum(key != null ? key : txt(cell));
      if (dist == null || isNaN(dist)) return;
      plunder += 2 * dist;
      routes++;
    });
    return { plunder, routes };
  }

  /* ── RANKINGS (top players) ─────────────────────────────────────────────
     LOCKED against real captures (ranks_{level,economy,fleet,technology}.html):
     URL /ranks.aspx?view=ply_<stat>&see=<page>, 100 players/page, top 1000 ranked.
     Each view's table (#ranks_table-players inner listing) shows ONLY its own stat:
     header  Rank | Player | <Fleet|Economy|Level|Technology> ; player cell is
     "[Guild] Name" linked to profile.aspx?player=ID. A full per-player record is
     therefore MERGED from multiple views. */
  const RANK_VIEWS = ['ply_fleet', 'ply_level', 'ply_economy', 'ply_technology'];
  const RANK_KEY = 'ae::rankings::' + SERVER;
  const RANKMIRROR_KEY = 'ae::rankmirror::' + SERVER;
  function parseRankingsPage(doc) {
    const out = [];
    for (const tbl of doc.querySelectorAll('table')) {
      const first = tbl.querySelector('tr'); if (!first) continue;
      const heads = [...first.querySelectorAll('th,td')].map(c => txt(c).toLowerCase());
      if (heads.length < 2) continue;   // guard: the outer wrapper table has ONE header cell
      // ("Top Players Ranked By Fleet") which contains both 'player' and the stat word —
      // the real listing has distinct Rank | Player | <Stat> columns.
      const playerIdx0 = heads.findIndex(h => h.includes('player'));
      if (playerIdx0 < 0) continue;
      const statIdx = { level: heads.findIndex(h => h.includes('level')),
                        fleet: heads.findIndex(h => h.includes('fleet')),
                        economy: heads.findIndex(h => h.includes('econom')),
                        tech: heads.findIndex(h => h.includes('tech')) };
      Object.keys(statIdx).forEach(k => { if (statIdx[k] === playerIdx0) statIdx[k] = -1; });
      if (Object.values(statIdx).every(i => i < 0)) continue;   // no distinct stat column
      const rankIdx = heads.findIndex(h => h.includes('rank'));
      const playerIdx = playerIdx0;
      for (const tr of [...tbl.querySelectorAll('tr')].slice(1)) {
        const tds = tr.querySelectorAll('td'); if (tds.length <= playerIdx) continue;
        const link = tds[playerIdx].querySelector('a[href*="player="]');
        if (!link) continue;
        const m = (link.getAttribute('href') || '').match(/player=(\d+)/);
        const pid = m ? m[1] : null;
        let name = txt(link), guild = null;
        const gm = name.match(/^\[([^\]]+)\]\s*(.*)$/);
        if (gm) { guild = gm[1]; name = gm[2]; }
        const rec = { playerId: pid, name, guild, rank: rankIdx >= 0 && tds[rankIdx] ? parseNum(txt(tds[rankIdx])) : null };
        for (const [k, i] of Object.entries(statIdx)) if (i >= 0 && tds[i]) rec[k] = parseNum(txt(tds[i]));
        out.push(rec);
      }
      if (out.length) return out;
    }
    return out;
  }
  /* Fetch + merge rankings. Defaults: all 4 views x pages 1..3 = top 300 with full
     stats (12 paced fetches). opts: {views, pages, intervalSec}. Rank stored per view
     as <stat>Rank; the row's own stat merges into the player record. */
  async function fetchRankings(opts, prog) {
    prog = prog || (() => {});
    const cfg = getCrawlCfg();
    const baseSec = Math.max(cfg.floorSec, (opts && opts.intervalSec) || cfg.baseDelaySec);
    const views = (opts && opts.views) || RANK_VIEWS;
    const pages = (opts && opts.pages) || 3;
    const rkOrigin = (typeof location !== 'undefined' && location.origin) ? location.origin : '';
    const players = {}; let errors = 0; let step = 0; const totalSteps = views.length * pages;
    for (const view of views) {
      const stat = view.replace('ply_', '');
      for (let pg = 1; pg <= pages; pg++) {
        if (step++ > 0) await sleep(jitterMs(baseSec, cfg.floorSec));
        prog({ phase: 'rank', step, total: totalSteps, view: stat, page: pg });
        try {
          const doc = await fetchDoc(rkOrigin + '/ranks.aspx?view=' + view + '&see=' + pg, null);
          const rows = parseRankingsPage(doc);
          rows.forEach(r => {
            const key = r.playerId || ('name:' + r.name);
            const p = players[key] || { playerId: r.playerId, name: r.name, guild: r.guild };
            if (r.guild) p.guild = r.guild;
            const statKey = stat === 'technology' ? 'tech' : stat;
            if (r[statKey] != null) { p[statKey] = r[statKey]; p[statKey + 'Rank'] = r.rank; }
            players[key] = p;
          });
          if (!rows.length) { errors++; prog({ phase: 'rank', step, total: totalSteps, view: stat, page: pg, error: 'no rows parsed' }); }
        } catch (e) { errors++; prog({ phase: 'rank', step, total: totalSteps, view: stat, page: pg, error: String(e) }); }
      }
    }
    const blob = { updated: Date.now(), players };
    try { gmSet(RANK_KEY, JSON.stringify(blob)); } catch (e) {}
    try { localStorage.setItem(RANKMIRROR_KEY, JSON.stringify(blob)); } catch (e) {}
    prog({ phase: 'rank-done', count: Object.keys(players).length, errors });
    return { count: Object.keys(players).length, errors };
  }
  function getRankings() {
    try { const raw = gmGet(RANK_KEY, null); if (raw) return JSON.parse(raw); } catch (e) {}
    try { return JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(RANKMIRROR_KEY)); } catch (e) { return null; }
  }
  /* ── DROP CALCULATOR (single-round estimates) ──────────────────────────
     Estimates how many drop units are needed to one-round-kill the unshielded and
     low-shielded (≤ Heavy Cruiser) portions of an enemy fleet.
     Composition estimate: AE fleet SIZE == credit value (verified: the Altair context
     inventory sums by UNIT cost to the stated Total Fleet Cost), so
       count_u = fleetSize × specShare_u / cost_u.
     Kill math mirrors the predictor engine: eff = base × (1 + 0.05 × tech);
     damage vs a shielded target = attack − 0.99×shield (non-ion) when shield < attack,
     else 1% passthrough; pool spread factor 0.848 (predictor FACTOR). */
  const DROP_STATS = {  // base a/r/s + cost + weapon, from the verified game table
    FT:{a:2,r:2,s:0,c:5,w:'Laser'}, BO:{a:5,r:2,s:0,c:10,w:'Missiles'}, HB:{a:10,r:5,s:0,c:30,w:'Plasma'},
    IB:{a:12,r:5,s:1,c:60,w:'Ion'}, CV:{a:4,r:5,s:0,c:20,w:'Laser'}, RC:{a:2,r:2,s:0,c:30,w:'Laser'},
    DD:{a:10,r:8,s:0,c:40,w:'Plasma'}, FR:{a:12,r:12,s:4,c:80,w:'Missiles'}, IF:{a:14,r:12,s:1,c:120,w:'Ion'},
    SS:{a:1,r:2,s:0,c:40,w:'Laser'}, OS:{a:2,r:4,s:0,c:100,w:'Laser'}, CR:{a:24,r:24,s:2,c:200,w:'Plasma'},
    CA:{a:12,r:24,s:2,c:400,w:'Missiles'}, HC:{a:48,r:48,s:4,c:500,w:'Plasma'}, BS:{a:160,r:128,s:12,c:2000,w:'Ion'},
    FC:{a:64,r:96,s:10,c:2500,w:'Ion'}, DN:{a:756,r:512,s:20,c:10000,w:'Photon'}, TI:{a:3500,r:2048,s:30,c:50000,w:'Disruptor'},
    LE:{a:10000,r:6600,s:40,c:200000,w:'Photon'}, DS:{a:28000,r:15000,s:60,c:500000,w:'Disruptor'}
  };
  const DROP_ION = new Set(['IB','IF','BS','FC']);           // ion weapons: 50% shield bypass
  const DEFAULT_ASSUMED_TECH = 15;   // used when a target has no harvested/archetype tech
  const ASSUMED_TECH_KEYS = ['Armour','Shielding','Laser','Missiles','Plasma','Ion','Photon','Disruptor'];
  const DROP_SUPPORT = new Set(['RC','SS','OS','CA','FC']);  // support types (excluded from SR attacker candidates)
  const DROP_FACTOR = 0.848;                       // predictor damage-distribution factor
  const LOWSHIELD_MAX = new Set(['IB','FR','IF','CR','CA','HC']);   // shielded, ≤ HC tier
  /* Core: single-round drop needs from REAL unit counts. */
  function computeDropFromCounts(counts, techs, attacker) {
    if (!counts) return null;
    const arm = 1 + 0.05 * ((techs && techs.Armour) || 0);
    const shl = 1 + 0.05 * ((techs && techs.Shielding) || 0);
    const groups = { unshielded: { units: 0, boNeed: 0, mainNeed: 0 }, lowshield: { units: 0, boNeed: 0, mainNeed: 0 } };
    let any = false;
    for (const [ab, count] of Object.entries(counts)) {
      const st = DROP_STATS[ab]; if (!st || !count) continue;
      any = true;
      const g = st.s === 0 ? 'unshielded' : (LOWSHIELD_MAX.has(ab) ? 'lowshield' : null);
      if (!g) continue;   // heavily shielded capitals are not drop targets
      const effArm = st.r * arm, effShl = st.s * shl;
      const dmgVs = (atk) => (effShl < atk) ? (atk - 0.99 * effShl) : (0.01 * atk);
      const armourPool = count * effArm;
      groups[g].units += count;
      groups[g].boNeed += armourPool / (DROP_FACTOR * dmgVs(attacker.boAttack));
