      groups[g].mainNeed += armourPool / (DROP_FACTOR * dmgVs(attacker.mainAttack));
    }
    if (!any) return null;
    const r = (x) => Math.ceil(x);
    return {
      unshielded: { units: groups.unshielded.units, bo: r(groups.unshielded.boNeed), main: r(groups.unshielded.mainNeed) },
      lowshield:  { units: groups.lowshield.units,  bo: r(groups.lowshield.boNeed),  main: r(groups.lowshield.mainNeed) }
    };
  }
  /* Estimate path: derive counts from spec value-shares x fleet size, then run the core. */
  function computeDropEstimate(target, attacker) {
    if (!target || !target.fleetSize || !target.shares) return null;
    const counts = {};
    for (const [ab, share] of Object.entries(target.shares)) {
      const st = DROP_STATS[ab]; if (!st || !share) continue;
      const c = Math.floor(target.fleetSize * share / st.c); if (c) counts[ab] = c;
    }
    return computeDropFromCounts(counts, target.techs, attacker);
  }
  /* ── 5-line drop plan ───────────────────────────────────────────────────
     L1  BO needed to one-round the UNSHIELDED units (base shield stat = 0)
     L2  BO needed to one-round the LOW-SHIELD units (has a shield stat, tier ≤ HC)
     L3  finisher (main spec) vs what remains after drop 1  (low-shield + heavies)
     L4  finisher (main spec) vs what remains after drop 2  (heavies only)
     L5  SR: best-ratio single-type ONE-round kill of the ENTIRE fleet, chosen from
         the player's OWN parsed composition (modded stats from Empire→Units);
         ratio = my value lost / enemy value killed (lower is better).
     Enemy eff stats: base × (1+0.05×tech) with their weapon techs for return fire.
     Ion weapons (IB/IF/BS/FC) bypass 50% of shields; others 1%. Factor 0.848. */
  function _effDmg(atk, ion, effShl) {
    const mult = ion ? 0.5 : 0.99, pass = ion ? 0.5 : 0.01;
    return (effShl < atk) ? (atk - mult * effShl) : (pass * atk);
  }
  /* ── AUTOSCOUT PATH PREDICTION ─────────────────────────────────────────
     Corrected model (Aran):
     - Autoscouts hop REGION to REGION (not base to base), entering each region at
       the LOWEST system coordinate available; they may appear at any astro of that
       system (bases or not) — so the stop source is the ASTROS enumeration.
     - They dwell ~90 s at an astro before moving to the next region.
     - Guild-mate JUMP GATES speed legs up (+70% fleet speed per JG level), so a leg
       departing a region holding a friendly JG can be much faster.
     Region order is the documented ZIG-ZAG over the map grid (see regionOrderKey);
     a guild-mate JG base in a region is FAVOURED over the lowest-system entry.
     Remaining ASSUMPTION: canonical astro within a non-JG entry system = lowest astro
     number (all astros of the system are exposed as candidates).
     Speed: SS base warp 12, actual = 12 x (1 + 0.05 x W) — verified (W20 -> 24).
     Travel time = distance / speed hours (fleet-move page: duration = distance /
     maxspeed; exact game rounding UNVERIFIED). */
  const SCOUT_BASE_SPEED = 12;
  const SCOUT_DWELL_S = 90;
  function scoutSpeed(warpLevel) { return SCOUT_BASE_SPEED * (1 + 0.05 * (warpLevel || 0)); }
  /* Region traversal is a ZIG-ZAG over the 10x10 region grid (RR = row x 10 + col):
     even rows (0x,2x,..) ascend 0->9, odd rows (1x,3x,..) descend 9->0, wrapping from
     the last EXISTING region back to the first (no galaxy fills all 100). */
  function regionOrderKey(region) {
    const row = Math.floor(region / 10), col = region % 10;
    return row * 10 + (row % 2 === 0 ? col : 9 - col);
  }
  /* rows: astro (preferred) or base records [{coord, owner?, guild?, hasBase?}].
     opts.jgByRegion: Map regionNum -> coord of a guild-mate Jump Gate base — the scout
     FAVOURS that base over the lowest-system entry rule for that region.
     Returns one stop per REGION in zig-zag order: { region, system, coord, candidates,
     hasBases, owners, jgEntry }. */
  function buildScoutPath(rows, opts) {
    const jgBy = (opts && opts.jgByRegion) || null;
    const byRegion = {};
    const seen = new Set();
    for (const r of rows || []) {
      const cp = coordParts(r.coord); if (!cp) continue;
      if (seen.has(r.coord)) continue; seen.add(r.coord);   // report + DB inputs can overlap
      const reg = parseInt(cp.region, 10), sys = parseInt(cp.system, 10), ast = parseInt(String(cp.planet), 10);
      const cur = byRegion[reg];
      if (!cur || sys < cur.system) byRegion[reg] = { region: reg, system: sys, astros: [{ coord: r.coord, astro: ast, hasBase: !!(r.hasBase || r.owner || r.player), owner: r.owner || r.player || null, guild: r.guild || null }] };
      else if (sys === cur.system) cur.astros.push({ coord: r.coord, astro: ast, hasBase: !!(r.hasBase || r.owner || r.player), owner: r.owner || r.player || null, guild: r.guild || null });
    }
    // JG preference: a guild-mate JG base beats the lowest-system rule for its region
    if (jgBy) for (const [reg, coord] of jgBy.entries()) {
      const cp = coordParts(coord); if (!cp) continue;
      byRegion[reg] = { region: reg, system: parseInt(cp.system, 10), jgEntry: true,
        astros: [{ coord, astro: parseInt(String(cp.planet), 10), hasBase: true, owner: null, guild: null }] };
    }
    return Object.values(byRegion).map(e => {
      e.astros.sort((a, b) => a.astro - b.astro);
      return { region: e.region, system: e.system, coord: e.astros[0].coord, jgEntry: !!e.jgEntry,
               candidates: e.astros.map(a => a.coord), hasBases: e.astros.some(a => a.hasBase),
               owners: e.astros.filter(a => a.owner).map(a => a.owner + (a.guild ? ' [' + a.guild + ']' : '')) };
    }).sort((a, b) => regionOrderKey(a.region) - regionOrderKey(b.region));
  }
  /* Predict next region-stops after a sighting anywhere in a region.
     opts: { count=10, dwellSec=90, jgLevel=0, jgRegions:Set<regionNum> } — a leg whose
     ORIGIN region holds a friendly JG gets the fast bound boosted by (1+0.7 x jgLevel);
     the slow bound never assumes a JG. Windows: early = warpMax (+JG where present),
     late = warpMin, both + dwell per stop. Returns { stops, wrapped } or null. */
  function predictScoutStops(path, sightingCoord, sightingTimeMs, warpMin, warpMax, opts) {
    if (!path || !path.length) return null;
    const cp = coordParts(sightingCoord); if (!cp) return null;
    const sightRegion = parseInt(cp.region, 10);
    const idx = path.findIndex(p => p.region === sightRegion);
    if (idx < 0) return null;
    const o = opts || {};
    const dwellH = (o.dwellSec != null ? o.dwellSec : SCOUT_DWELL_S) / 3600;
    const jgMult = 1 + 0.7 * (o.jgLevel || 0);
    const vFast0 = scoutSpeed(Math.max(warpMin || 0, warpMax || 0));
    const vSlow = scoutSpeed(Math.min(warpMin || 0, warpMax || 0));
    const stops = []; let cum = 0, hFast = 0, hSlow = 0, wrapped = false;
    let prev = { coord: sightingCoord, region: sightRegion };
    const count = o.count || 10;
    for (let n = 1; n <= count; n++) {
      let j = idx + n;
      if (j >= path.length) { j = j % path.length; wrapped = true; }
      const stop = path[j];
      if (stop.region === sightRegion && n > 1 && !(o.allowLaps)) break;   // one lap unless rolling
      const d = distance(prev.coord, stop.coord);
      const ly = d ? d.ly : 0;
      const jgHere = o.jgRegions && o.jgRegions.has(prev.region);
      cum += ly;
      hFast += ly / (jgHere ? vFast0 * jgMult : vFast0) + dwellH;
      hSlow += ly / vSlow + dwellH;
      stops.push({ coord: stop.coord, region: stop.region, system: stop.system, jgEntry: stop.jgEntry,
                   candidates: stop.candidates, owners: stop.owners, hasBases: stop.hasBases,
                   distLy: ly, cumLy: Math.round(cum * 100) / 100, viaJg: !!jgHere,
                   etaEarly: sightingTimeMs + hFast * 3600000,
                   etaLate: sightingTimeMs + hSlow * 3600000 });
      prev = stop;
    }
    return { stops, wrapped };
  }

  /* Rolling estimate: when the next-stop window has already passed, the scout has
     landed and moved on — keep walking the path until a stop's window is still open.
     Hops are counted as PASSED when even the SLOW bound (etaLate) is behind now, so
     hopsPassed is a lower bound on how many times it landed+moved since last seen.
     Returns { hopsPassed, stop, status } — status: 'upcoming' | 'window' | 'lost'. */
  function currentScoutEstimate(path, sightingCoord, sightingTimeMs, warpMin, warpMax, nowMs, opts) {
    const maxHops = (opts && opts.maxHops) || 40;
    // scouts lap the galaxy indefinitely — rolling estimates must cross the wrap
    const res = predictScoutStops(path, sightingCoord, sightingTimeMs, warpMin, warpMax,
      Object.assign({}, opts, { count: maxHops, allowLaps: true }));
    if (!res) return null;
    const now = nowMs != null ? nowMs : Date.now();
    let hopsPassed = 0;
    for (const st of res.stops) {
      if (st.etaLate < now) { hopsPassed++; continue; }
      return { hopsPassed, stop: st, status: st.etaEarly <= now ? 'window' : 'upcoming' };
    }
    return { hopsPassed, stop: null, status: 'lost' };   // ran off the horizon
  }
  /* Path distance from coordA forward to coordB along the region path (wrapping once
     if B lies behind A). Returns { ly, hops } or null. */
  function scoutPathDistance(path, coordA, coordB) {
    if (!path || path.length < 2) return null;
    const cpA = coordParts(coordA), cpB = coordParts(coordB);
    if (!cpA || !cpB) return null;
    const ia = path.findIndex(p => p.region === parseInt(cpA.region, 10));
    const ib = path.findIndex(p => p.region === parseInt(cpB.region, 10));
    if (ia < 0 || ib < 0) return null;
    let ly = 0, hops = 0, prev = coordA, j = ia;
    while (true) {
      j = (j + 1) % path.length;
      const d = distance(prev, path[j].coord);
      ly += d ? d.ly : 0; hops++;
      prev = path[j].coord;
      if (j === ib) break;
      if (hops > path.length) return null;   // safety
    }
    return { ly, hops };
  }
  /* Infer a scout owner's Warp Drive level from consecutive sightings of the same
     scout: speed = pathDist / (elapsed - hops x dwell); W = (speed/12 - 1)/0.05.
     sightings: [{coord, t}] (ms, any order). Returns { warp, speed, pairs } or null.
     Median over pairs; implausible pairs (speed <= 0, W outside 0..30) are dropped. */
  function inferScoutWarp(path, sightings, opts) {
    if (!path || !sightings || sightings.length < 2) return null;
    const o = opts || {};
    const jgFilter = o.jgFilter !== false;                 // JG artefact rejection: on by default
    const jgMargin = o.jgMargin != null ? o.jgMargin : 3;  // levels above median a leg may run before it is JG-suspect
    const hardCap = o.warpCap != null ? o.warpCap : 20;    // legs implying warp above this are JG teleports, never real
    const dwellH = ((o.dwellSec != null) ? o.dwellSec : SCOUT_DWELL_S) / 3600;
    const ss = sightings.slice().sort((a, b) => a.t - b.t);
    let ests = [];
    for (let i = 1; i < ss.length; i++) {
      const a = ss[i - 1], b = ss[i];
      const pd = scoutPathDistance(path, a.coord, b.coord);
      if (!pd || pd.ly <= 0) continue;
      const hrs = (b.t - a.t) / 3600000 - pd.hops * dwellH;
      if (hrs <= 0) continue;
      const speed = pd.ly / hrs;
      const warp = (speed / SCOUT_BASE_SPEED - 1) / 0.05;
      if (warp < 0 || warp > 30) continue;
      ests.push({ warp, speed });
    }
    if (!ests.length) return null;
    ests.sort((a, b) => a.warp - b.warp);
    const rawMid = ests[Math.floor(ests.length / 2)].warp;
    // JG legs (a Jump Gate hop outside our vision) only ever INFLATE the implied speed —
    // the scout covered path-distance it never actually flew. The median already resists a
    // lone outlier; here we additionally DROP any leg above median+margin or the hard cap,
    // so a minority of jump legs (or a single-pair jump) can't push the estimate up.
    let jgDropped = 0;
    if (jgFilter) {
      const ceil = Math.min(hardCap, rawMid + jgMargin);
      const kept = ests.filter(e => e.warp <= ceil);
      if (kept.length) { jgDropped = ests.length - kept.length; ests = kept; }
    }
    ests.sort((a, b) => a.warp - b.warp);
    const mid = ests[Math.floor(ests.length / 2)];
    return { warp: Math.round(mid.warp), speed: Math.round(mid.speed * 10) / 10, pairs: ests.length, jgDropped, rawWarp: Math.round(rawMid) };
  }

  /* ── SCOUT-KILL DOM PARSERS (verified against real AE pages) ──────────────
     All pure doc->data; the scout-killer module drives fetch + navigation. */

  /* Empire fleet list (fleet.aspx). Rows: Fleet(id+label) | Location | Destination
     | Arrival | Size. Idle fleets have empty destination/arrival. */
  function parseEmpireFleets(doc) {
    const out = [], seen = {};
    // iterate the primary fleet links (skip the per-row "edit comment" link); use the
    // innermost row and only its DIRECT-child cells (the page nests layout tables).
    for (const a of doc.querySelectorAll('a[href*="fleet.aspx?fleet="]')) {
      const href = a.getAttribute('href') || '';
      if (/action=/.test(href)) continue;                 // edit_comment / rename etc.
      const idm = href.match(/fleet=(\d+)/);
      if (!idm || seen[idm[1]]) continue;
      const tr = a.closest('tr');
      if (!tr) continue;
      const tds = [...tr.children].filter(n => n.tagName === 'TD');
      if (tds.length < 5) continue;                        // not a listing row
      seen[idm[1]] = true;
      const locA = tds[1].querySelector('a[href*="loc="]');
      const locm = locA && (locA.getAttribute('href') || '').match(/loc=([A-Z]\d{2}:\d{2}:\d{2}:\d+)/);
      const destA = tds[2].querySelector('a[href*="loc="]');
      const destm = destA && (destA.getAttribute('href') || '').match(/loc=([A-Z]\d{2}:\d{2}:\d{2}:\d+)/);
      const size = parseInt((tds[4].textContent || '').replace(/[^\d]/g, ''), 10);
      out.push({
        fleetId: idm[1],
        label: (a.textContent || '').trim(),
        loc: locm ? locm[1] : null,
        dest: destm ? destm[1] : null,
        moving: !!destm,
        size: isNaN(size) ? null : size
      });
    }
    return out;
  }

  /* Move page (fleet.aspx?fleet=ID&view=move). #distance/#maxspeed/#duration are
     computed by the game's calc_distance() when the destination changes; read them
     AFTER triggering it. Also the per-unit availability (name -> avail). durationSec
     parses AE's duration text ("1h 05m 30s" / "5m 30s" / "45s" / "2d 3h"). Returns
     null fields when calc has not run. */
  function parseDurationText(t) {
    if (!t) return null;
    let sec = 0, hit = false;
    const re = /(\d+)\s*(d|h|m|s)/gi; let m;
    while ((m = re.exec(t))) {
      hit = true; const n = parseInt(m[1], 10);
      const u = m[2].toLowerCase();
      sec += n * (u === 'd' ? 86400 : u === 'h' ? 3600 : u === 'm' ? 60 : 1);
    }
    return hit ? sec : null;
  }
  function parseMovePage(doc) {
    const g = (id) => { const e = doc.getElementById(id); return e ? (e.textContent || '').trim() : null; };
    const avail = {};
    for (const td of doc.querySelectorAll('[id^="avail"]')) {
      const unit = td.id.replace(/^avail/, '');
      const v = td.getAttribute('js_value');
      avail[unit] = v != null ? parseInt(v, 10) : parseInt((td.textContent || '').replace(/[^\d]/g, ''), 10);
    }
    const form = doc.getElementById('move_fleet_form');
    const unitsInput = doc.querySelector('input[name="units"]');
    const durText = g('duration');
    return {
      formAction: form ? (form.getAttribute('action') || '').replace(/&amp;/g, '&') : null,
      unitsCsv: unitsInput ? (unitsInput.getAttribute('value') || '') : null,
      start: g('start'),
      distance: g('distance') ? parseFloat(g('distance')) : null,
      maxspeed: g('maxspeed') ? parseFloat(g('maxspeed')) : null,
      durationText: durText,
      durationSec: parseDurationText(durText),
      avail
    };
  }

  /* System view (map.aspx?loc=GG:RR:SS). Each astro carries an overlay
     <div id="astro_icons_<coord>"> whose inner title reads "Fleet present: N" and/or
     "Incoming: N". This is how the randomized autoscout landing astro is located without
     polling every astro: present>0 == landed there, incoming>0 == inbound there.
     Returns [{ coord, present, incoming }] for astros with any fleet activity. */
  function parseSystemScoutAstros(doc) {
    const out = [];
    for (const div of doc.querySelectorAll('[id^="astro_icons_"]')) {
      const coord = div.id.replace('astro_icons_', '');
      let present = 0, incoming = 0, found = false;
      for (const t of div.querySelectorAll('[title]')) {
        const title = t.getAttribute('title') || '';
        const mp = title.match(/Fleet present:\s*([\d,]+)/i);
        const mi = title.match(/Incoming:\s*([\d,]+)/i);
        if (mp) { present = parseInt(mp[1].replace(/,/g, ''), 10); found = true; }
        if (mi) { incoming = parseInt(mi[1].replace(/,/g, ''), 10); found = true; }
      }
      if (found && (present > 0 || incoming > 0)) out.push({ coord, present, incoming });
    }
    return out;
  }

  /* Attack view (fleet.aspx?fleet=MINE&view=attack). Each target row carries the enemy
     fleet link, an autoscout marker <small title="Auto Scout">, player, size, and a form
     posting to combat.aspx?fleet=MINE&attack=ENEMY&code=TOKEN. */
  function parseAttackTargets(doc) {
    const out = [];
    for (const form of doc.querySelectorAll('form[action*="combat.aspx"]')) {
      const act = form.getAttribute('action') || '';
      const am = act.match(/attack=(\d+)/);
      if (!am) continue;
      const tr = form.closest('tr');
      if (!tr) continue;
      const fleetA = tr.querySelector('a[href*="fleet.aspx?fleet="]');
      const playerA = tr.querySelector('a[href*="player="]');
      const pm = playerA && (playerA.getAttribute('href') || '').match(/player=(\d+)/);
      const isAuto = !!tr.querySelector('small[title="Auto Scout"], small[title*="Auto Scout"]');
      // size: the numeric cell (skip the (0%) span text)
      let size = null;
      const sizeCell = tr.querySelector('td[data-ae-fleetpct]') || null;
      if (sizeCell) { const mm = (sizeCell.textContent || '').match(/^\s*([\d,]+)/); if (mm) size = parseInt(mm[1].replace(/,/g, ''), 10); }
      out.push({
        enemyFleetId: am[1],
        formAction: act.replace(/&amp;/g, '&'),
        name: fleetA ? (fleetA.textContent || '').trim() : null,
        isAutoScout: isAuto,
        playerId: pm ? pm[1] : null,
        playerName: playerA ? (playerA.textContent || '').trim() : null,
        size
      });
    }
    return out;
  }

  /* Pre-battle page (POST result of an Attack Fleet). The confirm form re-posts to the
     same combat.aspx action but adds confirm=true + confirm_code. Returns
     { action, confirmCode } or null if this is not a confirm page. */
  function parsePreBattleConfirm(doc) {
    for (const form of doc.querySelectorAll('form[action*="combat.aspx"]')) {
      const cc = form.querySelector('input[name="confirm_code"]');
      const cf = form.querySelector('input[name="confirm"]');
      if (cc && cf) return {
        action: (form.getAttribute('action') || '').replace(/&amp;/g, '&'),
        confirmCode: cc.getAttribute('value') || cc.value || null
      };
    }
    return null;
  }

  /* Battle-report result page. "Total cost of units destroyed: N (Attacker:a ; Defender:d)". */
  function parseBattleResult(doc) {
    const txt = doc.body ? doc.body.textContent : '';
    const m = txt.match(/units destroyed:\s*([\d,]+)\s*\(\s*Attacker:\s*([\d,]+)\s*;\s*Defender:\s*([\d,]+)/i);
    if (!m) return { ok: false };
    return {
      ok: true,
      destroyedTotal: parseInt(m[1].replace(/,/g, ''), 10),
      attackerLost: parseInt(m[2].replace(/,/g, ''), 10),
      defenderLost: parseInt(m[3].replace(/,/g, ''), 10)
    };
  }

  /* ── FLEET GATHERER MATH ────────────────────────────────────────────────
     Retention: keep a defensive force worth >= minKeep credits at the base,
     retaining CHEAP screen units first (they die first and cost least):
     FT > BO > CV > RC > DD > ... by unit cost ascending. Whole-unit steps; the
     kept value therefore lands AT or just above minKeep (never below unless the
     base simply has less). Returns { keep, send, keepValue, sendValue }. */
  function planRetention(units, minKeep) {
    const keep = {}, send = {};
    let keepValue = 0, sendValue = 0;
    const order = Object.keys(units || {}).filter(ab => DROP_STATS[ab])
      .sort((a, b) => DROP_STATS[a].c - DROP_STATS[b].c);
    for (const ab of order) {
      const c = DROP_STATS[ab].c;
      let q = units[ab];
      if (keepValue < (minKeep || 0)) {
        const need = Math.min(q, Math.ceil(((minKeep || 0) - keepValue) / c));
        if (need > 0) { keep[ab] = need; keepValue += need * c; q -= need; }
      }
      if (q > 0) { send[ab] = q; sendValue += q * c; }
    }
    return { keep, send, keepValue, sendValue };
  }
  /* Route chooser: direct vs via each declared Jump-Gate hub. Departing a base
     WITH a JG boosts that leg x(1+0.7 x level) — the same +70%/level annotation
     the scout model uses (leg from a NON-JG base is never boosted; whether AE
     requires a JG at BOTH ends is UNVERIFIED — this model boosts on origin only,
     matching observed scout behaviour). speed = base speed of the SLOWEST ship
     x (1+0.05 x warp). Times in hours. Returns
     { mode:'direct'|'via', hub?, legs:[{from,to,hours,jg}], totalH } — the best. */
  function chooseGatherRoute(fromCoord, destCoord, hubs, speedLyH, fromJgLevel) {
    const leg = (a, b, jgLvl) => {
      const d = distance(a, b); if (!d) return null;
      const v = speedLyH * (jgLvl > 0 ? (1 + 0.7 * jgLvl) : 1);
      return { from: a, to: b, hours: d.ly / v, jg: jgLvl > 0 ? jgLvl : 0, ly: d.ly };
    };
    const direct = leg(fromCoord, destCoord, fromJgLevel || 0);
    if (!direct) return null;
    let best = { mode: 'direct', legs: [direct], totalH: direct.hours };
    for (const h of hubs || []) {
      if (!h || !h.coord || h.coord === fromCoord || h.coord === destCoord) continue;
      const l1 = leg(fromCoord, h.coord, fromJgLevel || 0);
      const l2 = leg(h.coord, destCoord, h.jg || 0);
      if (!l1 || !l2) continue;
      const total = l1.hours + l2.hours;
      if (total < best.totalH) best = { mode: 'via', hub: h, legs: [l1, l2], totalH: total };
    }
    return best;
  }

  /* Export a galaxy's scout path in the AE-SCOUTPATH v1 wire format consumed by the
     guild's YAGPDB bot:
       AE-SCOUTPATH v1 B35 47
       B35:00:03:10,B35:01:01:22,...
     Stops are emitted in ZIG-ZAG VISIT ORDER (buildScoutPath already sorts by
     regionOrderKey), one per region, wrap implied. opts passes through to
     buildScoutPath (jgByRegion etc.). */
  function exportScoutPath(galaxy, rows, opts) {
    const path = buildScoutPath(rows, opts);
    if (!path.length) return null;
    const bad = path.find(p => p.coord.indexOf(galaxy + ':') !== 0);
    if (bad) return null;                    // rows from a different galaxy — refuse
    return 'AE-SCOUTPATH v1 ' + galaxy + ' ' + path.length + '\n'
      + path.map(p => p.coord).join(',');
  }

  /* ── BUILD ADVISOR ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
     Given a goal (economy | production | construction), rank the cheapest next
     structure on a base INCLUDING the support chain it forces (population -> Urban,
     energy -> cheapest plant, area -> Terraform/MLP). Model sources: the game
     structures table (cost/energy/eco/pop/area deltas — note energy plants and labs
     REDUCE economy by 1/level, which the economy goal nets out) + the BotZ effect
     annotations (per-astro scaling: Urban +fertility pop, Solar +solar E, Gas +gas E,
     MR +metal production AND construction). Cost of next level from L:
     ceil(base x 1.5^L) — verified against the BotZ tables. Crystal Mines are research-
     only (goal-neutral here) and Capital is once-per-empire: both excluded. */
  const BUILD_MODEL = {
    'Urban Structures':      { c:1,     e:0,   eco:0, pop:-1, area:0,  popCap:'fert' },
    'Solar Plants':          { c:1,     e:'solar', eco:-1, pop:-1, area:0 },
    'Gas Plants':            { c:1,     e:'gas',   eco:-1, pop:-1, area:0 },
    'Fusion Plants':         { c:20,    e:4,   eco:-1, pop:-1, area:0,  req:{Energy:6} },
    'Antimatter Plants':     { c:2000,  e:10,  eco:-1, pop:-1, area:0,  req:{Energy:20} },
    'Orbital Plants':        { c:40000, e:12,  eco:-1, pop:0,  area:0,  req:{Energy:25}, moonOnly:true },
    'Research Labs':         { c:2,     e:-1,  eco:-1, pop:-1, area:0 },
    'Metal Refineries':      { c:1,     e:-1,  eco:1,  pop:-1, area:-1, prod:'metal', constr:'metal' },
    'Crystal Mines':         { c:2,     e:-1,  eco:0,  pop:-1, area:-1 },
    'Robotic Factories':     { c:5,     e:-1,  eco:1,  pop:-1, area:-1, prod:2, constr:2, req:{Computer:2} },
    'Shipyards':             { c:5,     e:-1,  eco:1,  pop:-1, area:-1, prod:2 },
    'Orbital Shipyards':     { c:10000, e:-12, eco:2,  pop:-1, area:0,  prod:8, req:{Cybernetics:2} },
    'Spaceports':            { c:5,     e:-1,  eco:2,  pop:-1, area:-1 },
    'Command Centers':       { c:20,    e:-1,  eco:0,  pop:-1, area:-1, req:{Computer:6} },
    'Nanite Factories':      { c:80,    e:-2,  eco:2,  pop:-1, area:-1, prod:4, constr:4, req:{Computer:10, Laser:8} },
    'Android Factories':     { c:1000,  e:-4,  eco:2,  pop:-1, area:-1, prod:6, constr:6, req:{'Artificial Intelligence':4} },
    'Economic Centers':      { c:80,    e:-2,  eco:3,  pop:-1, area:-1, req:{Computer:10} },
    'Terraform':             { c:80,    e:0,   eco:0,  pop:0,  area:5,  req:{Computer:10, Energy:10} },
    'Multi-Level Platforms': { c:10000, e:0,   eco:0,  pop:0,  area:10, req:{Armour:22} },
    'Orbital Base':          { c:2000,  e:0,   eco:0,  pop:0,  area:0,  popCap:10, req:{Computer:20} },
    'Jump Gate':             { c:5000,  e:-12, eco:0,  pop:-1, area:0,  req:{'Warp Drive':12, Energy:20} }
  };
  function buildCost(name, curLevel, discount) {
    const m = BUILD_MODEL[name]; if (!m) return null;
    const raw = Math.ceil(m.c * Math.pow(1.5, curLevel || 0));
    return discount ? Math.round(raw * (1 - discount)) : raw;
  }
  /* Balances from structure levels + astro stats. energyBonus covers any innate base
     energy (uncertain; override if the page states otherwise). */
  function deriveBaseBalances(structures, astro, energyBonus) {
    const L = (n) => (structures && structures[n]) || 0;
    const popCap = L('Urban Structures') * (astro.fertility || 0) + L('Orbital Base') * 10;
    let popUsed = 0, areaUsed = 0, energyUsed = 0;
    for (const [n, m] of Object.entries(BUILD_MODEL)) {
      const lv = L(n); if (!lv) continue;
      if (m.pop === -1) popUsed += lv;
      if (m.area === -1) areaUsed += lv;
      if (typeof m.e === 'number' && m.e < 0) energyUsed += lv * (-m.e);
    }
    const energyProd = (energyBonus || 0)
      + L('Solar Plants') * (astro.solar || 0) + L('Gas Plants') * (astro.gas || 0)
      + L('Fusion Plants') * 4 + L('Antimatter Plants') * 10 + L('Orbital Plants') * 12;
    const areaCap = (astro.area || 0) + L('Terraform') * 5 + L('Multi-Level Platforms') * 10;
    return { popCap, popUsed, popFree: popCap - popUsed,
             energyProd, energyUsed, energyFree: energyProd - energyUsed,
             areaCap, areaUsed, areaFree: areaCap - areaUsed };
  }
  function _goalGain(name, m, goal, astro) {
    const scale = (v) => v === 'metal' ? (astro.metal || 0) : (typeof v === 'number' ? v : 0);
    if (goal === 'economy') return m.eco > 0 ? m.eco : 0;
    if (goal === 'production') return scale(m.prod);
    if (goal === 'construction') return scale(m.constr);
    return 0;
  }
  /* Rank next builds. state = { structures, astro:{area,fertility,solar,gas,metal},
     techs (optional: gates requirements; absent -> included with a req note),
     energyBonus, isMoon, discount }. Returns [{name, toLevel, chain, totalCost,
     goalGain, netEco, costPer, reqNote}] sorted by costPer. */
  function adviseNextBuild(state, goal, topN) {
    const astro = state.astro || {};
    const out = [];
    for (const [name, m] of Object.entries(BUILD_MODEL)) {
      const gain = _goalGain(name, m, goal, astro);
      if (!gain) continue;
      if (m.moonOnly && !state.isMoon) continue;
      let reqNote = null;
      if (m.req) {
        if (state.techs) { let ok = true; for (const [t, lv] of Object.entries(m.req)) if (((state.techs[t]) || 0) < lv) ok = false; if (!ok) continue; }
        else reqNote = 'req: ' + Object.entries(m.req).map(([t, lv]) => t + ' ' + lv).join(', ');
      }
      // DELTA accounting: only the chain's own needs are charged. Baseline slack is
      // clamped at >=0 so model imprecision (e.g. innate base energy not modelled)
      // can never make every candidate look infeasible.
      const st = Object.assign({}, state.structures);
      const base0 = deriveBaseBalances(st, astro, state.energyBonus);
      const avail = { pop: Math.max(0, base0.popFree), energy: Math.max(0, base0.energyFree), area: Math.max(0, base0.areaFree) };
      const chain = [];
      let guard = 0, feasible = true, ecoDelta = 0;
      const buildOne = (n) => {
        const lv = (st[n] || 0); const c = buildCost(n, lv, state.discount);
        chain.push({ name: n, toLevel: lv + 1, cost: c }); st[n] = lv + 1;
        const mm = BUILD_MODEL[n];
        if (typeof mm.eco === 'number') ecoDelta += mm.eco;
        if (mm.pop === -1) avail.pop -= 1;
        if (mm.area === -1) avail.area -= 1;
        if (typeof mm.e === 'number') avail.energy += mm.e;           // consumers negative
        else if (mm.e === 'solar') avail.energy += (astro.solar || 0);
        else if (mm.e === 'gas') avail.energy += (astro.gas || 0);
        if (mm.popCap === 'fert') avail.pop += (astro.fertility || 0);
        else if (typeof mm.popCap === 'number') avail.pop += mm.popCap;
        if (mm.area > 0) avail.area += mm.area;
        return c;
      };
      const needSupport = (target) => {
        const tm = BUILD_MODEL[target];
        if (tm.area === -1 && avail.area < 1) return 'area';
        if (tm.pop === -1 && avail.pop < 1) return 'pop';
        const eNeed = (typeof tm.e === 'number' && tm.e < 0) ? -tm.e : 0;
        if (eNeed && avail.energy < eNeed) return 'energy';
        return null;
      };
      while (guard++ < 16) {
        const need = needSupport(name);
        if (!need) break;
        if (need === 'pop') {
          let pick = 'Urban Structures';
          if (state.techs && (state.techs.Computer || 0) >= 20) {
            const uc = buildCost('Urban Structures', st['Urban Structures'] || 0, state.discount) / Math.max(1, (astro.fertility || 0) - 1);
            const oc = buildCost('Orbital Base', st['Orbital Base'] || 0, state.discount) / 10;
            if (oc < uc) pick = 'Orbital Base';
          }
          buildOne(pick);
        } else if (need === 'area') {
          let pick = null;
          const can = (n) => !BUILD_MODEL[n].req || !state.techs || Object.entries(BUILD_MODEL[n].req).every(([t, lv]) => ((state.techs[t]) || 0) >= lv);
          if (can('Terraform')) pick = 'Terraform';
          else if (can('Multi-Level Platforms')) pick = 'Multi-Level Platforms';
          if (!pick) { feasible = false; break; }
          buildOne(pick);
        } else {
          let best = null;
          const plants = [['Solar Plants', astro.solar || 0], ['Gas Plants', astro.gas || 0],
                          ['Fusion Plants', 4], ['Antimatter Plants', 10]];
          for (const [pn, g] of plants) {
            if (!g) continue;
            const pm = BUILD_MODEL[pn];
            if (pm.req && state.techs && !Object.entries(pm.req).every(([t, lv]) => ((state.techs[t]) || 0) >= lv)) continue;
            const cpe = buildCost(pn, st[pn] || 0, state.discount) / g;
            if (!best || cpe < best.cpe) best = { pn, cpe };
          }
          if (!best) { feasible = false; break; }
          if (BUILD_MODEL[best.pn].pop === -1 && avail.pop < 1) buildOne('Urban Structures');
          buildOne(best.pn);
        }
      }
      if (!feasible || guard >= 16) continue;
      if (!feasible || guard >= 8) continue;
      const mainCost = buildOne(name);
      const totalCost = chain.reduce((a, c) => a + c.cost, 0);
      const netGain = goal === 'economy' ? (gain + (ecoDelta - m.eco)) + 0 : gain;   // chain eco penalties net into economy
      const netEco = ecoDelta;   // full eco delta incl the candidate itself
      const effGain = goal === 'economy' ? netEco : gain;
      if (effGain <= 0) continue;
      const reqBits = [];
      if (reqNote) reqBits.push(reqNote);
      if (!state.techs) for (const c of chain.slice(0, -1)) {
        const cm = BUILD_MODEL[c.name];
        if (cm && cm.req) reqBits.push(c.name + ' req: ' + Object.entries(cm.req).map(([t, lv]) => t + ' ' + lv).join(', '));
      }
      out.push({ name, toLevel: chain[chain.length - 1].toLevel, chain, totalCost,
                 goalGain: effGain, netEco, costPer: totalCost / effGain,
                 reqNote: reqBits.length ? [...new Set(reqBits)].join(' \u00b7 ') : null });
    }
    out.sort((a, b) => a.costPer - b.costPer);
    return out.slice(0, topN || 5);
  }

  /* ── TECH ESTIMATION from rankings budget ──────────────────────────────
     A player's rankings "Technology" value is treated as their cumulative research
     spend (a budget). Estimation:
       1. minimum levels are FORCED by the units they field (unit tech requirements);
       2. the remaining combat budget is spent greedily, cheapest-next-level first,
         across the techs their fleet actually uses (+ Armour/Shielding always).
     COST MODEL: cum(t,L) = base_t x (r^L - 1)/(r - 1). r and base costs are marked
     ASSUMED (context doc says 2^(L-1) with a verify flag; structures scale x1.5) —
     BUT the calibration factor alpha absorbs any UNIFORM scale error exactly, so only
     the RELATIVE ordering of base costs matters. One capture of Empire->Technologies
     (Research Cost column + current levels) would pin base costs and r precisely. */
  const TECH_COST_R = 1.5;   // VERIFIED exact from the Borealis Empire->Technologies capture
  // Base costs VERIFIED exact (Research Cost / 1.5^level recovered clean powers of two
  // for every tech). Model total for the captured profile: 1,414,957 vs the player's
  // ranked Technology score 1,415,020 (99.996%) — proving the rank score is cumulative
  // research credits and this table is right.
  const TECH_BASE = { Laser: 4, Missiles: 8, Armour: 4, Plasma: 32, Shielding: 128,
                      Ion: 256, Photon: 1024, Disruptor: 4096 };
  const TECH_BASE_NONCOMBAT = { 'Energy': 2, 'Computer': 2, 'Stellar Drive': 16, 'Warp Drive': 64,
    'Stealth': 512, 'Artificial Intelligence': 2048, 'Cybernetics': 8192,
    'Tachyon Communications': 32768, 'Anti-Gravity': 100000 };
  // Combat share when UNCALIBRATED. Two measured profiles (exact bases): Borealis war
  // account 0.471, mature Altair eco account 0.261. Targets are Borealis-era war
  // accounts -> 0.47. Guildmate calibration overrides when samples exist.
  const DEFAULT_COMBAT_ALPHA = 0.47;
  // PREREQ RESERVE: both measured profiles hold combat techs their current composition
  // does not use (Borealis Photon 11 = 26% of combat spend; Altair Photon+Disruptor =
  // 14%) — old specs and unit prerequisites. A comp-driven estimator cannot see that
  // spend, and redistributing it inflates the visible weapon techs (Laser +3/+4,
  // Plasma +9 in the two allocation checks). Withhold this share of the combat budget
  // from the greedy spend instead.
  const TECH_PREREQ_RESERVE = 0.18;   // midpoint of the two measured shares (0.14, 0.26)
  const TECH_LEVEL_CAP = 30;
  function techCum(t, L) { const b = TECH_BASE[t]; if (!b || L <= 0) return 0; return b * (Math.pow(TECH_COST_R, L) - 1) / (TECH_COST_R - 1); }
  function techNext(t, L) { const b = TECH_BASE[t]; return b ? b * Math.pow(TECH_COST_R, L) : Infinity; }
  // combat-tech minimums implied by fielding each unit (from the game unit table)
  const UNIT_TECH_REQ = {
    FT:{Laser:1}, BO:{Missiles:1}, HB:{Plasma:14}, IB:{Ion:10,Shielding:10}, CV:{Laser:2,Armour:2},
    RC:{Laser:1,Armour:2}, DD:{Plasma:1,Armour:6}, FR:{Missiles:6,Armour:8},
    IF:{Ion:10,Armour:8,Shielding:10}, SS:{}, OS:{}, CR:{Plasma:4,Armour:10,Shielding:2},
    CA:{Missiles:6,Armour:10,Shielding:2}, HC:{Plasma:6,Armour:12,Shielding:4},
    BS:{Ion:6,Armour:16,Shielding:8}, FC:{Ion:4,Armour:14,Shielding:6},
    DN:{Photon:6,Armour:20,Shielding:10}, TI:{Disruptor:6,Armour:22,Shielding:14},
    LE:{Photon:12,Armour:24,Shielding:16}, DS:{Disruptor:10,Armour:26,Shielding:18} };
  /* Calibrate against players with BOTH harvested tech and a rank tech score (guild
     mates harvested from defense reports are the natural set). Returns
     { alpha, techPerFleet, samples } — alpha = combat spend / total score (median). */
  function computeTechCalibration(techPlayers, rankPlayers) {
    const alphas = [], tpf = [];
    for (const [pid, mp] of Object.entries(techPlayers || {})) {
      const rp = rankPlayers ? rankPlayers[pid] : null;
      if (rp && rp.fleet && rp.tech) tpf.push(rp.tech / rp.fleet);
      if (!rp || !rp.tech || !mp.techs) continue;
      let combat = 0, n = 0;
      for (const [t, L] of Object.entries(mp.techs)) { if (TECH_BASE[t] && L > 0) { combat += techCum(t, L); n++; } }
      if (n >= 3 && combat > 0) alphas.push(Math.min(1, combat / rp.tech));
    }
    const med = (a) => { if (!a.length) return null; const s = a.slice().sort((x, y) => x - y); return s[Math.floor(s.length / 2)]; };
    return { alpha: med(alphas), techPerFleet: med(tpf), samples: alphas.length };
  }
  /* Estimate a player's combat techs from their rank budget (or an extrapolated one).
     enemyCounts drives which techs are relevant + the forced minimums. */
  function estimateEnemyTechs(rankEntry, enemyCounts, calib, flatFallback) {
    // budget
    let T = rankEntry && rankEntry.tech ? rankEntry.tech : null, budgetSrc = 'rank';
    if (T == null) {
      const tpf = calib && calib.techPerFleet;
      const fleet = rankEntry && rankEntry.fleet;
      if (tpf && fleet) { T = tpf * fleet; budgetSrc = 'extrapolated'; }
    }
    if (T == null) {
      const lvl = flatFallback != null ? flatFallback : 15;
      const techs = {}; ASSUMED_TECH_KEYS.forEach(k => { techs[k] = lvl; });
      return { techs, label: 'assumed tech ' + lvl };
    }
    const alpha = (calib && calib.alpha) || DEFAULT_COMBAT_ALPHA;
    let budget = alpha * T * (1 - TECH_PREREQ_RESERVE);
    // forced minimums + relevant techs from the composition
    const techs = {}; Object.keys(TECH_BASE).forEach(t => { techs[t] = 0; });
    const relevant = new Set(['Armour', 'Shielding']);
    for (const ab of Object.keys(enemyCounts || {})) {
      const st = DROP_STATS[ab]; if (st && TECH_BASE[st.w]) relevant.add(st.w);
      const req = UNIT_TECH_REQ[ab] || {};
      for (const [t, L] of Object.entries(req)) if (techs[t] != null && L > techs[t]) techs[t] = L;
    }
    Object.keys(techs).forEach(t => { budget -= techCum(t, techs[t]); });
    // greedy spend: cheapest next level among RELEVANT techs until the budget is gone
    while (budget > 0) {
      let bestT = null, bestC = Infinity;
      for (const t of relevant) { if (techs[t] >= TECH_LEVEL_CAP) continue; const c = techNext(t, techs[t]); if (c < bestC) { bestC = c; bestT = t; } }
      if (!bestT || bestC > budget) break;
      techs[bestT]++; budget -= bestC;
    }
    return { techs, label: 'est. tech (' + budgetSrc + (calib && calib.samples ? ', cal n=' + calib.samples : ', uncal') + ')' };
  }

  /* Wave resolution helpers. All use the predictor engine's conventions:
     dmg = atk - 0.99*shield (1% passthrough) or atk - 0.5*shield (ion, 50% bypass);
     aggregate pools scaled by FACTOR 0.848. */
  function _mkEnemy(counts, techs) {
    const T = (k) => (techs && techs[k]) || 0;
    const armM = 1 + 0.05 * T('Armour'), shlM = 1 + 0.05 * T('Shielding');
    const out = [];
    for (const [ab, count] of Object.entries(counts || {})) {
      const st = DROP_STATS[ab]; if (!st || !count) continue;
      out.push({ ab, count, cost: st.c, effArm: st.r * armM, effShl: st.s * shlM,
                 effAtk: st.a * (1 + 0.05 * T(st.w)), ion: DROP_ION.has(ab) });
    }
    return out;
  }
  const _val = (list) => list.reduce((a, e) => a + e.count * e.cost, 0);
  /* Return fire: total damage the given enemy list deals to a defender with shield shl. */
  function _returnPool(enemies, shl) {
    let p = 0; for (const e of enemies) p += e.count * _effDmg(e.effAtk, e.ion, shl) * DROP_FACTOR;
    return p;
  }
  /* Units of one attacking type needed to one-round every target in `targets`. */
  function _needSingle(atk, ion, targets) {
    let n = 0; for (const e of targets) n += e.count * e.effArm / (DROP_FACTOR * _effDmg(atk, ion, e.effShl));
    return n;
  }
  /* One drop/finish wave with a SINGLE attacking type. Returns loss figures. */
  function _waveSingle(myAb, my, targets, shooters) {
    const need = Math.ceil(_needSingle(my.atk, DROP_ION.has(myAb), targets));
    const pool = _returnPool(shooters, my.shl || 0);
    const lost = Math.min(need, pool / Math.max(my.arm, 0.0001));
    const myCost = DROP_STATS[myAb] ? DROP_STATS[myAb].c : 0;
    const attLoss = lost * myCost, defLoss = _val(targets);
    // ratio = value KILLED per value LOST (higher is better for the attacker)
    return { need, attLoss, defLoss, ratio: attLoss > 0 ? defLoss / attLoss : Infinity,
             short: need > (my.qty || Infinity) };
  }
  /* One finishing wave with a COMPOSITION (value-proportional mix of own combat units).
     One "composition unit" = the mix scaled so its parts sum to 1 unit. */
  function _waveComp(mix, targets, shooters) {
    // damage one composition-unit delivers to a target of shield s
    const dmgVs = (s) => mix.reduce((a, m) => a + m.frac * _effDmg(m.atk, DROP_ION.has(m.ab), s), 0);
    let k = 0;
    for (const e of targets) { const d = dmgVs(e.effShl); if (d <= 0) return null; k += e.count * e.effArm / (DROP_FACTOR * d); }
    const parts = mix.map(m => ({ ab: m.ab, n: Math.ceil(k * m.frac), qty: m.qty, cost: m.cost, arm: m.arm, shl: m.shl }));
    const totalN = parts.reduce((a, p) => a + p.n, 0) || 1;
    let attLoss = 0, short = false;
    for (const p of parts) {
      if (p.n > (p.qty || Infinity)) short = true;
      // enemy fire spread proportionally across my committed units
      const pool = _returnPool(shooters, p.shl || 0) * (p.n / totalN);
      attLoss += Math.min(p.n, pool / Math.max(p.arm, 0.0001)) * p.cost;
    }
    const defLoss = _val(targets);
    return { parts: parts.filter(p => p.n > 0).sort((a, b) => (b.n * b.cost) - (a.n * a.cost)),
             attLoss, defLoss, ratio: attLoss > 0 ? defLoss / attLoss : Infinity, short };
  }
  /* SR: repeated attacks by ONE type until the whole enemy fleet is dead, with the
     surviving-but-damaged units repaired between attacks (AE: partial damage costs 50%
     of unit cost, proportional to damage). Returns attacks, losses (destroyed value +
     cumulative repair bill) and the ratio. Caps at 20 attacks to stay finite. */
  function _simSR(myAb, my, enemies) {
    const cost = DROP_STATS[myAb] ? DROP_STATS[myAb].c : 0;
    const ion = DROP_ION.has(myAb);
    let live = my.qty, destroyedVal = 0, repairVal = 0, killedVal = 0, attacks = 0;
    const rem = enemies.map(e => Object.assign({}, e));
    const alive = () => rem.some(e => e.count > 0.0001);
    while (alive() && attacks < 20 && live >= 1) {
      attacks++;
      // my fire, split across remaining targets in proportion to their armour pools
      // SIMULTANEOUS resolution: their return fire is computed from the counts at the
      // START of the round, BEFORE my kills are applied. (Resolving my kills first and
      // letting only survivors shoot back understates losses by an order of magnitude.)
      const incoming = _returnPool(rem.filter(e => e.count > 0), my.shl || 0);
      const pools = rem.map(e => e.count * e.effArm);
      const totalPool = pools.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0); if (totalPool <= 0) break;
      let killedThis = 0;
      rem.forEach((e, idx) => {
        if (e.count <= 0) return;
        const share = live * (pools[idx] / totalPool);
        const dmg = share * DROP_FACTOR * _effDmg(my.atk, ion, e.effShl);
        const kills = Math.min(e.count, dmg / e.effArm);
        e.count -= kills; killedThis += kills * e.cost;
      });
      killedVal += killedThis;
      if (killedThis <= 0) break;   // cannot hurt them — bail out as infeasible
      const destroyed = Math.min(live, Math.floor(incoming / Math.max(my.arm, 0.0001)));
      destroyedVal += destroyed * cost;
      const leftover = incoming - destroyed * my.arm;
      if (leftover > 0 && live - destroyed > 0) repairVal += 0.5 * cost * Math.min(1, leftover / my.arm);
      live -= destroyed;
    }
    const done = !alive();
    const attLoss = destroyedVal + repairVal;
    return { ab: myAb, attacks, done, attLoss, defLoss: killedVal, repairVal,
             ratio: attLoss > 0 ? killedVal / attLoss : Infinity };
  }
  /* Build the structured tooltip entries. Returns [{kind,text}] where kind is
     head | title | stat | note | gap. */
  function buildDropLines(enemyCounts, enemyTechs, profile, ownUnits) {
    const enemies = _mkEnemy(enemyCounts, enemyTechs);
    if (!enemies.length) return null;
    const unsh = enemies.filter(e => e.effShl === 0);
    const lowsh = enemies.filter(e => e.effShl > 0 && LOWSHIELD_MAX.has(e.ab));
    const heavy = enemies.filter(e => e.effShl > 0 && !LOWSHIELD_MAX.has(e.ab));
    const own = ownUnits && ownUnits.units;
    // my BO stats: parsed if available, else the configured attack + base armour
    const myBO = (own && own.BO && own.BO.atk)
      ? { atk: own.BO.atk, arm: own.BO.arm || 2, shl: own.BO.shl || 0, qty: own.BO.qty }
      : { atk: profile.boAttack, arm: 2, shl: 0, qty: Infinity };
    // finisher composition: value-proportional mix of owned COMBAT units
    let mix = null;
    if (own) {
      const combat = Object.entries(own).filter(([ab, u]) => !DROP_SUPPORT.has(ab) && ab !== 'BO' && ab !== 'FT'
        && u.qty > 0 && u.atk != null && u.arm != null && DROP_STATS[ab]);
      const totalVal = combat.reduce((a, [ab, u]) => a + u.qty * DROP_STATS[ab].c, 0);
      if (totalVal > 0) mix = combat.map(([ab, u]) => ({ ab, frac: (u.qty * DROP_STATS[ab].c) / totalVal,
        atk: u.atk, arm: u.arm, shl: u.shl || 0, qty: u.qty, cost: DROP_STATS[ab].c }));
    }
    const fmtN = (n) => Math.round(n).toLocaleString();
    const fmtV = (v) => v >= 1e6 ? ((v / 1e6).toFixed(2) + 'M') : (v >= 1e4 ? (Math.round(v / 1e3) + 'k') : fmtN(v));
    const ratioTxt = (r) => r === Infinity ? '\u221e' : (Math.round(r * 100) / 100).toFixed(2);
    // loss line: value LOST vs value KILLED, then kill-per-loss ratio (higher = better)
    const lossLine = (w) => 'lose ' + fmtV(w.attLoss) + ' \u2192 kill ' + fmtV(w.defLoss) + '   \u00b7   ratio ' + ratioTxt(w.ratio);
    const out = [];
    const push = (kind, text) => out.push({ kind, text });
    const cnt = (ts) => ts.reduce((a, e) => a + e.count, 0);
    // Resolve one finishing wave (composition if own units are known, else the
    // configured single main unit). Returns { w, amount } or null.
    const finWave = (targets) => {
      if (mix && mix.length) {
        const w = _waveComp(mix, targets, targets);
        if (!w) return null;
        let amount = w.parts.slice(0, 4).map(p => fmtN(p.n) + ' ' + p.ab).join(' \u00b7 ');
        const tot = w.parts.reduce((a, p) => a + p.n, 0);
        if (w.parts.length > 1) amount += '   (' + fmtN(tot) + ' units)';
        return { w, amount };
      }
      const single = { atk: profile.mainAttack, arm: DROP_STATS[profile.mainAbbr] ? DROP_STATS[profile.mainAbbr].r : 1,
                       shl: DROP_STATS[profile.mainAbbr] ? DROP_STATS[profile.mainAbbr].s : 0, qty: Infinity };
      const w = _waveSingle(profile.mainAbbr, single, targets, targets);
      return { w, amount: fmtN(w.need) + ' ' + profile.mainAbbr };
    };
    /* A PLAN links one drop to the finisher that follows it, and totals the pair.
       Because the drop clears one group and the finisher clears the rest, the combined
       "kill" equals the whole fleet — so the combined ratio is the true cost of wiping
       this fleet by that route, and plan A vs plan B are directly comparable. */
    const plan = (planLabel, dropLabel, dropTargets, finLabel, finTargets) => {
      if (!dropTargets.length) return false;
      const dw = _waveSingle('BO', myBO, dropTargets, enemies);
      if (out.length) push('divide', '');   // double rule separates blocks
      push('plan', planLabel);
      push('title', dropLabel + ' (' + fmtN(cnt(dropTargets)) + 'u)');
      push('stat', fmtN(dw.need) + ' BO' + (dw.short ? '   (exceeds owned)' : ''));
      push('loss', lossLine(dw));
      let att = dw.attLoss, def = dw.defLoss;
      if (finTargets.length) {
        const f = finWave(finTargets);
        if (f) {
          push('title', finLabel + ' (' + fmtN(cnt(finTargets)) + 'u left)');
          push('stat', f.amount + (f.w.short ? '   (exceeds owned)' : ''));
          push('loss', lossLine(f.w));
          if (f.w.ratio < 1) push('warn', 'Not suitable - Bad Ratio');
          att += f.w.attLoss; def += f.w.defLoss;
        }
      }
      const combined = { attLoss: att, defLoss: def, ratio: att > 0 ? def / att : Infinity };
      push('total', 'both attacks: ' + lossLine(combined));
      if (combined.ratio < 1) push('warn', 'Not suitable - Bad Ratio');
      return true;
    };
    const pA = plan('plan A \u00b7 drop 1 \u2192 finish', 'drop 1 \u00b7 unshielded', unsh,
                    'finisher after drop 1', lowsh.concat(heavy));
    const pB = plan('plan B \u00b7 drop 2 \u2192 finish', 'drop 2 \u00b7 low-shield \u2264HC', lowsh,
                    'finisher after drop 2', unsh.concat(heavy));
    if (!pA && !pB) {           // nothing droppable: straight main-spec attack only
      const f = finWave(enemies);
      if (f) {
        if (out.length) push('divide', '');
        push('title', 'straight attack (' + fmtN(cnt(enemies)) + 'u)');
        push('stat', f.amount + (f.w.short ? '   (exceeds owned)' : ''));
        push('loss', lossLine(f.w));
        if (f.w.ratio < 1) push('warn', 'Not suitable - Bad Ratio');
      }
    }
    // ── SR: repeated attacks with repair, best ratio among owned combat types
    if (own) {
      let best = null;
      for (const [ab, u] of Object.entries(own)) {
        if (DROP_SUPPORT.has(ab) || !u.qty || u.atk == null || u.arm == null || !DROP_STATS[ab]) continue;
        const r = _simSR(ab, { atk: u.atk, arm: u.arm, shl: u.shl || 0, qty: u.qty }, enemies);
        if (!r.done) continue;                       // could not finish the fleet
        if (!best || r.ratio > best.ratio) best = r;   // higher kill-per-loss wins
      }
      if (best) {
        if (out.length) push('divide', '');
        push('plan', 'plan C \u00b7 SR \u00b7 ' + best.attacks + ' attack' + (best.attacks === 1 ? '' : 's') + ' (repaired between)');
        push('stat', fmtN(own[best.ab].qty) + ' ' + best.ab);
        push('total', 'all attacks: ' + lossLine(best) + (best.repairVal > 0 ? '   \u00b7   incl. ' + fmtV(best.repairVal) + ' repairs' : ''));
        // best.ab is already the highest-ratio owned type, so if it cannot clear 1.0
        // nothing in the fleet can trade profitably here.
        if (best.ratio < 1) push('warn', 'Not suitable - Bad Ratio');
      } else {
        push('note', 'SR: no owned type finishes this fleet within 20 attacks');
      }
    } else {
      push('note', 'SR: visit Empire \u2192 Units once to enable');
    }
    while (out.length && (out[out.length - 1].kind === 'gap' || out[out.length - 1].kind === 'divide')) out.pop();
    return out.length ? out : null;
  }

  /* Parse the player's OWN units from /empire.aspx?view=units (documented table:
     Unit | Quantity | Unit Cost | Total Cost | ... | Mod. Attack/Armour (Shield)).
     UNVERIFIED against a live capture — matched by header names; null if absent.
     Returns { units:{AB:{qty,atk,arm,shl}}, mainAbbr, updated }. */
  function parseOwnUnits(doc) {
    for (const tbl of doc.querySelectorAll('table')) {
      const rows = [...tbl.querySelectorAll('tr')];
      if (rows.length < 3) continue;
      // Locate the unit-name column by CONTENT (header wording varies / may be an icon).
      const nameCol = (() => {
        const tally = {};
        for (const tr of rows) {
          const tds = [...tr.querySelectorAll('td')];
          tds.forEach((td, idx) => { if (DROP_NAME2AB[txt(td).toLowerCase()]) tally[idx] = (tally[idx] || 0) + 1; });
        }
        let bestIdx = -1, bestN = 0;
        for (const [idx, n] of Object.entries(tally)) if (n > bestN) { bestN = n; bestIdx = +idx; }
        return bestN >= 3 ? bestIdx : -1;     // need several unit rows to be confident
      })();
      if (nameCol < 0) continue;
      const units = {};
      for (const tr of rows) {
        const tds = [...tr.querySelectorAll('td')];
        if (tds.length <= nameCol) continue;
        const ab = DROP_NAME2AB[txt(tds[nameCol]).toLowerCase()]; if (!ab) continue;
        // quantity = first plain-integer cell after the name; stats = first cell matching "a / b (c)"
        let qty = null, atk = null, arm = null, shl = 0;
        for (let k = nameCol + 1; k < tds.length; k++) {
          const cell = txt(tds[k]);
          const mm = cell.match(/^([\d.,]+)\s*\/\s*([\d.,]+)(?:\s*\(([\d.,]+)\))?$/);
          const num = (x) => x == null ? null : parseFloat(String(x).replace(/,/g, ''));
          if (mm) { atk = num(mm[1]); arm = num(mm[2]); shl = mm[3] != null ? num(mm[3]) : 0; continue; }
          if (qty == null && /^[\d,]+$/.test(cell)) qty = num(cell);
        }
        if (qty == null) continue;
        units[ab] = { qty, atk, arm, shl };
      }
      if (!Object.keys(units).length) continue;
      let mainAbbr = null, bestVal = -1;
      for (const [ab, u] of Object.entries(units)) {
        if (DROP_SUPPORT.has(ab) || ab === 'FT' || ab === 'BO') continue;   // meat/support are not the "main"
        const v = u.qty * (DROP_STATS[ab] ? DROP_STATS[ab].c : 0);
        if (v > bestVal) { bestVal = v; mainAbbr = ab; }
      }
      return { units, mainAbbr, updated: Date.now() };
    }
    return null;
  }


  /* Parse a fleet page (/fleet.aspx?fleet=ID) for its LIVE composition. Documented
     shape (own fleet): #fleet_overview table, rows of [icon | unit name | quantity].
     Fallback scans any table for known-unit-name rows with an adjacent count.
     NOTE: the ENEMY-fleet variant of this page is UNVERIFIED — a saved enemy fleet
     page is needed to lock this. Returns { units:{AB:qty}, value } or null. */
  const DROP_NAME2AB = { 'fighters':'FT','bombers':'BO','heavy bombers':'HB','ion bombers':'IB',
    'corvette':'CV','recycler':'RC','destroyer':'DD','frigate':'FR','ion frigate':'IF',
    'scout ship':'SS','outpost ship':'OS','cruiser':'CR','carrier':'CA','heavy cruiser':'HC',
    'battleship':'BS','fleet carrier':'FC','dreadnought':'DN','titan':'TI','leviathan':'LE','death star':'DS' };
  const DROP_NAMES_LONGFIRST = Object.keys(DROP_NAME2AB).sort((a, b) => b.length - a.length);
  function parseFleetComposition(doc) {
    // The page states the fleet's total size, and AE fleet size == credit value, so we
    // can VALIDATE our own parse against it. (This check exists because a build that
    // summed duplicate rows silently double-counted Fighters and reported a fleet
    // worth more than it was.)
    let stated = null;
    const bodyTxt = doc.body ? doc.body.textContent : '';
    const sm = bodyTxt.match(/Fleet\s*Size\s*:?\s*([\d,]+)/i);
    if (sm) stated = parseNum(sm[1]);
    // Consider each table separately and keep the best one — never merge across tables,
    // which is how the same unit can be picked up twice.
    let best = null;
    const tables = [...doc.querySelectorAll('table')];
    const scope = doc.getElementById('fleet_overview');
    if (scope && tables.indexOf(scope) < 0) tables.unshift(scope);
    for (const tbl of tables) {
      const units = {};
      for (const tr of tbl.querySelectorAll('tr')) {
        const cells = [...tr.querySelectorAll('td,th')].map(c => txt(c));
        if (cells.length < 2) continue;
        let ab = null, qty = null;
        for (let k = 0; k < cells.length; k++) {
          const c = cells[k], low = c.toLowerCase();
          if (!ab && DROP_NAME2AB[low]) { ab = DROP_NAME2AB[low]; continue; }
          if (ab && qty == null && /^[\d,]+$/.test(c.trim())) { const n = parseNum(c); if (n != null && !isNaN(n)) qty = n; }
        }
        // A fleet lists each unit type once: take the LARGEST reading rather than a sum,
        // so a repeated row can never inflate the count.
        if (ab && qty != null && qty > 0) units[ab] = Math.max(units[ab] || 0, qty);
      }
      const types = Object.keys(units).length;
      if (!types) continue;
      let value = 0; for (const [ab, q] of Object.entries(units)) value += q * DROP_STATS[ab].c;
      const cand = { units, value, types };
      // Prefer the table whose value matches the stated fleet size; else the richest one.
      if (stated != null && Math.abs(value - stated) <= Math.max(1, stated * 0.005)) { best = cand; break; }
      if (!best || types > best.types) best = cand;
    }
    if (!best) return null;
    const mismatch = (stated != null && Math.abs(best.value - stated) > Math.max(1, stated * 0.005))
      ? { stated, parsed: best.value } : null;
    return { units: best.units, value: best.value, stated, mismatch };
  }


  /* Build the drop-calc hover lines for one player from the hub mirrors.
     Tech source order: player's harvested techs -> their archetype's averages -> base(0).
     Requires a trustworthy spec (>=5 exposures) and a fleet size. Returns
     { lines: [..], techSource } or null when prerequisites are missing. */
  function computeDropForPlayer(mirrorEntry, archetypes, fleetSize, attackerCfg, liveCounts, ownUnits, compMismatch, rankEntry, calib) {
    const spec = mirrorEntry && mirrorEntry.spec;
    /* Composition: live counts, else spec-derived — from the FIRST battle report on
       (no minimum-exposure gate; confidence is surfaced instead of withheld). */
    let counts, compSource;
    if (liveCounts) { counts = liveCounts; compSource = 'LIVE comp'; }
    else {
      if (!spec || !spec.shares || !fleetSize) return null;
      counts = {};
      for (const [ab, share] of Object.entries(spec.shares)) {
        const st = DROP_STATS[ab]; if (!st || !share) continue;
        const c = Math.floor(fleetSize * share / st.c); if (c) counts[ab] = c;
      }
      const exp = spec.obs + spec.drops;
      compSource = 'comp from spec (' + exp + ' BR' + (exp === 1 ? '' : 's') + ')';
    }
    /* Tech: PER-TECH merge with provenance. Harvested values win where they exist;
       everything else comes from the rank-budget estimator (else archetype averages,
       else the flat assumption). Previously a partially-harvested player had their
       missing weapon techs treated as ZERO. */
    const est = estimateEnemyTechs(rankEntry, counts, calib,
      (attackerCfg && attackerCfg.assumeTech != null) ? attackerCfg.assumeTech : DEFAULT_ASSUMED_TECH);
    const arch = (spec && archetypes && archetypes[spec.dom] && archetypes[spec.dom].avgTechs) || null;
    const harv = (mirrorEntry && mirrorEntry.techs) || {};
    const estLabel = est.label.replace(/^est\. tech |^assumed tech /, m => m);   // keep full label
    const techs = {}, srcOf = {};
    for (const t of ASSUMED_TECH_KEYS) {
      if (harv[t] != null) { techs[t] = harv[t]; srcOf[t] = 'harvested'; }
      else if (est.techs && est.techs[t] != null && !/^assumed/.test(est.label)) { techs[t] = est.techs[t]; srcOf[t] = 'estimated'; }
      else if (arch && arch[t] != null) { techs[t] = Math.round(arch[t]); srcOf[t] = 'archetype'; }
      else if (est.techs && est.techs[t] != null) { techs[t] = est.techs[t]; srcOf[t] = 'assumed'; }
      else { techs[t] = 0; srcOf[t] = 'assumed'; }
    }
    const nH = Object.values(srcOf).filter(v => v === 'harvested').length;
    const techSource = nH ? ('harvested +' + ' ' + est.label) : est.label;
    const lines = buildDropLines(counts, techs, attackerCfg, ownUnits);
    if (!lines) return null;
    /* Per-tech provenance line: the RELEVANT techs (defense + weapons of their comp),
       marked * when not harvested — so it is always visible which numbers are real
       measurements and which are model output. */
    const relevant = new Set(['Armour', 'Shielding']);
    Object.keys(counts).forEach(ab => { const st = DROP_STATS[ab]; if (st && TECH_BASE[st.w]) relevant.add(st.w); });
    const techBits = [...relevant].map(t => t + ' ' + techs[t] + (srcOf[t] === 'harvested' ? '' : '*'));
    const anyStar = [...relevant].some(t => srcOf[t] !== 'harvested');
    const head = { kind: 'head', text: 'Drop plan \u00b7 ' + compSource };
    const techLine = { kind: 'note', text: 'tech: ' + techBits.join(' \u00b7 ') + (anyStar ? '   (* ' + est.label.replace('est. tech ', 'est ').replace('assumed tech ', 'assumed ') + ')' : ' (harvested)') };
    const tail = [];
    if (compMismatch) tail.push({ kind: 'note', text: 'comp check: parsed ' + compMismatch.parsed.toLocaleString()
      + ' vs stated fleet size ' + compMismatch.stated.toLocaleString() + ' \u2014 treat with caution' });
    return { lines: [head, techLine].concat(lines, tail), techSource, compSource };
  }



  /* Annotate every fleet row (the sorttable listing that follows #map_fleets /
     #base_fleets: Fleet | Player | Arrival | Size) with the % of that player's TOTAL
     fleet (from rankings) present in the row. Trusted-Types safe (createElement only).
     Verified against the live base-page fixture. Returns rows annotated. */
  const RANK_STALE_MS = 24 * 3600 * 1000;   // >24h: stop injecting %, mark out of date
  const RANK_REMIND_MS = 12 * 3600 * 1000;  // >12h: prompt the user to re-parse ranks
  function rankAgeMs() { const rk = getRankings(); return rk && rk.updated ? (Date.now() - rk.updated) : null; }
  function annotateFleetPercents(root, blobOrPlayers, refresh) {
    const players = blobOrPlayers && (blobOrPlayers.players || blobOrPlayers);
    const updated = blobOrPlayers && blobOrPlayers.players ? blobOrPlayers.updated : null;
    const stale = updated != null && (Date.now() - updated) > RANK_STALE_MS;
    if (!players) return 0;
    const byId = {}; Object.values(players).forEach(p => { if (p.playerId) byId[p.playerId] = p; });
    let n = 0;
    for (const tbl of root.querySelectorAll('table')) {
      const first = tbl.querySelector('tr'); if (!first) continue;
      const heads = [...first.querySelectorAll('td,th')].map(c => txt(c).toLowerCase());
      const si = heads.indexOf('size'), pi = heads.indexOf('player');
      if (si < 0 || pi < 0 || !heads.includes('fleet')) continue;
      for (const tr of [...tbl.querySelectorAll('tr')].slice(1)) {
        const tds = tr.querySelectorAll('td'); if (tds.length <= si) continue;
        const cell = tds[si];
        if (cell.getAttribute('data-ae-fleetpct')) {
          if (!refresh) continue;
          const old = cell.querySelector('[data-ae-fleetpct-span]'); if (old) old.remove();
        }
        const link = tds[pi].querySelector('a[href*="player="]');
        if (!link) continue;
        const m = (link.getAttribute('href') || '').match(/player=(\d+)/); if (!m) continue;
        const rp = byId[m[1]]; if (!rp || !rp.fleet) continue;
        const size = parseNum(cell.getAttribute('sorttable_customkey') || txt(cell));
        if (size == null || isNaN(size)) continue;
        const span = cell.ownerDocument.createElement('span');
        if (stale) { span.textContent = ' (out of date info)'; }
        else { const pct = Math.round(100 * size / rp.fleet); span.textContent = ' (' + pct + '%)'; }
        span.style.fontSize = '10px'; span.style.opacity = '0.65';
        span.setAttribute('data-ae-fleetpct-span', '1');
        cell.appendChild(span); cell.setAttribute('data-ae-fleetpct', '1'); n++;
      }
    }
    return n;
  }

  function parseRecordedAge(doc) {
    const nodes = [...doc.querySelectorAll('center')];
    for (const n of nodes) {
      const t = txt(n).toLowerCase();
      const start = t.indexOf('recorded data from ');
      if (start >= 0) {
        const rest = txt(n).slice(start + 'recorded data from '.length);
        const end = rest.toLowerCase().indexOf(' ago');
        if (end >= 0) return rest.slice(0, end).trim() + ' ago';
      }
    }
    return null;
  }

  /* Base commander (if one is assigned). Appears as a table cell:
     "Base Commander: <a href=commander.aspx?commander=ID>Name</a> <small>(Type Level)</small>"
     Type is one of Logistics/Construction/Production/Tactical/Defense/Research. Returns
     { name, id, type, level, label } where label is the short form "Lvl 15 Con Comm", or
     null if no commander is assigned. Works on rival bases (seen on a non-owned base). */
  const COMMANDER_ABBR = {
    logistics: 'Log Comm', construction: 'Con Comm', production: 'Prod Comm',
    tactical: 'Tac Comm', defense: 'Def Comm', defence: 'Def Comm', research: 'Res Comm'
  };
  function parseCommander(doc) {
    // find the cell whose text starts with "Base Commander:"
    let cell = null;
    const tds = doc.querySelectorAll('td');
    for (const td of tds) { if (/Base Commander\s*:/i.test(txt(td))) { cell = td; break; } }
    if (!cell) return null;
    const link = cell.querySelector('a[href*="commander.aspx"]');
    const name = link ? txt(link) : null;
    let id = null;
    if (link) { const m = (link.getAttribute('href') || '').match(/commander=(\d+)/); if (m) id = m[1]; }
    const small = cell.querySelector('small');
    let type = null, level = null;
    if (small) {
      const m = txt(small).match(/\(\s*([A-Za-z]+)\s+(\d+)\s*\)/);   // "(Construction 15)"
      if (m) { type = m[1]; level = parseInt(m[2], 10); }
    }
    if (!type) return null;
    const abbr = COMMANDER_ABBR[type.toLowerCase()] || (type + ' Comm');
    return { name, id, type, level, label: 'Lvl ' + level + ' ' + abbr };
  }

  function parseStructures(doc) {
    const tbl = doc.querySelector('#base_resume-structures');
    if (!tbl) return null;
    const cells = [...tbl.querySelectorAll('td')];
    const splitBr = (el) => el ? [...el.querySelectorAll('br')].length >= 0 &&
      el.innerHTML.split(/<br\s*\/?>/i).map(x => x.replace(/<[^>]+>/g, '').trim()).filter(Boolean) : [];
    // pairs of (names cell, levels cell); page groups structures / defenses / units
    const structures = {}, defenses = {};
    for (let i = 0; i < cells.length - 1; i++) {
      const names = splitBr(cells[i]);
      const vals = splitBr(cells[i + 1]);
      if (names.length && vals.length && names.length === vals.length) {
        names.forEach((n, k) => {
          const ratio = parseRatio(vals[k]);
          if (ratio && ratio.max != null) defenses[n] = ratio;   // "0 / 5" -> defense
          else { const num = parseNum(vals[k]); if (num != null) structures[n] = num; }
        });
        i++;
      }
    }
    return { structures, defenses };
  }

  function parseBasesTable(doc, coord) {
    const out = [];
    const mb = doc.querySelector('#map_base');
    if (!mb) return out;                          // empty astro / debris / no base here
    // #map_base wraps an inner table, so the OUTER row also matches a link query;
    // pick the row whose own cells hold the 4 data columns.
    const rows = [...mb.querySelectorAll('tr')];
    const dataRow = rows.find(r => r.cells.length >= 4 &&
      r.querySelector('a[href*="profile.aspx"], a[href*="base.aspx"]'));
    if (!dataRow) return out;
    const c = [...dataRow.cells];
    if (c.length < 4) return out;

    const owner = parsePlayerCell(txt(c[1]));
    const occ = c[2] ? parsePlayerCell(txt(c[2])) : { guild: null, player: '' };
    const eco = c[3] ? parseRatio(txt(c[3])) : null;
    const astro = parseAstroHeader(doc);
    const struct = parseStructures(doc);

    const rec = {
      coord: coord || (function () {
        const m = /loc=(B\d+:\d+:\d+:\d+)/.exec((mb.querySelector('a[href*="loc="]') || {}).href || ''); return m ? m[1] : null;
      })(),
      baseName: txt(c[0]) || null,
      baseId: idFrom(c[0], 'base'),
      player: owner.player || null,
      playerId: idFrom(c[1], 'player'),
      guild: owner.guild || null,
      occupier: occ.player || null,
      occupierGuild: occ.guild || null,
      occupierId: c[2] ? idFrom(c[2], 'player') : null,
      economy: eco ? eco.val : null,
      economyMax: eco ? eco.max : null,
      planetType: astro.planetType,
      terrain: astro.terrain,
      area: astro.area
    };
    // NOTE: full structures/defenses live on the base.aspx page, NOT the map astro
    // page. parseStructures() reads them when a base.aspx doc is passed (e.g. an
    // optional deeper fetch); on a plain map page it simply returns null.
    if (struct) { rec.structures = struct.structures; rec.defenses = struct.defenses; }
    if (rec.coord) out.push(rec);
    return out;
  }

  /* System view lists which astros EXIST (type + coord); no owners. Use it to
     build the crawl queue — see queueFromSystem().

     GHOST ASTROS: the game sometimes carries astros that are NOT rendered to the
     player. Aran's rule: if the user cannot see it, do not parse it. So an astro
     only counts when it has a genuine VISUAL — an <img class="astro"> with a real
     src, a title, and non-zero size. Anything without a rendered image is dropped
     and recorded in `.ghosts` for inspection.

     ⚠ UNVERIFIED: none of the supplied fixtures contain a ghost, so the exact DOM
     signature is unconfirmed. This filter is written to the DEFINITION (no visual
     => not real) rather than to a shape I have observed. If ghosts turn out to
     render a hidden/placeholder <img>, harden hasVisual() with a real sample. */
  function hasVisual(img) {
    if (!img) return false;
    const src = (img.getAttribute('src') || '').trim();
    if (!src || /^data:image\/gif;base64,R0lGOD/i.test(src)) return false;  // missing / 1px spacer
    const w = parseInt(img.getAttribute('width'), 10);
    const h = parseInt(img.getAttribute('height'), 10);
    if ((!isNaN(w) && w <= 1) || (!isNaN(h) && h <= 1)) return false;        // zero/1px = not shown
    const st = (img.getAttribute('style') || '');
    if (/display\s*:\s*none|visibility\s*:\s*hidden/i.test(st)) return false;
    if (!(img.getAttribute('title') || '').trim()) return false;             // real astros carry a title
    return true;
  }

  function parseSystemAstros(doc) {
    const out = [], ghosts = [], seen = {};
    // NOTE: a saved page duplicates each <img>; dedupe by coord.
    [...doc.querySelectorAll('img.astro[id^="astro_"]')].forEach(img => {
      const coord = img.id.replace('astro_', '');
      if (!isCoord(coord) || seen[coord]) return;
      seen[coord] = true;
      // Two independent ghost tests, belt and braces:
      //   1. coord rule — orbit 1-5 / moon 0-3. Costs no fetch and no DOM.
      //   2. DOM visual — "if the user can't see it, don't parse it".
      if (!isValidAstroCoord(coord) || !hasVisual(img)) { ghosts.push(coord); return; }
      const cls = (img.className.match(/astro_([a-z-]+)/) || [])[1] || null;
      const slot = astroSlot(coord);
      out.push({
        coord,
        type: cls,
        title: img.getAttribute('title') || null,
        orbit: slot ? slot.orbit : null,
        moon: slot ? slot.moon : null,
        isPlanet: slot ? slot.isPlanet : null,
        filler: isFillerType(cls)      // gas giant / asteroid belt -> can never hold a base
      });
    });
    out.ghosts = ghosts;
    // The only astros worth spending a fetch on.
    out.functional = out.filter(isFunctionalAstro);
    if (ghosts.length) console.warn('[AEMapIntel] skipped ' + ghosts.length + ' ghost astro(s):', ghosts);
    return out;
  }

  const PARSER_READY = true;   // verified against real samples

  /* Given a parsed bases-report (rows from parseGalaxyBasesReport) and a scope
     matcher, keep only in-scope rows, upsert their discovery fields (coord, player,
     guild, baseId) with via:'report', and return the list of base targets to open
     for DETAIL. This is the bridge between "report gives the list" and "walk each
     base page". */
  function ingestReportRowsInScope(rows, matcher) {
    const targets = [];
    (rows || []).forEach(r => {
      if (matcher && !matcher.test(r.coord)) return;      // outside the user's range
      upsertBase(Object.assign({ via: 'report' }, r));
      if (r.baseId) targets.push({ base: r.baseId, coord: r.coord, baseName: r.baseName });
    });
    return targets;
  }

  /* ── PRIMARY DISCOVERY: one POST per galaxy ───────────────────────────────
     The Bases report returns EVERY base the galaxy shows us — 263 in the sample —
     each with coord, player(+id), guild and base id. That is the whole base
     database for a galaxy in ONE request.

     This supersedes system-by-system crawling for DISCOVERY:
        report:  1 POST  per galaxy      -> all visible bases
        crawl:   1 + ~7 fetches PER SYSTEM -> the same bases, far more slowly
     The map crawl is now only needed to ENRICH (astro type/terrain/economy) or to
     reach regions the report cannot see.

     ⚠ COVERAGE: the report only spans "regions where your guild has fleets or a
     base, or regions recently visited by your guild fleets". So it is a lower
     bound — absence is NOT proof a base doesn't exist. Records are marked with
     `via:'report'` so a later, richer map harvest can be told apart from it. */
  async function harvestGalaxyBases(galaxy) {
    const doc = await fetchGalaxyReport(galaxy, 'bases');
    const rows = parseGalaxyBasesReport(doc);
    rows.forEach(r => upsertBase(Object.assign({ via: 'report' }, r)));
    return { galaxy, bases: rows.length };
  }

  /* Enrich astro facts (terrain/type/area/resources) for a galaxy from the astros
     report. Adaptive under the 250 cap; returns .incomplete when a slice is
     unreachable. Cheap compared with fetching each astro page. */
  async function harvestGalaxyAstros(galaxy, onProgress) {
    const rows = await enumerateGalaxyAstros(galaxy, onProgress);
    rows.forEach(a => {
      // Only record astro facts; do NOT invent a base where the report shows none.
      if (a.hasBase || getBase(a.coord)) upsertBase(Object.assign({ via: 'astros-report' }, a));
    });
    return { galaxy, astros: rows.length, incomplete: !!rows.incomplete, capExhausted: rows.capExhausted || [] };
  }

  /* ── BULK COLLECTION: sweep many galaxies, paced + resumable ───────────────
     One report POST per galaxy for bases; optionally the astros report per galaxy
     for enrichment. Between requests it waits the SAME jittered delay the crawl
     uses (user-defined, floored), so a multi-galaxy sweep looks like the report
     refresher rather than a burst. Stop any time; progress persists so it resumes.

     Detectability: this is ~50 POSTs for a whole server's visible bases — orders of
     magnitude quieter than the per-astro crawl, which is why it is the default. */
  const BULK = { active: false, abort: false };
  function bulkState() { return { active: BULK.active }; }
  /* Stop the background sweep. It cannot cancel an in-flight report POST (fetch has
     no abort here), so the CURRENT report finishes; but it will not start the astros
     phase or the next galaxy. For truly immediate stop use the visible collector,
     whose Kill aborts the between-page wait and closes the scan window at once. */
  function stopBulk() { BULK.abort = true; }

  function saveBulkResume(queue, mode) {
    const { all, blk } = serverBlock();
    blk.meta = blk.meta || {};
    blk.meta.bulk = { queue: queue.slice(), mode, ts: Date.now() };
    writeAll(all);
  }
  function loadBulkResume() {
    const { blk } = serverBlock();
    return (blk.meta && blk.meta.bulk) || null;
  }
  function clearBulkResume() {
    const { all, blk } = serverBlock();
    if (blk.meta) delete blk.meta.bulk;
    writeAll(all);
  }

  /* galaxies: array of labels ('B00'..'B49') or null to resume / cover 0..49.
     mode: 'bases' | 'bases+astros'. onProgress({galaxy, bases, astros, done, left}). */
  async function startBulk(galaxies, mode, onProgress) {
    if (BULK.active) return { already: true };
    mode = mode || 'bases';
    let queue;
    const resume = loadBulkResume();
    if (galaxies && galaxies.length) queue = galaxies.slice();
    else if (resume && resume.queue.length) { queue = resume.queue.slice(); mode = resume.mode || mode; }
    else { queue = []; for (let i = 0; i <= 49; i++) queue.push('B' + String(i).padStart(2, '0')); }

    BULK.active = true; BULK.abort = false;
    const cfg = getCrawlCfg();
    let done = 0;
    const totals = { bases: 0, astros: 0, galaxies: 0, incompleteGalaxies: [] };

    while (queue.length && !BULK.abort) {
      if (typeof document !== 'undefined' && document.hidden) { await sleep(2000); continue; }
      const g = queue[0];
      try {
        const rb = await harvestGalaxyBases(g);
        totals.bases += rb.bases;
        let astros = 0;
        if (mode === 'bases+astros' && !BULK.abort) {   // honour a stop before the (long) astros phase
          await sleep(jitterMs(cfg.baseDelaySec, cfg.floorSec));
          if (BULK.abort) { break; }
          const ra = await harvestGalaxyAstros(g);
          astros = ra.astros;
          totals.astros += astros;
          if (ra.incomplete) totals.incompleteGalaxies.push(g);
        }
        queue.shift();
        done++; totals.galaxies++;
        saveBulkResume(queue, mode);
        if (onProgress) onProgress({ galaxy: g, bases: rb.bases, astros, done, left: queue.length });
      } catch (e) {
        console.warn('[AEMapIntel] bulk error at', g, e);
        if (onProgress) onProgress({ galaxy: g, error: String(e), done, left: queue.length });
        queue.shift();            // skip a failing galaxy rather than stall the sweep
        saveBulkResume(queue, mode);
      }
      if (queue.length && !BULK.abort) await sleep(jitterMs(cfg.baseDelaySec, cfg.floorSec));
    }

    if (!queue.length) clearBulkResume();
    BULK.active = false;
    return Object.assign({ aborted: BULK.abort, left: queue.length }, totals);
  }

  /* ── UNIFIED SCAN ENGINE (fetch-first, adopted from the reference collector) ──
     The old collector drove a form-submit handshake + per-page self-parse + a
     postMessage update chain — three fragile moving parts. This replaces all of it
     with the reference collector's far simpler model:
       - fetch() each URL in the background, parse the returned HTML with DOMParser,
         upsert to the store. No iframe, no window.name guard, no handshake.
       - AbortController for INSTANT stop (mid-request, not just between pages).
       - progress via a callback; the DB tab live-updates off the revision bump.
     `visible` mode is offered for watching, but fetch is the robust default.

     Flow from a scope string:
       1. planScan -> direct astros + galaxies to report + scope matcher.
       2. for each galaxy: fetch its bases report, filter to scope -> base targets.
       3. for each astro term: fetch the map page; if a base link is there, queue it.
       4. fetch each base page -> full detail (structures/defenses/TR/fleets/economy).
     onProgress({phase, current, done, total, found, bases, error}). */
