  let SCAN = { abort: null, active: false, paused: false };
  function scanActive() { return SCAN.active; }
  function scanPaused() { return SCAN.paused; }
  function pauseScan() { if (SCAN.active) SCAN.paused = true; }
  function resumeScanRun() { SCAN.paused = false; }
  function stopScan() { SCAN.paused = false; if (SCAN.abort) { try { SCAN.abort.abort(); } catch (e) {} } SCAN.active = false; }
  // Await this between fetches; it blocks while paused and throws if stopped mid-pause.
  async function pauseGate(signal) {
    while (SCAN.paused) {
      if (signal && signal.aborted) throw new Error('stopped');
      await sleep(300);
    }
  }

  async function fetchDoc(url, signal) {
    const res = await fetch(url, { credentials: 'include', cache: 'no-store', signal: signal });
    if (!res.ok) throw new Error('HTTP ' + res.status);
    return new DOMParser().parseFromString(await res.text(), 'text/html');
  }

  /* Prep step: run ONLY the discovery phase (galaxy reports + direct astro map
     visits) to determine the list of base pages that WILL be fetched, without
     fetching any base detail. Lets the UI show "N bases, ETA X" before committing
     to the slow part. Returns { targets:[{base,coord}], count, astros, galaxies }. */
  async function prepScan(scopeText, opts) {
    opts = opts || {};
    const plan = planScan(scopeText);
    if (!plan) throw new Error('could not parse scope: ' + scopeText);
    const cfg = getCrawlCfg();
    const baseSec = opts.intervalSec != null ? Math.max(cfg.floorSec, opts.intervalSec) : cfg.baseDelaySec;
    const origin = (typeof location !== 'undefined' && location.origin) ? location.origin : '';
    const targets = [];
    for (const g of plan.galaxies) {
      let rows = [];
      try { rows = parseGalaxyBasesReport(await fetchGalaxyReport(g, 'bases')); } catch (e) { continue; }
      ingestReportRowsInScope(rows, plan.matcher).forEach(t => targets.push(t));
    }
    for (const a of plan.astros) {
      try {
        const doc = await fetchDoc(origin + '/map.aspx?loc=' + encodeURIComponent(a.coord), null);
        const r = ingestLoadedPage(doc, { coord: a.coord });
        if (r.baseId) targets.push({ base: r.baseId, coord: a.coord });
      } catch (e) {}
    }
    // Trade-plunder adds a second fetch per base -> ~2x the per-base time (conservative;
    // only unfogged bases trade-fetch, but fog status is unknown until fetched).
    const perPage = meanDelaySec(baseSec, cfg.floorSec) * (cfg.tradePlunder ? 2 : 1);
    return { targets, count: targets.length, etaSec: Math.round(targets.length * perPage),
             perPageSec: perPage, galaxies: plan.galaxies.length, astros: plan.astros.length };
  }

  // ── resume persistence: survive the user navigating away mid-scan ──
  // Phase 3 (base fetching) is the long part. We persist the full target list + the
  // current index to localStorage after each base, so a reload can resume phase 3
  // exactly where it stopped (phases 1-2 are discovery and don't need replaying).
  const RESUME_KEY = 'ae::bases::scanresume::' + SERVER;
  function saveScanResume(rec) { try { localStorage.setItem(RESUME_KEY, JSON.stringify(rec)); } catch (e) {} }
  function loadScanResume() { try { return JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(RESUME_KEY)); } catch (e) { return null; } }
  function clearScanResume() { try { localStorage.removeItem(RESUME_KEY); } catch (e) {} }
  function scanResumeInfo() {
    const r = loadScanResume();
    if (!r || !r.targets || r.index >= r.targets.length) return null;
    return { scope: r.scope, left: r.targets.length - r.index, total: r.targets.length, index: r.index };
  }

  async function runScan(scopeText, opts, onProgress) {
    if (SCAN.active) return { already: true };
    opts = opts || {};
    const plan = planScan(scopeText);
    if (!plan) throw new Error('could not parse scope: ' + scopeText);
    const cfg = getCrawlCfg();
    const baseSec = opts.intervalSec != null ? Math.max(cfg.floorSec, opts.intervalSec) : cfg.baseDelaySec;
    const origin = (typeof location !== 'undefined' && location.origin) ? location.origin : '';
    SCAN.abort = new AbortController();
    SCAN.active = true;
    const signal = SCAN.abort.signal;
    const prog = (o) => { if (onProgress) onProgress(o); };
    const totals = { found: 0, bases: 0, errors: 0, done: 0 };

    // base targets to fetch for full detail: {base, coord, baseName}
    let baseTargets = [];
    let startIndex = 0;

    // If resuming, skip discovery and reuse the saved target list + index.
    if (opts.resume) {
      const rec = loadScanResume();
      if (rec && rec.targets && rec.index < rec.targets.length) {
        baseTargets = rec.targets;
        startIndex = rec.index;
        scopeText = rec.scope || scopeText;
      } else { opts.resume = false; }
    }

    try {
      if (!opts.resume) {
        // ---- phase 1: galaxy reports -> in-scope base targets ----
        for (let gi = 0; gi < plan.galaxies.length; gi++) {
          if (signal.aborted) throw new Error('stopped');
          const g = plan.galaxies[gi];
          prog({ phase: 'report', current: g, done: totals.done });
          let rows = [];
          try { rows = parseGalaxyBasesReport(await fetchGalaxyReport(g, 'bases')); }
          catch (e) { totals.errors++; prog({ phase: 'report', current: g, error: String(e) }); continue; }
          const targets = ingestReportRowsInScope(rows, plan.matcher);
          targets.forEach(t => baseTargets.push(t));
          totals.found += targets.length;
          prog({ phase: 'report', current: g, found: targets.length, totalRows: rows.length, done: totals.done });
          if (gi < plan.galaxies.length - 1) await sleep(jitterMs(baseSec, cfg.floorSec));
        }

        // ---- phase 2: direct astro terms -> map page -> queue base if present ----
        for (let ai = 0; ai < plan.astros.length; ai++) {
          if (signal.aborted) throw new Error('stopped');
          const coord = plan.astros[ai].coord;
          prog({ phase: 'astro', current: coord, done: totals.done });
          try {
            const doc = await fetchDoc(origin + '/map.aspx?loc=' + encodeURIComponent(coord), signal);
            const r = ingestLoadedPage(doc, { coord });
            if (r.empty) { prog({ phase: 'astro', current: coord, error: 'no base here' }); }
            else if (r.baseId) { baseTargets.push({ base: r.baseId, coord }); totals.found++; }
          } catch (e) {
            if (signal.aborted) throw new Error('stopped');
            totals.errors++; prog({ phase: 'astro', current: coord, error: String(e) });
          }
          totals.done++;
          if (ai < plan.astros.length - 1) await sleep(jitterMs(baseSec, cfg.floorSec));
        }

        // discovery complete — write the initial resume record before the long phase
        saveScanResume({ scope: scopeText, targets: baseTargets, index: 0, savedAt: Date.now() });
      }

      // ---- phase 3: fetch each base page for full detail (resumable) ----
      for (let bi = startIndex; bi < baseTargets.length; bi++) {
        if (signal.aborted) throw new Error('stopped');
        if (SCAN.paused) { prog({ phase: 'paused', done: totals.done, total: baseTargets.length, index: bi, startIndex: startIndex }); await pauseGate(signal); }
        const t = baseTargets[bi];
        prog({ phase: 'base', current: t.coord || t.base, done: totals.done, total: baseTargets.length, index: bi, startIndex: startIndex, resumed: !!opts.resume });
        try {
          const doc = await fetchDoc(origin + '/base.aspx?base=' + encodeURIComponent(t.base), signal);
          const r = ingestLoadedPage(doc, { base: t.base, coord: t.coord });
          if (r.bases) totals.bases++;
          prog({ phase: 'base', current: t.coord || t.base, done: totals.done + 1, total: baseTargets.length, parsed: true, index: bi, startIndex: startIndex, resumed: !!opts.resume });

          // FOGGED-BASE FALLBACK: a fogged base page omits the economy/capacity block, but
          // the MAP page's base table still shows economy (used/max), owner, and area. If we
          // have the coord but economy didn't come through, fetch the map page to fill it.
          const afterBase = getBase(r.coord || t.coord) || {};
          if ((r.coord || t.coord) && afterBase.economy == null) {
            if (signal.aborted) throw new Error('stopped');
            await sleep(jitterMs(baseSec, cfg.floorSec));   // its own paced interval
            prog({ phase: 'base', current: (r.coord || t.coord) + ' (map)', done: totals.done + 1, total: baseTargets.length, index: bi, startIndex: startIndex });
            try {
              const mdoc = await fetchDoc(origin + '/map.aspx?loc=' + encodeURIComponent(r.coord || t.coord), signal);
              ingestLoadedPage(mdoc, { coord: r.coord || t.coord });
            } catch (e) { if (signal.aborted) throw new Error('stopped'); totals.errors++; }
          }

          // Optional extra step: fetch the trade sub-page for TR plunder (2*distance summed).
          // Gated behind cfg.tradePlunder AND fog-of-war: TRs are only visible when the base
          // is UNFOGGED, which AE denotes by a BLANK Intel Age (recordedAge). If recordedAge
          // has a value the base is fogged and the trade link won't render — skip the fetch.
          const justParsed = getBase(r.coord) || {};
          const unfogged = !justParsed.recordedAge;   // blank/null Intel Age = current vision
          if (cfg.tradePlunder && r.coord && unfogged) {
            if (signal.aborted) throw new Error('stopped');
            await sleep(jitterMs(baseSec, cfg.floorSec));   // separate interval before the trade fetch
            prog({ phase: 'trade', current: t.coord || t.base, done: totals.done, total: baseTargets.length });
            try {
              const td = await fetchDoc(origin + '/base.aspx?base=' + encodeURIComponent(t.base) + '&view=trade', signal);
              const tp = parseTradePlunder(td);
              if (tp) upsertBase({ coord: r.coord, trPlunder: tp.plunder, trRoutesActive: tp.routes });
            } catch (e) {
              if (signal.aborted) throw new Error('stopped');
              totals.errors++; prog({ phase: 'trade', current: t.coord || t.base, error: String(e) });
            }
          }
        } catch (e) {
          if (signal.aborted) throw new Error('stopped');
          totals.errors++; prog({ phase: 'base', current: t.coord || t.base, error: String(e) });
        }
        totals.done++;
        // persist progress AFTER each base so a reload resumes at the next one
        saveScanResume({ scope: scopeText, targets: baseTargets, index: bi + 1, savedAt: Date.now() });
        if (bi < baseTargets.length - 1) await sleep(jitterMs(baseSec, cfg.floorSec));
      }

      SCAN.active = false;
      clearScanResume();   // finished cleanly — nothing to resume
      prog({ phase: 'done', done: totals.done, bases: totals.bases, found: totals.found, errors: totals.errors });
      return Object.assign({ complete: true }, totals);
    } catch (e) {
      SCAN.active = false;
      const stopped = /stopped/.test(String(e));
      // NOTE: on stop/error we deliberately KEEP the resume record so the user can
      // pick up where they left off. It's cleared only on clean completion or an
      // explicit new scan / clear.
      prog({ phase: stopped ? 'stopped' : 'error', done: totals.done, error: stopped ? null : String(e) });
      return Object.assign({ complete: false, stopped: stopped }, totals);
    }
  }

  /* ---------- LAZY harvest: parse the page the user is already on ---------- */
  /* Astro details from an own-base overview page (Fertility, Solar Energy, Resources
     Metal/Gas/Crystals + Area) — feeds the build advisor. Text-scanned; null fields
     when absent. */
  function parseAstroDetails(doc) {
    const t = (doc.body ? doc.body.textContent : '').replace(/\s+/g, ' ');
    const num = (re) => { const m = t.match(re); return m ? parseInt(m[1].replace(/,/g, ''), 10) : null; };
    return { area: num(/Area\s*:?\s*([\d,]+)/i), fertility: num(/Fertility\s*:?\s*([\d,]+)/i),
             solar: num(/Solar(?:\s*Energy)?\s*:?\s*([\d,]+)/i),
             metal: num(/Metal\s*:?\s*([\d,]+)/i), gas: num(/Gas\s*:?\s*([\d,]+)/i),
             crystal: num(/Crystals?\s*:?\s*([\d,]+)/i) };
  }
  const OWNUNITS_KEY = 'ae::ownunits::' + SERVER;
  function getOwnUnits() { try { return JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(OWNUNITS_KEY)); } catch (e) { return null; } }
  /* Called from every page build. Detects Empire -> Units (by URL or, if the URL shape
     differs, by the table's own headers) and stores the parsed composition. */
  function captureOwnUnitsIfOnPage(doc) {
    const onUnitsUrl = /\/empire\.aspx/i.test(location.pathname) && /view=units/i.test(location.search);
    if (!onUnitsUrl && !/\/empire\.aspx/i.test(location.pathname)) return null;
    const own = parseOwnUnits(doc);   // header-matched: safe to attempt on any empire page
    if (!own) return null;
    try { localStorage.setItem(OWNUNITS_KEY, JSON.stringify(own)); } catch (e) { return null; }
    return own;
  }
  function harvestCurrentPage() {
    if (!PARSER_READY) return { skipped: 'parser not implemented' };
    // Own-units capture: whenever the user is ON Empire -> Units, refresh the stored
    // composition + modified stats (feeds the drop-calc SR line and the main-spec
    // reconfirm nudge in the predictor).
    if (/\/empire\.aspx/i.test(location.pathname) && /view=units/i.test(location.search)) {
      try { const own = parseOwnUnits(document);
        if (own) { localStorage.setItem(OWNUNITS_KEY, JSON.stringify(own)); return { ownUnits: Object.keys(own.units).length, mainAbbr: own.mainAbbr }; }
      } catch (e) { console.warn('[AEMapIntel] own-units parse failed', e); }
      return { skipped: 'own-units not parsed' };
    }
    if (!/\/map\.aspx/i.test(location.pathname)) return { skipped: 'not a map page' };
    try {
      const coord = new URLSearchParams(location.search).get('loc');
      const rows = parseBasesTable(document, coord);
      rows.forEach(upsertBase);
      return { harvested: rows.length, coord };
    } catch (e) {
      console.warn('[AEMapIntel] lazy harvest failed', e);
      return { error: String(e) };
    }
  }

  /* =========================================================================
   * GALAXY BASES REPORT — existence cross-reference
   *
   * report.aspx?view=galaxy is a POST form (field names verified from a real
   * page):   galaxy = 0..49  (option value; label is B00..B49)
   *          report_type = 'bases' | 'fleets' | 'moving_fleets'
   *          submit "Get Report"
   * ONE request returns every base the game will show us for a galaxy — far
   * cheaper than walking astros, and it is authoritative about what EXISTS, so
   * it is the natural cross-check against crawled/ghost coords.
   *
   * ⚠ COVERAGE IS NOT COMPLETE. The page says so itself: the report covers only
   * "regions where your guild has fleets or base, or regions recently visited by
   * your guild fleets". So it CONFIRMS bases, but its silence does NOT prove a
   * base is absent — outside guild visibility it simply cannot see. Treat a hit
   * as fact and a miss as UNKNOWN, never as "does not exist".
   *
   * NOTE: the Report Scanner drives this same form IN-PAGE (form.submit(), full
   * navigation) for moving_fleets. This is a BACKGROUND POST for report_type=
   * bases — it must not navigate the user's page.
   * ========================================================================= */
  /* ── ASTROS REPORT (report.aspx?view=astros) ──────────────────────────────
     Form fields VERIFIED from a real page:
       form_status = 'submitted'   (hidden; REQUIRED)
       galaxy      = '0'..'49'
       terrain     = '' (Any) | one of ASTRO_TERRAINS
       astro_type  = '' (Any) | 'planet' | 'moon'
       solar_pos   = '0' (Any) | '1'..'5'

     This form independently CONFIRMS both astro rules:
       - solar_pos offers exactly 1..5  -> the GAME confirms orbit 1..5.
       - the terrain list has 'Asteroid' but NO Gas Giant and NO Asteroid Belt
         -> they are not real astro terrains, corroborating them as filler.
     The map's img class IS the terrain, lowercased (toxic, rocky, craters, asteroid).

     ⚠ 250-ROW CAP (Aran): a report returns at most 250 astros, so a whole galaxy
     needs repeated filtered queries. Do it ADAPTIVELY — query broad and subdivide
     ONLY buckets that come back capped. A blind full sweep would be
     50 x 5 x 2 x 15 = 7,500 POSTs, most returning nothing. */
  const ASTRO_TERRAINS = ['Arid', 'Asteroid', 'Craters', 'Crystalline', 'Earthly', 'Gaia',
    'Glacial', 'Magma', 'Metallic', 'Oceanic', 'Radioactive', 'Rocky', 'Toxic', 'Tundra', 'Volcanic'];
  const ASTRO_TYPES = ['planet', 'moon'];
  const SOLAR_POS = ['1', '2', '3', '4', '5'];
  const ASTROS_ROW_CAP = 250;
  const ASTROS_REPORT_READY = true;   // parser verified against a real submitted result

  /* ⚠ SEAM — the astros RESULT page has NOT been sampled: the supplied "submitted"
     file contained the form only, with zero result rows. Throws rather than
     returning [], which would falsely read as "no astros in this galaxy". */
  /* Result table for BOTH reports. Verified from submitted pages. */
  const RESULT_TBL = 'table.listing.sorttable, table.tbllisting1.sorttable, table.sorttable';

  function resultRows(doc) {
    const tbl = doc.querySelector(RESULT_TBL);
    if (!tbl) return [];
    return [...tbl.querySelectorAll('tr')].filter(r => r.cells.length >= 3 &&
      r.querySelector('a[href*="loc="]'));          // header row has no coord link
  }
  function coordOf(cell) {
    const a = cell && cell.querySelector('a[href*="loc="]');
    const m = a && /loc=([A-Za-z]\d{2}:\d{2}:\d{2}:\d+)/.exec(a.getAttribute('href') || '');
    return m ? m[1] : null;
  }

  /* Attributes cell: "A65 S2 F4 M4 G4 C2"
     A=Area  S=Solar energy  F=Fertility  M=Metal  G=Gas  C=Crystals
     (cross-checked against the map astro page: Area 75 / Solar 3 / Fert 5 / M4 G2 C2). */
  function parseAttributes(text) {
    const t = String(text || '');
    const get = (k) => { const m = new RegExp('\\b' + k + '(\\d+(?:\\.\\d+)?)').exec(t); return m ? parseFloat(m[1]) : null; };
    return { area: get('A'), solar: get('S'), fertility: get('F'),
             metal: get('M'), gas: get('G'), crystal: get('C'), raw: t.trim() || null };
  }

  /* ASTROS report rows: Location | Terrain | Type | Attributes | Has Base */
  const ASTROS_REPORT_READY_REAL = true;
  function parseAstrosReport(doc) {
    return resultRows(doc).map(r => {
      const c = [...r.cells];
      const coord = coordOf(c[0]);
      if (!coord) return null;
      const attrs = parseAttributes(txt(c[3]));
      return {
        coord,
        terrain: txt(c[1]) || null,
        astroType: txt(c[2]) || null,
        area: attrs.area, solar: attrs.solar, fertility: attrs.fertility,
        metal: attrs.metal, gas: attrs.gas, crystal: attrs.crystal,
        attributes: attrs.raw,
        hasBase: !!(c[4] && (txt(c[4]) || c[4].querySelector('a, img')))
      };
    }).filter(Boolean);
  }

  /* BASES report rows: Player "[GUILD] Name" | Base | Location
     One POST returns EVERY visible base in the galaxy (263 in the sample — note this
     is ABOVE the astros report's 250 cap, so the cap does not apply here). This makes
     base discovery a single request instead of a system-by-system crawl. */
  function parseGalaxyBasesReport(doc) {
    return resultRows(doc).map(r => {
      const c = [...r.cells];
      const coord = coordOf(c[2]) || coordOf(c[0]);
      if (!coord) return null;
      const who = parsePlayerCell(txt(c[0]));
      return {
        coord,
        player: who.player || null,
        guild: who.guild || null,
        playerId: idFrom(c[0], 'player'),
        baseName: txt(c[1]) || null,
        baseId: idFrom(c[1], 'base')
      };
    }).filter(Boolean);
  }

  async function fetchAstrosReport(opts) {
    const o = opts || {};
    const val = galaxyValue(o.galaxy);
    if (val == null) throw new Error('bad galaxy: ' + o.galaxy);
    const base = (typeof location !== 'undefined' && location.origin) ? location.origin : '';
    const body = new URLSearchParams({
      form_status: 'submitted',
      galaxy: val,
      terrain: o.terrain || '',
      astro_type: o.astroType || '',
      solar_pos: o.solarPos == null ? '0' : String(o.solarPos)
    });
    const res = await fetch(base + '/report.aspx?view=astros', {
      method: 'POST', credentials: 'include',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/x-www-form-urlencoded' },
      body: body.toString()
    });
    if (!res.ok) throw new Error('HTTP ' + res.status);
    return new DOMParser().parseFromString(await res.text(), 'text/html');
  }

  /* Adaptive enumeration under the 250 cap: subdivide ONLY when a bucket is capped.
     galaxy -> solar_pos -> astro_type -> terrain. */
  async function enumerateGalaxyAstros(galaxy, onProgress, control) {
    if (!ASTROS_REPORT_READY) throw new Error('[AEMapIntel] astros-report parser not implemented');
    const found = new Map();
    const capExhausted = [];        // slices the report physically cannot enumerate
    // control: { isPaused(): bool, isStopped(): bool } — checked between paced requests.
    // Stop throws (partial results are NOT returned: a partial astro list would
    // silently produce wrong scout paths). Pause holds before the next request.
    const ctl = control || {};
    // Determinate-ish progress: leaf-bucket weights by split depth (terrain 15 ->
    // solar 5 -> type 2). done fraction accumulates when a bucket completes.
    const FRAC_W = [1, 1 / 15, 1 / 75, 1 / 150];
    let fracDone = 0;
    const bump = (level) => { fracDone = Math.min(0.99, fracDone + (FRAC_W[level] || 0)); };
    // The report endpoint rate-limits (observed HTTP 429 on rapid bucket splits: a
    // capped galaxy can mean up to ~150 POSTs). Pace every request with the crawl's
    // jitter model and back off on 429.
    // Pace + jitter are user-definable (control.paceSec / control.jitterPct),
    // floored at the crawl's minimums so a typo cannot hammer the endpoint.
    const paceBase = Math.max(0.6, (ctl.paceSec != null ? ctl.paceSec : 1.1)) * 1000;
    const jit = Math.max(0, Math.min(0.6, (ctl.jitterPct != null ? ctl.jitterPct : 30) / 100));
    const paceMs = () => paceBase * (1 - jit + Math.random() * 2 * jit);
    const sleep = (ms) => new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, ms));
    let first = true;
    async function fetchPaced(filter) {
      if (!first) await sleep(paceMs()); first = false;
      while (ctl.isPaused && ctl.isPaused()) {
        if (onProgress) onProgress({ paused: true, frac: fracDone });
        await sleep(300);
        if (ctl.isStopped && ctl.isStopped()) break;
      }
      if (ctl.isStopped && ctl.isStopped()) throw new Error('stopped by user');
      for (let attempt = 0; ; attempt++) {
        try { return await fetchAstrosReport(Object.assign({ galaxy }, filter)); }
        catch (e) {
          if (!/HTTP 429/.test(e.message) || attempt >= 3) throw e;
          const wait = 5000 * Math.pow(2, attempt);          // 5s, 10s, 20s
          if (onProgress) onProgress({ rateLimited: true, waitMs: wait });
          await sleep(wait);
        }
      }
    }
    async function run(filter, level) {
      const rows = parseAstrosReport(await fetchPaced(filter));
      rows.forEach(r => found.set(r.coord, r));
      const capped = rows.length >= ASTROS_ROW_CAP;
      if (!capped || level >= 3) bump(level);               // bucket complete (leaves at 3)
      if (onProgress) onProgress({ level, filter, rows: rows.length, capped, frac: fracDone, total: found.size });
      if (!capped) return;
      // Split on the HIGHEST-CARDINALITY axis first so buckets shrink fastest.
      // Observed: terrain='Asteroid' with every other filter Any ALREADY returns a
      // capped 250 rows — so terrain alone is not fine enough, and splitting the
      // low-cardinality axes first would waste round-trips on still-capped buckets.
      // Order: terrain (15) -> solar_pos (5) -> astro_type (2) = 150 finest buckets.
      if (level === 0)      for (const tr of ASTRO_TERRAINS) await run(Object.assign({}, filter, { terrain: tr }), 1);
      else if (level === 1) for (const sp of SOLAR_POS)      await run(Object.assign({}, filter, { solarPos: sp }), 2);
      else if (level === 2) for (const t of ASTRO_TYPES)     await run(Object.assign({}, filter, { astroType: t }), 3);
      else {
        // Finest bucket STILL capped -> the report cannot enumerate this slice.
        // Report it loudly: silently keeping 250 of N would look like success while
        // quietly losing astros.
        capExhausted.push(Object.assign({}, filter));
        console.warn('[AEMapIntel] bucket still capped at the finest filter ' +
                     '(terrain+solar_pos+astro_type) — astros in this slice are ' +
                     'UNREACHABLE via the report and must be filled in by map crawl:', filter);
      }
    }
    await run({}, 0);
    // Return the incompleteness alongside the data — a caller must be able to tell
    // "these are all the astros" from "these are all the astros the report would give me".
    const astros = [...found.values()];
    astros.incomplete = capExhausted.length > 0;
    astros.capExhausted = capExhausted;
    return astros;
  }

  const GALAXY_RE = /^B?(\d{1,2})$/i;
  function galaxyValue(g) {                    // 'B35' | 35 -> '35' (the option value)
    const m = GALAXY_RE.exec(String(g).trim());
    if (!m) return null;
    const n = parseInt(m[1], 10);
    return (n >= 0 && n <= 49) ? String(n) : null;
  }

  async function fetchGalaxyReport(galaxy, reportType) {
    const val = galaxyValue(galaxy);
    if (val == null) throw new Error('[AEMapIntel] bad galaxy: ' + galaxy);
    const base = (typeof location !== 'undefined' && location.origin) ? location.origin : '';
    const body = new URLSearchParams();
    body.set('galaxy', val);
    body.set('report_type', reportType || 'bases');
    const res = await fetch(base + '/report.aspx?view=galaxy', {
      method: 'POST',
      credentials: 'include',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/x-www-form-urlencoded' },
      body: body.toString()
    });
    if (!res.ok) throw new Error('HTTP ' + res.status);
    return new DOMParser().parseFromString(await res.text(), 'text/html');
  }

  /* ⚠ SEAM — the RESULT page has not been sampled (only the empty form was), so
     the results table's id/class and column order are unknown. Throws rather
     than returning [] : an empty array would read as "galaxy has no bases",
     which would then wrongly mark real astros as non-existent. */
  function parseGalaxyReport(doc) {
    throw new Error('[AEMapIntel] galaxy-report parser not implemented — needs a sample of the ' +
                    'RESULT page (report.aspx?view=galaxy after submitting report_type=bases).');
  }
  const GALAXY_REPORT_READY = true;   // parser verified against a real submitted result

  /* Cross-reference: confirm which coords the galaxy report actually knows about.
     Returns { confirmed:Set, unknown:[] } — `unknown` is NOT "does not exist",
     it is "outside guild visibility, cannot say" (see coverage caveat above). */
  async function confirmExistence(galaxy, coords) {
    if (!GALAXY_REPORT_READY) throw new Error('[AEMapIntel] galaxy-report parser not implemented');
    const doc = await fetchGalaxyReport(galaxy, 'bases');
    const rows = parseGalaxyReport(doc);
    const confirmed = new Set(rows.map(r => normCoord(r.coord)).filter(Boolean));
    const unknown = (coords || []).filter(c => !confirmed.has(normCoord(c)));
    return { confirmed, unknown, reportRows: rows };
  }

  /* ---------- OPT-IN crawl: throttled, jittered, resumable ----------------- */
  const RUN = { active: false, abort: false, done: 0, errors: 0, queue: [], onTick: null };

  /* Same shape as reports-monitor's jitterMs: ±30% swing plus a random sub-second
     smear, floored. Keeping ONE pacing model across the suite means the traffic
     signature of a crawl looks like the refresher the user already runs. */
  /* Mean of jitterMs for a given base delay — the ETA must use the SAME model as
     the pacing, including the jitter, or it will drift. jitterMs = base*(1±0.30)
     + rand(0..1.5)s, floored. E[±30%]=0, E[rand(0..1.5)]=0.75, so the mean is
     max(floor, base + 0.75). */
  function meanDelaySec(baseSec, floorSec) {
    return Math.max(floorSec, baseSec + 0.75);
  }

  const jitterMs = (baseSec, floorSec) => {
    const pct = getCrawlCfg().jitterPct;                     // user-configurable ± fraction
    const j = (Math.random() * 2 * pct) - pct;
    const sec = Math.max(floorSec, baseSec * (1 + j) + Math.random() * 1.5);
    return Math.round(Math.min(sec, 3600) * 1000);
  };
  const sleep = (ms) => new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, ms));

  /* Parse a user scope string into seed targets. Grammar (comma-separated terms):
       B35:45-B35:47   region range  -> region pages B35:45, B35:46, B35:47
       B35, B42        galaxy list   -> galaxy region-sweep seeds (each expands to its regions)
       B34:43:56       system        -> one system page
       B34:43:56:31    astro         -> one astro page
     A term's depth (colon count) sets its kind. Ranges are allowed at region and
     system depth (same galaxy/region prefix, numeric last segment). Returns an
     array of { coord } (+ kind) or null if nothing parsed. */
  function parseScope(text) {
    const t = String(text || '').trim().toUpperCase();
    if (!t) return null;
    const pad = n => String(n).padStart(2, '0');
    const out = [];
    const seenTerm = {};
    const push = c => { if (isCoord(c) || /^[A-Za-z]\d{2}(:\d{2}){0,2}$/.test(c)) { if (!seenTerm[c]) { seenTerm[c] = 1; out.push({ coord: c }); } } };

    t.split(',').map(x => x.trim()).filter(Boolean).forEach(term => {
      // range: PREFIX:aa-PREFIX:bb  (or bare galaxy range B35-B42)
      const rng = /^(.+?)-(.+)$/.exec(term);
      if (rng) {
        const A = rng[1].trim(), B = rng[2].trim();
        // bare galaxy range: B35-B42
        const gA = /^B?(\d{1,2})$/.exec(A), gB = /^B?(\d{1,2})$/.exec(B);
        if (gA && gB) {
          let a = +gA[1], b = +gB[1]; if (a > b) [a, b] = [b, a];
          for (let i = Math.max(0, a); i <= Math.min(49, b); i++) push('B' + pad(i));
          return;
        }
        // structured range: share all but the last numeric segment
        const pa = A.split(':'), pb = B.split(':');
        if (pa.length === pb.length && pa.length >= 2 && pa.slice(0, -1).join(':') === pb.slice(0, -1).join(':')) {
          let a = parseInt(pa[pa.length - 1], 10), b = parseInt(pb[pb.length - 1], 10);
          if (!isNaN(a) && !isNaN(b)) {
            if (a > b) [a, b] = [b, a];
            const prefix = pa.slice(0, -1).join(':');
            for (let i = a; i <= b; i++) push(prefix + ':' + pad(i));
            return;
          }
        }
        // unrecognised range -> ignore this term (do not silently mis-scan)
        console.warn('[AEMapIntel] unparseable range term, skipped:', term);
        return;
      }
      // single term: galaxy / region / system / astro by depth
      const gm = /^B?(\d{1,2})$/.exec(term);
      if (gm) { push('B' + pad(+gm[1])); return; }
      if (/^[A-Za-z]\d{2}(:\d{2}){1,3}$/.test(term)) { push(term); return; }
      console.warn('[AEMapIntel] unparseable scope term, skipped:', term);
    });
    return out.length ? out : null;
  }

  /* ── SCOPE MATCHING ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
     The base-report flow pulls a whole galaxy's bases, then keeps only those inside
     the user's scope. parseScope gives us the scope TERMS; scopeMatcher turns them
     into a predicate testing a coord by PREFIX depth:
       term B35          (galaxy) matches any coord starting 'B35:'
       term B35:45       (region) matches any coord starting 'B35:45:'
       term B35:45:12    (system) matches any coord starting 'B35:45:12:'
       term B35:45:12:31 (astro)  matches exactly that coord
     A region-range term expands (in parseScope) to individual region terms, so each
     is a plain prefix here. Returns { galaxies:Set, test(coord)->bool, terms }. */
  function scopeMatcher(scopeText) {
    const parsed = parseScope(scopeText);
    if (!parsed) return null;
    const terms = parsed.map(x => x.coord);
    const galaxies = new Set();
    terms.forEach(t => { const g = /^([A-Za-z]\d{2})/.exec(t); if (g) galaxies.add(g[1]); });
    function test(coord) {
      const c = String(coord || '');
      return terms.some(term => {
        if (term === c) return true;                 // exact (astro-level term)
        const depth = term.split(':').length;
        if (depth >= 4) return term === c;           // astro term: exact only
        return c.indexOf(term + ':') === 0;          // galaxy/region/system: prefix
      });
    }
    return { galaxies, test, terms };
  }

  /* Plan a scan from a scope string. Returns:
       astros:  [{coord}]            astro-level terms -> open that map location directly
                                     (a base there -> update; empty astro -> error, no report)
       galaxies: ['B37', ...]        galaxy/region/system terms -> pull that galaxy's bases
                                     report, then keep only rows in scope.
       matcher:  scopeMatcher        the in-scope predicate for the report rows.
     This is what fixes "asked for one astro, scanned all of B37": a 4-segment coord
     never triggers a galaxy report. */
  function planScan(scopeText) {
    const parsed = parseScope(scopeText);
    if (!parsed) return null;
    const astros = [], reportTerms = [];
    parsed.forEach(t => {
      const depth = String(t.coord).split(':').length;
      if (depth >= 4) astros.push({ coord: t.coord });   // astro -> direct map visit
      else reportTerms.push(t.coord);                     // galaxy/region/system -> report
    });
    // galaxies to report = the galaxies the NON-astro terms touch
    const galaxies = [];
    reportTerms.forEach(term => { const g = /^([A-Za-z]\d{2})/.exec(term); if (g && galaxies.indexOf(g[1]) < 0) galaxies.push(g[1]); });
    return { astros, galaxies: galaxies.sort(), matcher: scopeMatcher(scopeText), hasReport: reportTerms.length > 0 };
  }

  /* Galaxies a scope touches, in order (for looping report submissions). */
  function scopeGalaxies(scopeText) {
    const m = scopeMatcher(scopeText);
    if (!m) return [];
    return [...m.galaxies].sort();
  }

  /* Classify a target into the page it should load. Every collector step loads
     exactly ONE of: a map location (region / system / astro) or a base page.
     - region  Bxx:yy         -> map.aspx (grid of systems)
     - system  Bxx:yy:zz      -> map.aspx (grid of astros in that system)
     - astro   Bxx:yy:zz:pp   -> map.aspx (the astro; owner/eco/base link)
     - base    base=<id>      -> base.aspx (structures/defences/economy) */
  function classifyTarget(t) {
    if (t && t.base) return { kind: 'base', url: '/base.aspx?base=' + encodeURIComponent(t.base), label: 'base ' + t.base };
    const c = (t && t.coord) || t;
    const segs = String(c || '').split(':').length;
    const kind = segs >= 4 ? 'astro' : segs === 3 ? 'system' : 'region';
    return { kind, url: '/map.aspx?loc=' + encodeURIComponent(c), label: kind + ' ' + c, coord: c };
  }

  /* Parse an already-loaded document by page kind, upsert, and report what it found.
     Used by the VISIBLE collector after each navigation completes. */
  function ingestLoadedPage(doc, target) {
    const cls = classifyTarget(target);
    const res = { kind: cls.kind, coord: cls.coord || null, bases: 0, astros: 0, queued: [] };
    try {
      if (cls.kind === 'base') {
        // The base page carries its own coord (a loc= link); read it rather than
        // relying on the caller to supply one.
        let coord = (target && target.coord) || null;
        if (!coord) {
          const a = doc.querySelector('a[href*="loc="]');
          const m = a && /loc=([A-Za-z]\d{2}:\d{2}:\d{2}:\d+)/.exec(a.getAttribute('href') || '');
          coord = m ? m[1] : null;
        }
        const st = parseStructures(doc);
        const commander = parseCommander(doc);
        const astro = parseAstroHeader(doc);
        const hdr = parseBaseHeader(doc);
        if (coord && (st || astro.planetType || hdr)) {
          const rec = { coord: coord, via: 'base-page', planetType: astro.planetType, terrain: astro.terrain, area: astro.area };
          if (st) { rec.structures = st.structures; rec.defenses = st.defenses; }
          // owner guild off the base page (for the JG registry + self-guild detection)
          const gEl = doc.querySelector('a[href*="guild.aspx?guild="]');
          const gm = gEl && /\[([^\]]+)\]/.exec(gEl.textContent || '');
          if (gm) rec.guild = gm[1].trim();
          // an OWN base carries the structures sub-nav; its guild is the user's guild
          if (gm && doc.querySelector('a[href*="view=structures"]')) setSelfGuild(gm[1].trim());
          if (commander) rec.commander = commander;   // { name, id, type, level, label }
          if (hdr) {
            if (hdr.baseName) rec.baseName = hdr.baseName;
            rec.energy = hdr.energy; rec.population = hdr.population;
            rec.tradeRoutes = hdr.tradeRoutes;       // TR = Trade Routes (detail scan)
            if (hdr.area && hdr.area.max) rec.areaUsed = hdr.area;
          }
          // Economy/capacity block + game's own staleness stamp (from reference collector).
          const cap = parseCapacities(doc);
          if (cap) {
            if (cap.economy != null) rec.economy = cap.economy;
            if (cap.ownerIncome != null) rec.ownerIncome = cap.ownerIncome;
            if (cap.construction != null) rec.construction = cap.construction;
            if (cap.production != null) rec.production = cap.production;
            if (cap.research != null) rec.research = cap.research;
          }
          const recAge = parseRecordedAge(doc);
          if (recAge) rec.recordedAge = recAge;    // AE's "recorded data from X ago
          // Fleet split needs the base owner's id + guild. Prefer an existing record
          // (the report already stored player/guild/playerId); fall back to the map
          // occupier if present on this page.
          const known = getBase(coord) || {};
          const ownerId = known.playerId || null;
          const ownerGuild = known.guild || null;
          const fl = parseFleets(doc, ownerId, ownerGuild);
          if (fl) {
            rec.ownerFleet = fl.ownerFleet; rec.guildFleet = fl.guildFleet; rec.otherFleet = fl.otherFleet;
            rec.totalFleet = fl.totalFleet;
          }
          upsertBase(rec);
          res.coord = coord; res.bases = 1;
        }
      } else if (cls.kind === 'astro') {
        // A specific astro (map location). parseBasesTable returns the base if one is
        // present. If empty, signal it explicitly — the caller wants an error, not a
        // silent no-op, when a user asks for a coord that has no base.
        const rows = parseBasesTable(doc, cls.coord);
        if (rows.length) {
          rows.forEach(r => upsertBase(Object.assign({ via: 'map' }, r)));
          res.bases = rows.length;
          if (rows[0].baseId) res.baseId = rows[0].baseId;
        }
        // Whether or not the #map_base table parsed, look across the WHOLE page for a
        // base.aspx?base=ID link — the live map DOM may present the base link outside
        // the table my fixture had. This is "find the base link on the page" directly.
        if (!res.baseId) {
          const bl = findBaseLink(doc);
          if (bl) res.baseId = bl;
        }
        if (!res.bases && !res.baseId) {
          res.empty = true;                 // genuinely no base at this astro
          res.error = 'no base at ' + cls.coord;
        }
      } else if (cls.kind === 'system') {
        const list = parseSystemAstros(doc);
        res.astros = list.length;
        res.queued = list.functional.map(a => a.coord);   // only worth-fetching astros
      } else { // region
        res.queued = parseSystemAstros(doc).functional.map(a => a.coord);
      }
    } catch (e) { res.error = String(e); }
    return res;
  }

  async function fetchMapDoc(coord) {
    const base = (typeof location !== 'undefined' && location.origin) ? location.origin : '';
    const url = base + '/map.aspx?loc=' + encodeURIComponent(coord);
    const res = await fetch(url, { credentials: 'include' });
    if (!res.ok) throw new Error('HTTP ' + res.status);
    return new DOMParser().parseFromString(await res.text(), 'text/html');
  }

  /* Resume state persists, so a run can be stopped and continued across
     sessions — the point of the "resumable" requirement: coverage never
     depends on one long, conspicuous burst of traffic. */
  function saveResume(queue) {
    const { all, blk } = serverBlock();
    blk.meta = blk.meta || {};
    blk.meta.resume = queue.slice(0, 5000);
    blk.meta.resumeAt = Date.now();
    writeAll(all);
  }
  function loadResume() {
    const { blk } = serverBlock();
    return (blk.meta && blk.meta.resume) || [];
  }
  function clearResume() {
    const { all, blk } = serverBlock();
    if (blk.meta) { delete blk.meta.resume; delete blk.meta.resumeAt; }
    writeAll(all);
  }

  async function startCrawl(coords, onTick) {
    if (!PARSER_READY) throw new Error('[AEMapIntel] cannot crawl: bases parser not implemented');
    if (RUN.active) return { already: true };
    const cfg = getCrawlCfg();
    RUN.active = true; RUN.abort = false; RUN.done = 0; RUN.errors = 0;
    RUN.queue = (coords && coords.length) ? coords.slice() : loadResume();
    RUN.onTick = onTick || null;

    while (RUN.queue.length && !RUN.abort && RUN.done < cfg.maxPerSession) {
      // Never crawl while the tab is hidden: a background tab issuing a steady
      // stream of requests is the most obviously non-human pattern there is.
      if (typeof document !== 'undefined' && document.hidden) { await sleep(2000); continue; }

      const coord = RUN.queue[0];
      try {
        const doc = await fetchMapDoc(coord);
        const rows = parseBasesTable(doc, coord);
        rows.forEach(upsertBase);
        RUN.queue.shift();
        RUN.done++;
        RUN.errors = 0;
        saveResume(RUN.queue);
        if (RUN.onTick) RUN.onTick({ coord, found: rows.length, done: RUN.done, left: RUN.queue.length });
      } catch (e) {
        RUN.errors++;
        console.warn('[AEMapIntel] crawl error at', coord, e);
        if (RUN.errors >= cfg.stopOnErrors) {
          if (RUN.onTick) RUN.onTick({ coord, error: String(e), aborted: true });
          break;
        }
      }
      await sleep(jitterMs(cfg.baseDelaySec, cfg.floorSec));
    }

    const capped = RUN.done >= cfg.maxPerSession && RUN.queue.length > 0;
    if (!RUN.queue.length) clearResume();
    RUN.active = false;
    return { done: RUN.done, left: RUN.queue.length, capped, aborted: RUN.abort };
  }
  function stopCrawl() { RUN.abort = true; }
  function crawlState() {
    return { active: RUN.active, done: RUN.done, left: RUN.queue.length, resumable: loadResume().length };
  }

  /* AE coords are FOUR segments: galaxy:region:system:planet (e.g. B35:55:11:41).
     Same regex the bookmarks module uses — moons extend the last group. */
  const COORD_RE = /^[A-Za-z]\d{2}:\d{2}:\d{2}:\d{2,}$/;

  /* ── GAME RULES (from Aran; corroborated by every sampled system) ──────────
     A REAL astro has orbit 1..5 and moonIndex 0..3, encoded as orbit*10 + moonIndex:
       moonIndex 0     -> always the primary body (planet / gas giant / asteroid belt)
       moonIndex 1..3  -> a moon or an asteroid
     Valid last segments are therefore exactly 10-13, 20-23, 30-33, 40-43, 50-53.
     Anything else (:00, :05, :64, :99 ...) CANNOT exist. This is a GHOST test that
     costs no fetch and no DOM — strictly cheaper than the visual check.

     FILLER: gas giants and asteroid BELTS are visual filler; they can never hold a
     base, so the crawl must never spend a fetch on them.
     ⚠ TRAP: 'asteroid-belt' is filler but 'asteroid' is a REAL colonisable astro, and
     one is a substring of the other — these must be matched EXACTLY, never by substring. */
  const ORBIT_MIN = 1, ORBIT_MAX = 5, MOON_MIN = 0, MOON_MAX = 3;
  const FILLER_TYPES = ['gas-giant', 'asteroid-belt'];

  function astroSlot(coord) {
    const p = coordParts(coord);
    if (!p) return null;
    return { orbit: Math.floor(p.planet / 10), moon: p.planet % 10, isPlanet: (p.planet % 10) === 0 };
  }
  function isValidAstroCoord(coord) {
    if (!isCoord(coord)) return false;
    const sl = astroSlot(coord);
    return !!sl && sl.orbit >= ORBIT_MIN && sl.orbit <= ORBIT_MAX &&
                   sl.moon  >= MOON_MIN  && sl.moon  <= MOON_MAX;
  }
  function isFillerType(type) {
    return FILLER_TYPES.indexOf(String(type || '').toLowerCase()) !== -1;   // EXACT match
  }
  function isFunctionalAstro(a) {
    return !!a && isValidAstroCoord(a.coord) && !isFillerType(a.type);
  }
  const isCoord = c => COORD_RE.test(String(c || '').trim());

  const pad2 = n => String(n).padStart(2, '0');
  const clamp = (v, lo, hi, dflt) => {
    const n = parseInt(v, 10);
    return isNaN(n) ? dflt : Math.max(lo, Math.min(hi, n));
  };

  /* ── COORD SCHEME (inferred from real samples, NOT from AE docs) ──────────
     The last segment is orbit*10 + moonIndex, where moonIndex 0 IS the planet:
        :10 Gas Giant (orbit 1)   :11 :12  moons of orbit 1
        :40 Planet    (orbit 4)   :41      moon  of orbit 4
     Every sampled `...0` was a planet/gas-giant and every non-zero a Moon
     (B35:33:52:10/11/12/20..., B35:55:11:41=Moon, B38:44:38:40=Planet,
     B38:22:43:31=Moon). So sequential :01..:99 expansion is WRONG — :01..:09
     cannot exist, and a system holds ~10 astros, not 99.

     THEREFORE: do not guess coords. Ask the system page which astros exist
     (one cheap fetch) and crawl exactly those. queueFromSystem() does this and
     is the ONLY expansion path the crawl should use. The orbit-aware expander
     below exists purely as an offline fallback and is flagged accordingly. */

  /* Authoritative: 1 system fetch -> the real astro coords in it.
     Cost per system = 1 + N (N ~= 10) instead of 99 blind planet fetches. */
  async function queueFromSystem(systemCoord) {
    const base = (typeof location !== 'undefined' && location.origin) ? location.origin : '';
    const res = await fetch(base + '/map.aspx?loc=' + encodeURIComponent(systemCoord), { credentials: 'include' });
    if (!res.ok) throw new Error('HTTP ' + res.status);
    const doc = new DOMParser().parseFromString(await res.text(), 'text/html');
    // Only functional astros: skip ghosts (impossible slot / no visual) AND filler
    // (gas giant, asteroid belt) which can never hold a base. Every coord dropped
    // here is a fetch never made.
    return parseSystemAstros(doc).functional.map(a => a.coord);
  }

  /* OFFLINE FALLBACK ONLY — uses the INFERRED orbit/moon scheme above. Prefer
     queueFromSystem(); this can still emit coords that do not exist. */
  /* Every POSSIBLE slot in a system: orbit 1-5 x moon 0-3 = 20 coords (not 99).
     Prefer queueFromSystem(), which returns which astros actually EXIST and drops
     filler — typically ~7 fetches instead of 20. This is the offline fallback. */
  function expandSystemSlots(system) {
    const out = [];
    for (let o = ORBIT_MIN; o <= ORBIT_MAX; o++) {
      for (let m = MOON_MIN; m <= MOON_MAX; m++) out.push(system + ':' + pad2(o * 10 + m));
    }
    return out.filter(isValidAstroCoord);
  }


  /* Distance between two coords, for the "Dist" column / threat ETA. Segment
     weights are UNVERIFIED against AE's real distance model — see below. */
  /* ── DISTANCE between two coords ───────────────────────────────────────────
     THREE mutually-exclusive calculations, chosen by the coarsest level at which
     the coords differ. They do NOT stack (Aran, verified against the game):
       1. Different GALAXY  -> galaxy formula only.
            same cluster (both in a group of 10): |g1-g2| * 200
            diff clusters: (loEnd-lo)*200 + (hi-hiStart)*200 + 2000 (jump once)
            (loEnd = x9 of lower's cluster, hiStart = x0 of higher's cluster)
            Verified vs sourced examples: x44->x03=4000, x05->x14=3600, x05->x08=600.
       2. Same galaxy, different REGION or SYSTEM -> planar formula only ("golden").
            region:system digits deinterleave onto a 100x100 plane:
              x = tensDigit(region)*10 + tensDigit(system)
              y = onesDigit(region)*10 + onesDigit(system)
            d = ceil( euclidean(dx,dy) + (raw>45 ? 0.8 : 0) )   (portal floor adj)
       3. Same system, different ASTRO -> ring formula only.
            ring = orbit = floor(lastSeg/10); same ring 0.1, else 0.2*|Δring|.
     distance() returns { ly, level } where level is 'galaxy' | 'planar' | 'system'
     | 'same'. All three are game-accurate now — no modelled/approx terms remain. */
  const CLUSTER_JUMP = 2000, GALAXY_STEP = 200;

  function galaxyNum(coord) { const p = coordParts(coord); return p ? parseInt(p.galaxy.slice(1), 10) : null; }
  function clusterOf(gnum) { return Math.floor(gnum / 10); }

  function galaxyDistance(g1, g2) {
    if (g1 === g2) return 0;
    const c1 = clusterOf(g1), c2 = clusterOf(g2);
    if (c1 === c2) return Math.abs(g1 - g2) * GALAXY_STEP;
    const lo = Math.min(g1, g2), hi = Math.max(g1, g2);
    const loEnd = (clusterOf(lo) * 10 + 9);      // x9 of lo's cluster
    const hiStart = (clusterOf(hi) * 10);        // x0 of hi's cluster
    return (loEnd - lo) * GALAXY_STEP + (hi - hiStart) * GALAXY_STEP + CLUSTER_JUMP;
  }

  /* Golden intra-galaxy planar distance (region+system), from Aran's game-verified
     function. Deinterleaves the region:system digits into (x,y) on a 100x100 plane. */
  function planarDistance(a, b) {
    const rg = String(a.region).padStart(2, '0'), sy = String(a.system).padStart(2, '0');
    const rg2 = String(b.region).padStart(2, '0'), sy2 = String(b.system).padStart(2, '0');
    const x1 = parseInt(rg[0] + sy[0], 10), y1 = parseInt(rg[1] + sy[1], 10);
    const x2 = parseInt(rg2[0] + sy2[0], 10), y2 = parseInt(rg2[1] + sy2[1], 10);
    const dx = x1 - x2, dy = y1 - y2;
    let d = Math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);
    if (d > 45) d += 0.8;      // portal floor adjustment to match the server
    return Math.ceil(d);
  }

  /* Returns { ly, level }. level tells you which of the three formulas applied. */
  function distance(coordA, coordB) {
    const a = coordParts(coordA), b = coordParts(coordB);
    if (!a || !b) return null;
    const gA = parseInt(a.galaxy.slice(1), 10), gB = parseInt(b.galaxy.slice(1), 10);
    if (gA !== gB) return { ly: galaxyDistance(gA, gB), level: 'galaxy' };
    if (a.region !== b.region || a.system !== b.system) return { ly: planarDistance(a, b), level: 'planar' };
    // same galaxy:region:system -> intra-system by astro ring
    const ringA = Math.floor(a.planet / 10), ringB = Math.floor(b.planet / 10);
    if (ringA === ringB && a.planet === b.planet) return { ly: 0, level: 'same' };
    return { ly: (ringA === ringB) ? 0.1 : Math.round(0.2 * Math.abs(ringA - ringB) * 100) / 100, level: 'system' };
  }

  function coordParts(c) {
    const m = /^([A-Za-z])(\d{2}):(\d{2}):(\d{2}):(\d+)$/.exec(String(c || '').trim());
    if (!m) return null;
    return { galaxy: m[1] + m[2], region: +m[3], system: +m[4], planet: +m[5] };
  }

  return {
    VERSION, STORE_KEY, SERVER, CRAWL_DEFAULTS, PARSER_READY, MIRROR_KEY,
    getCrawlCfg, setCrawlCfg, parseRatio, parseNum, parsePlayerCell,
    upsertBase, deleteBasesInRange, allBases, getBase, stats, search, toCSV,
    parseBasesTable, parseSystemAstros, parseAstroHeader, parseStructures, parseCommander, harvestCurrentPage,
    startCrawl, stopCrawl, crawlState, loadResume, clearResume,
    isCoord, coordParts, distance, galaxyDistance, queueFromSystem, expandSystemSlots, parseSystemAstros, hasVisual,
    isValidAstroCoord, isFillerType, isFunctionalAstro, astroSlot, FILLER_TYPES,
    GALAXY_REPORT_READY, galaxyValue, fetchGalaxyReport, parseGalaxyReport, confirmExistence,
    ASTROS_REPORT_READY, fetchAstrosReport, parseAstrosReport, enumerateGalaxyAstros,
    parseGalaxyBasesReport, parseAttributes, harvestGalaxyBases, harvestGalaxyAstros,
    DROP_STATS, techCum, techNext, TECH_BASE, computeTechCalibration, estimateEnemyTechs,
    ingestReportRowsInScope, rankAgeMs, buildCost, deriveBaseBalances, adviseNextBuild, parseAstroDetails, scoutSpeed, regionOrderKey, buildScoutPath, predictScoutStops, currentScoutEstimate, scoutPathDistance, inferScoutWarp, exportScoutPath, planRetention, chooseGatherRoute, parseEmpireFleets, parseMovePage, parseDurationText, getJgRegistry, recordJumpGate, friendlyJgLevel, getSelfGuild, setSelfGuild, parseSystemScoutAstros, parseAttackTargets, parsePreBattleConfirm, parseBattleResult, computeTechCalibration, estimateEnemyTechs, computeDropEstimate, computeDropFromCounts, buildDropLines, parseOwnUnits, getOwnUnits, captureOwnUnitsIfOnPage, parseFleetComposition, computeDropForPlayer, parseRankingsPage, fetchRankings, getRankings, annotateFleetPercents,
    startBulk, stopBulk, bulkState, loadBulkResume, clearBulkResume,
    runScan, stopScan, scanActive, prepScan, pauseScan, resumeScanRun, scanPaused,
    scanResumeInfo, clearScanResume,
    meanDelaySec,
    classifyTarget, ingestLoadedPage, parseScope, parseBaseHeader, findBaseLink,
    parseCapacities, parseRecordedAge, parseTradePlunder,
    scopeMatcher, scopeGalaxies, planScan, parseFleets, currentRev,
    ASTRO_TERRAINS, ASTRO_TYPES, SOLAR_POS, ASTROS_ROW_CAP,
    _readAll: readAll, _writeAll: writeAll
  };
})();

/* ---- debug handle -------------------------------------------------------
   The suite runs in an isolated userscript scope, so `AEMapIntel` is NOT on the
   page's `window` by default — that is why typing it in the console throws
   "AEMapIntel is not defined". Expose a handle for manual testing. In Tampermonkey
   `unsafeWindow` is the page's real window; fall back to `window` otherwise. */
try {
  var __aeWin = (typeof unsafeWindow !== 'undefined') ? unsafeWindow : (typeof window !== 'undefined' ? window : null);
  if (__aeWin) {
    __aeWin.AEMapIntel = AEMapIntel;

    /* One-shot self-test: run on any base / report page, prints pass/fail per parser.
       Usage in console:  AEMapIntel_selfTest()  */
    __aeWin.AEMapIntel_selfTest = function () {
      var doc = (typeof document !== 'undefined') ? document : null;
      if (!doc) { console.log('[AE self-test] no document'); return; }
      var path = (doc.location && doc.location.pathname) || '';
      var out = [];
      function line(label, ok, detail) {
        console.log('%c' + (ok ? 'PASS' : 'FAIL') + '%c ' + label + (detail ? '  ' + detail : ''),
          'color:' + (ok ? '#3fb6c4' : '#f0655a') + ';font-weight:700', 'color:inherit');
        out.push({ label: label, ok: ok, detail: detail });
      }
      console.log('[AE self-test] page: ' + path);

      if (/base\.aspx/i.test(path)) {
        try { var h = AEMapIntel.parseBaseHeader(doc);
          line('parseBaseHeader', !!(h && h.baseName), h ? 'name=' + h.baseName + ' TR=' + (h.tradeRoutes && h.tradeRoutes.raw) : 'null'); } catch (e) { line('parseBaseHeader', false, String(e)); }
        try { var st = AEMapIntel.parseStructures(doc);
          line('parseStructures', !!(st && Object.keys(st.structures).length), st ? Object.keys(st.structures).length + ' structures' : 'null'); } catch (e) { line('parseStructures', false, String(e)); }
        try { var fl = AEMapIntel.parseFleets(doc, null, null);
          line('parseFleets (table present)', !!fl, fl ? fl.totalFleet.toLocaleString() + ' total across all players' : 'no #map_fleets'); } catch (e) { line('parseFleets', false, String(e)); }
        console.log('[AE self-test] fleet split needs owner id/guild — run: AEMapIntel.parseFleets(document, "<ownerId>", "<guild>")');
      } else if (/report\.aspx/i.test(path)) {
        try { var rows = AEMapIntel.parseGalaxyBasesReport(doc);
          line('parseGalaxyBasesReport', rows.length > 0, rows.length + ' rows' + (rows[0] ? ' e.g. ' + rows[0].coord + ' ' + rows[0].player : '')); } catch (e) { line('parseGalaxyBasesReport', false, String(e)); }
      } else if (/map\.aspx/i.test(path)) {
        try { var b = AEMapIntel.parseBasesTable(doc, null); line('parseBasesTable', true, b.length + ' base(s) on this astro'); } catch (e) { line('parseBasesTable', false, String(e)); }
        try { var sys = AEMapIntel.parseSystemAstros(doc); line('parseSystemAstros', true, sys.length + ' astros, ' + sys.functional.length + ' functional'); } catch (e) { line('parseSystemAstros', false, String(e)); }
      } else {
        console.log('[AE self-test] open a base.aspx, report.aspx, or map.aspx page to test its parser.');
      }
      return out;
    };
  }
} catch (e) { /* non-fatal: debug handle is best-effort */ }


/* =============================================================================
 * AE HUB — virtual centralization layer
 * One joined view per player, assembled lazily from the existing stores (no data
 * migration): tech mirror, rank mirror, base mirror, the report scanner's GM
 * observation log, and the tech harvester's GM DB. Physical keys stay separate
 * (write granularity, isolation); the hub is READ-ONLY.
 * ========================================================================== */
const AEHub = (function () {
  'use strict';
  const SERVER = (typeof location !== 'undefined' ? location.hostname.split('.')[0] : 'unknown');
  const HOST = (typeof location !== 'undefined' ? location.hostname.toLowerCase() : 'unknown');
  const ls = (k) => { try { return JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(k)); } catch (e) { return null; } };
  const gm = (k) => { try { if (typeof GM_getValue !== 'function') return null; const raw = GM_getValue(k, null); return raw ? JSON.parse(raw) : null; } catch (e) { return null; } };

  const techMirror = () => ls('ae::techmirror::' + SERVER) || { players: {}, archetypes: {} };
  const rankMirror = () => ls('ae::rankmirror::' + SERVER) || { players: {} };
  const baseMirror = () => { const m = ls('ae::bases::mirror::' + SERVER); return m && m.bases ? m.bases : (m || {}); };
  const repLog = () => { const l = gm(HOST + '::aeplus_rep_obs_log'); return Array.isArray(l) ? l : []; };
  const harvesterDb = () => { const d = gm('aeTechHarvester::' + SERVER); return d && d.players ? d.players : {}; };

  /* Enumerate every player any source knows: { id, name, guild, sources:[...] }. */
  function index() {
    const out = {};
    const touch = (id, name, guild, src) => {
      if (!id) return;
      const r = out[id] || (out[id] = { id: String(id), name: null, guild: null, sources: [] });
      if (name && !r.name) r.name = name;
      if (guild && !r.guild) r.guild = guild;
      if (!r.sources.includes(src)) r.sources.push(src);
    };
    for (const [id, p] of Object.entries(techMirror().players || {})) touch(id, p.name, null, 'tech');
    for (const [id, p] of Object.entries(rankMirror().players || {})) touch(id, p.name, null, 'rank');
    for (const b of Object.values(baseMirror())) if (b && b.playerId) touch(b.playerId, b.player, b.guild, 'base');
    for (const e of repLog()) if (e && e.playerId) touch(e.playerId, e.player, e.guild, 'obs');
    for (const [id, p] of Object.entries(harvesterDb())) touch(id, p.name, null, 'harvest');
    return out;
  }

  /* Activity histogram: 24 UTC-hour bins of GENUINE player-activity signals.
     Autoscout sightings (isScout40) are EXCLUDED — autoscouts fly while the player
     sleeps. Signals: non-scout fleet observations (their fleet moved) and battle-report
     times (they fought). AE server clocks run on GMT == UTC hour — flagged assumption;
     verify against the in-game server clock once. */
  function activityHistogram(pid) {
    const bins = new Array(24).fill(0);
    let n = 0;
    const add = (ms) => { if (!ms || isNaN(ms)) return; bins[new Date(ms).getUTCHours()]++; n++; };
    for (const e of repLog()) {
      if (!e || String(e.playerId) !== String(pid) || e.isScout40) continue;
      add(Date.parse(e.observedAt));
    }
    const hp = harvesterDb()[pid];
    if (hp) {
      if (hp.lastReportTime) add(hp.lastReportTime);
      // Only observations whose timestamp is a REAL battle-report time (rt flag).
      // fleetObs.t falls back to OUR scan time when the report time was unknown —
      // counting those would pollute the histogram with the harvester's play hours.
      // Legacy entries without the flag are excluded (undercount beats pollution).
      for (const o of hp.fleetObs || []) if (o.rt) add(o.t);
    }
    return { bins, samples: n + ((hp && (hp.fleetObs || []).length) || 0) + (hp && hp.lastReportTime ? 1 : 0), tz: 'UTC (server time — assumption: AE server clock is GMT)' };
  }

  /* Fleet composition by CREDIT SHARE from the harvester's richest observation
     (largest total value). Returns { shares: {ABBR: fraction}, total, side, t } or null. */
  function fleetSpecPie(pid) {
    const hp = harvesterDb()[pid];
    if (!hp || !(hp.fleetObs || []).length) return null;
    let best = null;
    for (const o of hp.fleetObs) {
      if (!o.units) continue;
      let v = 0; const per = {};
      for (const [ab, q] of Object.entries(o.units)) {
        const stats = AEMapIntel.DROP_STATS[ab];
        const c = stats ? stats.c : null;
        if (c && q > 0) { per[ab] = c * q; v += c * q; }
      }
      if (v > 0 && (!best || v > best.total)) best = { per, total: v, side: o.side, t: o.t };
    }
    if (!best) return null;
    const shares = {};
    for (const [ab, val] of Object.entries(best.per)) shares[ab] = val / best.total;
    return { shares, total: best.total, side: best.side, t: best.t };
  }

  /* Tech-rank validation: rank Technology = CUMULATIVE research credits (verified).
     combatSpend = sum of techCum over harvested/manual combat techs. Expected combat
     share of the budget = alpha (0.47 uncalibrated). Verdicts:
       spend > rankTech           -> 'impossible' (data error somewhere)
       spend < 40% of alpha-share -> 'hidden'     (they hold far more than we harvested)
       else                       -> 'consistent'
     The 40% floor is a judgment threshold, not a measured constant. */
  function techValidation(pid) {
    const rp = rankMirror().players[pid];
    const tp = techMirror().players[pid];
    if (!rp || !rp.tech || !tp || !tp.techs) return null;
    let spend = 0, counted = 0;
    for (const [t, L] of Object.entries(tp.techs)) {
      const lvl = typeof L === 'number' ? L : (L && L.level);
      const cum = AEMapIntel.techCum(t, lvl);
      if (cum) { spend += cum; counted++; }
    }
    if (!counted) return null;
    const expected = 0.47 * rp.tech;
    const ratio = spend / rp.tech;
    const verdict = spend > rp.tech ? 'impossible' : (spend < 0.4 * expected ? 'hidden' : 'consistent');
    return { spend: Math.round(spend), rankTech: rp.tech, expectedCombat: Math.round(expected), ratio, counted, verdict };
  }

  /* The joined record. Every section is null/empty when its source has nothing. */
  function player(pid) {
    pid = String(pid);
    const tp = techMirror().players[pid] || null;
    const rp = rankMirror().players[pid] || null;
    const bases = Object.values(baseMirror()).filter(b => b && String(b.playerId) === pid);
    const obs = repLog().filter(e => e && String(e.playerId) === pid);
    const hp = harvesterDb()[pid] || null;
    const idx = index()[pid] || null;
    return {
      id: pid,
      name: (tp && tp.name) || (rp && rp.name) || (idx && idx.name) || null,
      guild: (idx && idx.guild) || (bases[0] && bases[0].guild) || null,
      sources: idx ? idx.sources : [],
      techs: tp ? tp.techs : null,
      warpInferred: tp ? tp.warpInferred || null : null,
      spec: tp ? tp.spec || null : null,
      ranks: rp,
      bases,
      observations: obs,
      scoutSightings: obs.filter(e => e.isScout40),
      harvest: hp ? { history: hp.history || [], anomalies: hp.anomalies || [], fleetObs: hp.fleetObs || [], firstSeen: hp.firstSeen, lastSeen: hp.lastSeen } : null,
      activity: activityHistogram(pid),
      fleetPie: fleetSpecPie(pid),
      techCheck: techValidation(pid)
    };
  }

  return { index, player, activityHistogram, fleetSpecPie, techValidation };
})();


/* ============================ SUITE RUNNER ================================ */
const AESuite = (function () {
  'use strict';
  const CFG_KEY = 'ae::suite::v1';
  const mods = [];
  function cfg() { try { return JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(CFG_KEY)) || {}; } catch (e) { return {}; } }
  function saveCfg(c) { try { localStorage.setItem(CFG_KEY, JSON.stringify(c)); } catch (e) {} }
  function register(m) { mods.push(m); }

  function migrationShim() {
    try {
      if (GM_getValue('ae::suite::migrated', null)) return;
      let imported = 0;
      Object.keys(localStorage).filter(k => k.indexOf('ae::migrate::') === 0).forEach(k => {
        try {
          const dump = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(k)) || {};
          Object.keys(dump).forEach(dk => { GM_setValue(dk, dump[dk]); imported++; });
          localStorage.removeItem(k);
        } catch (e) { console.warn('[AESuite] import failed for', k, e); }
      });
      // rebuild overseer cross-server master from this origin's live store
      try {
        const local = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('ae::overseer::v1') || 'null');
        const master = JSON.parse(GM_getValue('ae::overseer::master', 'null') || 'null');
        if (local && (!master || (local.ts || 0) >= (master.ts || 0))) {
          local.ts = local.ts || Date.now();
          GM_setValue('ae::overseer::master', JSON.stringify(local));
        }
      } catch (e) {}
      GM_setValue('ae::suite::migrated', '1.0.0');
      console.log('[AESuite] migration shim: imported ' + imported + ' GM entries');
    } catch (e) { console.warn('[AESuite] migration shim failed', e); }
  }

  function warnDuplicates() {
    setTimeout(() => {
      const seen = {};
      document.querySelectorAll('[data-ae-script]').forEach(el => {
        const id = el.getAttribute('data-ae-script');
        seen[id] = (seen[id] || 0) + 1;
      });
      Object.keys(seen).forEach(id => {
        if (seen[id] > 1) console.warn('[AESuite] DUPLICATE panel "' + id + '" — an old standalone script is still enabled. Remove it.');
      });
    }, 3000);
  }

  function boot() {
    migrationShim();
    // union-merge the two legacy bookmark stores into the shared location
    // catalog. Idempotent (guarded per server); legacy keys kept as backup.
    try { AEData.migrateLocations(); } catch (e) { console.warn('[AESuite] location migration failed', e); }
    const disabled = cfg().disabled || {};
    const here = location.pathname + location.search;
    mods.forEach(m => {
      if (disabled[m.id]) return;
      if (m.pages && !m.pages.test(here)) return;
      try { m.init(); } catch (e) { console.error('[AESuite] module "' + m.id + '" failed:', e); }
    });
    if (typeof GM_registerMenuCommand === 'function') {
      mods.forEach(m => {
        const off = !!disabled[m.id];
        GM_registerMenuCommand((off ? 'Enable ' : 'Disable ') + m.title, () => {
          const c = cfg(); c.disabled = c.disabled || {};
          if (c.disabled[m.id]) delete c.disabled[m.id]; else c.disabled[m.id] = true;
          saveCfg(c); location.reload();
        });
      });
    }
    warnDuplicates();
  }
  return { register, boot, cfg };
})();


/* ============ MODULE: reports-monitor ============ */
AESuite.register({ id: "reports-monitor", title: "Report Scanner + Intercept Solver", pages: null, init: function () {
/*
 * ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
 *  TWO INDEPENDENT SUBSYSTEMS (4.0.0)
 * ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
 *  A) REPORT REFRESHER  — only on the Galaxy "Moving Fleets" report page.
 *     Reloads that page on a jittered timer; colors rows; alerts on new
 *     blacklist fleets; logs observations. (Validated against a live B37 DOM.)
 *
 *  B) MAP FLEET MONITOR — works on ANY AstroEmpires page.
 *     Background-fetches up to 3 map locations on a round-robin schedule,
 *     parses each location's recorded fleet list, diffs successive scans to
 *     detect fleets that DEPARTED/ARRIVED above a user-set size threshold,
 *     beeps, and logs movement observations for the Intercept Solver.
 *
 *  INDEPENDENCE: each subsystem's RUN state is per-tab (sessionStorage), so a
 *  refresher in one tab and a monitor in another do not interfere. All settings
 *  are per-server (GM storage, shared & persistent). Storage namespaces are
 *  disjoint (aeplus_rep_* vs aeplus_map_*).
 *
 *  LOCATION PARSING — finalised against live Borealis map samples (B29, B21).
 *  The recorded fleet list is table#map_fleets → a sortable listing with columns
 *  Fleet | Player | Arrival | Size. We scope to #map_fleets so the #map_base
 *  owner row is never mistaken for a fleet, read the "[GUILD] Name" profile cell,
 *  the bare/sorttable_customkey size (split fleets show "x (N)"), the id="timerN"
 *  arrival, and the "Recorded data from N hour(s) ago" staleness line. The full
 *  page (incl. #server-time) is in the server HTML, so background fetch works;
 *  visible mode navigates one reused scanner tab that parses itself on load.
 * ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
 */

(function () {
  'use strict';

  /* ===== embedded AEShared (canonical ae_shared.js v0.3.1, verbatim) ===== */


  /* ===== end embedded AEShared ===== */


  /* ═══════════════════════ server-scoped storage ═══════════════════════ */

  const SERVER_ID = location.hostname.toLowerCase();
  const SERVER_NAME = SERVER_ID.replace('.astroempires.com', '');
  const SERVER_PREFIX = SERVER_ID.charAt(0).toUpperCase();

  // Persistent, shared-across-tabs configuration (GM storage).
  const BASE_KEY = {
    // report refresher
    rep_galaxy:      'aeplus_rep_galaxy',
    rep_baseSec:     'aeplus_rep_base_seconds',
    rep_durMin:      'aeplus_rep_dur_min',
    rep_collapsed:   'aeplus_rep_collapsed',
    rep_whitelist:   'aeplus_rep_whitelist',
    rep_blacklist:   'aeplus_rep_blacklist',
    rep_scope:       'aeplus_rep_capture_scope',
    rep_sound:       'aeplus_rep_sound',
    rep_seenPrefix:  'aeplus_rep_seen_',
    rep_obsLog:      'aeplus_rep_obs_log',
    rep_logOpen:     'aeplus_rep_log_open',
    // map monitor
    map_loc1:        'aeplus_map_loc1',
    map_loc2:        'aeplus_map_loc2',
    map_loc3:        'aeplus_map_loc3',
    map_baseSec:     'aeplus_map_base_seconds',
    map_minSize:     'aeplus_map_min_size',
    map_sound:       'aeplus_map_sound',
    map_visible:     'aeplus_map_visible_tabs',
    map_obsLog:      'aeplus_map_obs_log',
    map_snapPrefix:  'aeplus_map_snap_',
    map_solverPrefix: 'aeplus_map_solver_',
    map_masters:     'aeplus_map_masters',
    map_loggedout:   'aeplus_map_loggedout',
    map_logOpen:     'aeplus_map_log_open',
    // shared UI
    collapsed:       'aeplus_panel_collapsed',
  };
  const keyForServer = (b) => `${SERVER_ID}::${b}`;
  const KEY = Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(BASE_KEY).map(([k, v]) => [k, keyForServer(v)]));

  const SCOPES = {
    all: 'All moving fleets', nonwhitelisted: 'Non-whitelisted only',
    blacklist: 'Blacklist only', scout40: 'Scouts (size 40)',
  };

  const DEFAULTS = {
    rep_galaxy: `${SERVER_PREFIX}39`, rep_baseSec: 90, rep_durMin: 0,
    rep_whitelist: '', rep_blacklist: '', rep_scope: 'nonwhitelisted', rep_sound: true, rep_logOpen: false,
    map_loc1: '', map_loc2: '', map_loc3: '',
    map_baseSec: 120, map_minSize: 100000, map_sound: true, map_visible: true, map_logOpen: false,
    collapsed: false,
  };

  // Per-TAB run state (sessionStorage): survives same-tab reloads, not tab close.
  // This is what makes the refresher and the monitor independent across tabs.
  const TAB = {
    getRun(feat) { try { return sessionStorage.getItem(`aeplus_run_${feat}_${SERVER_ID}`) === '1'; } catch { return false; } },
    setRun(feat, on) { try { sessionStorage.setItem(`aeplus_run_${feat}_${SERVER_ID}`, on ? '1' : '0'); } catch { /* noop */ } },
  };

  const STATE = {
    statusTimerId: null,
    beepTimer: null, audioCtx: null,
    // report
    rep_timerId: null, rep_nextAtMs: null, rep_lastRowCount: 0,
    // map
    map_timerId: null, map_nextAtMs: null, map_idx: 0,
    map_lastAt: {}, map_lastMove: {}, map_lastErr: {}, map_lastCount: {}, map_busy: false,
    map_lastLogLen: 0, scanWin: null, scanFallback: false, loggedOut: false,
  };

  /* ═══════════════════════════════ utils ═══════════════════════════════ */

  const $ = (s, r = document) => r.querySelector(s);
  // TWO panels, one codebase (shared parsing/state/storage):
  //   HOST  / SHADOW  -> 'intercept-solver'  (fleet monitor + movements + solver)
  //   HOST2 / SHADOW2 -> 'report-scanner'    (galaxy report refresher + detections)
  let SHADOW = null, HOST = null, PANEL = null;
  let SHADOW2 = null, HOST2 = null, PANEL2 = null;
  // Query across BOTH panels, so existing call sites keep working no matter which
  // panel now owns a given control.
  const $ui = (sel) => (SHADOW && SHADOW.querySelector(sel)) || (SHADOW2 && SHADOW2.querySelector(sel)) || null;
  const norm = (s) => (s || '').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim();
  const clamp = (v, lo, hi) => Math.max(lo, Math.min(hi, v));
  const getVal = (k, d) => { const v = GM_getValue(k, undefined); return v === undefined ? d : v; };
  const setVal = (k, v) => GM_setValue(k, v);

  const fmt = (s) => { const n = Math.max(0, Math.round(s)); return n < 60 ? `${n}s` : `${Math.floor(n / 60)}m ${n % 60}s`; };
  const fmtClock = (ms) => { try { return new Date(ms).toLocaleTimeString(); } catch { return '—'; } };

  // ±30% jitter, floored at floorSec, in ms.
  const jitterMs = (baseSec, floorSec = 5) => {
    const j = (Math.random() * 0.6) - 0.3;
    const sec = Math.max(floorSec, baseSec * (1 + j) + Math.random() * 1.5);
    return Math.round(clamp(sec, floorSec, 3600) * 1000);
  };

  function safeUrlParam(href, param) {
    try { return new URL(href, location.origin).searchParams.get(param) || ''; } catch { return ''; }
  }
  function escapeHtml(s) {
    return String(s || '').replace(/&/g, '&amp;').replace(/</g, '&lt;').replace(/>/g, '&gt;')
      .replace(/"/g, '&quot;').replace(/'/g, '&#039;');
  }
  function normalizeGalaxyInput(value) {
    let g = (value || '').trim().toUpperCase();
    if (/^\d{1,2}$/.test(g)) g = `${SERVER_PREFIX}${g.padStart(2, '0')}`;
    return g;
  }
  function isValidServerGalaxy(galaxy) {
    const g = normalizeGalaxyInput(galaxy);
    if (!new RegExp(`^${SERVER_PREFIX}\\d{2}$`).test(g)) return false;
    const n = parseInt(g.slice(1), 10); return n >= 0 && n <= 49;
  }
  function normalizeCoord(v) {
    const t = (v || '').trim().toUpperCase().replace(/\s+/g, '');
    return /^[A-Z]\d{1,2}(:\d{1,3}){2,3}$/.test(t) ? t : '';
  }
  function getServerClock(doc = document) { return norm(doc.querySelector('#server-time')?.textContent || ''); }

  // Logged-in detection: authenticated game pages carry the live server clock,
  // the top nav, or a logout link. If none are present the page is a login /
  // session-expired / error page and the script should not keep refreshing it.
  function gameUiPresent(doc = document) {
    if (!doc || !doc.querySelector) return false;
    return !!(doc.querySelector('#server-time')
      || doc.querySelector('a[href*="session=logout"]')
      || doc.querySelector('#map a[href*="map.aspx"], #fleets a[href*="fleet.aspx"], #account a[href*="account.aspx"], #bases a[href*="base.aspx"]'));
  }
  // Strict logout signal: a real login form present AND no game UI. Stricter than
  // !gameUiPresent so a logged-in non-game page (forum, etc.) can't false-trigger.
  function isLoginPage(doc = document) {
    return !gameUiPresent(doc) && !!(doc.querySelector && doc.querySelector('input[type="password"]'));
  }

  async function copyText(text) {
    try { if (typeof GM_setClipboard === 'function') { GM_setClipboard(text, 'text'); return true; } } catch { /* */ }
    try { if (navigator.clipboard?.writeText) { await navigator.clipboard.writeText(text); return true; } } catch { /* */ }
    try {
      const ta = document.createElement('textarea'); ta.value = text;
      ta.style.position = 'fixed'; ta.style.opacity = '0';
      document.body.appendChild(ta); ta.select();
      const ok = document.execCommand('copy'); ta.remove(); return ok;
    } catch { return false; }
  }
  function downloadFile(filename, text, mime = 'text/plain;charset=utf-8') {
    const blob = new Blob([text], { type: mime }); const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    const a = document.createElement('a'); a.href = url; a.download = filename;
    document.body.appendChild(a); a.click(); a.remove(); URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
  }

  /* ═══════════════════════ DOM detection (report) ══════════════════════ */

  // The galaxy report FORM lives at report.aspx?view=galaxy (singular "report", and the
  // view param is required — /reports.aspx 404s, and bare /report.aspx has no galaxy
  // form to submit). Same page every fetch helper posts to.
  const REPORT_PAGE_URL = '/report.aspx?view=galaxy';
  const formSel = "form[action*='report.aspx']";
  const getForm = () => $(formSel);
  const getGalaxySel = () => $(`${formSel} select[name='galaxy']`);
  const getTypeSel = () =>
    $(`${formSel} select[name='report_type']`) || $(`${formSel} select[name='show']`) || $(`${formSel} select[name='report']`);

  function isGalaxyReportsPage() {
    return !!(getForm() && getGalaxySel() && getTypeSel());
  }
  function findMovingFleetsValue(sel) {
    for (const opt of sel.options) if (String(opt.value).toLowerCase() === 'moving_fleets') return opt.value;
    for (const opt of sel.options) if (/moving\s*fleets/i.test(opt.textContent || '')) return opt.value;
    return null;
  }
  function ensureMovingFleets() {
    const sel = getTypeSel(); if (!sel) return false;
    const want = findMovingFleetsValue(sel); if (want == null) return false;
    if (sel.value !== want) { sel.value = want; return true; } return false;
  }
  function setGalaxy(target) {
    const sel = getGalaxySel(); if (!sel) return false;
    const want = normalizeGalaxyInput(target);
    for (const opt of sel.options) if (norm(opt.textContent).toUpperCase() === want) {
      if (sel.value !== opt.value) { sel.value = opt.value; return true; } return false;
    }
    const m = want.match(/^([A-Z])?(\d{1,2})$/i);
    if (m) { const idx = String(parseInt(m[2], 10));
      for (const opt of sel.options) if (String(opt.value) === idx) {
        if (sel.value !== opt.value) { sel.value = opt.value; return true; } return false;
      }
    }
    return false;
  }
  function submitReportFormIfRunning() {
    if (!TAB.getRun('report')) return;
    if (!gameUiPresent(document)) { renderReportStatus(); return; }   // logged out → don't reload the login page
    const form = getForm(); if (!form) return;
    setTimeout(() => { if (TAB.getRun('report') && gameUiPresent(document)) form.submit(); }, 60 + Math.random() * 240);
  }

    .aeplus-new-red { animation:aeplus-pulse 1.6s ease-out 3; border-radius:6px; }
  `);
