    .aeplus-empty { background:var(--ae-surface-2); border:1px dashed var(--ae-border); border-radius:var(--ae-radius-sm); padding:10px; color:var(--ae-text-muted); font-size:11px; margin-top:9px; }

  /* ═══════════════════════ whitelist / blacklist ═══════════════════════ */

  function readTags(key) { return (getVal(key, '') || '').split(',').map(s => s.trim().toUpperCase()).filter(Boolean); }
  const readWhitelist = () => readTags(KEY.rep_whitelist);
  const readBlacklist = () => readTags(KEY.rep_blacklist);
  const hasTag = (u, t) => u.includes(`[${t}]`) || u.includes(t);
  const isWhitelisted = (text, wl) => { const u = text.toUpperCase(); return wl.some(t => hasTag(u, t)); };
  const isBlacklisted = (text, bl) => { const u = text.toUpperCase(); return bl.some(t => hasTag(u, t)); };

  /* ══════════════ shared fleet-row parsing (validated on report) ════════ */

  const ETA_RE = /\b\d{1,3}:\d{2}(:\d{2})?\b|\b\d+\s*[dhm]\b/i;
  const CLOCK_RE = /@|\bToday\b|\bTomorrow\b|\d{1,2}\s+\w{3}\s+\d{4}/i;

  // Locate a fleet row's cells by content signals (proven on the report DOM).
  function locateCells(tr) {
    const tds = Array.from(tr.querySelectorAll('td'));
    if (tds.length < 2) return null;
    let playerTd = null, destTd = null, arrivalTd = null, sizeTd = null;
    for (const td of tds) {
      if (!playerTd && td.querySelector('a[href*="profile.aspx?player="]')) playerTd = td;
      if (!destTd && td.querySelector('a[href*="map.aspx?loc="]')) destTd = td;
      if (!arrivalTd && /^timer/i.test(td.id || '')) arrivalTd = td;
    }
    const sizeCands = tds.filter(td =>
      td !== playerTd && td !== destTd && td !== arrivalTd && /^\d[\d,]*$/.test(norm(td.textContent)));
    if (sizeCands.length) {
      sizeTd = sizeCands.find(td => {
        const k = td.getAttribute('sorttable_customkey');
        return k && /^\d+$/.test(k) && k === norm(td.textContent).replace(/,/g, '');
      }) || sizeCands[0];
    }
    if (!arrivalTd) {
      for (const td of tds) {
        if (td === playerTd || td === destTd || td === sizeTd) continue;
        const t = norm(td.textContent);
        if (t === '-' || ETA_RE.test(t) || CLOCK_RE.test(t)) { arrivalTd = td; break; }
      }
    }
    if (!playerTd) playerTd = tds[1] || tds[0] || null;
    if (!sizeTd) sizeTd = tds[tds.length - 1] || null;
    return { playerTd, destTd, sizeTd, arrivalTd };
  }

  function parseArrival(arrivalTd) {
    const raw = norm(arrivalTd?.textContent || '');
    if (!raw || raw === '-') return { raw, eta: '', clock: '' };
    const etaMatch = raw.match(/\b\d{1,3}:\d{2}:\d{2}\b/);
    const clockMatch = raw.match(/(?:(Today|Tomorrow|Yesterday)\s*)?@\s*(\d{1,2}:\d{2}:\d{2})/i)
                    || raw.match(/(Today|Tomorrow|Yesterday)\s+(\d{1,2}:\d{2}:\d{2})/i);
    return { raw, eta: etaMatch ? etaMatch[0].trim() : '',
      clock: clockMatch ? `${clockMatch[1] ? clockMatch[1] + ' ' : ''}@ ${clockMatch[2]}`.replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim() : '' };
  }

  function parseRow(tr) {
    const cells = locateCells(tr); if (!cells) return null;
    const { playerTd, destTd, sizeTd, arrivalTd } = cells;
    const playerText = norm(playerTd?.textContent || '');
    const playerA = playerTd?.querySelector('a[href*="profile.aspx?player="]') || tr.querySelector('a[href*="profile.aspx?player="]');
    const playerId = playerA ? safeUrlParam(playerA.href, 'player') : '';
    const destText = norm(destTd?.textContent || '');
    const destA = destTd?.querySelector('a[href*="map.aspx?loc="]') || tr.querySelector('a[href*="map.aspx?loc="]');
    const destLoc = destA ? safeUrlParam(destA.href, 'loc') : '';
    const sizeRaw = sizeTd?.getAttribute('sorttable_customkey') || norm(sizeTd?.textContent || '');
    const sizeDigits = String(sizeRaw).replace(/[^\d]/g, '');
    const size = sizeDigits ? parseInt(sizeDigits, 10) : null;
    const arrival = parseArrival(arrivalTd);
    if (!playerId && !destLoc && size == null) return null;
    return { tr, playerText, playerId, destText, destLoc, size,
      arrivalRaw: arrival.raw, arrivalEta: arrival.eta, arrivalClock: arrival.clock,
      rowKey: `${playerId}|${destLoc || destText}|${size ?? ''}` };
  }

  // Parse fleet rows inside any container/document (report table OR fetched map page).
  function parseFleetRowsIn(root) {
    if (!root) return [];
    return Array.from(root.querySelectorAll('tr'))
      .filter(tr => !tr.querySelector('table')   // skip wrapper rows that contain a nested table
        && tr.querySelector('a[href*="profile.aspx?player="]') && tr.querySelectorAll('td').length >= 2)
      .map(parseRow).filter(Boolean);
  }

  /* ════════════════ guild/arrival helpers (solver-shaped) ═══════════════ */

  function splitPlayerGuild(playerText) {
    const t = (playerText || '').trim();
    const gm = t.match(/\[([^\]]+)\]/);
    if (gm) { const guild = gm[1].trim() || '?';
      const name = (t.slice(0, gm.index) + ' ' + t.slice(gm.index + gm[0].length)).replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim();
      return { name: name || guild, guild };
    }
    return { name: t, guild: '?' };
  }

  /* ═══════════════════ REPORT REFRESHER subsystem ══════════════════════ */

  function getResultTable() {
    const scope = document.getElementById('empire_reports') || getForm()?.closest('table') || document;
    return scope.querySelector('table.layout.listing.btnlisting.tbllisting1')
        || scope.querySelector('table.sorttable') || scope.querySelector('table.listing')
        || document.querySelector('table.sorttable');
  }
  function reportRows() {
    const tbl = getResultTable(); if (!tbl) return [];
    const body = tbl.tBodies?.[0] || tbl;
    return Array.from(body.querySelectorAll('tr')).map(parseRow).filter(Boolean);
  }
  function getGalaxyLabel() {
    const opt = getGalaxySel()?.selectedOptions?.[0];
    return opt ? norm(opt.textContent) : getVal(KEY.rep_galaxy, DEFAULTS.rep_galaxy);
  }
  const seenKeyForGalaxy = (g) => `${KEY.rep_seenPrefix}${g}`;
  function getSeenSet(g) { const raw = getVal(seenKeyForGalaxy(g), ''); if (!raw) return new Set(); try { return new Set(JSON.parse(raw)); } catch { return new Set(); } }
  function setSeenSet(g, set) { let a = Array.from(set); if (a.length > 800) a = a.slice(a.length - 800); try { setVal(seenKeyForGalaxy(g), JSON.stringify(a)); } catch { /* */ } }

  function moveNewEnemyRowsToTop() {
    const tbl = getResultTable(); if (!tbl) return;
    const tbody = tbl.tBodies?.[0] || tbl;
    const rows = Array.from(tbody.querySelectorAll('tr'));
    const marked = rows.filter(tr => tr.classList.contains('aeplus-new-enemy-top'));
    if (!marked.length) return;
    const firstUnmarked = rows.find(tr => tr.querySelectorAll('td').length >= 3 && !tr.classList.contains('aeplus-new-enemy-top'));
    const frag = document.createDocumentFragment();
    for (const r of marked) frag.appendChild(r);
    if (firstUnmarked && firstUnmarked.parentNode === tbody) tbody.insertBefore(frag, firstUnmarked); else tbody.appendChild(frag);
  }

  // report observation log
  function getRepLog() { const raw = getVal(KEY.rep_obsLog, ''); if (!raw) return []; try { const p = JSON.parse(raw); return Array.isArray(p) ? p : []; } catch { return []; } }
  function setRepLog(e) { try { setVal(KEY.rep_obsLog, JSON.stringify(e)); } catch { /* */ } }
  function repObsKey(e) { return [e.server, e.galaxy, e.playerId, e.destLoc, e.destText, e.size, e.arrivalRaw].join('|'); }
  function inScope(scope, { whitelisted, blacklisted, size }) {
    switch (scope) {
      case 'all': return true; case 'nonwhitelisted': return !whitelisted;
      case 'blacklist': return blacklisted; case 'scout40': return !whitelisted && size === 40;
      default: return !whitelisted;
    }
  }
  function captureReportObservations() {
    const galaxy = getGalaxyLabel(), serverClock = getServerClock();
    const scope = getVal(KEY.rep_scope, DEFAULTS.rep_scope), wl = readWhitelist(), bl = readBlacklist();
    const log = getRepLog(); const keys = new Set(log.map(repObsKey)); let added = 0;
    for (const r of reportRows()) {
      const whitelisted = isWhitelisted(r.playerText, wl), blacklisted = isBlacklisted(r.playerText, bl);
      if (!inScope(scope, { whitelisted, blacklisted, size: r.size })) continue;
      const entry = { observedAt: new Date().toISOString(), serverClock, server: SERVER_NAME, galaxy,
        player: r.playerText, playerId: r.playerId, destText: r.destText, destLoc: r.destLoc, size: r.size,
        arrivalRaw: r.arrivalRaw, arrivalEta: r.arrivalEta, arrivalClock: r.arrivalClock,
        whitelisted, blacklisted, isScout40: r.size === 40 };
      const k = repObsKey(entry); if (keys.has(k)) continue;
      keys.add(k); log.unshift(entry); added++;
    }
    if (log.length > 1000) log.length = 1000;
    if (added > 0) { setRepLog(log); renderRepLog(); }
    return added;
  }
  function clearRepLog() { if (!confirm(`Clear the report observation log for ${SERVER_NAME}?`)) return; setRepLog([]); renderRepLog(); toast('Report log cleared'); }

  function colorAndDetect() {
    const galaxy = getGalaxyLabel(), wl = readWhitelist(), bl = readBlacklist();
    const seen = getSeenSet(galaxy), rows = reportRows(); STATE.rep_lastRowCount = rows.length;
    let alertNeeded = false;
    for (const r of rows) {
      const isNew = !seen.has(r.rowKey);
      const whitelisted = isWhitelisted(r.playerText, wl), blacklisted = isBlacklisted(r.playerText, bl);
      r.tr.classList.remove('aeplus-row-green', 'aeplus-row-red', 'aeplus-new-red', 'aeplus-new-enemy-top');
      r.tr.classList.add(blacklisted ? 'aeplus-row-red' : whitelisted ? 'aeplus-row-green' : 'aeplus-row-red');
      if (isNew) { seen.add(r.rowKey);
        if (!whitelisted) r.tr.classList.add('aeplus-new-enemy-top');
        if (blacklisted) { alertNeeded = true; r.tr.classList.add('aeplus-new-red'); }
      }
    }
    setSeenSet(galaxy, seen); moveNewEnemyRowsToTop();
    if (alertNeeded && getVal(KEY.rep_sound, DEFAULTS.rep_sound)) startBeeping(); else stopBeeping();
  }
  function scanReportPage() { colorAndDetect(); captureReportObservations(); renderReportStatus(); }

  function alignReportFilters() {
    const form = getForm(), g = getGalaxySel(), r = getTypeSel();
    if (!(form && g && r)) return false;
    let changed = false;
    changed = setGalaxy(getVal(KEY.rep_galaxy, DEFAULTS.rep_galaxy)) || changed;
    changed = ensureMovingFleets() || changed;
    return changed;
  }
  const DEADLINE_KEY = `aeplus_rep_deadline_${SERVER_ID}`;
  function repDeadline() { const v = parseInt(sessionStorage.getItem(DEADLINE_KEY), 10); return Number.isFinite(v) ? v : null; }
  function setRepDeadline(ms) { try { if (ms) sessionStorage.setItem(DEADLINE_KEY, String(ms)); else sessionStorage.removeItem(DEADLINE_KEY); } catch { /* */ } }
  function repSessionExpired() { const d = repDeadline(); return d != null && Date.now() >= d; }
  function stopReportSession(reason) {
    TAB.setRun('report', false); setRepDeadline(null);
    clearTimeout(STATE.rep_timerId); STATE.rep_timerId = null; STATE.rep_nextAtMs = null;
    STATE.rep_stopReason = reason || null;
    renderRepToggle(); renderReportStatus();
  }
  function scheduleReportNext() {
    clearTimeout(STATE.rep_timerId); STATE.rep_timerId = null; STATE.rep_nextAtMs = null;
    if (!TAB.getRun('report')) { renderReportStatus(); return; }
    if (repSessionExpired()) { stopReportSession('duration reached'); return; }
    if (AEShared.isMasterHidden && AEShared.isMasterHidden()) { stopReportSession('suite hidden'); return; }
    const baseSec = +getVal(KEY.rep_baseSec, DEFAULTS.rep_baseSec) || DEFAULTS.rep_baseSec;
    const ms = jitterMs(baseSec, 5);
    STATE.rep_nextAtMs = Date.now() + ms; renderReportStatus();
    STATE.rep_timerId = setTimeout(() => {
      if (!TAB.getRun('report')) return;
      if (repSessionExpired()) { stopReportSession('duration reached'); return; }
      alignReportFilters(); submitReportFormIfRunning();
    }, ms);
  }

  /* ═══════════════════ MAP FLEET MONITOR subsystem ═════════════════════ */

  function mapLocations() {
    return [getVal(KEY.map_loc1, '')].map(normalizeCoord).filter(Boolean);   // single location
  }
  // Spacing between consecutive location fetches: ≥15s ALWAYS, otherwise the
  // user's interval split across active locations (so each location is revisited
  // about every `interval`, but never closer together than 15s).
  function mapSpacingSec() {
    const n = mapLocations().length || 1;
    const baseSec = +getVal(KEY.map_baseSec, DEFAULTS.map_baseSec) || DEFAULTS.map_baseSec;
    return Math.max(15, baseSec / n);
  }
  function mapMinSize() { return Math.max(0, parseInt(getVal(KEY.map_minSize, DEFAULTS.map_minSize), 10) || 0); }

  // last snapshot per coord (persisted so detection survives reloads)
  const mapSnapKey = (c) => `${KEY.map_snapPrefix}${c}`;
  function getMapSnap(c) { const raw = getVal(mapSnapKey(c), ''); if (!raw) return null; try { return JSON.parse(raw); } catch { return null; } }
  function setMapSnap(c, snap) { try { setVal(mapSnapKey(c), JSON.stringify(snap)); } catch { /* */ } }

  // map movement observation log
  function getMapLog() { const raw = getVal(KEY.map_obsLog, ''); if (!raw) return []; try { const p = JSON.parse(raw); return Array.isArray(p) ? p : []; } catch { return []; } }
  function setMapLog(e) { try { setVal(KEY.map_obsLog, JSON.stringify(e)); } catch { /* */ } }
  function clearMapLog() { if (!confirm(`Clear the movement log for ${SERVER_NAME}?`)) return; setMapLog([]); renderMapLog(); toast('Movement log cleared'); }

  // ── cross-tab "master" presence (so two tabs scanning at once is visible) ──
  // Each running master tab heartbeats into a shared registry { tabId: lastMs }.
  // A tab is "alive" if its beat is newer than HB_STALE_MS; dead entries are
  // pruned on every beat and ignored on read, so closed/crashed tabs self-expire.
  const HB_BEAT_MS = 2500, HB_STALE_MS = 8000;
  function tabId() {
    if (STATE._tabId) return STATE._tabId;
    let id;
    try { id = sessionStorage.getItem('aeplus_tab_id'); if (!id) { id = 't' + Date.now().toString(36) + Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 8); sessionStorage.setItem('aeplus_tab_id', id); } }
    catch { id = 't' + Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 10); }
    return (STATE._tabId = id);
  }
  function readMasters() { const raw = getVal(KEY.map_masters, ''); if (!raw) return {}; try { const o = JSON.parse(raw); return (o && typeof o === 'object') ? o : {}; } catch { return {}; } }
  function writeMasters(o) { try { setVal(KEY.map_masters, JSON.stringify(o)); } catch { /* */ } }
  function beatMaster(alive) {
    const id = tabId(), now = Date.now(), reg = readMasters();
    if (alive) reg[id] = now; else delete reg[id];
    for (const k of Object.keys(reg)) if (now - reg[k] > HB_STALE_MS) delete reg[k];
    writeMasters(reg);
  }
  function otherMastersRunning() {
    const id = tabId(), now = Date.now(), reg = readMasters();
    return Object.keys(reg).some(k => k !== id && (now - reg[k]) < HB_STALE_MS);
  }
  function heartbeatTick() {
    const running = TAB.getRun('map');
    if (running) {
      const now = Date.now();
      if (now - (STATE._lastBeat || 0) >= HB_BEAT_MS) { beatMaster(true); STATE._lastBeat = now; }
    } else if (STATE._wasRunning) {
      beatMaster(false);
    }
    STATE._wasRunning = running;
  }

  // ── location fetch + parse (finalised against live B29/B21 map samples) ──
  const SCAN_WIN_NAME = 'ae_map_scanner';
  const LOCATION_URL = (coord) => `/map.aspx?loc=${coord}`;
  const visibleMode = () => !!getVal(KEY.map_visible, DEFAULTS.map_visible) && !STATE.scanFallback;

  // The recorded fleet list lives in table#map_fleets. We scope to it so the
  // #map_base owner row (which also has a profile link + a "107 / 107" cell) is
  // never mistaken for a fleet. Returns
  //   { serverClock, recordedHoursAgo, coordSeen, fleets:[{player,playerId,guild,size,arrivalRaw,arrivalEta,arrivalClock}] }
  function parseLocationFleetList(doc, coord) {
    const serverClock = getServerClock(doc);
    const bodyText = doc.body ? doc.body.textContent : '';
    const recM = bodyText.match(/Recorded data from\s+([\d.]+)\s+hour/i);
    const recordedHoursAgo = recM ? parseFloat(recM[1]) : null;   // null = live/current vision
    const coordSeen = (bodyText.match(/\(([A-Z]\d{1,2}(?::\d{1,3}){2,3})\)/) || [])[1] || '';

    const scope = doc.getElementById('map_fleets');
    const tbl = scope ? scope.querySelector('table.sorttable') : null;
    const body = tbl ? (tbl.tBodies[0] || tbl) : scope;     // the 44 data rows live here
    const rows = parseFleetRowsIn(body).map(r => {
      const { guild } = splitPlayerGuild(r.playerText);
      return { player: r.playerText, playerId: r.playerId, guild, size: r.size,
        arrivalRaw: r.arrivalRaw, arrivalEta: r.arrivalEta, arrivalClock: r.arrivalClock };
    }).filter(f => f.size != null && f.player);
    return { serverClock, recordedHoursAgo, coordSeen, fleets: rows };
  }

  // Recorded sizes are rounded by the game (e.g. 3,300,000), so a present fleet that
  // merely stayed put must not read as changed. Tolerance absorbs that rounding.
  const SIZE_ABS_TOL = 100000, SIZE_REL_TOL = 0.02;
  function sameSize(a, b) { return Math.abs(a - b) <= Math.max(SIZE_ABS_TOL, SIZE_REL_TOL * Math.max(a, b)); }

  // Domain model (game truth): a fleet with NO arrival timer is PRESENT (stationed)
  // AT the location; a fleet WITH a timer is INBOUND (in transit toward it, not here
  // yet). A player has at most one present fleet and may have several inbound fleets
  // with independent timers. Snapshots carry a `stationed` flag; older snapshots
  // without it fall back to the arrival fields.
  function fleetPresent(f) {
    if (f.stationed === true) return true;
    if (f.stationed === false) return false;
    return !(f.arrivalEta || f.arrivalClock);
  }

  // Robust categorisation: report a movement only from the change in each player's
  // PRESENT (stationed) mass between scans. Inbound fleets are ignored — one has not
  // arrived until its timer expires, and a fleet in transit is not "at" this location.
  // This removes the phantom departed+arrived pairs the old size-bag matcher produced
  // whenever a player's fleets re-partitioned (inbound fleets merging into the present
  // fleet, or the present fleet splitting into launches) — those conserve total mass,
  // so the present-mass delta is zero and nothing is logged. A real launch shrinks the
  // present mass (departed); a real arrival grows it (arrived); the size logged is the
  // net mass that actually moved.
  function diffLocation(prevFleets, newFleets, minSize) {
    const presentByPlayer = (arr) => {
      const g = {};
      for (const f of arr) {
        if (!fleetPresent(f)) continue;                       // inbound → not present, skip
        const e = g[f.player] || (g[f.player] = { player: f.player, guild: f.guild || '?', size: 0 });
        e.size += (f.size || 0);                               // sum only defensively; rule says ≤1 present
        if ((!e.guild || e.guild === '?') && f.guild) e.guild = f.guild;
      }
      return g;
    };
    const pa = presentByPlayer(prevFleets), pb = presentByPlayer(newFleets);
    const departed = [], arrived = [];
    for (const p of new Set([...Object.keys(pa), ...Object.keys(pb)])) {
      const prev = pa[p], now = pb[p];
      const ps = prev ? prev.size : 0, ns = now ? now.size : 0;
      if (ps > 0 && ns > 0 && sameSize(ps, ns)) continue;     // present fleet stayed (absorb rounding)
      const delta = ns - ps;
      if (delta === 0) continue;
      const guild = (now && now.guild) || (prev && prev.guild) || '?';
      if (delta > 0) arrived.push({ player: p, guild, size: delta });
      else departed.push({ player: p, guild, size: -delta });
    }
    const big = arr => arr.filter(x => x.size >= minSize);
    return { departed: big(departed), arrived: big(arrived) };
  }

  function logMovements(coord, prevSnap, newSnap, moves) {
    const log = getMapLog(); let added = 0;
    const push = (m, direction) => {
      log.unshift({ kind: 'movement', detectedAt: new Date().toISOString(), server: SERVER_NAME,
        location: coord, player: m.player, guild: m.guild, size: m.size, direction,
        windowStart: prevSnap?.at || null, windowEnd: newSnap.at,
        prevServerClock: prevSnap?.serverClock || '', nowServerClock: newSnap.serverClock || '',
        recordedHoursAgo: newSnap.recordedHoursAgo ?? null });
      added++;
    };
    moves.departed.forEach(m => push(m, 'departed'));
    moves.arrived.forEach(m => push(m, 'arrived'));
    if (log.length > 1000) log.length = 1000;
    if (added) { setMapLog(log); renderMapLog(); }
    return added;
  }

  // Shared by background fetch AND the visible self-scan: diff vs the stored
  // snapshot, log movements, persist the new snapshot. Does NOT beep — the
  // controller tab beeps when it sees the shared log grow (so the sound plays
  // in the tab the user is actually looking at).
  function processScan(coord, parsed) {
    STATE.map_lastAt[coord] = Date.now();
    STATE.map_lastCount[coord] = parsed.fleets.length;
    pushSolverScan(coord, buildSolverText(parsed), parsed);
    const newSnap = { at: Date.now(), serverClock: parsed.serverClock,
      recordedHoursAgo: parsed.recordedHoursAgo,
      fleets: parsed.fleets.map(f => ({ player: f.player, guild: f.guild, size: f.size, stationed: !(f.arrivalEta || f.arrivalClock) })) };
    const prev = getMapSnap(coord);
    if (parsed.fleets.length === 0) {
      STATE.map_lastErr[coord] = 'no fleet rows found at this location';
    } else {
      STATE.map_lastErr[coord] = '';
      if (prev && Array.isArray(prev.fleets)) {
        const moves = diffLocation(prev.fleets, newSnap.fleets, mapMinSize());
        if (moves.departed.length || moves.arrived.length) {
          STATE.map_lastMove[coord] = Date.now();
          logMovements(coord, prev, newSnap, moves);
        }
      }
      setMapSnap(coord, newSnap);
    }
    renderMapStatus();
  }

  // ── logged-out handling: STOP and flag; the user restarts after logging in ──
  const LOGOUT_STALE_MS = 90000;
  function setLoggedOutFlag(on) { try { setVal(KEY.map_loggedout, on ? String(Date.now()) : ''); } catch { /* */ } }
  function loggedOutActive() { const t = parseInt(getVal(KEY.map_loggedout, ''), 10); return t > 0 && (Date.now() - t) < LOGOUT_STALE_MS; }
  // Hard stop: clear the run flag, kill timers, drop the scanner tab, leave the
  // session marked logged-out so the UI explains why. No automatic re-probing —
  // the user logs back in and presses Start.
  function enterLoggedOutStop() {
    STATE.loggedOut = true;
    setLoggedOutFlag(true);
    TAB.setRun('map', false);
    clearTimeout(STATE.map_timerId); STATE.map_timerId = null; STATE.map_nextAtMs = null;
    try { if (STATE.scanWin && !STATE.scanWin.closed) STATE.scanWin.close(); } catch { /* */ }
    STATE.scanWin = null;
    beatMaster(false); STATE._wasRunning = false;
    renderMapToggle(); renderMapStatus();
  }
  // Called from the status ticker so a logout reported by ANY tab/scanner stops
  // this tab's monitor within ~1s, not only on the next scan tick.
  function checkLoggedOutStop() { if (TAB.getRun('map') && loggedOutActive()) enterLoggedOutStop(); }

  async function fetchLocationDoc(coord) {
    const res = await fetch(LOCATION_URL(coord), { credentials: 'include' });
    if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`HTTP ${res.status}`);
    const html = await res.text();
    return new DOMParser().parseFromString(html, 'text/html');
  }

  // Background-fetch path (visible tabs OFF).
  async function pollLocation(coord) {
    let doc;
    try { doc = await fetchLocationDoc(coord); }
    catch (e) { STATE.map_lastErr[coord] = e.message || 'fetch failed'; STATE.map_lastAt[coord] = Date.now(); renderMapStatus(); return; }
    if (!gameUiPresent(doc)) { enterLoggedOutStop(); return; }   // login / session-expired page → stop
    processScan(coord, parseLocationFleetList(doc, coord));
  }

  // Visible path: drive the single reused scanner tab to the coord; that tab's
  // own script instance parses + logs on load (see scanSelfAndLog).
  function driveScanWindow(coord) {
    const url = LOCATION_URL(coord);
    try {
      if (!STATE.scanWin || STATE.scanWin.closed) STATE.scanWin = window.open(url, SCAN_WIN_NAME);
      else { STATE.scanWin.location.href = url; }
    } catch { STATE.scanWin = null; }
    if (!STATE.scanWin) {
      // Popup blocked or open failed → fall back to background fetch for this run.
      STATE.scanFallback = true;
      STATE.map_lastErr[coord] = 'popup blocked — using background fetch (allow pop-ups to use visible tabs)';
      return pollLocation(coord);
    }
    STATE.map_lastAt[coord] = Date.now(); renderMapStatus();
  }

  // Runs inside the scanner tab on each load: parse THIS page, diff, log.
  function injectScannerBanner() {
    if (document.getElementById('aeplus-scanner-host')) return;
    const coord = (new URLSearchParams(location.search).get('loc') || '').toUpperCase();
    const host = document.createElement('div');
    host.id = 'aeplus-scanner-host';
    host.style.cssText = 'position:fixed;top:0;left:0;right:0;z-index:2147483647;';
    const sh = host.attachShadow({ mode: 'open' });
    AEShared.adopt(sh);                                      // themed banner in its own shadow root
    const extra = document.createElement('style');
    extra.textContent = '.blink{animation:aeplus-blink 1.1s steps(1) infinite}@keyframes aeplus-blink{50%{opacity:.3}}'
      + '.ae-banner--alert{text-align:center;border-bottom-width:2px}.ae-banner--alert small{display:block;font-weight:400;opacity:.9;font-size:10px;margin-top:2px}';
    sh.appendChild(extra);
    const el = document.createElement('div');
    el.id = 'aeplus-scanner-banner'; el.className = 'ae-banner ae-banner--alert';
    el.innerHTML = `<b><span class="blink">●</span> FLEET SCANNER ACTIVE${coord ? ' — parsing ' + escapeHtml(coord) : ''}</b>` +
      `<small>This tab is driven by the AE Map Monitor and cycles your locations automatically. ` +
      `Leave it open; close it (or Stop the monitor) to end visible scanning.</small>`;
    sh.appendChild(el);
    (document.body || document.documentElement).appendChild(host);
    try { document.title = '🔴 SCANNER · ' + (coord || 'AE'); } catch { /* */ }
  }

  function scanSelfAndLog() {
    if (!gameUiPresent(document)) { setLoggedOutFlag(true); return; }   // login page → tell the controller
    setLoggedOutFlag(false);
    const coord = (new URLSearchParams(location.search).get('loc') || '').toUpperCase()
      || (document.body.textContent.match(/\(([A-Z]\d{1,2}(?::\d{1,3}){2,3})\)/) || [])[1] || '';
    if (!coord) return;
    try { processScan(coord, parseLocationFleetList(document, coord)); } catch (e) { /* leave a soft trace only */ }
  }

  function scheduleMapNext() {
    clearTimeout(STATE.map_timerId); STATE.map_timerId = null; STATE.map_nextAtMs = null;
    if (!TAB.getRun('map') || !mapLocations().length) { renderMapStatus(); return; }
    const ms = jitterMs(mapSpacingSec(), 15);   // ±jitter, never below 15s
    STATE.map_nextAtMs = Date.now() + ms; renderMapStatus();
    STATE.map_timerId = setTimeout(mapTick, ms);
  }
  async function mapTick() {
    if (!TAB.getRun('map')) return;
    const locs = mapLocations();
    if (!locs.length) { scheduleMapNext(); return; }
    if (STATE.map_busy) { scheduleMapNext(); return; }
    if (loggedOutActive()) { enterLoggedOutStop(); return; }   // a scanner/other tab saw logout → stop
    STATE.map_busy = true;
    try {
      const coord = locs[STATE.map_idx % locs.length];
      STATE.map_idx = (STATE.map_idx + 1) % locs.length;
      if (visibleMode()) driveScanWindow(coord); else await pollLocation(coord);
    } finally { STATE.map_busy = false; if (TAB.getRun('map')) scheduleMapNext(); else renderMapStatus(); }
  }

  // Controller beeps when the shared movement log grows (covers both modes).
  function checkMovementBeep() {
    const len = getMapLog().length;
    if (len > STATE.map_lastLogLen) {
      if (TAB.getRun('map') && getVal(KEY.map_sound, DEFAULTS.map_sound)) beepBurst(3);
      renderMapLog();
    }
    STATE.map_lastLogLen = len;
  }

  function beepBurst(n = 3) {
    let ctx; try { ctx = new (window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext)(); } catch { return; }
    const tick = (i) => {
      if (i >= n) { setTimeout(() => { try { ctx.close(); } catch { /* */ } }, 250); return; }
      try {
        const osc = ctx.createOscillator(), gain = ctx.createGain();
        osc.frequency.value = 600; gain.gain.value = 0;
        osc.connect(gain).connect(ctx.destination);
        const t0 = ctx.currentTime;
        gain.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(0.5, t0 + 0.02);
        gain.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(0.0, t0 + 0.16);
        osc.start(t0); osc.stop(t0 + 0.18);
      } catch { /* */ }
      setTimeout(() => tick(i + 1), 230);
    };
    tick(0);
  }

  /* ═════════════════════════════ beeper ════════════════════════════════ */

  function startBeeping() {
    stopBeeping();
    try { STATE.audioCtx = new (window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext)(); }
    catch { console.warn('[AE+] WebAudio unavailable.'); return; }
    STATE.beepTimer = setInterval(() => beepOnce(440, 0.15, 0.02), 700);
    if (STATE.audioCtx.state === 'suspended') {
      const resume = () => { STATE.audioCtx?.resume(); window.removeEventListener('click', resume); };
      window.addEventListener('click', resume);
    }
    const btn = $ui('#aeplus-stop-alert'); if (btn) { btn.disabled = false; btn.textContent = 'Stop alert (beeping)'; }
  }
  function beepOnce(freq = 440, dur = 0.15, ramp = 0.02) {
    if (!STATE.audioCtx) return;
    const osc = STATE.audioCtx.createOscillator(), gain = STATE.audioCtx.createGain();
    osc.frequency.value = freq; gain.gain.value = 0;
    osc.connect(gain).connect(STATE.audioCtx.destination);
    const t0 = STATE.audioCtx.currentTime;
    gain.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(0.5, t0 + ramp);
    gain.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(0.0, t0 + dur);
    osc.start(t0); osc.stop(t0 + dur + 0.01);
  }
  function stopBeeping() {
    if (STATE.beepTimer) { clearInterval(STATE.beepTimer); STATE.beepTimer = null; }
    if (STATE.audioCtx) { try { STATE.audioCtx.close(); } catch { /* */ } STATE.audioCtx = null; }
    const btn = $ui('#aeplus-stop-alert'); if (btn) { btn.disabled = true; btn.textContent = 'Stop alert'; }
  }

  /* ════════════════════════ export (both logs) ═════════════════════════ */

  const REP_COLS = [['observedAt', 'observed_at'], ['serverClock', 'server_clock'], ['galaxy', 'galaxy'],
    ['player', 'player'], ['playerId', 'player_id'], ['destLoc', 'dest_loc'], ['destText', 'dest_text'],
    ['size', 'size'], ['arrivalEta', 'arrival_eta'], ['arrivalClock', 'arrival_clock'], ['arrivalRaw', 'arrival_raw']];
  const MAP_COLS = [['detectedAt', 'detected_at'], ['location', 'location'], ['direction', 'direction'],
    ['player', 'player'], ['guild', 'guild'], ['size', 'size'], ['windowStart', 'window_start'],
    ['windowEnd', 'window_end'], ['prevServerClock', 'prev_server_clock'], ['nowServerClock', 'now_server_clock']];

  function exportJson(which) {
    const log = which === 'map' ? getMapLog() : getRepLog();
    const env = { tool: which === 'map' ? 'ae-map-monitor' : 'ae-reports-refresher', schemaVersion: 1,
      server: SERVER_NAME, exportedAt: new Date().toISOString(), serverClockAtExport: getServerClock(),
      count: log.length, observations: log };
    downloadFile(`ae-${which}-observations-${SERVER_NAME}.json`, JSON.stringify(env, null, 2), 'application/json;charset=utf-8');
    toast(`Exported ${log.length} ${which} observation(s) as JSON`);
  }
  function exportCsv(which) {
    const log = which === 'map' ? getMapLog() : getRepLog();
    const cols = which === 'map' ? MAP_COLS : REP_COLS;
    const esc = v => `"${String(v ?? '').replace(/"/g, '""')}"`;
    const csv = [cols.map(c => c[1]).join(','), ...log.map(r => cols.map(c => esc(r[c[0]])).join(','))].join('\n');
    downloadFile(`ae-${which}-observations-${SERVER_NAME}.csv`, csv, 'text/csv;charset=utf-8');
    toast(`Exported ${log.length} ${which} observation(s) as CSV`);
  }

  // One solver-paste line per fleet: the location is parked in the harmless
  // fleet-name slot (validated), so it parses as "<name=loc> [guild] player size".
  function movementSolverLine(m) {
    const name = splitPlayerGuild(m.player).name || m.player;
    const guild = (m.guild && m.guild !== '?') ? m.guild : '?';
    return `${m.location} [${guild}] ${name} ${m.size}`;
  }

  /* ═══════════════ Intercept Solver hand-off (baked-in, opens in a tab) ═══════════════ */

  // Retain the last TWO DISTINCT recorded scans per location, rendered in the
  // Intercept Solver's own paste format ("[guild] player  eta - clock  size";
  // the guild bracket splits an optional fleet name from player+arrival+size).
  // "Distinct" = the fleet-list text changed, i.e. a fresh scout re-recorded the
  // location (the recorded view is otherwise frozen), so the retained pair always
  // represents two DIFFERENT recording times — exactly what the comparator diffs.
  const solverScansKey = (c) => `${KEY.map_solverPrefix}${c}`;
  function getSolverScans(c) { const raw = getVal(solverScansKey(c), ''); if (!raw) return []; try { const p = JSON.parse(raw); return Array.isArray(p) ? p : []; } catch { return []; } }
  function fleetSolverRow(f) {
    const g = splitPlayerGuild(f.player);
    let arr = '';
    if (f.arrivalEta && f.arrivalClock) arr = `${f.arrivalEta} - ${f.arrivalClock}`;
    else if (f.arrivalEta) arr = f.arrivalEta;
    else if (f.arrivalRaw && f.arrivalRaw !== '-' && /\d+:\d{2}:\d{2}/.test(f.arrivalRaw)) arr = f.arrivalRaw;
    return [`[${g.guild}]`, g.name, arr, String(f.size)].filter(s => s && s.length).join(' ');
  }
  function buildSolverText(parsed) {
    const lines = [];
    if (parsed.recordedHoursAgo != null) lines.push(`Recorded data from ${parsed.recordedHoursAgo} hour(s) ago`);
    for (const f of parsed.fleets) { if (f.size == null || !f.player) continue; lines.push(fleetSolverRow(f)); }
    return lines.join('\n');
  }
  function pushSolverScan(coord, text, parsed) {
    if (!text) return;
    const arr = getSolverScans(coord);
    const last = arr[arr.length - 1];
    if (last && last.text === text) { last.at = Date.now(); }        // same recording — refresh timestamp only
    else arr.push({ text, at: Date.now(), serverClock: parsed.serverClock || '', recordedHoursAgo: parsed.recordedHoursAgo ?? null });
    while (arr.length > 2) arr.shift();
    try { setVal(solverScansKey(coord), JSON.stringify(arr)); } catch { /* */ }
  }

  // local − server offset from the live header (both current => correct; absorbs drift).
  function fmtSignedHMS(sec) {
    const sign = sec < 0 ? '-' : ''; let a = Math.abs(Math.round(sec));
    const h = Math.floor(a / 3600); a -= h * 3600; const m = Math.floor(a / 60), s = a - m * 60;
    const p = n => String(n).padStart(2, '0');
    return `${sign}${h}:${p(m)}:${p(s)}`;
  }
  function liveOffsetStr() {
    const sc = getServerClock(document); const m = sc.match(/(\d{1,2}):(\d{2}):(\d{2})/);
    if (!m) return null;
    const serverSec = (+m[1]) * 3600 + (+m[2]) * 60 + (+m[3]);
    const d = new Date(); const localSec = d.getHours() * 3600 + d.getMinutes() * 60 + d.getSeconds();
    let off = ((localSec - serverSec) % 86400 + 86400) % 86400; if (off > 43200) off -= 86400;
    return fmtSignedHMS(off);
  }

  // The full Intercept Solver (v11) is baked in, base64 so its own backticks need
  // no escaping. Opened in a new tab; a tiny appended script pre-loads the retained
  // scans as snapshots and pre-fills the offset, so the comparator diff is ready.

  function b64ToUtf8(b64) {
    const bin = atob(b64); const bytes = new Uint8Array(bin.length);
    for (let i = 0; i < bin.length; i++) bytes[i] = bin.charCodeAt(i);
    return new TextDecoder('utf-8').decode(bytes);
  }
  // Opens the baked solver, pre-captures the retained scans, and injects a prominent
  // "Detected departures -> confirm as observations" panel wired to the solver's own
  // addObsFromLaunch(). Each detected departure gets a warp/stellar ship <select> +
  // drive-level range + Confirm. Needs >=2 distinct recordings (else a clear message).
  function buildSolverDoc(coord, scans, offsetStr) {
    const solverHtml = b64ToUtf8(SOLVER_B64);
    const j = (x) => JSON.stringify(x).replace(/</g, '\\u003c');
    const style = '<style>' + AEShared.cssVarsFor('#ae-imported') + '#ae-imported{margin:0 0 18px}#ae-imported .imp-row{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:9px;padding:8px 11px;border:1px solid var(--line-soft);border-radius:8px;margin-bottom:6px;background:var(--ae-surface-2,#0a1120);flex-wrap:wrap}#ae-imported .pl{flex:1;min-width:120px;color:var(--ink)}#ae-imported .sz{color:var(--muted);font-family:var(--mono)}#ae-imported select,#ae-imported .imp-min,#ae-imported .imp-max{background:var(--ae-bg,#060c17);border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:6px;color:var(--ink);font-family:var(--mono);font-size:11px;padding:4px 6px;outline:none}#ae-imported .imp-min,#ae-imported .imp-max{width:44px;text-align:center}#ae-imported .lv-lbl{font-size:10px;color:var(--muted);text-transform:uppercase}#ae-imported .imp-done{color:var(--green);font-size:11px}</style>';
    const script = `<script>(function(){var LOC=${j(coord)},OFF=${j(offsetStr || null)},SCANS=${j(scans.map(s => s.text))};
function $(id){return document.getElementById(id);}
function esc(s){return String(s).replace(/[&<>"]/g,function(c){return ({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;'})[c];});}
function ready(){return $('pasteBox')&&typeof captureSnapshot==='function'&&typeof onPaste==='function'&&typeof addObsFromLaunch==='function'&&typeof SHIPS!=='undefined';}
function boot(){if(!ready()){return setTimeout(boot,80);}try{if(OFF){var o=$('localOffset');if(o){o.value=OFF;o.dispatchEvent(new Event('input'));}}for(var i=0;i<SCANS.length;i++){$('pasteBox').value=SCANS[i];$('pasteBox')._parsed=null;onPaste();if($('snapTime')&&$('snapTime').value){captureSnapshot();}}renderImported();}catch(e){console.warn('[AE] solver preload failed',e);}}
function renderImported(){
  var host=$('ae-imported');
  if(!host){host=document.createElement('section');host.id='ae-imported';host.className='panel';var h=document.querySelector('header');if(h){h.insertAdjacentElement('afterend',host);}else{document.body.insertBefore(host,document.body.firstChild);}}
  var have2=(typeof snapshots!=='undefined'&&snapshots.length>=2);
  var gone=(typeof diffCtx!=='undefined'&&diffCtx&&diffCtx.gone)?diffCtx.gone:[];
  if(!have2||!diffCtx||diffCtx.windowSec<=0||!gone.length){
    var msg=!have2?('Only one recorded scan of '+esc(LOC)+' so far \u2014 scout it again later so a second recording can be compared to detect departures.'):((diffCtx&&diffCtx.windowSec<=0)?('Both recorded scans resolve to the same time \u2014 scout '+esc(LOC)+' again later for a fresh recording.'):('No departures detected at '+esc(LOC)+' between the two recordings.'));
    host.innerHTML='<div class="panel-title">Detected departures</div><div class="empty">'+msg+'</div>';return;
  }
  var shipOpts='<option value="" selected disabled>ship\u2026</option>'+SHIPS.map(function(s){return '<option value="'+esc(s.name)+'">'+esc(s.name)+' ('+(s.drive==='warp'?'W':'S')+s.base+')</option>';}).join('');
  var rows=gone.map(function(g,i){return '<div class="imp-row" data-i="'+i+'"><span class="pl">'+esc(g.player)+'</span><span class="sz">'+g.size.toLocaleString()+'</span><select class="imp-ship">'+shipOpts+'</select><span class="lv-lbl">lvl</span><input class="imp-min" type="number" min="1" value="1"><span class="lv-lbl">\u2013</span><input class="imp-max" type="number" min="1" value="16"><button class="mini imp-add">\u2713 add</button><span class="imp-done"></span></div>';}).join('');
  var uncM=Math.max(1,Math.round(diffCtx.windowSec/120));
  host.innerHTML='<div class="panel-title">Detected departures from '+esc(LOC)+' \u2014 assign ship &amp; confirm</div><div class="comp-intro">Departure \u2248 <b>'+fmtHMS(((diffCtx.serverMid%86400)+86400)%86400)+'</b> server \u00b1'+uncM+'m \u00b7 '+gone.length+' fleet(s). Pick each fleet\u2019s slowest ship (W=warp / S=stellar) and drive-level range, then confirm to add it as an observation.</div>'+rows+'<div class="btn-row" style="margin-top:10px"><button class="btn" id="imp-all">Confirm all</button></div>';
  host.querySelectorAll('.imp-row').forEach(function(row){var i=+row.dataset.i,g=gone[i];row.querySelector('.imp-add').onclick=function(){var sel=row.querySelector('.imp-ship');if(!sel.value){sel.style.borderColor='var(--red)';return;}var mn=Math.max(1,parseInt(row.querySelector('.imp-min').value)||1),mx=Math.max(mn,parseInt(row.querySelector('.imp-max').value)||mn);addObsFromLaunch(sel.value,mn,mx,g);row.querySelector('.imp-done').textContent='\u2713 added';row.querySelector('.imp-add').disabled=true;sel.disabled=true;};});
  var all=$('imp-all');if(all){all.onclick=function(){host.querySelectorAll('.imp-row').forEach(function(row){var add=row.querySelector('.imp-add');if(!add.disabled&&row.querySelector('.imp-ship').value){add.click();}});};}
}
if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',boot);else boot();
})();<\/script>`;
    return solverHtml.replace('</body>', style + script + '\n</body>');
  }
  function openSolverTab() {
    const locs = mapLocations();
    let chosen = null, scans = null;
    for (const c of locs) { const s = getSolverScans(c); if (s && s.length) { chosen = c; scans = s; break; } }
    if (!scans || !scans.length) { toast('No scans yet — Save locations and Start the monitor first'); return; }
    const html = buildSolverDoc(chosen, scans, liveOffsetStr());
    let win; try { win = window.open('', 'ae_solver'); } catch { win = null; }
    if (!win) { toast('Allow pop-ups to open the Intercept Solver'); return; }
    win.document.open(); win.document.write(html); win.document.close(); win.focus();
    toast(`Solver opened · ${chosen} · ${scans.length} scan${scans.length > 1 ? 's' : ''}`);
  }

  function buildMovementsHtml(log) {
    const rows = log.map((m, i) => ({
      i, detectedAt: m.detectedAt, location: m.location, direction: m.direction,
      guild: (m.guild && m.guild !== '?') ? m.guild : '?',
      playerName: splitPlayerGuild(m.player).name || m.player,
      size: m.size, windowStart: m.windowStart, windowEnd: m.windowEnd, nowServerClock: m.nowServerClock || '',
    }));
    const data = JSON.stringify(rows).replace(/<\//g, '<\\/');
    return `<!DOCTYPE html><html><head><meta charset="utf-8"><title>AE movements → solver (${escapeHtml(SERVER_NAME)})</title>
<style>
/* Popup runs in its OWN document, so the shadow-root :host tokens cannot reach it.

   any overseer theme override) instead of drifting on hardcoded hexes. */
${AEShared.cssVarsFor(':root')}
  body{background:var(--ae-bg);color:var(--ae-text);font:13px/1.5 var(--ae-font);margin:0;padding:16px;}
  h1{font-size:15px;margin:0 0 4px;} .sub{color:var(--ae-text-muted);font-size:12px;margin-bottom:12px;}
  .bar{display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:8px;align-items:center;margin-bottom:10px;}

  button.primary{background:var(--ae-primary);color:var(--ae-primary-ink);border-color:var(--ae-primary);font-weight:600;}
  button.primary:hover{filter:brightness(1.08);}
  table{border-collapse:collapse;width:100%;font-size:12px;font-variant-numeric:tabular-nums;} th,td{border-bottom:1px solid var(--ae-border-soft);padding:6px 8px;text-align:left;vertical-align:top;}
  th{color:var(--ae-text-dim);font-weight:600;position:sticky;top:0;background:var(--ae-surface);border-bottom-color:var(--ae-border);}
  tr:hover td{background:var(--ae-surface);}
  tr.departed td.dir{color:var(--ae-orange);} tr.arrived td.dir{color:var(--ae-primary);}

  textarea:focus{outline:none;border-color:var(--ae-primary);}
  .empty{color:var(--ae-text-dim);padding:18px;border:1px dashed var(--ae-border);border-radius:var(--ae-radius);text-align:center;}
  .rowbtn{padding:2px 7px;font-size:11px;}
</style></head><body>
<h1>Fleet movements → Intercept Solver</h1>

<script>
const DATA = ${data};
const host = document.getElementById('host');
function fmtTime(s){ try{ return new Date(s).toLocaleString(); }catch(e){ return s||''; } }
function solverLine(m){ return m.location + ' [' + m.guild + '] ' + m.playerName + ' ' + m.size; }
function render(){
  if(!DATA.length){ host.innerHTML='<div class="empty">No movements recorded yet.</div>'; return; }
  let h='<table><thead><tr><th><input type="checkbox" id="hdr"></th><th>Detected</th><th>Dir</th><th>Location</th><th>Player</th><th>Guild</th><th>Size</th><th></th></tr></thead><tbody>';
  for(const m of DATA){
    h+='<tr class="'+m.direction+'"><td><input type="checkbox" class="rc" data-i="'+m.i+'"></td>'
      +'<td>'+fmtTime(m.detectedAt)+'</td><td class="dir">'+m.direction+'</td>'
      +'<td class="mono">'+m.location+'</td><td>'+m.playerName+'</td><td>'+m.guild+'</td>'
      +'<td class="mono">'+Number(m.size).toLocaleString()+'</td>'
      +'<td><button class="rowbtn" data-copy="'+m.i+'">Copy</button></td></tr>';
  }
  host.innerHTML=h+'</tbody></table>';
  document.getElementById('hdr').addEventListener('change',e=>{ document.querySelectorAll('.rc').forEach(c=>c.checked=e.target.checked); updateCount(); });
  document.querySelectorAll('.rc').forEach(c=>c.addEventListener('change',updateCount));
  document.querySelectorAll('[data-copy]').forEach(b=>b.addEventListener('click',()=>{ const m=DATA.find(x=>x.i==b.dataset.copy); copyText(solverLine(m)); }));
}
function selected(){ return Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('.rc')).filter(c=>c.checked).map(c=>DATA.find(x=>x.i==c.dataset.i)); }
function buildLines(list){
  const byLoc={}; list.forEach(m=>{ (byLoc[m.location]=byLoc[m.location]||[]).push(m); });
  const out=[];
  for(const loc of Object.keys(byLoc)){
    out.push('# '+loc+' — '+byLoc[loc].length+' fleet(s)');
    byLoc[loc].forEach(m=>out.push(solverLine(m)));
    out.push('');
  }
  return out.join('\\n').trim();
}
function updateCount(){ const n=selected().length; document.getElementById('count').textContent=n+' selected'; document.getElementById('out').value = n?buildLines(selected()):''; }
async function copyText(t){
  try{ await navigator.clipboard.writeText(t); }catch(e){ const o=document.getElementById('out'); o.value=t; o.select(); try{document.execCommand('copy');}catch(_){} }
  const o=document.getElementById('out'); o.value=t;
}
document.getElementById('all').addEventListener('click',()=>{ document.querySelectorAll('.rc,#hdr').forEach(c=>c.checked=true); updateCount(); });
document.getElementById('none').addEventListener('click',()=>{ document.querySelectorAll('.rc,#hdr').forEach(c=>c.checked=false); updateCount(); });
document.getElementById('copy').addEventListener('click',()=>{ const s=selected(); if(!s.length){ alert('Select at least one movement.'); return; } copyText(buildLines(s)); });
document.getElementById('json').addEventListener('click',()=>{ const s=selected(); if(!s.length){ alert('Select at least one movement.'); return; }
  const blob=new Blob([JSON.stringify(s,null,2)],{type:'application/json'}); const u=URL.createObjectURL(blob);
  const a=document.createElement('a'); a.href=u; a.download='ae-movements-selected.json'; a.click(); URL.revokeObjectURL(u); });
render(); updateCount();
</script></body></html>`;
  }

  function openMovementsTab() {
    const html = buildMovementsHtml(getMapLog());
    let win;
    try { win = window.open('', 'ae_movements'); } catch { win = null; }
    if (!win) { toast('Allow pop-ups to open the movements tab'); return; }
    win.document.open(); win.document.write(html); win.document.close(); win.focus();
  }

  let toastTimer = null;
  function toast(msg) {
    if (!SHADOW) return;
    let el = $ui('#aeplus-toast');
    if (!el) { el = document.createElement('div'); el.id = 'aeplus-toast'; el.className = 'aeplus-toast'; SHADOW.appendChild(el); }
    el.textContent = msg; el.classList.add('show');
    clearTimeout(toastTimer); toastTimer = setTimeout(() => el.classList.remove('show'), 2400);
  }

  // ---- observations overlay: FULL log as a sortable-ish table (own shadow host) ----
  let OBS_HOST = null;
  function closeObservations() { if (OBS_HOST) { OBS_HOST.remove(); OBS_HOST = null; } }
  function openObservations() {
    if (OBS_HOST) return;
    const log = getRepLog();
    OBS_HOST = document.createElement('div');
    OBS_HOST.setAttribute('data-ae-script', 'reports-monitor-observations');
    OBS_HOST.style.cssText = 'position:fixed;inset:0;z-index:2147483200;';
    const sh = OBS_HOST.attachShadow({ mode: 'open' });
    AEShared.adopt(sh);
    const st = document.createElement('style');
    st.textContent = SETTINGS_CSS + `
      .obs-wrap { max-width: 860px; width: 100%; }
      .obs-tbl { width:100%; border-collapse:collapse; font:11px/1.5 var(--ae-font-mono); }
      .obs-tbl th { position:sticky; top:0; background:var(--ae-surface-2); color:var(--ae-text-dim);
        text-align:left; font-weight:600; padding:5px 7px; border-bottom:1px solid var(--ae-border); font-size:10px;
        text-transform:uppercase; letter-spacing:.05em; }
      .obs-tbl td { padding:4px 7px; border-bottom:1px solid var(--ae-border-soft); white-space:nowrap; }
      .obs-tbl tr:hover td { background:var(--ae-surface-2); }
      .obs-scroll { max-height:60vh; overflow:auto; border:1px solid var(--ae-border); border-radius:var(--ae-radius-sm); }
      .obs-scout td { color:var(--ae-secondary); }
      .obs-black td { color:var(--ae-danger); }`;
    sh.appendChild(st);
    const back = document.createElement('div'); back.className = 'ae-modal-back';
    const card = document.createElement('div'); card.className = 'ae-modal obs-wrap';
    const head = document.createElement('div'); head.className = 'ae-modal__bar';
    const title = document.createElement('span'); title.style.flex = '1';
    title.textContent = 'Report observations \u00b7 ' + log.length + ' stored';
    const close = document.createElement('button'); close.className = 'ae-btn'; close.textContent = 'Close';
    close.addEventListener('click', closeObservations);
    head.appendChild(title); head.appendChild(close); card.appendChild(head);
    const bodyEl = document.createElement('div'); bodyEl.className = 'ae-modal__body';
    const scroll = document.createElement('div'); scroll.className = 'obs-scroll';
    const tbl = document.createElement('table'); tbl.className = 'obs-tbl';
    const thead = document.createElement('thead'); const hr = document.createElement('tr');
    ['Seen', 'Player', 'Guild', 'Kind', 'Size', 'Target', 'Arrival'].forEach(h => {
      const th = document.createElement('th'); th.textContent = h; hr.appendChild(th);
    });
    thead.appendChild(hr); tbl.appendChild(thead);
    const tb = document.createElement('tbody');
    for (const r of log) {
      const tr = document.createElement('tr');
      if (r.blacklisted) tr.className = 'obs-black'; else if (r.isScout40) tr.className = 'obs-scout';
      const cells = [
        r.observedAt ? new Date(r.observedAt).toLocaleString() : '\u2014',
        r.player || '(unknown)', r.guild || '\u2014',
        r.blacklisted ? 'BLACKLIST' : (r.isScout40 ? 'SCOUT 40' : 'FLEET'),
        r.size != null ? String(r.size) : '\u2014',
        r.destLoc || r.destText || 'Unknown',
        r.arrivalRaw || r.arrivalEta || 'Unknown'
      ];
      cells.forEach(v => { const td = document.createElement('td'); td.textContent = v; tr.appendChild(td); });
      tb.appendChild(tr);
    }
    tbl.appendChild(tb); scroll.appendChild(tbl); bodyEl.appendChild(scroll); card.appendChild(bodyEl);
    if (!log.length) { const e = document.createElement('div'); e.className = 'ae-muted'; e.style.padding = '10px';
      e.textContent = 'No report observations yet.'; bodyEl.appendChild(e); }
    back.appendChild(card); sh.appendChild(back);
    back.addEventListener('click', (e) => { if (e.target === back) closeObservations(); });
    document.documentElement.appendChild(OBS_HOST);
  }

  // ---- settings overlay (own shadow host; shares this script's storage) ----
  const SETTINGS_CSS = `
    .ae-modal-back { position:absolute; inset:0; background:rgba(3,6,12,.55); display:flex; align-items:flex-start; justify-content:center; padding:40px 16px; overflow:auto; }
    .ae-modal { width:min(420px,100%); }
    .ae-modal .aeplus-section:last-child { margin-bottom:0; }`;
  let SETTINGS_HOST = null, SETTINGS_SHADOW = null;
  const $set = (s) => (SETTINGS_SHADOW ? SETTINGS_SHADOW.querySelector(s) : null);
  function settingsOverlayMarkup() {
    const scopeOptions = Object.entries(SCOPES).map(([v, l]) => `<option value="${v}">${escapeHtml(l)}</option>`).join('');
    return `
      <div class="ae-modal ae-panel">
        <div class="ae-panel__bar"><span class="ae-panel__dot is-ok"></span>
          <span class="ae-panel__titles"><span class="ae-panel__title">Settings</span>
          <span class="ae-panel__sub">${escapeHtml(SERVER_NAME)} · monitor &amp; report</span></span>
          <button class="aeplus-min" id="aeplus-set-close" title="Close">\u00d7</button></div>
        <div class="ae-panel__sweep"></div>
        <div class="ae-panel__body">
          <div class="aeplus-section map">
            <div class="aeplus-legend"><span>Fleet monitor</span></div>
            <div class="aeplus-row"><label>Interval (s)</label><input id="aeplus-map-interval" class="ae-input" type="number" min="15" step="5"/></div>
            <div class="aeplus-row"><label>Min size</label><input id="aeplus-map-minsize" class="ae-input" type="number" min="0" step="1000" placeholder="ignore movements below this"/></div>
            <div class="aeplus-row"><label>Sound</label><select id="aeplus-map-sound" class="ae-select"><option value="on">Beep on large movement</option><option value="off">Muted</option></select></div>
            <div class="aeplus-row"><label>Visible tabs</label><label style="width:auto;display:flex;align-items:center;gap:7px;color:var(--ae-text);"><input type="checkbox" id="aeplus-map-visible"/> Drive one visible scanner tab</label></div>
          </div>
          <div class="aeplus-section">
            <div class="aeplus-legend"><span>Report refresher</span></div>
            <div class="aeplus-row"><label>Galaxy</label><input id="aeplus-galaxy" class="ae-input" placeholder="${SERVER_PREFIX}00..${SERVER_PREFIX}49 (or 09)"/></div>
            <div class="aeplus-row"><label>Interval (s)</label><input id="aeplus-interval" class="ae-input" type="number" min="5" step="5"/></div>
            <div class="aeplus-row"><label>Run for (min, 0=\u221e)</label><input id="aeplus-durmin" class="ae-input" type="number" min="0" step="5"/></div>
            <div class="aeplus-row"><label>Whitelist</label><input id="aeplus-whitelist" class="ae-input" placeholder="friendly tags, e.g. FUSE,ALLY"/></div>
            <div class="aeplus-row"><label>Blacklist</label><input id="aeplus-blacklist" class="ae-input" placeholder="alert tags, e.g. FOE,ENEMY"/></div>
            <div class="aeplus-row"><label>Log scope</label><select id="aeplus-scope" class="ae-select">${scopeOptions}</select></div>
            <div class="aeplus-row"><label>Sound</label><select id="aeplus-rep-sound" class="ae-select"><option value="on">Beep on new blacklist</option><option value="off">Muted</option></select></div>
          </div>
          <div class="aeplus-section">
            <div class="aeplus-legend"><span>Data</span></div>
            <div class="aeplus-actions">
              <button id="aeplus-map-json" class="ae-btn">Movements JSON</button>
              <button id="aeplus-map-csv" class="ae-btn">Movements CSV</button>
              <button id="aeplus-map-clear" class="ae-btn danger">Clear movements</button>
            </div>
            <div class="aeplus-actions" style="margin-top:6px;">
              <button id="aeplus-rep-json" class="ae-btn">Report JSON</button>
              <button id="aeplus-rep-csv" class="ae-btn">Report CSV</button>
              <button id="aeplus-rep-clear" class="ae-btn danger">Clear report log</button>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div class="aeplus-actions">
            <button id="aeplus-set-save" class="ae-btn primary">Save settings</button>
            <button id="aeplus-set-cancel" class="ae-btn">Close</button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>`;
  }
  function populateSettings() {
    $set('#aeplus-map-interval').value = getVal(KEY.map_baseSec, DEFAULTS.map_baseSec);
    $set('#aeplus-map-minsize').value = getVal(KEY.map_minSize, DEFAULTS.map_minSize);
    $set('#aeplus-map-sound').value = getVal(KEY.map_sound, DEFAULTS.map_sound) ? 'on' : 'off';
    $set('#aeplus-map-visible').checked = !!getVal(KEY.map_visible, DEFAULTS.map_visible);
    $set('#aeplus-galaxy').value = getVal(KEY.rep_galaxy, DEFAULTS.rep_galaxy);
    $set('#aeplus-interval').value = getVal(KEY.rep_baseSec, DEFAULTS.rep_baseSec);
    $set('#aeplus-durmin').value = getVal(KEY.rep_durMin, DEFAULTS.rep_durMin);
    $set('#aeplus-whitelist').value = getVal(KEY.rep_whitelist, DEFAULTS.rep_whitelist);
    $set('#aeplus-blacklist').value = getVal(KEY.rep_blacklist, DEFAULTS.rep_blacklist);
    $set('#aeplus-scope').value = getVal(KEY.rep_scope, DEFAULTS.rep_scope);
    $set('#aeplus-rep-sound').value = getVal(KEY.rep_sound, DEFAULTS.rep_sound) ? 'on' : 'off';
  }
  function onSettingsSave() {
    const ms = +($set('#aeplus-map-interval')?.value || '').trim();
    if (!(ms >= 15 && ms <= 3600)) { alert('Monitor interval must be 15–3600 seconds'); return; }
    const g = normalizeGalaxyInput($set('#aeplus-galaxy')?.value || '');
    const rs = +($set('#aeplus-interval')?.value || '').trim();
    if (!isValidServerGalaxy(g)) { alert(`Galaxy must be ${SERVER_PREFIX}00..${SERVER_PREFIX}49`); return; }
    if (!(rs >= 5 && rs <= 3600)) { alert('Report interval must be 5–3600 seconds'); return; }
    const min = parseInt($set('#aeplus-map-minsize')?.value, 10);
    setVal(KEY.map_baseSec, Math.round(ms));
    setVal(KEY.map_minSize, Number.isFinite(min) && min >= 0 ? min : DEFAULTS.map_minSize);
    setVal(KEY.map_sound, ($set('#aeplus-map-sound')?.value || 'on') === 'on');
    setVal(KEY.map_visible, !!$set('#aeplus-map-visible')?.checked);
    const dm = parseInt($set('#aeplus-durmin')?.value, 10);
    setVal(KEY.rep_durMin, Number.isFinite(dm) && dm >= 0 ? dm : 0);
    setVal(KEY.rep_galaxy, g); setVal(KEY.rep_baseSec, Math.round(rs));
    // keep the panel's inline controls in step with a settings-overlay save
    const gs = $ui('#aeplus-rep-galsel'); if (gs) gs.value = g;
    const fs2 = $ui('#aeplus-rep-freq'); if (fs2) fs2.value = Math.round(rs);
    const ds = $ui('#aeplus-rep-dur'); if (ds) ds.value = getVal(KEY.rep_durMin, DEFAULTS.rep_durMin);
    setVal(KEY.rep_whitelist, ($set('#aeplus-whitelist')?.value || '').trim());
    setVal(KEY.rep_blacklist, ($set('#aeplus-blacklist')?.value || '').trim());
    setVal(KEY.rep_scope, $set('#aeplus-scope')?.value || DEFAULTS.rep_scope);
    setVal(KEY.rep_sound, ($set('#aeplus-rep-sound')?.value || 'on') === 'on');
    STATE.scanFallback = false;
    renderMapStatus(); renderReportStatus();
    if (TAB.getRun('map')) scheduleMapNext();
    if (TAB.getRun('report')) { alignReportFilters(); scheduleReportNext(); }
    if (isGalaxyReportsPage()) scanReportPage();
    closeSettings(); toast('Settings saved');
  }
  function wireSettings() {
    $set('#aeplus-set-save').addEventListener('click', onSettingsSave);
    $set('#aeplus-set-cancel').addEventListener('click', closeSettings);
    $set('#aeplus-set-close').addEventListener('click', closeSettings);
    SETTINGS_SHADOW.querySelector('.ae-modal-back').addEventListener('click', (e) => { if (e.target === e.currentTarget) closeSettings(); });
    $set('#aeplus-map-json').addEventListener('click', () => exportJson('map'));
    $set('#aeplus-map-csv').addEventListener('click', () => exportCsv('map'));
    $set('#aeplus-map-clear').addEventListener('click', () => { clearMapLog(); });
    $set('#aeplus-rep-json').addEventListener('click', () => exportJson('rep'));
    $set('#aeplus-rep-csv').addEventListener('click', () => exportCsv('rep'));
    $set('#aeplus-rep-clear').addEventListener('click', () => { clearRepLog(); });
  }
  function openSettings() {
    if (!SETTINGS_HOST) {
      SETTINGS_HOST = document.createElement('div');
      SETTINGS_HOST.setAttribute('data-ae-script', 'reports-monitor-settings');
      SETTINGS_HOST.style.cssText = 'position:fixed;inset:0;z-index:2147483600;';
      SETTINGS_SHADOW = SETTINGS_HOST.attachShadow({ mode: 'open' });
      AEShared.adopt(SETTINGS_SHADOW);
      const st = document.createElement('style'); st.textContent = PANEL_CSS + SETTINGS_CSS; SETTINGS_SHADOW.appendChild(st);
      const back = document.createElement('div'); back.className = 'ae-modal-back'; back.innerHTML = settingsOverlayMarkup();
      SETTINGS_SHADOW.appendChild(back);
      document.documentElement.appendChild(SETTINGS_HOST);
      wireSettings();
    }
    populateSettings();
    SETTINGS_HOST.style.display = 'block';
  }
  function closeSettings() { if (SETTINGS_HOST) SETTINGS_HOST.style.display = 'none'; }

  // Build one themed, tiled shadow host. Used for BOTH panels.
  function mountPanel(scriptId, markup) {
    const host = document.createElement('div');
    host.setAttribute('data-ae-script', scriptId);
    const shadow = host.attachShadow({ mode: 'open' });
    AEShared.adopt(shadow);                                  // theme tokens + base .ae-* CSS
    const st = document.createElement('style'); st.textContent = PANEL_CSS; shadow.appendChild(st);
    const panel = document.createElement('div');
    panel.id = 'aeplus-panel'; panel.className = 'ae-panel';
    panel.innerHTML = markup;
    shadow.appendChild(panel);
    document.documentElement.appendChild(host);
    AEShared.placeHost(host, scriptId, shadow);              // zone slot from AEShared.REGIONS
    return { host, shadow, panel };
  }

  function buildPanel() {
    if (HOST) return;

    // --- panel 1: Intercept Solver (fleet monitor + movements + solver) ---
    const a = mountPanel('intercept-solver', solverPanelMarkup());
    HOST = a.host; SHADOW = a.shadow; PANEL = a.panel;

    // --- panel 2: Report Scanner (galaxy report refresher + detections) ---
    const b = mountPanel('report-scanner', scannerPanelMarkup());
    HOST2 = b.host; SHADOW2 = b.shadow; PANEL2 = b.panel;

    AEShared.startLiveUpdates();                             // watch overseer overrides; re-tile/re-theme both

    // single location — persists on change (no Save button)
    $ui('#aeplus-loc1').value = getVal(KEY.map_loc1, DEFAULTS.map_loc1);
    $ui('#aeplus-loc1').addEventListener('change', () => { saveLoc1(); renderMapStatus(); });
    $ui('#aeplus-map-toggle').addEventListener('click', onMapToggle);
    $ui('#aeplus-map-now').addEventListener('click', onMapNow);
    $ui('#aeplus-map-open').addEventListener('click', openMovementsTab);
    $ui('#aeplus-map-solver').addEventListener('click', openSolverTab);
    $ui('#aeplus-settings').addEventListener('click', openSettings);
    $ui('#aeplus-min').addEventListener('click', toggleCollapsed);

    // Report controls now live in their own panel and exist on EVERY page. Off the
    // galaxy report page they simply idle: refreshing needs that page, so the
    // controls disable themselves and the panel says why, instead of vanishing.
    $ui('#aeplus-rep-toggle').addEventListener('click', onRepToggle);
    $ui('#aeplus-rep-now').addEventListener('click', onRepNow);
    $ui('#aeplus-stop-alert').addEventListener('click', stopBeeping);
    $ui('#aeplus-rep-gear').addEventListener('click', (e) => { e.stopPropagation(); openSettings(); });
    // inline refresher controls: persist immediately and re-arm a running session so
    // a changed interval/duration takes effect without a stop/start round-trip
    const galSel = $ui('#aeplus-rep-galsel');
    if (galSel) {
      // Options come from the page's own <select> when available (authoritative), else
      // the server's full 00-49 range.
      const live = getGalaxySel();
      const opts = [];
      if (live && live.options.length) {
        for (const o of live.options) {
          const v = String(o.value).trim();
          if (!/^\d{1,2}$/.test(v)) continue;
          opts.push({ value: SERVER_PREFIX + String(parseInt(v, 10)).padStart(2, '0'), label: (o.textContent || '').trim() || (SERVER_PREFIX + v) });
        }
      }
      if (!opts.length) for (let i = 0; i <= 49; i++) { const g = SERVER_PREFIX + String(i).padStart(2, '0'); opts.push({ value: g, label: g }); }
      const cur = normalizeGalaxyInput(getVal(KEY.rep_galaxy, DEFAULTS.rep_galaxy));
      for (const o of opts) {
        const el = document.createElement('option');
        el.value = o.value; el.textContent = o.label;
        if (o.value === cur) el.selected = true;
        galSel.appendChild(el);
      }
      galSel.addEventListener('change', () => {
        if (!isValidServerGalaxy(galSel.value)) return;
        setVal(KEY.rep_galaxy, galSel.value);
        // realign the live form and re-arm so the next refresh uses the new galaxy
        if (isGalaxyReportsPage()) alignReportFilters();
        if (TAB.getRun('report')) scheduleReportNext(); else renderReportStatus();
      });
    }
    const freqIn = $ui('#aeplus-rep-freq'), durIn = $ui('#aeplus-rep-dur');
    if (freqIn) {
      freqIn.value = getVal(KEY.rep_baseSec, DEFAULTS.rep_baseSec);
      freqIn.addEventListener('change', () => {
        const v = Math.round(+freqIn.value);
        if (!(v >= 5 && v <= 3600)) { freqIn.value = getVal(KEY.rep_baseSec, DEFAULTS.rep_baseSec); return; }
        setVal(KEY.rep_baseSec, v);
        if (TAB.getRun('report')) scheduleReportNext(); else renderReportStatus();
      });
    }
    if (durIn) {
      durIn.value = getVal(KEY.rep_durMin, DEFAULTS.rep_durMin);
      durIn.addEventListener('change', () => {
        const v = Math.round(+durIn.value);
        if (!(v >= 0 && v <= 1440)) { durIn.value = getVal(KEY.rep_durMin, DEFAULTS.rep_durMin); return; }
        setVal(KEY.rep_durMin, v);
        // reshape the CURRENT session's deadline from its original start
        if (TAB.getRun('report')) { setRepDeadline(v > 0 ? Date.now() + v * 60000 : null); renderReportStatus(); }
      });
    }
    $ui('#aeplus-rep-min').addEventListener('click', toggleScannerCollapsed);

    applyCollapsed();
    applyScannerCollapsed();
    applyReportPageState();
    renderRepToggle(); renderMapToggle();
    renderReportStatus(); renderMapStatus();
    renderRepLog();
    renderMapLog();
  }

  // Enable/disable the scanner's controls depending on whether this page can be
  // refreshed, and say so — the panel stays visible either way.
  function applyReportPageState() {
    const on = isGalaxyReportsPage();
    const now = $ui('#aeplus-rep-now'); if (now) now.disabled = !on;   // Start works anywhere (it navigates)
    const hint = $ui('#aeplus-rep-hint');
    if (hint) hint.textContent = on ? '' : 'Start opens the Galaxy fleet reports page and begins the scan.';
  }

  function toggleScannerCollapsed() { setVal(KEY.rep_collapsed, !getVal(KEY.rep_collapsed, false)); applyScannerCollapsed(); }
  function applyScannerCollapsed() {
    if (!PANEL2) return;
    const c = !!getVal(KEY.rep_collapsed, false);
    PANEL2.classList.toggle('is-collapsed', c);
    const btn = $ui('#aeplus-rep-min');
    if (btn) {
      btn.textContent = c ? '+' : '\u2212';
      btn.title = c ? 'Expand' : 'Collapse';
      btn.setAttribute('aria-expanded', String(!c));
    }
  }

  // read the single location input, validate, persist. Returns false only for a non-empty invalid coord.
  function saveLoc1() {
    const el = $ui('#aeplus-loc1'); if (!el) return true;
    const v = (el.value || '').trim();
    if (v === '') { setVal(KEY.map_loc1, ''); el.classList.remove('bad'); return true; }
    const n = normalizeCoord(v);
    if (n === '') { el.classList.add('bad'); return false; }
    el.classList.remove('bad'); el.value = n; setVal(KEY.map_loc1, n); return true;
  }

  function toggleCollapsed() { setVal(KEY.collapsed, !getVal(KEY.collapsed, DEFAULTS.collapsed)); applyCollapsed(); }
  function applyCollapsed() {
    if (!PANEL) return;
    const c = !!getVal(KEY.collapsed, DEFAULTS.collapsed);
    PANEL.classList.toggle('is-collapsed', c);
    const btn = $ui('#aeplus-min');
    if (btn) { btn.textContent = c ? '+' : '\u2212'; btn.title = c ? 'Expand' : 'Collapse'; btn.setAttribute('aria-expanded', String(!c)); }
  }

  function renderRepToggle() {
    const t = $ui('#aeplus-rep-toggle'); if (!t) return;
    const on = TAB.getRun('report');
    t.textContent = on ? 'Stop refresh' : 'Start refresh';
    t.classList.toggle('is-on', on); t.classList.toggle('is-off', !on);
  }
  function renderMapToggle() {
    const t = $ui('#aeplus-map-toggle'); if (!t) return;
    const on = TAB.getRun('map');
    t.textContent = on ? 'Stop monitor' : 'Start monitor';
    t.classList.toggle('is-on', on); t.classList.toggle('is-off', !on);
  }

  function renderReportStatus() {
    const s = $ui('#aeplus-rep-status');
    if (s) {
      const on = TAB.getRun('report');
      const galaxy = getVal(KEY.rep_galaxy, DEFAULTS.rep_galaxy);
      const baseSec = +getVal(KEY.rep_baseSec, DEFAULTS.rep_baseSec) || DEFAULTS.rep_baseSec;
      let msg = `Status: <b class="${on ? 'aeplus-on' : 'aeplus-off'}">${on ? 'Running' : 'Stopped'}</b>`;
      msg += ` · Galaxy <b>${escapeHtml(galaxy)}</b> · Base <b>${fmt(baseSec)}</b>`;
      if (on && STATE.rep_nextAtMs) msg += ` · Next in <b>${fmt(Math.max(0, Math.round((STATE.rep_nextAtMs - Date.now()) / 1000)))}</b>`;
      const dl = repDeadline();
      if (on && dl) msg += ` · Session ends in <b>${fmt(Math.max(0, Math.round((dl - Date.now()) / 1000)))}</b>`;
      if (!on && STATE.rep_stopReason === 'duration reached') msg += ` · <span class="ae-muted">stopped: duration reached</span>`;
      s.innerHTML = msg;
    }
    const meta = $ui('#aeplus-rep-meta'); if (meta) meta.textContent = `${STATE.rep_lastRowCount} on page`;
    refreshDot();
  }

  function renderMapStatus() {
    const s = $ui('#aeplus-map-status'); if (!s) return;
    const on = TAB.getRun('map');
    const locs = mapLocations();
    let html = `Status: <b class="${on ? 'aeplus-on' : 'aeplus-off'}">${on ? 'Running' : 'Stopped'}</b>`;
    const sp = mapSpacingSec();
    const lo = Math.max(15, sp * 0.7), hi = sp * 1.3 + 1.5;   // the ±30% jitter window
    html += ` · ${locs.length} location${locs.length !== 1 ? 's' : ''} · spacing <b>${fmt(lo)}–${fmt(hi)}</b> <span style="color:var(--ae-muted)">(±30% jitter)</span>`;
    if (on && STATE.map_nextAtMs) html += ` · Next in <b>${fmt(Math.max(0, Math.round((STATE.map_nextAtMs - Date.now()) / 1000)))}</b>`;
    if (locs.length) {
      html += `<div class="aeplus-loc-stat">`;
      for (const c of locs) {
        const snap = getMapSnap(c);
        const lastMs = STATE.map_lastAt[c] || snap?.at;
        const last = lastMs ? fmtClock(lastMs) : '—';
        const n = STATE.map_lastCount[c] != null ? STATE.map_lastCount[c] : (snap?.fleets?.length);
        const cnt = n != null ? `${n} fleets` : '';
        const mv = STATE.map_lastMove[c] ? ` · move @ ${fmtClock(STATE.map_lastMove[c])}` : '';
        const err = STATE.map_lastErr[c] ? ` · <span style="color:var(--ae-amber)">${escapeHtml(STATE.map_lastErr[c])}</span>` : '';
        html += `<div><span class="c">${escapeHtml(c)}</span> — last ${last} ${cnt}${mv}${err}</div>`;
      }
      html += `</div>`;
    }
    s.innerHTML = html;
    const meta = $ui('#aeplus-map-meta'); if (meta) meta.textContent = `min ${mapMinSize().toLocaleString()}`;
    renderTraffic();
    refreshDot();
  }

  // Traffic light for the master tab: red=stopped, amber=running but no recent
  // scan / warning, green=running with a fresh scan. Uses persisted snapshots so
  // it reflects the scanner tab's work in visible mode too.
  function mapTraffic() {
    if (!TAB.getRun('map')) return { state: 'red', label: STATE.loggedOut ? 'Logged out — Stopped · log in, then Start' : 'Stopped' };
    const locs = mapLocations();
    if (!locs.length) return { state: 'amber', label: 'Running · no locations set' };
    if (STATE.scanFallback) return { state: 'amber', label: 'Pop-up blocked · background fetch' };
    const now = Date.now();
    const horizon = Math.max(60000, mapSpacingSec() * 1000 * 1.6);
    const freshest = Math.max(0, ...locs.map(c => (getMapSnap(c)?.at) || STATE.map_lastAt[c] || 0));
    if (freshest && (now - freshest) < horizon) return { state: 'green', label: 'Scanning · healthy' };
    if (freshest) return { state: 'amber', label: 'Running · scan overdue' };
    return { state: 'amber', label: 'Running · waiting for first scan' };
  }
  function renderTraffic() {
    const tl = mapTraffic();
    ['red', 'amber', 'green'].forEach(c => {
      const el = $ui(`#aeplus-map-traffic .aeplus-tl.${c}`); if (el) el.classList.toggle('on', tl.state === c);
    });
    const other = otherMastersRunning();
    const orange = $ui('#aeplus-map-traffic .aeplus-tl.orange'); if (orange) orange.classList.toggle('on', other);
    const lbl = $ui('#aeplus-map-tl-label'); if (lbl) lbl.textContent = tl.label + (other ? ' · ⚠ another tab scanning' : '');
  }

  function refreshDot() { const dot = $ui('#aeplus-dot'); if (dot) dot.classList.toggle('live', TAB.getRun('report') || TAB.getRun('map')); }
  function renderLoginState() {
    const sub = $ui('#aeplus-sub'); if (!sub) return;
    if (!gameUiPresent(document)) { sub.textContent = '⚠ Logged out — script paused on this page'; sub.style.color = 'var(--ae-danger)'; }
    else { sub.textContent = `${SERVER_NAME} · fleet intel`; sub.style.color = ''; }
  }
  function setHeaderDot() {
    const dot = $ui('#aeplus-dot'); if (!dot) return;
    dot.classList.remove('is-ok', 'is-warn', 'is-bad');
    const repOn = TAB.getRun('report'), mapOn = TAB.getRun('map'), tl = mapTraffic();
    let s = '';
    if ((mapOn && tl.state === 'green') || (repOn && gameUiPresent(document))) s = 'is-ok';
    else if (mapOn || repOn) s = (tl.state === 'red' ? 'is-bad' : 'is-warn');
    if (s) dot.classList.add(s);
  }
  function startStatusTicker() { if (STATE.statusTimerId) clearInterval(STATE.statusTimerId); STATE.statusTimerId = setInterval(() => { heartbeatTick(); checkLoggedOutStop(); renderLoginState(); setHeaderDot(); renderReportStatus(); renderMapStatus(); checkMovementBeep(); }, 1000); }


  function logToggleRow(id, open, label) {
    return `<div class="aeplus-actions" style="margin-top:0;"><button id="${id}" class="ae-btn">${open ? 'Hide ' + label : 'Show ' + label}</button></div>`;
  }

  /* Warp Drive level per player from the tech-harvester mirror (harvested, or inferred
     by the scout killer). Read fresh per render so it updates as evidence accumulates.
     Mirror key matches the rest of the suite: the hostname's first label. */
  function warpByPlayer() {
    try {
      const mir = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('ae::techmirror::' + SERVER_ID.split('.')[0]));
      if (!mir || !mir.players) return {};
      const out = {};
      for (const [pid, rec] of Object.entries(mir.players)) {
        const lvl = (rec.warpInferred && rec.warpInferred.level != null) ? rec.warpInferred.level
                  : (rec.techs && rec.techs['Warp Drive']);
        if (lvl != null) out[pid] = { lvl, inferred: !!(rec.warpInferred && rec.warpInferred.level != null) };
      }
      return out;
    } catch (e) { return {}; }
  }
  const hhmm = (iso) => { const d = new Date(iso); return isNaN(d) ? '—'
    : String(d.getHours()).padStart(2, '0') + ':' + String(d.getMinutes()).padStart(2, '0'); };

  function renderRepLog() {
    const box = $ui('#aeplus-rep-log'); if (!box) return;
    const log = getRepLog(), latest = log.slice(0, 3);   // panel always shows the newest 3
    // one compact line each: player (guild+name) | location | HH:MM | warp when known
    const warps = warpByPlayer();
    const cards = latest.map(r => {
      const w = r.playerId && warps[r.playerId];
      const tip = (r.player || '') + ' · ' + (r.destLoc || r.destText || '?')
        + ' · size ' + (r.size != null ? r.size : '?')
        + ' · arrival ' + (r.arrivalRaw || r.arrivalEta || '?');
      return `<div class="aeplus-obs ${r.blacklisted ? 'is-black' : r.isScout40 ? 'is-scout' : ''}" title="${escapeHtml(tip)}">`
        + `<span class="p">${escapeHtml(r.player || '(unknown)')}</span>`
        + `<span class="l">${escapeHtml(r.destLoc || r.destText || '—')}</span>`
        + `<span class="t">${hhmm(r.observedAt)}</span>`
        + (w ? `<span class="w" title="${w.inferred ? 'inferred from sightings' : 'harvested'}">W${w.lvl}${w.inferred ? '~' : ''}</span>` : '')
        + `</div>`;
    }).join('');
    box.innerHTML = `<div class="aeplus-section">
        <div class="aeplus-legend">
          <span>Report observations</span>
          <span class="meta">${log.length} stored${log.length > latest.length ? ` · showing ${latest.length}` : ''}
            <button id="aeplus-rep-logall" class="ae-btn mini" ${log.length ? '' : 'disabled'}>All ↗</button></span>
        </div>
        ${log.length ? `<div class="aeplus-obs-list">${cards}</div>` : `<div class="aeplus-empty">No report observations yet — start the refresher to begin logging.</div>`}
      </div>`;
    const t = $ui('#aeplus-rep-logall'); if (t) t.addEventListener('click', openObservations);
  }

  function renderMapLog() {
    const box = $ui('#aeplus-map-log'); if (!box) return;
    const log = getMapLog(), open = !!getVal(KEY.map_logOpen, DEFAULTS.map_logOpen), latest = log.slice(0, 12);
    const cards = latest.map(r => `
      <div class="aeplus-card ${r.direction === 'departed' ? 'depart' : 'arrive'}">
        <div class="aeplus-card-top"><span class="aeplus-card-player">${escapeHtml(r.player || '(unknown)')}</span>
          <span class="ae-badge ${r.direction === 'departed' ? 'depart' : 'arrive'}">${r.direction === 'departed' ? 'DEPARTED' : 'ARRIVED'} ${escapeHtml(String(r.size))}</span></div>
        <div class="aeplus-grid">
          <div class="k">When</div><div class="v">${escapeHtml(new Date(r.detectedAt).toLocaleString())}</div>
          <div class="k">Location</div><div class="v">${escapeHtml(r.location)}</div>
          <div class="k">Window</div><div class="v">${escapeHtml(r.windowStart ? fmtClock(r.windowStart) : '—')} → ${escapeHtml(fmtClock(r.windowEnd))}</div>
          <div class="k">Svr clock</div><div class="v">${escapeHtml(r.nowServerClock || '—')}</div>
        </div></div>`).join('');
    box.innerHTML = `<div class="aeplus-section map">
        <div class="aeplus-legend"><span>Detected movements</span><span class="meta">${log.length} stored${open && log.length ? ` · latest ${latest.length}` : ''}</span></div>
        ${logToggleRow('aeplus-map-logtoggle', open, 'movements')}
        ${open ? (log.length ? `<div class="aeplus-log-list">${cards}</div>` : `<div class="aeplus-empty">No movements detected yet — add locations and start the monitor.</div>`) : ''}
      </div>`;
    const t = $ui('#aeplus-map-logtoggle'); if (t) t.addEventListener('click', () => { setVal(KEY.map_logOpen, !getVal(KEY.map_logOpen, DEFAULTS.map_logOpen)); renderMapLog(); });
  }

  /* ═══════════════════════════ actions ═════════════════════════════════ */

  // master hide: stop the session and refuse starts while engaged
  window.addEventListener('ae-master-hidden', (e) => {
    if (e.detail && e.detail.hidden && TAB.getRun('report')) stopReportSession('suite hidden');
  });
