                <label><input type="checkbox" id="al-confirm-opt"> Pre-launch confirm window (${CONFIRM_WINDOW_SECONDS}s)</label>
                <label><input type="checkbox" id="al-sound-opt" checked> Audio + visual alert (${ALERT_LEAD_SECONDS}s before)</label>
            </div>
            <div class="al-btns">
                <button class="ae-btn ae-btn--primary" id="al-go">ARM</button>
                <button class="ae-btn" id="al-cancel" disabled>Cancel</button>
            </div>`;
        const $ = (s) => body.querySelector(s);
        const input = $('#al-input'), hint = $('#al-hint'), goBtn = $('#al-go'), cancelBtn = $('#al-cancel'),
              countEl = $('#al-count'), countCap = body.querySelector('.al-count-cap'),
              vNow = $('#al-now'), vDur = $('#al-dur'), vLaunch = $('#al-launch'), vLand = $('#al-land'),
              modeSeg = $('#al-mode'), optConfirm = $('#al-confirm-opt'), optSound = $('#al-sound-opt');
        const panel = shell.panel;

        let mode = 'launch', launchTime = null, landingTime = null,
            armed = false, alerting = false, confirmActive = false, confirmDeadline = 0, fired = false;

        const saved = loadSettings();
        setMode(saved.mode === 'landing' ? 'landing' : 'launch');
        if (saved.lastValue) input.value = saved.lastValue;
        if (typeof saved.optConfirm === 'boolean') optConfirm.checked = saved.optConfirm;
        if (typeof saved.optSound === 'boolean') optSound.checked = saved.optSound;

        function setMode(m) {
            mode = m;
            modeSeg.querySelectorAll('.ae-tab').forEach(b => b.setAttribute('aria-selected', String(b.dataset.mode === m)));
            hint.innerHTML = (m === 'launch')
                ? 'Fleet will <b>launch</b> at this server time.'
                : 'Fleet will <b>land</b> at this server time (launch back-calculated from travel time).';
            countCap.textContent = 'Time to launch';
            saveSettings({ mode: m });
        }
        function setStatus(state, t) { shell.setStatus(state, t); }
        function disarm() {
            armed = false; alerting = false; confirmActive = false; fired = false;
            launchTime = null; landingTime = null;
            input.disabled = false; goBtn.disabled = false; cancelBtn.disabled = true;
            panel.classList.remove('al-flash'); removeConfirmOverlay();
        }
        function arm() {
            const serverNow = getServerTimeNow();
            if (!serverNow) { setStatus('bad', 'no server time'); return; }
            const raw = input.value.trim();
            if (!raw) { setStatus('warn', 'enter a time'); return; }
            const t = parseTimeInput(raw, serverNow);
            if (!t) { setStatus('warn', 'bad time format'); return; }
            if (mode === 'launch') {
                if (t.getTime() <= serverNow.getTime()) { setStatus('warn', 'must be future'); return; }
                launchTime = t; landingTime = null;
            } else {
                const dur = getDurationMs();
                if (dur == null) { setStatus('bad', 'no duration'); return; }
                if (t.getTime() - dur <= serverNow.getTime()) { setStatus('warn', 'launch already past'); return; }
                landingTime = t; launchTime = null;
            }
            armed = true; fired = false;
            input.disabled = true; goBtn.disabled = true; cancelBtn.disabled = false;
            saveSettings({ lastValue: raw });
            beep(0, 1, 0);
            setStatus('ok', 'armed');
        }
        function showConfirmOverlay() {
            if (panel.querySelector('.al-confirm')) return;
            confirmActive = true; confirmDeadline = Date.now() + CONFIRM_WINDOW_SECONDS * 1000;
            const ov = document.createElement('div');
            ov.className = 'al-confirm';
            ov.innerHTML = `<h3>⚠ LAUNCHING SOON</h3><div class="cd" id="al-cd">${CONFIRM_WINDOW_SECONDS}</div>
                <p>Auto-launches when this hits 0.<br>Confirm now or abort.</p>
                <div class="al-btns"><button class="ae-btn ae-btn--primary" id="al-confirm-now">Launch now</button>
                <button class="ae-btn" id="al-confirm-abort">Abort</button></div>`;
            panel.appendChild(ov);
            ov.querySelector('#al-confirm-now').addEventListener('click', () => doFire());
            ov.querySelector('#al-confirm-abort').addEventListener('click', () => { disarm(); setStatus('warn', 'aborted'); });
        }
        function removeConfirmOverlay() { const ov = panel.querySelector('.al-confirm'); if (ov) ov.remove(); confirmActive = false; }
        function doFire() {
            if (fired) return;
            fired = true; setStatus('ok', 'launching…');
            if (!submitMove()) { setStatus('bad', 'submit failed'); fired = false; }
        }
        function tick() {
            const serverNow = getServerTimeNow(), dur = getDurationMs();
            vNow.textContent = serverNow ? formatDateTime(serverNow) : '—';
            vDur.textContent = (dur != null) ? formatDuration(dur) : '—';
            let effLaunch = null, effLand = null;
            if (mode === 'launch' && launchTime) {
                effLaunch = launchTime; if (dur != null) effLand = new Date(launchTime.getTime() + dur);
            } else if (mode === 'landing' && landingTime) {
                effLand = landingTime; if (dur != null) effLaunch = new Date(landingTime.getTime() - dur);
            } else if (serverNow) {
                const t = parseTimeInput(input.value.trim(), serverNow);
                if (t) {
                    if (mode === 'launch') { effLaunch = t; if (dur != null) effLand = new Date(t.getTime() + dur); }
                    else { effLand = t; if (dur != null) effLaunch = new Date(t.getTime() - dur); }
                }
            }
            vLaunch.textContent = effLaunch ? formatDateTime(effLaunch) : '—';
            vLand.textContent = effLand ? formatDateTime(effLand) : '—';
            if (confirmActive) {
                const left = Math.ceil((confirmDeadline - Date.now()) / 1000);
                const cd = panel.querySelector('#al-cd');
                if (cd) cd.textContent = Math.max(0, left);
                if (optSound.checked) beep(660, 90, 0.12);
                if (left <= 0) doFire();
                return;
            }
            if (!serverNow) { setStatus('bad', 'no server time'); return; }
            if (armed && effLaunch) {
                const remaining = effLaunch.getTime() - serverNow.getTime();
                countEl.textContent = formatClock(remaining);
                countEl.classList.remove('warn', 'fire');
                if (remaining <= 0) countEl.classList.add('fire');
                else if (remaining <= ALERT_LEAD_SECONDS * 1000) countEl.classList.add('warn');
                if (optSound.checked && remaining > 0 && remaining <= ALERT_LEAD_SECONDS * 1000) {
                    if (!alerting) { alerting = true; panel.classList.add('al-flash'); }
                    beep(880, 80, 0.12);
                }
                if (remaining <= 0) {
                    panel.classList.remove('al-flash');
                    if (optConfirm.checked && !fired) { showConfirmOverlay(); setStatus('warn', 'confirm window'); }
                    else doFire();
                } else {
                    setStatus('ok', (mode === 'launch' ? 'launch' : 'landing') + ' armed');
                }
            } else {
                countEl.classList.remove('warn', 'fire');
                countEl.textContent = effLaunch ? formatClock(effLaunch.getTime() - serverNow.getTime()) : '--:--:--';
            }
        }
        modeSeg.querySelectorAll('.ae-tab').forEach(b =>
            b.addEventListener('click', () => { if (armed) disarm(); setMode(b.dataset.mode); }));
        goBtn.addEventListener('click', (e) => { e.preventDefault(); arm(); });
        cancelBtn.addEventListener('click', (e) => { e.preventDefault(); disarm(); setStatus('', 'cancelled'); });
        optConfirm.addEventListener('change', () => saveSettings({ optConfirm: optConfirm.checked }));
        optSound.addEventListener('change', () => saveSettings({ optSound: optSound.checked }));
        input.addEventListener('input', () => { if (!armed) saveSettings({ lastValue: input.value.trim() }); });

        if (!input.value) {
            const s = getServerTimeNow();
            if (s) { const p = new Date(s.getTime() + 5 * 60000); input.value = pad(p.getHours()) + ':' + pad(p.getMinutes()) + ':' + pad(p.getSeconds()); }
        }
        setStatus('', 'idle');
        tick();
        setInterval(tick, 1000);
    }

    /* =====================================================================
     * DESTINATIONS + DROP INTEGRATION — logic identical to v2.3
     * ===================================================================== */
    function initBookmarks(shell) {
        const body = shell.body;
        body.innerHTML = `
            <div class="bm-loc">Server: <span class="srv">${SERVER}</span><br>Location: <b id="bm-here">—</b></div>
            <div class="bm-legend">
                <span><i class="bm-ldot" style="background:var(--ae-success)"></i>same galaxy</span>
                <span><i class="bm-ldot" style="background:var(--ae-warn)"></i>same cluster</span>
                <span><i class="bm-ldot" style="background:var(--ae-danger)"></i>other cluster</span>
            </div>
            <button class="ae-btn ae-btn--primary bm-drop safe" id="bm-drop" title="Pick a random SAFE bookmark in the current galaxy and fill the destination">🎲 Random safe drop</button>
            <input class="ae-input bm-filter" id="bm-filter" type="text" placeholder="Filter by name or coord…" />
            <div class="bm-grp"><span>My bookmarks</span><span id="bm-cnt-c"></span></div>
            <div class="bm-list" id="bm-custom"></div>
            <div class="bm-grp"><span>Fast destinations (game)</span><span id="bm-cnt-g"></span></div>
            <div class="bm-list" id="bm-game"></div>
            <div class="bm-add">
                <input class="ae-input" id="bm-name" type="text" placeholder="Bookmark name" />
                <div class="coordrow">
                    <input class="ae-input ae-input--mono" id="bm-coord" type="text" placeholder="A12:34:56:13" />
                    <button class="ae-btn" id="bm-grab" title="Pull from destination field">⤓ field</button>
                </div>
                <label class="safe-line"><input type="checkbox" id="bm-safe"> 🛡 Mark as safe drop point</label>
                <div class="err" id="bm-err"></div>
                <div class="saverow">
                    <button class="ae-btn ae-btn--primary" id="bm-save" style="flex:1">Save bookmark</button>
                    <button class="ae-btn" id="bm-clear" title="Clear">✕</button>
                </div>
            </div>`;
        const $ = (s) => body.querySelector(s);
        const hereEl = $('#bm-here'), filterEl = $('#bm-filter'),
              customList = $('#bm-custom'), gameList = $('#bm-game'),
              cntC = $('#bm-cnt-c'), cntG = $('#bm-cnt-g'),
              nameIn = $('#bm-name'), coordIn = $('#bm-coord'), safeIn = $('#bm-safe'),
              grabBtn = $('#bm-grab'), saveBtn = $('#bm-save'), clearBtn = $('#bm-clear'),
              errEl = $('#bm-err'), dropBtn = $('#bm-drop');

        const gameDest = scrapeFastDestinations();
        let here = getCurrentLocation();
        let editIndex = -1;

        function setBmStatus(msg, kind) {
            // kind: 'ok' | 'warn' | 'bad' | '' -> header dot + short status text
            shell.setStatus(kind || '', msg || '');
        }

        function flashFill(coord, rowEl) {
            if (!isMovePage) { setBmStatus('move page only', 'warn'); return; }
            const ok = setDestination(coord);
            if (rowEl) { rowEl.classList.add('flash'); setTimeout(() => rowEl.classList.remove('flash'), 250); }
            setBmStatus(ok ? ('set ' + coord) : 'set failed', ok ? 'ok' : 'bad');
        }
        function toggleSafe(item, kind, index) {
            const list = loadBookmarks();
            if (kind === 'custom') {
                list[index].safe = !list[index].safe;
            } else {
                const ix = list.findIndex(b => b.coord === item.coord);
                if (ix >= 0) list[ix].safe = !list[ix].safe;
                else list.push({ name: item.name, coord: item.coord, safe: true });
            }
            saveBookmarks(list); render();
        }
        function safeInGalaxy(galaxy) {
            return loadBookmarks().filter(b => {
                if (!b.safe) return false;
                const c = parseCoord(b.coord);
                return c && c.galaxy === galaxy;
            });
        }
        function pickRandomSafe(galaxy) {
            const arr = safeInGalaxy(galaxy);
            return arr.length ? arr[Math.floor(Math.random() * arr.length)] : null;
        }
        function fillSafeDrop(reason) {
            here = getCurrentLocation();
            if (!here) { setBmStatus('no location', 'bad'); return null; }
            const pick = pickRandomSafe(here.galaxy);
            if (!pick) { setBmStatus('no safe in g' + here.galaxy, 'warn'); return null; }
            const ok = setDestination(pick.coord);
            if (!ok) { setBmStatus('set failed', 'bad'); return null; }
            setBmStatus((reason || 'drop') + ': ' + pick.coord, 'ok');
            return pick;
        }
        function maybeAutoFillDrop() {
            if (!isMovePage) return;
            let plan = null;
            try { plan = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(DROP_PLAN_KEY) || 'null'); } catch (e) {}
            if (!plan || plan.ts == null) return;
            const curFleet = url.searchParams.get('fleet');
            if (plan.fleetId && curFleet && String(plan.fleetId) !== String(curFleet)) return;
            const age = Date.now() - plan.ts;
            if (age > DROP_KEEP_MS) return;
            let chosen = null;
            try { chosen = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(DROP_DEST_KEY) || 'null'); } catch (e) {}
            if (chosen && chosen.planTs === plan.ts && chosen.coord) {
                const ok = setDestination(chosen.coord);
                setBmStatus(ok ? ('drop (kept): ' + chosen.coord) : 'set failed', ok ? 'ok' : 'bad');
                return;
            }
            if (age > DROP_FRESH_MS) return;
            const pick = fillSafeDrop('drop move');
            if (pick) {
                try { localStorage.setItem(DROP_DEST_KEY, JSON.stringify({ planTs: plan.ts, coord: pick.coord, name: pick.name })); } catch (e) {}
            }
        }

        function makeRow(item, opts) {
            const cls = galaxyClass(parseCoord(item.coord), here);
            const row = document.createElement('div');
            row.className = 'bm-item g-' + cls + (item.safe ? ' safe' : '');
            row.title = isMovePage ? ('Click to set destination: ' + item.coord) : item.coord;
            const meta = document.createElement('div'); meta.className = 'meta';
            const nm = document.createElement('div'); nm.className = 'nm';
            nm.textContent = item.name;
            if (item.safe) { const b = document.createElement('span'); b.className = 'badge'; b.textContent = '🛡'; nm.appendChild(b); }
            const co = document.createElement('div'); co.className = 'co'; co.textContent = item.coord;
            meta.appendChild(nm); meta.appendChild(co);
            row.appendChild(meta);
            row.addEventListener('click', () => flashFill(item.coord, row));

            const act = document.createElement('div'); act.className = 'act';
            const safeBtn = document.createElement('button');
            safeBtn.textContent = '🛡';
            safeBtn.title = item.safe ? 'Safe drop point (click to unset)' : 'Mark as safe drop point';
            if (item.safe) safeBtn.classList.add('safe-on');
            safeBtn.addEventListener('click', (e) => { e.stopPropagation(); toggleSafe(item, opts.kind, opts.index); });
            act.appendChild(safeBtn);

            if (opts.kind === 'custom') {
                const ed = document.createElement('button'); ed.textContent = '✎'; ed.title = 'Edit';
                ed.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
                    e.stopPropagation();
                    nameIn.value = item.name; coordIn.value = item.coord; safeIn.checked = !!item.safe;
                    editIndex = opts.index; saveBtn.textContent = 'Update'; errEl.textContent = ''; nameIn.focus();
                });
                const del = document.createElement('button'); del.textContent = '🗑'; del.title = 'Delete';
                del.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
                    e.stopPropagation();
                    const list = loadBookmarks(); list.splice(opts.index, 1); saveBookmarks(list); render();
                });
                act.appendChild(ed); act.appendChild(del);
            }
            row.appendChild(act);
            return row;
        }

        const rankOf = (coord) => GAL_RANK[galaxyClass(parseCoord(coord), here)];

        function render() {
            here = getCurrentLocation();
            if (here) hereEl.textContent = here.raw + '  (galaxy ' + here.galaxy + ', cluster ' + here.cluster + '0s)';
            else hereEl.textContent = isMovePage ? 'unknown' : 'n/a (open a fleet Move page)';

            if (!isMovePage) {
                dropBtn.disabled = true; dropBtn.textContent = '🎲 Safe drop (Move page only)';
            } else if (here) {
                const n = safeInGalaxy(here.galaxy).length;
                dropBtn.disabled = n === 0;
                dropBtn.textContent = n ? ('🎲 Random safe drop (g' + here.galaxy + ' · ' + n + ')') : ('🎲 No safe drops in g' + here.galaxy);
            } else {
                dropBtn.disabled = true; dropBtn.textContent = '🎲 Random safe drop (location unknown)';
            }

            const f = (filterEl.value || '').trim().toLowerCase();
            const match = (it) => !f || it.name.toLowerCase().includes(f) || it.coord.toLowerCase().includes(f);

            const custom = loadBookmarks();
            const customCoords = new Set(custom.map(b => b.coord));
            customList.innerHTML = '';
            const shownCustom = custom.map((it, i) => ({ it, i })).filter(x => match(x.it))
                .sort((a, b) => (rankOf(a.it.coord) - rankOf(b.it.coord)) || (a.i - b.i));
            if (!custom.length) customList.innerHTML = '<div class="bm-empty">No bookmarks yet for this server.</div>';
            else if (!shownCustom.length) customList.innerHTML = '<div class="bm-empty">No matches.</div>';
            else shownCustom.forEach(x => customList.appendChild(makeRow(x.it, { kind: 'custom', index: x.i })));
            cntC.textContent = custom.length ? custom.length : '';

            const gameUnique = gameDest.filter(it => !customCoords.has(it.coord));
            gameList.innerHTML = '';
            const shownGame = gameUnique.map((it, i) => ({ it, i })).filter(x => match(x.it))
                .sort((a, b) => (rankOf(a.it.coord) - rankOf(b.it.coord)) || (a.i - b.i));
            if (!gameUnique.length) gameList.innerHTML = '<div class="bm-empty">None detected on this page.</div>';
            else if (!shownGame.length) gameList.innerHTML = '<div class="bm-empty">No matches.</div>';
            else shownGame.forEach(x => gameList.appendChild(makeRow(x.it, { kind: 'game' })));
            cntG.textContent = gameUnique.length ? gameUnique.length : '';
        }

        function resetForm() {
            nameIn.value = ''; coordIn.value = ''; safeIn.checked = false; errEl.textContent = '';
            editIndex = -1; saveBtn.textContent = 'Save bookmark';
        }
        function saveCurrent() {
            const name = nameIn.value.trim(), coord = coordIn.value.trim();
            if (!coord) { errEl.textContent = 'Enter a coordinate.'; return; }
            if (!isValidCoordString(coord)) { errEl.textContent = 'Bad coord. Use e.g. A12:34:56:13'; return; }
            const list = loadBookmarks();
            const entry = { name: name || coord, coord, safe: !!safeIn.checked };
            if (editIndex >= 0 && editIndex < list.length) list[editIndex] = entry;
            else list.push(entry);
            saveBookmarks(list); resetForm(); render();
        }

        grabBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
            const dest = document.getElementById('destination');
            if (dest && dest.value.trim()) { coordIn.value = dest.value.trim(); errEl.textContent = ''; }
            else errEl.textContent = isMovePage ? 'Destination field is empty.' : 'No destination field on this page.';
        });
        saveBtn.addEventListener('click', saveCurrent);
        clearBtn.addEventListener('click', resetForm);
        coordIn.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => { if (e.key === 'Enter') saveCurrent(); });
        nameIn.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => { if (e.key === 'Enter') coordIn.focus(); });
        filterEl.addEventListener('input', render);
        dropBtn.addEventListener('click', () => fillSafeDrop('random safe'));

        setBmStatus(isMovePage ? 'ready' : 'manage', '');
        render();
        if (isMovePage) {
            maybeAutoFillDrop();
            setTimeout(() => { render(); maybeAutoFillDrop(); }, 1500);
            setTimeout(() => { render(); maybeAutoFillDrop(); }, 4000);
        } else {
            setTimeout(render, 1500);
        }
    }

    /* =====================================================================
     * INIT — single shadow-root host, BR slot, Destinations stacked over Auto-Launch
     * ===================================================================== */
    function init() {
        const SCRIPT_ID = 'bookmarks2';  // BL slot. Renamed from 'bookmarks' to avoid
                                         // scriptId collision with the separate Quick Bookmarks script.
        const host = document.createElement('div');
        host.setAttribute('data-ae-script', SCRIPT_ID);
        const shadow = host.attachShadow({ mode: 'open' });

        // Theme (adopted into THIS shadow root) + script-local component CSS.
        AEShared.adopt(shadow);
        const localStyle = document.createElement('style');
        localStyle.setAttribute('data-ae-local', '1');
        localStyle.textContent = LOCAL_CSS;
        shadow.appendChild(localStyle);

        const stack = document.createElement('div');
        stack.className = 'ae-stack';
        shadow.appendChild(stack);

        // Destinations on top (all fleet pages)…
        const bmWin = loadBmWin();
        const destShell = buildShell('Destinations', 'per-server bookmarks', {
            // v2.6: use a v2.5+ key so a legacy `minimized:true` from the old
            // floating-window versions doesn't auto-collapse the panel on load.
            startMinimized: !!bmWin.minimizedV25,
            onMin: (hidden) => saveBmWin({ minimizedV25: hidden })
        });
        stack.appendChild(destShell.panel);

        // …Auto-Launch stacked beneath (move page only).
        let alShell = null;
        if (isMovePage) {
            const alSaved = loadSettings();
            alShell = buildShell('Fleet Auto-Launch', 'launch + landing scheduler', {
                startMinimized: !!alSaved.minimizedV25,
                onMin: (hidden) => saveSettings({ minimizedV25: hidden })
            });
            stack.appendChild(alShell.panel);
        }

        document.documentElement.appendChild(host);
        AEShared.placeHost(host, SCRIPT_ID, shadow);
        AEShared.startLiveUpdates();

        // BUG FIX (v2.5): the two stacked panels can be taller than the viewport.
        // A corner-anchored fixed host has no height bound, so the far end (the
        // Auto-Launch panel) could overflow off-screen and look "disappeared".
        // Cap the host to the viewport and give it a single internal scroll so
        // both panels stay reachable regardless of the anchor the overseer sets.
        // placeHost never sets maxHeight/overflow, so these survive re-tiling.
        host.style.maxHeight = 'calc(100vh - 16px)';
        host.style.overflowY = 'auto';
        host.style.overflowX = 'hidden';

        // v2.6: isolate each panel's init so an error in one can't blank the other.
        try { initBookmarks(destShell); }
        catch (e) { console.error('[AE bookmarks] init failed', e); destShell.setStatus('bad', 'init error'); }
        if (alShell) {
            try { initAutoLaunch(alShell); }
            catch (e) { console.error('[AE auto-launch] init failed', e); alShell.setStatus('bad', 'init error'); }
        }
    }

    init();
})();

} });


/* ============ MODULE: bookmarks ============ */
AESuite.register({ id: "bookmarks", title: "Quick Bookmarks", pages: null, init: function () {
/* ========================================================================
 * EMBEDDED: ae_shared.js v0.3.1 (verbatim, from project canonical).
 * NOTE: the module's own header comment still reads 0.3.0, but const
 * VERSION = '0.3.1'. v0.3.1 adds data-ae-script tagging inside placeHost().
 * ===================================================================== */



/* ========================================================================
 * Quick Bookmarks — this script
 * ===================================================================== */
(function () {
  'use strict';

  /* ---------- single-injection guard ---------- */
  if (window.__aeQuickBookmarksLoaded) return;
  window.__aeQuickBookmarksLoaded = true;

  /* ---------- environment ---------- */
  // Server = subdomain, e.g. "ceres" from ceres.astroempires.com. Bookmarks are namespaced by this.
  const SERVER   = (location.hostname.split('.')[0] || 'unknown').toLowerCase();
  const KEY_DATA = `ae_bm::${SERVER}`; // per-server bookmark list — UNCHANGED from v1
  const KEY_UI   = 'ae_bm::ui';        // collapsed flag only (position/open dropped under tiling)
  const COORD_RE = /^[A-Za-z]\d{2}:\d{2}:\d{2}:\d{2,}$/; // e.g. A09:45:49:30 (moons extend last group)
  const ORIGIN   = (srv) => `https://${srv}.astroempires.com/`;

  /* ---------- storage seam ----------
     The whole UI talks to Store, never to GM_* directly. If you later want
     cross-device sync, reimplement just these methods against chrome.storage
     or a backend — that is the point where you'd move to an extension. */
  /* Store now routes through the shared location catalog (AEData). Legacy key
     KEY_DATA is migrated once and then kept as a read-only backup — not deleted.
     Panel/UX unchanged: this view returns ONLY bookmark-sourced records. */
  const Store = {
    load()     { try { return AEData.viewBookmarks(); } catch (e) { console.warn('[AE BM] AEData read failed', e); return []; } },
    save(list) { try { AEData.saveBookmarks(list); } catch (e) { console.warn('[AE BM] AEData write failed', e); } },
    loadUI()   { try { return JSON.parse(GM_getValue(KEY_UI, '{}')); } catch { return {}; } },
    saveUI(o)  { GM_setValue(KEY_UI, JSON.stringify(o)); },
  };

  /* ---------- state ---------- */
  let bookmarks = Store.load();
  let editingId = null;
  let collapsed = !!Store.loadUI().collapsed;

  const uid = () =>
    (crypto.randomUUID ? crypto.randomUUID()
      : 'b' + Date.now().toString(36) + Math.random().toString(36).slice(2));

  /* ---------- record factory ----------
     kind 'coord' : navigates to map.aspx?loc=coord  (coord is the nav target)
     kind 'base'  : navigates to base.aspx?base=baseId (coord, if set, is just a label)
     kind 'link'  : navigates to a captured path      (coord, if set, is just a label) */
  function mk(kind, fields) {
    return Object.assign(
      { id: uid(), kind, coord: null, baseId: null, path: '', name: '', note: '', addedAt: Date.now() },
      fields
    );
  }

  /* ---------- drop parsing (UNCHANGED, validated logic) ---------- */
  function firstUriLine(text) {
    // text/uri-list can hold comment lines (#...) and multiple URLs
    return text.split(/\r?\n/).map(s => s.trim()).find(s => s && !s.startsWith('#')) || '';
  }
  function hrefFromHtml(html) {
    try {
      const doc = new DOMParser().parseFromString(html, 'text/html');
      const a = doc.querySelector('a[href]');
      return a ? a.getAttribute('href') : '';
    } catch { return ''; }
  }
  // Returns a partial bookmark from a drop, or null if nothing usable was dropped.
  function bookmarkFromDrop(dt) {
    const raw =
      firstUriLine(dt.getData('text/uri-list') || '') ||
      (dt.getData('text/plain') || '').trim() ||
      hrefFromHtml(dt.getData('text/html') || '');

    if (!raw) return null;

    // Plain coordinate text (e.g. dragging a selected "A09:45:49:30")
    if (COORD_RE.test(raw)) {
      const coord = raw.toUpperCase();
      return mk('coord', { coord, path: `map.aspx?loc=${coord}` });
    }

    let url;
    try { url = new URL(raw, location.href); } catch { return null; }

    const file   = url.pathname.split('/').pop().toLowerCase();
    const params = url.searchParams;

    if (file === 'map.aspx' && params.get('loc')) {
      const coord = params.get('loc');
      return mk('coord', { coord, path: `map.aspx?loc=${coord}` });
    }
    if (file === 'base.aspx' && params.get('base')) {
      const baseId = params.get('base');
      return mk('base', { baseId, path: `base.aspx?base=${baseId}` });
    }
    if (url.hostname.endsWith('astroempires.com')) {
      // any other in-site link: keep it server-relative so it rebuilds per server
      return mk('link', { path: url.pathname.replace(/^\//, '') + url.search });
    }
    // off-site URL: store absolute (won't be server-rebuilt)
    return mk('link', { path: url.href });
  }

  /* ---------- derived display / navigation (UNCHANGED) ---------- */
  function hrefFor(bm) {
    if (!bm.path) return null;
    if (/^https?:\/\//i.test(bm.path)) return bm.path;   // off-site absolute
    return ORIGIN(SERVER) + bm.path;                      // server-relative -> current server
  }
  function labelFor(bm) {
    if (bm.coord) return bm.coord;
    if (bm.kind === 'base' && bm.baseId) return `Base ${bm.baseId}`;
    if (bm.path) return bm.path.replace(/\?.*$/, '');
    return '(no target)';
  }

  /* ---------- tiny DOM helpers ---------- */
  function el(tag, props = {}, child) {
    const n = document.createElement(tag);
    for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(props)) {
      if (k === 'class') n.className = v;
      else if (k === 'value') n.value = v;
      else n.setAttribute(k, v);
    }
    if (child != null) n.appendChild(document.createTextNode(String(child)));
    return n;
  }
  function labeled(text, input) {
    const w = el('label', { class: 'bm-field' });
    w.appendChild(el('span', { class: 'bm-flabel' }, text));
    w.appendChild(input);
    return w;
  }

  /* ---------- panel shell via AEShared (themed + tiled to bottom-right) ---------- */
  const ui = AEShared.createPanel({
    scriptId: 'bookmarks',
    title: 'Quick Bookmarks',
    subtitle: SERVER
  });

  // Script-specific structure. Coloured ONLY via --ae-* tokens; injected into the
  // same shadow root as the theme, so nothing leaks into the page.
  const style = document.createElement('style');
  style.setAttribute('data-bm-style', '1');
  style.textContent = BM_CSS();
  ui.shadow.appendChild(style);

  // Collapse-to-header toggle. Position-neutral (hides only the body in place),
  // so it is compatible with fixed-slot tiling. Replaces the old launcher/hide/drag.
  const collapseBtn = AEShared.makeToggle({ collapsed: false });  // shared +/- control
  ui.bar.appendChild(collapseBtn);
  function applyCollapse() {
    ui.body.style.display = collapsed ? 'none' : '';
    collapseBtn.set(collapsed);
    collapseBtn.title = collapsed ? 'Expand' : 'Collapse';
  }
  collapseBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
    collapsed = !collapsed; Store.saveUI({ collapsed }); applyCollapse();
  });

  /* ---------- body content ---------- */
  ui.body.innerHTML = `
    <div class="bm-drop" data-bm="drop">Drag a map or base link here</div>
    <div class="bm-list" data-bm="list"></div>
    <div class="bm-foot">
      <button class="ae-btn ae-btn--ghost" data-bm="add">+ Add manually</button>
      <span class="bm-spacer"></span>
      <button class="ae-btn ae-btn--ghost" data-bm="export" title="Copy all bookmarks as JSON">Export</button>
      <button class="ae-btn ae-btn--ghost" data-bm="import" title="Replace list from pasted JSON">Import</button>
    </div>`;
  const listEl   = ui.body.querySelector('[data-bm="list"]');
  const dropzone = ui.body.querySelector('[data-bm="drop"]');

  /* ---------- drag-and-drop capture (ui.body is the target) ---------- */
  ['dragenter', 'dragover'].forEach(ev =>
    ui.body.addEventListener(ev, (e) => {
      e.preventDefault();                 // REQUIRED, or 'drop' never fires
      e.dataTransfer.dropEffect = 'copy';
      dropzone.classList.add('is-drag');
    })
  );
  ui.body.addEventListener('dragleave', (e) => {
    if (!ui.body.contains(e.relatedTarget)) dropzone.classList.remove('is-drag');
  });
  ui.body.addEventListener('drop', (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    dropzone.classList.remove('is-drag');
    const bm = bookmarkFromDrop(e.dataTransfer);
    if (!bm) { flash(dropzone, "Couldn't read a link there"); return; }
    bookmarks.unshift(bm);
    Store.save(bookmarks);
    editingId = bm.id;                     // open editor so the name/note can be filled in
    render();
  });

  /* ---------- footer actions ---------- */
  ui.body.querySelector('[data-bm="add"]').addEventListener('click', () => {
    const bm = mk('coord', {});            // manual entries default to a coordinate bookmark
    bookmarks.unshift(bm);
    Store.save(bookmarks);
    editingId = bm.id;
    render();
  });
  ui.body.querySelector('[data-bm="export"]').addEventListener('click', async () => {
    const json = JSON.stringify(bookmarks, null, 2);
    try { await navigator.clipboard.writeText(json); flash(dropzone, 'Copied JSON to clipboard'); }
    catch { window.prompt('Copy your bookmarks JSON:', json); }
  });
  ui.body.querySelector('[data-bm="import"]').addEventListener('click', () => {
    const text = window.prompt('Paste bookmarks JSON to import (replaces the current list):', '');
    if (text == null) return;
    try {
      const arr = JSON.parse(text);
      if (!Array.isArray(arr)) throw new Error('not an array');
      bookmarks = arr; Store.save(bookmarks); render(); flash(dropzone, 'Imported');
    } catch { flash(dropzone, 'Invalid JSON'); }
  });

  /* ---------- rendering ---------- */
  function refreshStatus() {
    const n = bookmarks.length;
    ui.setStatus(n ? 'ok' : 'idle', n ? `${n} saved` : 'empty');
  }
  function render() {
    listEl.textContent = '';
    if (!bookmarks.length) {
      listEl.appendChild(el('div', { class: 'bm-empty' }, 'No bookmarks yet. Drag a link in, or "Add manually".'));
    } else {
      for (const bm of bookmarks) {
        listEl.appendChild(bm.id === editingId ? rowEditor(bm) : rowView(bm));
      }
    }
    refreshStatus();
  }

  function rowView(bm) {
    const row = el('div', { class: 'bm-row' });
    row.appendChild(el('span', { class: bm.coord ? 'bm-badge' : 'bm-badge is-other' }, labelFor(bm)));

    const main = el('div', { class: 'bm-main' });
    const href = hrefFor(bm);
    const nameText = bm.name || labelFor(bm);
    if (href) main.appendChild(el('a', { class: 'bm-name', href, title: href }, nameText));
    else      main.appendChild(el('span', { class: 'bm-name is-plain' }, nameText));
    if (bm.note) main.appendChild(el('div', { class: 'bm-note' }, bm.note));
    if (bm.kind === 'base' && bm.baseId && bm.coord) {
      main.appendChild(el('div', { class: 'bm-sub' }, `Base ${bm.baseId}`));
    }
    row.appendChild(main);

    const actions = el('div', { class: 'bm-actions' });
    const edit = el('button', { class: 'ae-btn ae-btn--ghost bm-iconbtn', title: 'Edit' }, '\u270E');
    edit.addEventListener('click', () => { editingId = bm.id; render(); });
    const del = el('button', { class: 'ae-btn ae-btn--ghost bm-iconbtn is-danger', title: 'Delete' }, '\u2715');
    del.addEventListener('click', () => {
      if (window.confirm(`Delete "${bm.name || labelFor(bm)}"?`)) {
        bookmarks = bookmarks.filter(b => b.id !== bm.id);
        Store.save(bookmarks); render();
      }
    });
    actions.appendChild(edit); actions.appendChild(del);
    row.appendChild(actions);
    return row;
  }

  function rowEditor(bm) {
    const row  = el('div', { class: 'bm-row is-editing' });
    const grid = el('div', { class: 'bm-editgrid' });

    const coordIn = el('input',    { class: 'ae-input ae-input--mono', type: 'text', placeholder: 'A09:45:49:30', value: bm.coord || '' });
    const nameIn  = el('input',    { class: 'ae-input', type: 'text', placeholder: 'Name', value: bm.name || '' });
    const noteIn  = el('textarea', { class: 'ae-input bm-textarea', placeholder: 'Note', rows: '2' });
    noteIn.value = bm.note || '';

    grid.appendChild(labeled('Coord', coordIn));
    grid.appendChild(labeled('Name',  nameIn));
    grid.appendChild(labeled('Note',  noteIn));

    if (bm.kind === 'base' && bm.baseId) {
      grid.appendChild(el('div', { class: 'bm-hint' }, `Links to base #${bm.baseId}. The coordinate here is just a label.`));
    } else if (bm.kind === 'link' && bm.path) {
      grid.appendChild(el('div', { class: 'bm-hint' }, `Links to ${bm.path}`));
    } else {
      grid.appendChild(el('div', { class: 'bm-hint' }, 'Links to the map at the coordinate above.'));
    }
    row.appendChild(grid);

    const actions = el('div', { class: 'bm-actions is-col' });
    const save    = el('button', { class: 'ae-btn ae-btn--primary' }, 'Save');
    const cancel  = el('button', { class: 'ae-btn ae-btn--ghost' }, 'Cancel');

    save.addEventListener('click', () => {
      const coord = coordIn.value.trim().toUpperCase();
      bm.name  = nameIn.value.trim();
      bm.note  = noteIn.value.trim();
      bm.coord = coord || null;
      if (bm.kind === 'coord') bm.path = coord ? `map.aspx?loc=${coord}` : '';
      if (coord && !COORD_RE.test(coord)) flash(dropzone, 'Saved (coord format looks unusual)');
      editingId = null; Store.save(bookmarks); render();
    });
    cancel.addEventListener('click', () => {
      // discard a brand-new empty manual entry on cancel; keep anything with content/target
      if (bm.kind === 'coord' && !bm.path && !bm.name && !bm.note && !bm.coord) {
        bookmarks = bookmarks.filter(b => b.id !== bm.id);
      }
      editingId = null; Store.save(bookmarks); render();
    });
    actions.appendChild(save); actions.appendChild(cancel);

    [coordIn, nameIn].forEach(inp => inp.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => {
      if (e.key === 'Enter')  { e.preventDefault(); save.click(); }
      if (e.key === 'Escape') { e.preventDefault(); cancel.click(); }
    }));

    row.appendChild(actions);
    setTimeout(() => nameIn.focus(), 0);
    return row;
  }

  /* ---------- transient message in the dropzone ---------- */
  let flashTimer = null;
  function flash(node, msg) {
    if (node.dataset.base == null) node.dataset.base = node.textContent;
    node.textContent = msg;
    node.classList.add('is-flash');
    clearTimeout(flashTimer);
    flashTimer = setTimeout(() => {
      node.textContent = node.dataset.base;
      node.classList.remove('is-flash');
    }, 1600);
  }

  /* ---------- script-specific CSS (only --ae-* tokens, no hardcoded colours) ---------- */
  function BM_CSS() {
    return `
      .bm-drop {
        margin-bottom: var(--ae-gap);
        padding: 12px; text-align: center; font-size: 11px;
        font-family: var(--ae-font-mono); color: var(--ae-text-dim);
        border: 1px dashed var(--ae-border); border-radius: var(--ae-radius-sm);
        transition: border-color .12s ease, color .12s ease;
      }
      .bm-drop.is-drag  { border-color: var(--ae-primary); color: var(--ae-text); }
      .bm-drop.is-flash { border-color: var(--ae-primary); color: var(--ae-primary); }

      .bm-list { display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: var(--ae-gap); }
      .bm-empty { color: var(--ae-text-dim); font-size: 11px; text-align: center; padding: 12px 4px; }

      .bm-row {
        display: flex; gap: 8px; align-items: flex-start;
        background: var(--ae-surface-2); border: 1px solid var(--ae-border-soft);
        border-radius: var(--ae-radius-sm); padding: 8px;
      }
      .bm-row.is-editing { flex-direction: column; }

      .bm-badge {
        display: inline-block; flex-shrink: 0; white-space: nowrap;
        font-family: var(--ae-font-mono); font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums;
        font-size: 10px; padding: 2px 6px; border-radius: 999px;
        border: 1px solid var(--ae-primary); color: var(--ae-primary);
      }
      .bm-badge.is-other { border-color: var(--ae-border); color: var(--ae-text-muted); }

      .bm-main { flex: 1; min-width: 0; }
      .bm-name { color: var(--ae-primary); text-decoration: none; font-weight: 600; word-break: break-word; }
      .bm-name:hover { text-decoration: underline; }
      .bm-name.is-plain { color: var(--ae-text); }
      .bm-note { color: var(--ae-text-muted); font-size: 11px; margin-top: 2px; white-space: pre-wrap; word-break: break-word; }
      .bm-sub  { color: var(--ae-text-dim); font-size: 10px; margin-top: 2px; font-family: var(--ae-font-mono); }

      .bm-actions { display: flex; gap: 4px; flex-shrink: 0; }
      .bm-actions.is-col { width: 100%; justify-content: flex-end; margin-top: 8px; }
      .bm-iconbtn { padding: 3px 7px; }
      .bm-iconbtn.is-danger:hover { border-color: var(--ae-danger); color: var(--ae-danger); }

      .bm-editgrid { display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 8px; width: 100%; }
      .bm-field { display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 3px; }
      .bm-flabel { font-size: 10px; text-transform: uppercase; letter-spacing: .5px; color: var(--ae-text-dim); }
      .bm-hint { font-size: 10px; color: var(--ae-text-dim); }
      .bm-textarea { resize: vertical; font-family: var(--ae-font); min-height: 38px; }

      .bm-foot { display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 6px; margin-top: 10px; padding-top: 8px; border-top: 1px solid var(--ae-border-soft); }
      .bm-spacer { flex: 1; }
      .bm-collapse { padding: 1px 6px; font-size: 11px; line-height: 1; border-color: transparent; }
      .bm-collapse:hover { border-color: var(--ae-primary); }
    `;
  }

  /* ---------- go ---------- */
  applyCollapse();
  render();
})();

} });


/* ============ MODULE: prebattle-predictor ============ */
AESuite.register({ id: "prebattle-predictor", title: "Pre-Battle Predictor", pages: null, init: function () {
/* =============================================================================
 * EMBEDDED SHARED MODULE - ae_shared.js (canonical v0.3.1)
 * Pasted verbatim per the retrofit standard, with ONE addition vs. the version
 * shipped before this script: a REGIONS entry for 'prebattle-predictor'
 * (bottom-right, width 260). That entry must also live in the canonical
 * ae_shared.js and in the AE Layout Overseer's embedded copy so the overseer
 * can detect and tile this panel.
 * ========================================================================== */


(function () {
  'use strict';

  const SCRIPT_ID = 'prebattle-predictor';

  // ----------------------------------------------------------------- CONFIG
  const CFG = {
    FACTOR: 0.848,        // damage-distribution exponent (calibrated to aebits / live V5)
    // Predicted numbers are injected into the GAME's light DOM, where shadow
    // tokens don't reach — resolve from the canonical palette so it follows
    // the shared theme (and any overseer override) instead of drifting.
    COLOR: (typeof AEShared !== 'undefined' && AEShared.token('--ae-secondary')) || '#d40000',
    AUTO_RUN: true,       // auto-calculate when a pre-battle report is detected
    DEBUG: true,          // verbose console logging (open DevTools console to read)
    DECIMALS: 1,          // round predicted survivors to this many decimals
    DEBRIS_RATE: 0.40,    // fraction of destroyed (fleet) value that becomes debris
    DEBRIS_SPLIT: 0.50,   // attacker's share of the debris field (your loot)
    PILLAGE: 0,           // extra credits pillaged from the base (not in the report -> set if known)
    SHOW_PANEL: true,     // show the profit / ratio summary box
    MOVEMENT_URL: '',     // fleet-movement page URL (set once known -> button navigates there)
    DROP_BUFFER: 0,       // extra drop units added on top of the calculated minimum (safety margin)
  };
  const log = (...a) => { if (CFG.DEBUG) console.log('%c[AE-Predict]', 'color:#d40000;font-weight:bold', ...a); };
  // v1.4 — predictor + profit + drop + finishing-wave + optimal-finisher + movement auto-fill (drop & finisher)

  // ------------------------------------------------- unit table (index order)
  // Indices 0-19 fleet, 20-29 defences. Matches engine.py MyConstants order.
  const UNIT_LABEL = ['Fighter','Bomber','Heavy Bomber','Ion Bomber','Corvette','Recycler','Destroyer',
    'Frigate','Ion Frigate','Scout Ship','Outpost Ship','Cruiser','Carrier','Heavy Cruiser',
    'Battleship','Fleet Carrier','Dreadnought','Titan','Leviathan','Death Star',
    'Barracks','Laser Turrets','Missile Turrets','Plasma Turrets','Ion Turrets','Photon Turrets',
    'Disruptor Turrets','Deflection Shields','Planetary Shield','Planetary Ring'];
  const UNIT_NAME = ['fighters','bombers','heavybombers','ionbombers','corvette','recycler','destroyer',
    'frigate','ionfrigate','scoutship','outpostship','cruiser','carrier','heavycruiser','battleship',
    'fleetcarrier','dreadnought','titan','leviathan','deathstar','barracks','laserturrets',
    'missileturrets','plasmaturrets','ionturrets','photonturrets','disruptorturrets','deflectionshields',
    'planetaryshield','planetaryring'];
  // weapon technology per unit -> which fire 'ion' (bypass half shield)
  const POWER_TECH = ['laser','missiles','plasma','ion','laser','laser','plasma','missiles','ion',
    'laser','laser','plasma','missiles','plasma','ion','ion','photon','disruptor','photon','disruptor',
    'laser','laser','missiles','plasma','ion','photon','disruptor','ion','ion','photon'];
  const IS_ION = POWER_TECH.map(t => t === 'ion');
  // unit cost = unit value (from JAR; FT/BO/FR/HC validated unchanged in V5). Editable.
  const UNIT_COST = [5,10,30,60,20,30,40,80,120,40,100,200,400,500,2000,2500,10000,50000,200000,500000,
    5,10,20,100,256,1024,4096,4096,25000,50000];
  const DEFENSE_FIRST = 20;   // indices >=20 are planetary defences: NO debris, NOT counted in destroyed value
  // support ship types excluded from the finishing wave (per user categorisation)
  const SUPPORT_IDX = new Set(['Recycler', 'Scout Ship', 'Outpost Ship', 'Carrier', 'Fleet Carrier'].map(l => UNIT_LABEL.indexOf(l)));

  // name -> index lookup (normalised), plus longest-first phrases for loose matching
  const norm = s => String(s).toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]/g, '');
  const NAME_LOOKUP = {};
  const NAME_FORMS = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < UNIT_LABEL.length; i++) {
    const forms = [UNIT_NAME[i], UNIT_NAME[i].replace(/s$/, ''), UNIT_LABEL[i], UNIT_LABEL[i] + 's'];
    for (const f of forms) NAME_LOOKUP[norm(f)] = i;
    NAME_FORMS.push([UNIT_LABEL[i].toLowerCase(), i]);
    NAME_FORMS.push([UNIT_LABEL[i].toLowerCase() + 's', i]);
  }
  NAME_FORMS.sort((a, b) => b[0].length - a[0].length);

  function matchName(cell) {
    const n = norm(cell);
    if (n in NAME_LOOKUP) return NAME_LOOKUP[n];
    const low = String(cell).toLowerCase();
    for (const [phrase, idx] of NAME_FORMS) {
      const pos = low.indexOf(phrase);
      if (pos !== -1) {
        const before = pos > 0 ? low[pos - 1] : ' ';
        const after = pos + phrase.length < low.length ? low[pos + phrase.length] : ' ';
        if (!/[a-z]/.test(before) && !/[a-z]/.test(after)) return idx;
      }
    }
    return null;
  }

  const SUFFIX = { k: 1e3, m: 1e6, g: 1e9, t: 1e12 };
  function toNumber(tok) {
    if (tok == null) return null;
    tok = String(tok).trim();
    if (!tok || tok === '?' || tok === '-') return null;
    const m = tok.match(/^(-?\d[\d,]*\.?\d*)\s*([kmgtKMGT])?$/);
    if (!m) return null;
    let v = parseFloat(m[1].replace(/,/g, ''));
    if (m[2]) v *= SUFFIX[m[2].toLowerCase()];
    return v;
  }

  // ----------------------------------------------------------- battle engine
  // Faithful port of kahar_streamlit/engine.py (observed-stats path).
  function mkUnit(idx, qty, power, armour, shield) {
    const ion = idx != null && idx >= 0 ? IS_ION[idx] : false;
    return {
      idx, qty, power, armour, shield, cur: qty,
      ptMult: ion ? 0.5 : 0.99,        // shield multiplier subtracted from power
      passthrough: ion ? 0.5 : 0.01,   // fraction of power that leaks when shield >= power
    };
  }
  function copyFleet(f) {
    const nf = {};
    for (const k in f) nf[k] = Object.assign({}, f[k]);
    return nf;
  }
  function singleDmg(att, dfn) {
    if (dfn.cur === 0) return 0;
    const p = att.power, s = dfn.shield;
    return (s < p) ? (p - s * att.ptMult) : (p * att.passthrough);
  }
  function attackOtherFleet(attacker, target, factor) {
    const result = copyFleet(target);
    const aOrder = Object.keys(attacker).map(Number).sort((a, b) => a - b);
    for (const ai of aOrder) {
      const au = attacker[ai];
      if (au.cur <= 0) continue;
      let alive = Object.keys(result).map(Number).sort((a, b) => a - b)
        .filter(di => result[di].cur > 1e-12 && singleDmg(au, result[di]) > 0);
      let pool = au.cur, guard = 0;
      while (pool > 1e-12 && alive.length && guard < 64) {
        guard++;
        const weights = {}; let totalw = 0;
        for (const di of alive) { const w = Math.pow(singleDmg(au, result[di]), factor); weights[di] = w; totalw += w; }
        if (totalw <= 0) break;
        const overkilled = [];
        for (const di of alive) {
          const du = result[di];
          const dmg = singleDmg(au, du);
          const alloc = pool * weights[di] / totalw;
          const needed = du.cur * du.armour / dmg;
          if (alloc >= needed) overkilled.push([di, needed]);
        }
        if (overkilled.length === 0) {
          for (const di of alive) {
            const du = result[di];
            const dmg = singleDmg(au, du);
            const alloc = pool * weights[di] / totalw;
            du.cur = Math.max(du.cur - alloc * dmg / du.armour, 0);
          }
          pool = 0; break;
        }
        for (const [di, needed] of overkilled) {
          result[di].cur = 0;
          pool -= needed;
          alive = alive.filter(x => x !== di);
        }
      }
    }
    return result;
  }
  // Simultaneous engagement: both sides fire on the pre-battle copies.
  function performBattle(att, def, factor) {
    return {
      defAfter: attackOtherFleet(att, def, factor),
      attAfter: attackOtherFleet(def, att, factor),
    };
  }

  // ----- drop calculator: fewest of one unit type to clear chosen targets -----
  function singleTypeFleet(src, n) {
    const u = Object.assign({}, src); u.qty = n; u.cur = n; return { [u.idx]: u };
  }
  function minUnitsToDestroy(src, defFleet, targets, factor, cap) {
    cap = cap || 1e7;
    const tgt = targets.filter(i => defFleet[i] && defFleet[i].qty > 1e-9);
    if (!tgt.length) return 0;
    const clears = (n) => {
      if (n <= 0) return false;
      const r = attackOtherFleet(singleTypeFleet(src, n), defFleet, factor);
      return tgt.every(i => !r[i] || r[i].cur <= 1e-9);
    };
    let hi = 1;
    while (!clears(hi)) { hi *= 2; if (hi > cap) return null; }
    let lo = Math.floor(hi / 2);
    while (lo + 1 < hi) { const mid = Math.floor((lo + hi) / 2); if (clears(mid)) hi = mid; else lo = mid; }
    return hi;
  }
  // suggest the cheapest drop, restricted to unit types the attacker actually has
  function suggestDrop(att, def, factor) {
    const unsh = Object.keys(def).map(Number).filter(i => def[i].shield <= 0 && def[i].cur > 1e-9);
    if (!unsh.length) return { none: true };
    const cands = [];
    for (const i of Object.keys(att).map(Number)) {
      if (att[i].power <= 0) continue;
      const need = minUnitsToDestroy(att[i], def, unsh, factor);
      if (need != null) cands.push({ idx: i, need, have: att[i].qty, cost: need * UNIT_COST[i], enough: need <= att[i].qty + 1e-9 });
    }
    if (!cands.length) return { unsh, cands, best: null, cheapest: null };
    cands.sort((a, b) => a.cost - b.cost);
    return { unsh, cands, best: cands.find(c => c.enough) || null, cheapest: cands[0] };
  }

  // Build the fleet-movement plan so that, after moving, ONLY the drop remains.
  // For the drop unit type: move (available - dropCount). For all other types: move all.
  // Drop count is rounded UP (ceil) and a safety buffer can be added.
  function buildDropPlan(att, drop) {
    if (!drop || !drop.best) return null;
    const di = drop.best.idx;
    const have = att[di] ? att[di].qty : 0;
    const dropCount = Math.min(Math.ceil(drop.best.need) + CFG.DROP_BUFFER, Math.floor(have));
    const moves = Object.keys(att).map(Number).sort((a, b) => a - b).map(i => {
      const avail = Math.floor(att[i].qty + 1e-9);
      const leave = (i === di) ? dropCount : 0;
      return { idx: i, label: UNIT_LABEL[i], have: avail, leave, move: Math.max(avail - leave, 0) };
    });
    return { dropIdx: di, dropCount, moves };
  }

  // fleet of survivors (cur > 0), preserving stats, as a fresh fleet
  function survivorsFleet(startFleet, afterFleet) {
    const nf = {};
    for (const i of Object.keys(startFleet).map(Number)) {
      const c = afterFleet[i] ? afterFleet[i].cur : 0;
      if (c > 1e-9) nf[i] = mkUnit(i, c, startFleet[i].power, startFleet[i].armour, startFleet[i].shield);
    }
    return nf;
  }
  // Two-wave drop + finishing wave. Finish uses the attacker fleet minus the drop
  // units AND minus all support types; it engages the defenders that survived the drop.
  function dropFinishAnalysis(att, def, drop, factor) {
    if (!drop || !drop.best || !att[drop.best.idx]) return null;
    const di = drop.best.idx;
    const dropCount = Math.min(Math.ceil(drop.best.need) + CFG.DROP_BUFFER, Math.floor(att[di].qty));
    const w1 = singleTypeFleet(att[di], dropCount);
    const r1 = performBattle(w1, def, factor);
    const dropLost = valueLost(w1, r1.attAfter);
    const defLost1 = valueLost(def, r1.defAfter);
    const defSurv = survivorsFleet(def, r1.defAfter);
    const w2 = {};
    for (const i of Object.keys(att).map(Number)) {
      if (SUPPORT_IDX.has(i)) continue;
      const q = att[i].qty - (i === di ? dropCount : 0);
      if (q > 1e-9) w2[i] = mkUnit(i, q, att[i].power, att[i].armour, att[i].shield);
    }
    let finAtt = 0, finDef = 0, r2 = null;
    const hasFinish = Object.keys(w2).length > 0;
    if (Object.keys(defSurv).length && hasFinish) {
      r2 = performBattle(w2, defSurv, factor);
      finAtt = valueLost(w2, r2.attAfter);
      finDef = valueLost(defSurv, r2.defAfter);
    }
    const finalDef = r2 ? r2.defAfter : defSurv;
    const cleared = Object.values(finalDef).every(u => u.cur <= 1e-9);
    const left = Object.keys(finalDef).map(Number).filter(i => finalDef[i].cur > 1e-9)
      .map(i => `${fmtCr(Math.ceil(finalDef[i].cur))} ${UNIT_LABEL[i]}`);
    const attTot = dropLost + finAtt, defTot = defLost1 + finDef;
    return {
      dropCount, di, dropLost, defLost1, finAtt, finDef, attTot, defTot,
      overall: defTot > 0 ? attTot / defTot : Infinity,
      finRatio: finDef > 0 ? finAtt / finDef : Infinity,
      cleared, left, noFinish: !hasFinish,
    };
  }

  const profitOf = (attLost, defLost) => {
    const debris = CFG.DEBRIS_RATE * (attLost + defLost);
    return CFG.DEBRIS_SPLIT * debris + CFG.PILLAGE + debris - attLost;
  };

  // ---- optimal finisher: lowest-loss subset of the remaining fleet that one-shots ----
  // Because the finisher must destroy ALL survivors, defender loss is fixed, so
  // profit = 0.6*defLost - 0.4*attLost (at default rates): maximising profit AND profit
  // ratio both reduce to MINIMISING attacker value lost. Cheap ships are therefore kept
  // as fire-absorbers; only the expensive excess that doesn't help is trimmed.
  function fleetFromCounts(counts, pool) {
    const f = {};
    for (const i in counts) if (counts[i] > 0) f[i] = mkUnit(+i, counts[i], pool[i].power, pool[i].armour, pool[i].shield);
    return f;
  }
  function evalFinisher(counts, pool, def2, factor) {
    const fl = fleetFromCounts(counts, pool);
    if (!Object.keys(fl).length) return { clears: false, attLost: Infinity };
    const r = performBattle(fl, def2, factor);
    const clears = Object.values(r.defAfter).every(u => u.cur <= 1e-9);
    return { clears, attLost: valueLost(fl, r.attAfter), defLost: valueLost(def2, r.defAfter) };
  }
  function minCountToClear(i, counts, pool, def2, factor) {
    const maxc = Math.floor(pool[i].qty);
    const clears = c => { const t = Object.assign({}, counts); t[i] = c; return evalFinisher(t, pool, def2, factor).clears; };
    if (clears(0)) return 0;
    if (!clears(maxc)) return null;
    let lo = 0, hi = maxc;
    while (lo + 1 < hi) { const mid = Math.floor((lo + hi) / 2); if (clears(mid)) hi = mid; else lo = mid; }
    return hi;
  }
  function optimalFinisher(pool, def2, factor) {
    const idxs = Object.keys(pool).map(Number);
    if (!idxs.length || !Object.keys(def2).length) return { clears: false };
    const counts = {}; for (const i of idxs) counts[i] = Math.floor(pool[i].qty);
    if (!evalFinisher(counts, pool, def2, factor).clears) return { clears: false };  // full pool can't one-shot
    for (let pass = 0; pass < 3; pass++) {
      let improved = false;
      for (const i of idxs) {
        const mc = minCountToClear(i, counts, pool, def2, factor);
        if (mc == null) continue;
        const maxc = Math.floor(pool[i].qty);
        const cands = new Set([mc, maxc]);
        for (let k = 1; k < 8; k++) cands.add(mc + Math.round((maxc - mc) * k / 8));
        let bestc = counts[i], bestLoss = evalFinisher(counts, pool, def2, factor).attLost;
        for (const c of cands) {
          const t = Object.assign({}, counts); t[i] = c;
          const e = evalFinisher(t, pool, def2, factor);
          if (e.clears && e.attLost < bestLoss - 1e-6) { bestLoss = e.attLost; bestc = c; }
        }
        if (bestc !== counts[i]) { counts[i] = bestc; improved = true; }
      }
      if (!improved) break;
    }
    for (const i of idxs) if (counts[i] <= 0) delete counts[i];
    return Object.assign({ clears: true, counts }, evalFinisher(counts, pool, def2, factor));
  }
  // Build the finisher pool (remaining non-support fleet after the drop) + target, then optimise.
  function finisherSetup(att, def, drop, factor) {
    const di = (drop && drop.best) ? drop.best.idx : null;
    const dropCount = di != null ? Math.min(Math.ceil(drop.best.need) + CFG.DROP_BUFFER, Math.floor(att[di].qty)) : 0;
    const def2 = di != null
      ? survivorsFleet(def, performBattle(singleTypeFleet(att[di], dropCount), def, factor).defAfter)
      : survivorsFleet(def, def);
    if (!Object.keys(def2).length) return { noNeed: true };       // drop alone clears everything
    const pool = {};
    for (const i of Object.keys(att).map(Number)) {
      if (SUPPORT_IDX.has(i)) continue;
      const q = att[i].qty - (i === di ? dropCount : 0);
      if (q > 1e-9) pool[i] = mkUnit(i, q, att[i].power, att[i].armour, att[i].shield);
    }
    if (!Object.keys(pool).length) return { noPool: true };
    return { opt: optimalFinisher(pool, def2, factor) };
  }

  const fleetIdFromUrl = () => new URLSearchParams(location.search).get('fleet');

  // Store a "leave these units behind" plan (idx -> count) and open the movement page.
  // The movement page fills move = available - leave for each unit type.
  function gotoMovement(leavesByIdx, kind) {
    const fid = fleetIdFromUrl();
    const stored = { leavesByIdx, kind, fleetId: fid, ts: Date.now() };
    try { localStorage.setItem('ae_drop_plan', JSON.stringify(stored)); } catch (e) { /* ignore */ }
    log(`${kind} move plan stored:`, stored);
    const url = CFG.MOVEMENT_URL || (fid ? `${location.origin}/fleet.aspx?fleet=${fid}&view=move` : null);
    if (url) { location.href = url; return; }
    const leaveStr = Object.keys(leavesByIdx).map(i => `${fmtCr(leavesByIdx[i])} ${UNIT_LABEL[i]}`).join(', ');
    alert(`Could not read the fleet id from the URL. On the move page, leave: ${leaveStr} (move everything else away).`);
  }
  function setupDropMove(plan) { gotoMovement({ [plan.dropIdx]: plan.dropCount }, 'drop'); }
  function setupFinisherMove(counts) { gotoMovement(Object.assign({}, counts), 'finisher'); }

  // ---------------------------------------------------- movement-page auto-fill
  function readAvail(name) {
    const el = document.getElementById('avail' + name);
    if (!el) return null;
    const raw = el.getAttribute('js_value');
    const v = parseFloat(((raw != null && raw !== '') ? raw : (el.textContent || '')).replace(/[,\s]/g, ''));
    return isNaN(v) ? null : v;
  }
  function setQty(inp, val) {
    inp.value = String(val);
    inp.dispatchEvent(new Event('input', { bubbles: true }));
    inp.dispatchEvent(new Event('change', { bubbles: true }));
  }
  function fillMovementPage() {
    let plan = null;
    try { plan = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('ae_drop_plan') || 'null'); } catch (e) { /* ignore */ }
    if (!plan || !plan.leavesByIdx || !Object.keys(plan.leavesByIdx).length) { log('Movement: no move plan stored.'); return; }
    const curFleet = fleetIdFromUrl();
    if (plan.fleetId && curFleet && String(plan.fleetId) !== String(curFleet)) {
      log('Movement: stored plan is for fleet', plan.fleetId, 'but this is', curFleet, '- not filling.'); return;
    }
    if (plan.ts && Date.now() - plan.ts > 15 * 60 * 1000) { log('Movement: plan stale (>15 min), not filling.'); return; }
    const leaves = plan.leavesByIdx || {};
    const uf = document.getElementById('units');
    const names = (uf && uf.value) ? uf.value.split(',')
      : [...document.querySelectorAll('input[id^="quant"]')].map(i => i.name).filter(Boolean);
    if (!names.length) { log('Movement: no unit inputs found.'); return; }
    const rows = []; let filled = 0;
    for (const name of names) {
      const inp = document.getElementById('quant' + name) || document.getElementsByName(name)[0];
      if (!inp) continue;
      const idx = matchName(name);
      const avail = readAvail(name);
      if (avail == null) continue;
      const want = (idx != null && leaves[idx] != null) ? leaves[idx] : 0;
      const leave = Math.min(want, Math.floor(avail));
      const move = Math.max(Math.floor(avail) - leave, 0);
      setQty(inp, move);
      filled++;
      rows.push({ name, avail: Math.floor(avail), move, leave });
    }
    log('Movement: filled', filled, 'fields (kind=' + (plan.kind || '?') + '):', rows);
    renderMoveBanner(rows, plan.kind);
  }

  // ------------------------------------------------------------- DOM reading
  function headerRoles(cells) {
    const low = cells.map(c => c.toLowerCase());
    const joined = low.join(' ');
    if (!/armour|armor/.test(joined)) return null;
    if (!/shield|attack|power/.test(joined)) return null;
    const roles = {};
    low.forEach((name, ci) => {
      if (/end/.test(name) && /quant/.test(name)) { if (roles.end == null) roles.end = ci; return; }
      if ((/start/.test(name) && /quant/.test(name)) || /^(quantity|number|amount|quant\.?)$/.test(name)) { if (roles.qty == null) roles.qty = ci; }
      else if (/^attack|^power/.test(name) || name === 'attack' || name === 'power') { if (roles.power == null) roles.power = ci; }
      else if (/armour|armor/.test(name)) { if (roles.armour == null) roles.armour = ci; }
      else if (/shield/.test(name)) { if (roles.shield == null) roles.shield = ci; }
    });
    if (roles.qty == null) roles.qty = cells.length > 1 ? 1 : 0;
    return roles;
  }

  function sideOfTable(table) {
    // climb a few ancestors / look at the table's own text + preceding text for force keywords
    let txt = '';
    let el = table;
    for (let up = 0; up < 4 && el; up++) { txt += ' ' + (el.textContent || ''); el = el.parentElement; }
    // also nearest previous sibling headings
    let prev = table.previousElementSibling, hops = 0;
    while (prev && hops < 6) { txt = (prev.textContent || '') + ' ' + txt; prev = prev.previousElementSibling; hops++; }
    txt = txt.toLowerCase();
    const a = txt.indexOf('attack');         // 'attack force' / 'attacking force'
    const d = txt.search(/defens|defenc|defend/);
    if (a !== -1 && (d === -1 || a < d)) return 'attacker';
    if (d !== -1) return 'defender';
    return null;
  }

  // Parse one table into {side, rows:[{idx,qty,power,armour,shield,endCell}]}
  function parseTable(table) {
    const rows = [];
    let roles = null;
    let side = sideOfTable(table);
    for (const tr of table.rows) {
      const cellEls = [...tr.cells];
      const cells = cellEls.map(c => c.textContent.trim());
      const joined = cells.join(' ').toLowerCase();
      if (cells.length <= 2 && /attack(ing)? force/.test(joined)) { side = 'attacker'; continue; }
      if (cells.length <= 2 && /(defensive|defending|defense|defence) force/.test(joined)) { side = 'defender'; continue; }
      const hdr = headerRoles(cells);
      if (hdr) { roles = hdr; continue; }
      if (!cells.length) continue;
      const idx = matchName(cells[0]);
      if (idx == null) continue;
      // target cell = the one currently showing "?"
      let endCell = cellEls.find(c => c.textContent.trim() === '?');
      if (!endCell && roles && roles.end != null) endCell = cellEls[roles.end];
      if (!endCell) continue; // nothing to fill on this row (already resolved?)
      const get = (role) => (roles && roles[role] != null) ? cells[roles[role]] : null;
      let qty = toNumber(get('qty'));
      if (qty == null) { for (let i = 1; i < cells.length; i++) { qty = toNumber(cells[i]); if (qty != null) break; } }
      const power = toNumber(get('power'));
      const armour = toNumber(get('armour'));
      const shield = toNumber(get('shield'));
      rows.push({ idx, qty: qty || 0, power, armour, shield, endCell });
    }
    return { side, rows };
  }

  function collectFleets() {
    const tables = [...document.querySelectorAll('table')];
    const parsed = [];
    for (const t of tables) {
      // only bother with tables that have a "?" somewhere and at least one unit row
      if (!/\?/.test(t.textContent)) continue;
      const pt = parseTable(t);
      if (pt.rows.length) parsed.push(pt);
    }
    if (!parsed.length) return null;

    // Group into attacker / defender. Fallback: if exactly two unit-tables and side
    // unknown, assume first = attacker, second = defender (AE shows attacker first).
    let attRows = [], defRows = [];
    const unknown = parsed.filter(p => !p.side);
    if (parsed.length === 2 && unknown.length === 2) {
      parsed[0].side = 'attacker'; parsed[1].side = 'defender';
    }
    for (const p of parsed) {
      if (p.side === 'attacker') attRows.push(...p.rows);
      else if (p.side === 'defender') defRows.push(...p.rows);
    }
    // single table holding both sides via section markers already split via row.side? -
    // parseTable assigns one side per table; if everything landed on one side, try to
    // split a single combined table by its internal markers (handled above per-row).
    log('parsed tables:', parsed.map(p => ({ side: p.side, units: p.rows.length })));
    return { attRows, defRows };
  }

  function buildFleet(rows) {
    const f = {};
    for (const r of rows) {
      if (r.power == null || r.armour == null) continue; // need stats to fight
      const idx = r.idx;
      if (f[idx]) { f[idx].qty += r.qty; f[idx].cur += r.qty; }   // merge duplicates
      else f[idx] = mkUnit(idx, r.qty, r.power, r.armour, r.shield == null ? 0 : r.shield);
    }
    return f;
  }

  function fmt(v) {
    const r = Math.round(v * Math.pow(10, CFG.DECIMALS)) / Math.pow(10, CFG.DECIMALS);
    return Number.isInteger(r) ? String(r) : r.toFixed(CFG.DECIMALS);
  }
  const fmtCr = v => Math.round(v).toLocaleString('en-US');

  // value lost (debris/profit basis): MOBILE fleet only (idx < 20); defences leave no debris
  function valueLost(startFleet, endFleet) {
    let v = 0;
    for (const i in startFleet) {
      if (+i >= DEFENSE_FIRST) continue;
      const lost = Math.max(startFleet[i].qty - (endFleet[i] ? endFleet[i].cur : 0), 0);
      v += lost * UNIT_COST[i];
    }
    return v;
  }
  // total fleet value committed (all units, incl. defences = defending strength)
  function committedValue(f) {
    let v = 0;
    for (const i in f) v += f[i].qty * UNIT_COST[i];
    return v;
  }
  function computeMetrics(att, def, attAfter, defAfter) {
    const attLost = valueLost(att, attAfter);
    const defLost = valueLost(def, defAfter);
    const debris = CFG.DEBRIS_RATE * (attLost + defLost);
    const loot = CFG.DEBRIS_SPLIT * debris;
    const profit = loot + CFG.PILLAGE + debris - attLost;
    const profitPct = attLost > 0 ? (profit / attLost * 100) : Infinity;
    // loss ratio = value the attacker lost vs value the defender lost (A : D); < 1 favours attacker
    const lossRatio = defLost > 0 ? attLost / defLost : Infinity;
    const attVal = committedValue(att), defVal = committedValue(def);   // kept for the console log
    const defDead = Object.values(defAfter).every(u => u.cur <= 1e-9);
    const attDead = Object.values(attAfter).every(u => u.cur <= 1e-9);
    const outcome = defDead ? 'win' : (attDead ? 'lose' : 'partial');
    return { attLost, defLost, debris, loot, profit, profitPct, lossRatio, attVal, defVal, outcome };
  }

  // ===================================================================== UI
  // Retrofit: the panel + banners are now AEShared shadow-DOM hosts tiled by
  // the AE Layout Overseer. All chrome is built with DOM node methods (no
  // innerHTML) to stay safe under AstroEmpires' Trusted-Types CSP. Battle-cell
  // writing (writeResult) still paints predicted survivors red in-place.

  const LOCAL_CSS = `
.ae-hidden { display: none !important; }
.ae-panel__body { max-height: 70vh; overflow-y: auto; }
.ae-panel__body .ae-kv:first-child { border-top: 0; }
`;

  // Original inline hex -> AEShared theme tokens (red identity preserved in the
  // in-cell predicted numbers via CFG.COLOR; panel section heads map to amber).
  const C = {
    head: 'var(--ae-secondary)', // #d40000 section headers -> amber
    good: 'var(--ae-success)',   // #86e6a8
    bad:  'var(--ae-danger)',    // #ff5d5d
    warn: 'var(--ae-warn)',      // #ecd97e
    key:  'var(--ae-text-muted)',// #9fc1e6 labels
    dim:  'var(--ae-text-dim)',  // #6b7b90 dim
  };

  // Tiny DOM builder. attrs: {text, class, title, css:{...}, on<Event>:fn, ...}
  function h(tag, attrs, ...kids) {
    const el = document.createElement(tag);
    if (attrs) {
      for (const k in attrs) {
        const v = attrs[k];
        if (v == null) continue;
        if (k === 'text') el.textContent = v;
        else if (k === 'class') el.className = v;
        else if (k === 'css') Object.assign(el.style, v);
        else if (k === 'title') el.title = v;
        else if (k.length > 2 && k.slice(0, 2) === 'on' && typeof v === 'function') el.addEventListener(k.slice(2).toLowerCase(), v);
        else el.setAttribute(k, v);
      }
    }
    for (const kid of kids) {
      if (kid == null) continue;
      el.appendChild(typeof kid === 'string' ? document.createTextNode(kid) : kid);
    }
    return el;
  }

  function clear(node) { while (node && node.firstChild) node.removeChild(node.firstChild); }
  function cbold(text, color) { return h('b', { text, css: color ? { color } : null }); }
  function numVal(text) { return h('span', { class: 'ae-num', text, css: { color: 'var(--ae-text)' } }); }
  function hr() { return h('div', { class: 'ae-hr' }); }
  function sectionHead(text) { return h('div', { text, css: { fontWeight: 'bold', color: C.head, marginBottom: '2px', marginTop: '2px' } }); }
  function kvRow(k, valNode) { return h('div', { class: 'ae-kv' }, h('span', { text: k }), valNode); }

  // --- UI persistence (collapse state only) ----------------------------------
  const UI_KEY = 'ae_pbp::ui';
  const PREDCFG_KEY = 'ae::predcfg::' + (location.hostname.split('.')[0] || 'srv');
  function loadUi() { try { return JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(UI_KEY)) || {}; } catch (e) { return {}; } }
  function saveUi(patch) {
    let cur = {}; try { cur = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(UI_KEY)) || {}; } catch (e) { /* ignore */ }
    try { localStorage.setItem(UI_KEY, JSON.stringify(Object.assign(cur, patch))); } catch (e) { /* ignore */ }
    mirrorPredCfg();
  }
  // Cross-script settings mirror: the drop-calc hover overlays are injected by a DIFFERENT
  // script (Base Intel runs on fleet pages), so the on/off switch must be readable outside
  // this script's own storage.
  function dropCalcEnabled() { const u = loadUi(); return u.dropCalc !== false; }   // default ON
  // Drop-calc attacker profile. Defaults from the Altair context inventory (BS-led,
  // BO mod attack 11.5, BS mod attack 264) — editable in the panel.
  function dropCalcCfg() {
    const u = loadUi();
    return { dropCalc: dropCalcEnabled(),
             mainAbbr: u.dcMainAbbr || 'BS',
             boAttack: parseFloat(u.dcBoAttack) || 11.5,
             mainAttack: parseFloat(u.dcMainAttack) || 264,
             assumeTech: u.dcAssumeTech != null && u.dcAssumeTech !== '' ? parseFloat(u.dcAssumeTech) : 15 };
  }
  function mirrorPredCfg() {
    try { localStorage.setItem(PREDCFG_KEY, JSON.stringify(Object.assign(dropCalcCfg(), { updated: Date.now() }))); } catch (e) { /* ignore */ }
  }

  // --- shared shell (DOM-only .ae-panel chrome) ------------------------------
  function buildShell(title, subtitle, opts) {
    opts = opts || {};
    const panel = h('div', { class: 'ae-panel ae-stack-item' });
    const bar = h('div', { class: 'ae-panel__bar' });
    const dot = h('span', { class: 'ae-panel__dot' });
    const titles = h('span', { class: 'ae-panel__titles' });
    titles.appendChild(h('span', { class: 'ae-panel__title', text: title }));
    titles.appendChild(h('span', { class: 'ae-panel__sub', text: subtitle || '' }));
    const statusEl = h('span', { class: 'ae-panel__status' });
    const minBtn = h('button', { class: 'ae-toggle', title: 'Collapse', text: '−' });  // shared +/- control
    bar.appendChild(dot); bar.appendChild(titles); bar.appendChild(statusEl); bar.appendChild(minBtn);
    const sweep = h('div', { class: 'ae-panel__sweep' });
    const body = h('div', { class: 'ae-panel__body' });
    // settings: drop-calc hover overlays on/off (read cross-script via PREDCFG_KEY)
    const dcRow = h('div', { class: 'ae-kv', style: 'display:flex;align-items:center;gap:6px;font-size:11px;' });
    const dcBox = h('input', { type: 'checkbox' });
    dcBox.checked = dropCalcEnabled();
    dcBox.addEventListener('change', () => { saveUi({ dropCalc: dcBox.checked }); });
    const dcLbl = h('span', {}, 'Drop-calc fleet overlays');
    dcLbl.title = 'Show BO + main-spec drop estimates when hovering enemy fleets';
    dcRow.appendChild(dcBox); dcRow.appendChild(dcLbl); body.appendChild(dcRow);
    // attacker profile: main unit + modified attacks (BO and main)
    const cfg0 = dropCalcCfg();
    const dcRow2 = h('div', { class: 'ae-kv', style: 'display:flex;align-items:center;gap:4px;font-size:11px;flex-wrap:wrap;' });
    const mainSel = h('select', { class: 'ae-input', style: 'width:58px' });
    ['BS','HC','CR','DN','FC','TI','FR','IF','DD','BO','FT'].forEach(ab => { const o = h('option', { value: ab }, ab); if (ab === cfg0.mainAbbr) o.selected = true; mainSel.appendChild(o); });
    const boIn = h('input', { class: 'ae-input', type: 'number', step: '0.1', style: 'width:52px', value: String(cfg0.boAttack), title: 'Your BO modified attack' });
    const mainIn = h('input', { class: 'ae-input', type: 'number', step: '0.1', style: 'width:58px', value: String(cfg0.mainAttack), title: 'Your main unit modified attack' });
    const saveDc = () => saveUi({ dcMainAbbr: mainSel.value, dcBoAttack: boIn.value, dcMainAttack: mainIn.value });
    mainSel.addEventListener('change', saveDc); boIn.addEventListener('change', saveDc); mainIn.addEventListener('change', saveDc);
    dcRow2.appendChild(h('span', { class: 'ae-muted' }, 'main')); dcRow2.appendChild(mainSel);
    dcRow2.appendChild(h('span', { class: 'ae-muted' }, 'BO atk')); dcRow2.appendChild(boIn);
    dcRow2.appendChild(h('span', { class: 'ae-muted' }, 'main atk')); dcRow2.appendChild(mainIn);
    // assumed enemy tech when a target has no harvested/archetype tech. Base stats
    // (tech 0) badly overstate your ratios, so this is never allowed to be 0 by default.
    const dcRow3 = h('div', { class: 'ae-kv', style: 'display:flex;align-items:center;gap:4px;font-size:11px;' });
    const atIn = h('input', { class: 'ae-input', type: 'number', step: '1', min: '0', style: 'width:52px',
      value: String(cfg0.assumeTech), title: 'Tech level assumed for targets with no harvested or archetype tech' });
    atIn.addEventListener('change', () => saveUi({ dcAssumeTech: atIn.value }));
    dcRow3.appendChild(h('span', { class: 'ae-muted' }, 'assumed enemy tech'));
    dcRow3.appendChild(atIn);
    body.appendChild(dcRow2); body.appendChild(dcRow3);
    // main-spec reconfirm: if Empire->Units was parsed and the detected main differs
    // from the configured one, surface it with a one-click adopt (also refreshes the
    // main attack from the parsed modified stats).
    try {
      const own = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('ae::ownunits::' + (location.hostname.split('.')[0] || 'srv')));
      if (own && own.mainAbbr) {
        const ageD = Math.round((Date.now() - (own.updated || 0)) / 86400000);
        if (own.mainAbbr !== dropCalcCfg().mainAbbr) {
          const warn = h('div', { class: 'ae-kv', style: 'font-size:10px;display:flex;align-items:center;gap:6px;' });
          const wtxt = h('span', { class: 'ae-muted' }, 'Units page says your main is ' + own.mainAbbr + ' (' + ageD + 'd old)');
          const adopt = h('button', { class: 'ae-btn', style: 'font-size:10px;padding:1px 6px;' }, 'Adopt');
          adopt.addEventListener('click', () => {
            const u = own.units[own.mainAbbr] || {};
            saveUi({ dcMainAbbr: own.mainAbbr, dcMainAttack: u.atk != null ? String(u.atk) : undefined });
            mainSel.value = own.mainAbbr; if (u.atk != null) mainIn.value = String(u.atk);
          });
          warn.appendChild(wtxt); warn.appendChild(adopt); body.appendChild(warn);
        } else if (own.units[own.mainAbbr] && own.units[own.mainAbbr].atk != null
                   && Math.abs(own.units[own.mainAbbr].atk - dropCalcCfg().mainAttack) > 0.01) {
          const warn2 = h('div', { class: 'ae-kv ae-muted', style: 'font-size:10px;' },
            'Units page: ' + own.mainAbbr + ' attack is ' + own.units[own.mainAbbr].atk + ' (configured ' + dropCalcCfg().mainAttack + ')');
          body.appendChild(warn2);
        }
      }
    } catch (e) { /* no own-units yet */ }
    mirrorPredCfg();   // publish current setting on every build
    panel.appendChild(bar); panel.appendChild(sweep); panel.appendChild(body);

    function setStatus(state, text) {
      dot.classList.remove('is-ok', 'is-warn', 'is-bad');
