      if (state === 'ok') dot.classList.add('is-ok');
      if (state === 'warn') dot.classList.add('is-warn');
      if (state === 'bad') dot.classList.add('is-bad');
      if (text != null) statusEl.textContent = text;
    }
    function setCollapsed(hidden) {
      body.classList.toggle('ae-hidden', !!hidden);
      minBtn.textContent = hidden ? '+' : '−';          // shared idiom
      minBtn.title = hidden ? 'Expand' : 'Collapse';
      minBtn.setAttribute('aria-expanded', String(!hidden));
    }
    if (opts.startCollapsed) setCollapsed(true);
    minBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
      const hidden = !body.classList.contains('ae-hidden');
      setCollapsed(hidden);
      if (opts.onToggle) opts.onToggle(hidden);
    });
    return { panel, body, dot, statusEl, setStatus, setCollapsed, minBtn };
  }

  // --- main prediction panel host (all non-move pages) -----------------------
  let PANEL = null;
  let MOVEHOST = null;

  function mountPanel() {
    if (PANEL) return PANEL;
    const host = document.createElement('div');
    host.setAttribute('data-ae-script', SCRIPT_ID);
    const shadow = host.attachShadow({ mode: 'open' });
    AEShared.adopt(shadow);
    const localStyle = document.createElement('style');
    localStyle.setAttribute('data-ae-local', '1');
    localStyle.textContent = LOCAL_CSS;
    shadow.appendChild(localStyle);

    const ui = loadUi();
    const shell = buildShell('Battle prediction', 'pre-combat forecast', {
      startCollapsed: !!ui.collapsed,
      onToggle: (hidden) => saveUi({ collapsed: hidden }),
    });
    shadow.appendChild(shell.panel);

    // Folds in the old always-present floating red button as a themed control.
    const predictBtn = h('button', {
      class: 'ae-btn ae-btn--primary',
      css: { width: '100%', marginBottom: '8px' },
      text: '⚔ Predict battle',
      onclick: onPredictClick,
    });
    const content = h('div');
    shell.body.appendChild(predictBtn);
    shell.body.appendChild(content);

    document.documentElement.appendChild(host);
    AEShared.placeHost(host, SCRIPT_ID, shadow);
    AEShared.startLiveUpdates();

    PANEL = { host, shadow, shell, content, predictBtn, setStatus: shell.setStatus };
    renderIdle();
    return PANEL;
  }

  function renderIdle() {
    if (!PANEL) return;
    clear(PANEL.content);
    PANEL.setStatus('', 'idle');
    PANEL.content.appendChild(h('div', { text: 'No pre-battle report detected on this page yet.', css: { color: C.dim, fontSize: '11px' } }));
    PANEL.content.appendChild(h('div', { text: 'Open a pre-battle report, or press Predict to scan.', css: { color: C.dim, fontSize: '11px', marginTop: '3px' } }));
  }

  function onPredictClick() {
    const ok = run();
    const btn = PANEL && PANEL.predictBtn;
    if (btn) {
      btn.textContent = ok ? '⚔ Recalculate' : '⚔ No report found';
      setTimeout(() => { btn.textContent = '⚔ Predict battle'; }, 1500);
    }
    if (!ok) renderIdle();
  }

  // --- rewritten render helpers (return DOM nodes, no innerHTML) --------------
  function finishBlock(fin) {
    if (!fin) return null;
    const rs = v => isFinite(v) ? v.toFixed(2) + ' : 1' : '∞';
    const col = v => (isFinite(v) && v <= 1) ? C.good : C.bad;
    const wrap = h('div', { css: { marginTop: '4px' } });
    wrap.appendChild(kvRow('Drop+finish loss (A : D)', cbold(rs(fin.overall), col(fin.overall))));
    wrap.appendChild(h('div', { text: 'Finish wave (non-support):', css: { color: C.key, marginTop: '1px' } }));
    const txt = h('div', { css: { fontSize: '11px' } });
    if (fin.noFinish) {
      txt.appendChild(h('span', { text: 'no non-support units left to finish', css: { color: C.dim } }));
    } else if (fin.cleared) {
      txt.appendChild(h('span', { text: 'clears defender ✓', css: { color: C.good } }));
      txt.appendChild(document.createTextNode(` · lose ${fmtCr(fin.finAtt)} (`));
      txt.appendChild(h('span', { text: rs(fin.finRatio), css: { color: col(fin.finRatio) } }));
      txt.appendChild(document.createTextNode(')'));
    } else {
      txt.appendChild(h('span', { text: 'defender holds:', css: { color: C.bad } }));
      txt.appendChild(document.createTextNode(` ${fin.left.join(', ') || '—'} left · lose ${fmtCr(fin.finAtt)}`));
    }
    wrap.appendChild(txt);
    return wrap;
  }

  function optimalBlock(o) {
    if (!o) return null;
    if (o.noNeed) return h('div', { css: { marginTop: '4px', fontSize: '11px', color: C.good }, text: 'Drop alone clears the defender — no finisher needed.' });
    if (o.noPool) return h('div', { css: { marginTop: '4px', fontSize: '11px', color: C.dim }, text: 'No non-support units left for a finisher.' });
    if (!o.opt || !o.opt.clears) return h('div', { css: { marginTop: '4px', fontSize: '11px', color: C.bad }, text: "Remaining fleet can't one-shot the survivors." });
    const comp = Object.keys(o.opt.counts).filter(i => o.opt.counts[i] > 0)
      .map(i => `${fmtCr(o.opt.counts[i])} ${UNIT_LABEL[i]}`).join(' + ') || '—';
    const profit = profitOf(o.opt.attLost, o.opt.defLost);
    const ratio = o.opt.attLost > 0 ? profit / o.opt.attLost * 100 : Infinity;
    const pcol = profit >= 0 ? C.good : C.bad;
    const wrap = h('div', { css: { marginTop: '5px' } });
    wrap.appendChild(sectionHead('Optimal finisher (best profit)'));
    wrap.appendChild(h('div', { text: comp, css: { color: C.good } }));
    const line = h('div', { css: { fontSize: '11px' } });
    line.appendChild(document.createTextNode('profit '));
    line.appendChild(cbold(fmtCr(profit), pcol));
    line.appendChild(document.createTextNode(` · ratio ${isFinite(ratio) ? ratio.toFixed(0) + '%' : '∞'} · lose ${fmtCr(o.opt.attLost)}`));
    wrap.appendChild(line);
    wrap.appendChild(h('button', {
      class: 'ae-btn ae-btn--primary', css: { marginTop: '5px', width: '100%' },
      text: '➤ Move all except finisher',
      onclick: () => setupFinisherMove(o.opt.counts),
    }));
    return wrap;
  }

  function dropLine(drop) {
    if (!drop) return null;
    const frag = document.createDocumentFragment();
    frag.appendChild(hr());
    frag.appendChild(sectionHead('Suggested drop (clear unshielded)'));
    const bodyDiv = h('div');
    if (drop.none) {
      bodyDiv.appendChild(h('span', { text: 'none needed (all defenders shielded)', css: { color: C.good } }));
    } else if (!drop.cheapest) {
      bodyDiv.appendChild(h('span', { text: 'no attacker type can clear', css: { color: C.dim } }));
    } else if (drop.best) {
      bodyDiv.appendChild(cbold(`${fmtCr(drop.best.need)} × ${UNIT_LABEL[drop.best.idx]}`, C.good));
      bodyDiv.appendChild(document.createTextNode(' '));
      bodyDiv.appendChild(h('span', { text: `(${fmtCr(drop.best.cost)} cr)`, css: { color: C.key } }));
    } else {
      const c = drop.cheapest;
      bodyDiv.appendChild(h('span', { text: `need ${fmtCr(c.need)} × ${UNIT_LABEL[c.idx]}, have ${fmtCr(c.have)}`, css: { color: C.bad } }));
    }
    frag.appendChild(bodyDiv);
    if (drop.cands && drop.cands.length > 1) {
      frag.appendChild(h('div', {
        css: { color: C.dim, fontSize: '11px', marginTop: '2px' },
        text: drop.cands.slice(0, 3).map(c => `${fmtCr(c.need)} ${UNIT_LABEL[c.idx]}${c.enough ? '' : '✗'} ${fmtCr(c.cost)}cr`).join(' · '),
      }));
    }
    const fb = finishBlock(drop.finish);
    if (fb) frag.appendChild(fb);
    if (drop.best) {
      frag.appendChild(h('button', {
        class: 'ae-btn ae-btn--primary', css: { marginTop: '6px', width: '100%' },
        text: '➤ Set up drop move',
        onclick: () => { if (drop.plan) setupDropMove(drop.plan); },
      }));
    }
    const ob = optimalBlock(drop.opt);
    if (ob) frag.appendChild(ob);
    return frag;
  }

  function renderPanel(m, drop) {
    if (!CFG.SHOW_PANEL) return;
    mountPanel();
    const body = PANEL.content;
    clear(body);

    const st = m.outcome === 'win' ? 'ok' : (m.outcome === 'lose' ? 'bad' : 'warn');
    PANEL.setStatus(st, m.outcome === 'win' ? 'attacker wins' : (m.outcome === 'lose' ? 'defender holds' : 'both survive'));

    const headLine = h('div', { css: { marginBottom: '5px' } });
    if (m.outcome === 'win') headLine.appendChild(cbold('Attacker wins', C.good));
    else if (m.outcome === 'lose') headLine.appendChild(cbold('Defender holds', C.bad));
    else headLine.appendChild(cbold('Both survive', C.warn));
    body.appendChild(headLine);

    const pcol = m.profit >= 0 ? C.good : C.bad;
    const pct = isFinite(m.profitPct) ? m.profitPct.toFixed(0) + '%' : '∞ (no losses)';
    const lr = m.lossRatio;
    const lrStr = isFinite(lr) ? lr.toFixed(2) + ' : 1' : (m.attLost > 0 ? '∞ (no enemy loss)' : '—');
    const lrCol = !isFinite(lr) ? (m.attLost > 0 ? C.bad : C.key) : (lr <= 1 ? C.good : C.bad);

    body.appendChild(kvRow('Profit', cbold(fmtCr(m.profit), pcol)));
    body.appendChild(kvRow('Profit / losses', cbold(pct, pcol)));
    body.appendChild(kvRow('Attacker lost', numVal(fmtCr(m.attLost))));
    body.appendChild(kvRow('Defender lost', numVal(fmtCr(m.defLost))));
    body.appendChild(kvRow('Debris (40%)', numVal(fmtCr(m.debris))));
    body.appendChild(kvRow('Your loot (50%)', numVal(fmtCr(m.loot))));
    body.appendChild(hr());
    body.appendChild(kvRow('Loss ratio (A : D)', cbold(lrStr, lrCol)));
    body.appendChild(h('div', { text: 'your losses per 1 enemy lost (lower = better)', css: { color: C.dim, fontSize: '11px', marginTop: '-1px' } }));

    const dl = dropLine(drop);
    if (dl) body.appendChild(dl);
    if (!CFG.PILLAGE) body.appendChild(h('div', { text: 'Pillage not included (set CFG.PILLAGE)', css: { color: C.dim, marginTop: '5px', fontSize: '11px' } }));
  }

  // --- move-page banner host (same scriptId; move page never mounts PANEL) ----
  function renderMoveBanner(rows, kind) {
    if (MOVEHOST) { try { MOVEHOST.host.remove(); } catch (e) { /* ignore */ } MOVEHOST = null; }
    const host = document.createElement('div');
    host.setAttribute('data-ae-script', SCRIPT_ID);
    const shadow = host.attachShadow({ mode: 'open' });
    AEShared.adopt(shadow);
    const localStyle = document.createElement('style');
    localStyle.setAttribute('data-ae-local', '1');
    localStyle.textContent = LOCAL_CSS;
    shadow.appendChild(localStyle);

    const shell = buildShell(kind === 'finisher' ? 'Finisher move set up' : 'Drop move set up', 'review, then Move yourself', {});
    shell.setStatus('ok', 'ready');
    const body = shell.body;

    const leaveStr = rows.filter(r => r.leave > 0).map(r => `${fmtCr(r.leave)} ${r.name}`).join(', ') || '(none)';
    const remain = h('div', { css: { marginBottom: '4px', color: C.good } });
    remain.appendChild(document.createTextNode('Remaining after move = '));
    remain.appendChild(h('b', { text: leaveStr }));
    body.appendChild(remain);

    body.appendChild(h('div', { text: 'Move quantities filled:', css: { color: C.key } }));
    const movedRows = rows.filter(r => r.move > 0);
    const movedWrap = h('div', { css: { fontSize: '11px', margin: '2px 0 6px' } });
    if (movedRows.length) movedRows.forEach(r => movedWrap.appendChild(h('div', { text: `${r.name}: ${fmtCr(r.move)}` })));
    else movedWrap.appendChild(document.createTextNode('(nothing)'));
    body.appendChild(movedWrap);

    body.appendChild(h('div', { text: 'Set a destination, review, then click Move yourself (not auto-submitted).', css: { color: C.warn, fontSize: '11px' } }));
    const clearBtn = h('button', {
      class: 'ae-btn', css: { marginTop: '6px' }, text: 'Clear plan',
      onclick: () => { try { localStorage.removeItem('ae_drop_plan'); } catch (e) { /* ignore */ } try { host.remove(); } catch (e) { /* ignore */ } MOVEHOST = null; },
    });
    body.appendChild(h('div', { css: { marginTop: '6px' } }, clearBtn));

    document.documentElement.appendChild(host);
    AEShared.placeHost(host, SCRIPT_ID, shadow);
    AEShared.startLiveUpdates();
    MOVEHOST = { host, shadow, shell };
  }

  function writeResult(cell, value) {
    cell.textContent = fmt(value);
    cell.style.color = CFG.COLOR;
    cell.style.fontWeight = 'bold';
    cell.title = `Predicted survivors (engine factor ${CFG.FACTOR})`;
    cell.setAttribute('data-ae-predicted', '1');
  }

  // ------------------------------------------------------------------ run it
  function run() {
    try {
      const fleets = collectFleets();
      if (!fleets) { log('No pre-battle table with "?" cells found on this page.'); return false; }
      const { attRows, defRows } = fleets;
      if (!attRows.length || !defRows.length) {
        log('Found unit rows but could not split into BOTH an attacker and a defender.',
            { attacker: attRows.length, defender: defRows.length },
            'If the page shows two forces, send me the table HTML so I can fix the side detection.');
        return false;
      }
      const att = buildFleet(attRows);
      const def = buildFleet(defRows);
      if (!Object.keys(att).length || !Object.keys(def).length) {
        log('Could not read Attack/Armour stats for one side; nothing to compute.'); return false;
      }
      const { defAfter, attAfter } = performBattle(att, def, CFG.FACTOR);

      let filled = 0;
      for (const r of attRows) {
        if (r.endCell && attAfter[r.idx] != null) { writeResult(r.endCell, attAfter[r.idx].cur); filled++; }
        else if (r.endCell) { writeResult(r.endCell, r.qty); filled++; } // no stats -> assume untouched
      }
      for (const r of defRows) {
        if (r.endCell && defAfter[r.idx] != null) { writeResult(r.endCell, defAfter[r.idx].cur); filled++; }
        else if (r.endCell) { writeResult(r.endCell, r.qty); filled++; }
      }
      const m = computeMetrics(att, def, attAfter, defAfter);
      const drop = suggestDrop(att, def, CFG.FACTOR);
      drop.plan = buildDropPlan(att, drop);
      drop.finish = dropFinishAnalysis(att, def, drop, CFG.FACTOR);
      drop.opt = (drop.best || drop.none) ? finisherSetup(att, def, drop, CFG.FACTOR) : null;
      renderPanel(m, drop);
      log(`Filled ${filled} cell(s).`,
          'Attacker survivors:', Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(attAfter).map(([k, u]) => [UNIT_LABEL[k], +fmt(u.cur)])),
          'Defender survivors:', Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(defAfter).map(([k, u]) => [UNIT_LABEL[k], +fmt(u.cur)])),
          '| metrics:', { profit: Math.round(m.profit), profitPct: +m.profitPct.toFixed(1), lossRatio: +m.lossRatio.toFixed(2) });
      return filled > 0;
    } catch (e) {
      console.error('[AE-Predict] error:', e);
      return false;
    }
  }

  // ------------------------------------------------------------- bootstrap
  const isMovePage = !!document.getElementById('move_fleet_form') ||
    new URLSearchParams(location.search).get('view') === 'move';

  if (isMovePage) {
    // movement page: wait for the move form, then auto-fill the quantities.
    let t = 0;
    const iv = setInterval(() => {
      t++;
      if (document.getElementById('move_fleet_form')) { clearInterval(iv); fillMovementPage(); }
      else if (t > 12) clearInterval(iv);
    }, 300);
  } else {
    // pre-battle report page: mount the tiled panel, then predict + drop suggestion.
    // NOTE: the panel host lives on documentElement (a sibling of <body>), so the
    // body MutationObserver below never sees it -> no self-triggering loop.
    mountPanel();
    if (CFG.AUTO_RUN) {
      let tries = 0;
      const tick = setInterval(() => { tries++; if (run() || tries > 6) clearInterval(tick); }, 600);
      const mo = new MutationObserver(() => {
        if (document.querySelector('[data-ae-predicted]')) return; // already done
        if (/\?/.test(document.body.textContent)) run();
      });
      mo.observe(document.body, { childList: true, subtree: true });
    }
  }
})();

} });


/* ============ MODULE: empire-menu ============ */
AESuite.register({ id: "empire-menu", title: "Empire Menu Everywhere", pages: null, init: function () {
(function () {
  'use strict';

  const COPY_ID = 'ae-empire-menu-copy';

  // Only act where the native Empire menu is NOT present. On the empire.aspx
  // views the game already renders #empire_menu, so we leave those alone.
  if (document.getElementById('empire_menu')) return;

  // Idempotency guard: never insert twice.
  if (document.getElementById(COPY_ID)) return;

  // Capture the server the user is currently on, so every link is absolute and
  // points at that server (e.g. https://borealis.astroempires.com/...).
  // Relative links would also resolve to the current server, but pinning the
  // origin makes the target explicit and robust outside page context.
  const origin = window.location.origin;

  // [label, path] in the native menu's row order (2 rows of 5).
  const ITEMS = [
    ['Events',       'empire.aspx?view=bases_events'],
    ['Production',   'empire.aspx?view=bases_production'],
    ['Economy',      'empire.aspx?view=economy'],
    ['Trade',        'empire.aspx?view=trade'],
    ['Reports',      'report.aspx'],
    ['Capacities',   'empire.aspx?view=bases_capacities'],
    ['Structures',   'empire.aspx?view=structures'],
    ['Fleets',       'empire.aspx?view=fleets'],
    ['Units',        'empire.aspx?view=units'],
    ['Technologies', 'empire.aspx?view=technologies'],
  ];

  // IDs are prefixed "ae2-" so they never collide with the native menu's IDs
  // or with other userscripts that query #empire-events etc. (Cosmetic only on
  // these pages — there is no active-tab highlight to inherit here anyway.)
  const IDS = [
    'ae2-empire-events', 'ae2-empire-production', 'ae2-empire-economy',
    'ae2-empire-trade', 'ae2-empire-reports', 'ae2-empire-capacities',
    'ae2-empire-structures', 'ae2-empire-fleets', 'ae2-empire-units',
    'ae2-empire-technologies',
  ];

  function buildCell(id, width, label, path) {
    const cell = document.createElement('th');
    cell.id = id;
    cell.setAttribute('width', width);
    const a = document.createElement('a');
    a.setAttribute('href', origin + '/' + path); // absolute, current-server URL
    a.textContent = label;
    cell.appendChild(a);
    return cell;
  }

  function buildMenu() {
    const table = document.createElement('table');
    table.id = COPY_ID;
    table.setAttribute('align', 'center');
    table.setAttribute('width', '850');
    table.setAttribute('height', '25');
    table.className = 'header';
    table.style.marginTop = '6px';

    const tbody = document.createElement('tbody');
    const row1 = document.createElement('tr');
    const row2 = document.createElement('tr');

    ITEMS.forEach((item, i) => {
      const width = (i === 9) ? '*' : '10%';
      const cell = buildCell(IDS[i], width, item[0], item[1]);
      (i < 5 ? row1 : row2).appendChild(cell);
    });

    tbody.appendChild(row1);
    tbody.appendChild(row2);
    table.appendChild(tbody);
    return table;
  }

  function insertAfter(newNode, refNode) {
    refNode.parentNode.insertBefore(newNode, refNode.nextSibling);
  }

  // Anchor the menu beneath the main header nav bar.
  const header = document.querySelector('table.top');
  if (!header) return; // no sensible anchor on this page — bail safely.
  insertAfter(buildMenu(), header);
})();

} });


/* ============ MODULE: map-intel ============ */
AESuite.register({ id: "map-intel", title: "Base Intel Database", pages: null, init: function () {
/* =============================================================================
 * AE MAP INTEL — Base Collector module (report-first, rewritten 2026-07-14u)
 *
 * Flow (Aran's design):
 *   1. User gives a coord range (galaxy / region / system / astro terms).
 *   2. User sets a scan interval; it applies to EVERY navigation that follows.
 *   3. Collector opens the Galaxy Bases report page, shows the red IN-PROGRESS banner.
 *   4. For each galaxy in the range: fill the report form's galaxy dropdown, select
 *      report_type=bases, click "Get Report" (visible submit).
 *   5. Parse the resulting table; keep only rows whose coord is inside the range.
 *   6. Open each in-scope base page (base.aspx?base=ID) sequentially, at the interval,
 *      parsing defenses / structures / trade routes / economy into the database.
 *   7. Banner shows live ETA countdown + bases remaining. Loop until done.
 *
 * Details (defenses, structures, TRs, economy) are the DEFAULT and only mode here —
 * bulk-vs-detail options removed. Fleet size: TODO, location on the page TBC.
 *
 * The report page and each base page parse THEMSELVES (a suite instance runs on them
 * because they are real game URLs) and hand back via a localStorage handshake — the
 * same mechanism the fleet monitor uses. The collector never reads another document
 * cross-window. One scan window is reused throughout.
 * ========================================================================== */
(function () {
  'use strict';
  if (typeof AEMapIntel === 'undefined') { console.warn('[map-intel] AEMapIntel missing'); return; }

  const SERVER = AEMapIntel.SERVER;
  const DB_WIN_NAME = 'ae_base_db';
  let dbWin = null;

  /* ---------- tiled control panel (shadow DOM, suite theme) ---------------- */
  let HOST, SHADOW, PANEL;
  const $ = s => (SHADOW ? SHADOW.querySelector(s) : null);

  function panelMarkup() {
    const s = AEMapIntel.stats();
    return `
      <div class="ae-panel__bar">
        <span class="ae-panel__dot mi-traffic idle" id="mi-traffic"></span>
        <span class="ae-panel__titles">
          <span class="ae-panel__title">Base Intel</span>
          <span class="ae-panel__sub">${SERVER} · rival base database</span>
        </span>
      </div>
      <div class="ae-panel__body" id="mi-body">
        <div class="mi-stats">
          <span><b id="mi-nbases">${s.bases}</b> bases</span>
          <span><b id="mi-nplayers">${s.players}</b> players</span>
          <span><b id="mi-nguilds">${s.guilds}</b> guilds</span>
        </div>
        <div class="mi-sec">
          <div class="mi-legend">Base scan</div>
          <div class="mi-row">
            <input id="mi-range" class="ae-input" placeholder="B35, B36:10-B36:14, B34:43:56">
          </div>
          <div class="mi-row">
            <label class="mi-sw" style="flex:1">interval s<input id="mi-delay" class="ae-input" type="number" min="0.6" step="0.1" style="width:56px"></label>
          </div>
          <div class="mi-row mi-jitter" id="mi-jitter" style="font-size:10px;color:var(--ae-text-dim)"></div>
          <div class="mi-row">
            <label class="mi-sw"><input type="checkbox" id="mi-visible"> open a watch tab (follow along)</label>
          </div>
          <div class="mi-row">
            <button id="mi-prep" class="ae-btn ae-btn--ghost" style="flex:1" title="Preview target count + ETA before scanning">Prep scan</button>
          </div>
          <div class="mi-row mi-prepinfo" id="mi-prepinfo" style="display:none;font-size:11px;color:var(--ae-secondary)"></div>
          <div class="mi-row">
            <button id="mi-scan" class="ae-btn ae-btn--primary" style="flex:1">▶ Scan bases</button>
          </div>
          <div class="mi-row" id="mi-runctl" style="display:none">
            <button id="mi-pause" class="ae-btn mi-btn-pause" style="flex:1">❚❚ Pause</button>
            <button id="mi-stop" class="ae-btn mi-btn-stop" style="flex:1">■ Stop</button>
          </div>
          <div class="mi-row" id="mi-resume-row" style="display:none">
            <button id="mi-resume" class="ae-btn mi-btn-resume" style="flex:1"></button>
            <button id="mi-resume-clear" class="ae-btn" title="Discard the unfinished scan">✕</button>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div class="mi-row mi-dbrow">
          <button id="mi-open" class="ae-btn ae-btn--primary" style="flex:2">Open database ↗</button>
          <button id="mi-csv" class="ae-btn ae-btn--ghost" style="flex:1">CSV</button>
        </div>
        <div class="ae-muted" id="mi-status" style="font-size:10px;min-height:12px"></div>
      </div>`;
  }

  const PANEL_CSS = `
    .mi-stats { display:flex; gap:10px; font-size:11px; margin-bottom:8px; color:var(--ae-text-muted); }
    .mi-stats b { color:var(--ae-primary); font-variant-numeric:tabular-nums; }
    .mi-row { display:flex; gap:6px; margin-bottom:6px; }
    .mi-row .ae-btn { flex:1; }
    .mi-sec { border:1px solid var(--ae-border); border-radius:var(--ae-radius-sm); padding:7px; margin:8px 0; }
    .mi-legend { font-size:10px; text-transform:uppercase; letter-spacing:.06em; color:var(--ae-text-dim); margin-bottom:6px; }
    .mi-sw { display:flex; align-items:center; gap:6px; font-size:11px; color:var(--ae-text-muted); }
    .mi-settings-back { position:fixed; inset:0; background:rgba(0,0,0,.55); display:flex; align-items:center; justify-content:center; z-index:20; }
    .mi-settings { background:var(--ae-surface); border:1px solid var(--ae-border); border-radius:10px; padding:16px; min-width:260px; max-width:320px; }
    .mi-settings-h { font-size:13px; color:var(--ae-primary); letter-spacing:.04em; margin-bottom:12px; }
    .mi-set-row { display:flex; align-items:center; justify-content:space-between; gap:10px; margin-bottom:6px; font-size:12px; }
    .mi-set-row input { width:80px; background:var(--ae-surface-2); color:var(--ae-text); border:1px solid var(--ae-border); border-radius:5px; padding:4px 6px; font:inherit; text-align:right; }
    .mi-set-hint { font-size:10px; color:var(--ae-text-dim); margin:-2px 0 8px; }
    .mi-set-preview { font-size:11px; color:var(--ae-secondary); margin:8px 0; padding:6px; border:1px solid var(--ae-border-soft); border-radius:6px; }
    .mi-set-btns { display:flex; gap:8px; margin-top:12px; }
    .mi-set-btns .ae-btn { flex:1; }
    .mi-banner { margin:6px 0; border:1px solid var(--ae-border); border-radius:var(--ae-radius-sm); overflow:hidden; background:var(--ae-surface); }
    .mi-banner-row { padding:5px 8px; }
    .mi-pill { font-size:10px; color:var(--ae-secondary); }
    .mi-pill.live { color:var(--ae-danger); }
    .mi-pill.done { color:var(--ae-success); }
    .mi-progwrap { height:5px; background:var(--ae-surface-2); overflow:hidden; }
    .mi-progbar { display:block; height:100%; width:0; background:linear-gradient(90deg,var(--ae-primary),var(--ae-secondary)); transition:width .3s ease; }
    .mi-traffic { transition: background .3s, box-shadow .3s; }
    .mi-traffic.idle { background: var(--ae-text-dim); box-shadow: none; }
    .mi-traffic.running { background: #ff4444; box-shadow: 0 0 8px #ff4444; animation: miTL 1s ease-in-out infinite; }
    .mi-traffic.paused { background: #ffb454; box-shadow: 0 0 6px #ffb454; animation: miTL 2.6s ease-in-out infinite; }
    .mi-traffic.done { background: #57e08b; box-shadow: 0 0 8px #57e08b; animation: miTL 3.2s ease-in-out infinite; }
    @keyframes miTL { 0%,100% { opacity: 1; } 50% { opacity: .4; } }
    .mi-btn-pause { border-color: var(--ae-secondary); color: var(--ae-secondary); }
    .mi-btn-pause:hover { background: color-mix(in srgb, var(--ae-secondary) 15%, transparent); }
    .mi-btn-pause.resuming { border-color: var(--ae-success); color: var(--ae-success); }
    .mi-btn-stop { border-color: var(--ae-danger); color: var(--ae-danger); }
    .mi-btn-stop:hover { background: color-mix(in srgb, var(--ae-danger) 15%, transparent); }
    .mi-btn-resume { border-color: var(--ae-secondary); color: var(--ae-secondary); }
    .mi-dbrow { margin-top: 6px; }
    .ae-hidden { display:none !important; }`;

  // Collapse persistence (per server)
  const UI_KEY = 'aeMapIntelUI::' + SERVER;
  function loadUi() { try { return JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(UI_KEY)) || {}; } catch (e) { return {}; } }
  function saveUi(patch) { let c = {}; try { c = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(UI_KEY)) || {}; } catch (e) {} try { localStorage.setItem(UI_KEY, JSON.stringify(Object.assign(c, patch))); } catch (e) {} }

  function setStatus(msg) { const el = $('#mi-status'); if (el) el.textContent = msg || ''; }
  function refreshStats() {
    const s = AEMapIntel.stats();
    if ($('#mi-nbases')) { $('#mi-nbases').textContent = s.bases; $('#mi-nplayers').textContent = s.players; $('#mi-nguilds').textContent = s.guilds; }
    pushToDb();
  }

  // ── live database-tab updates (restored: removed by mistake in the engine rewrite) ──
  let dbStatus = 'idle', dbLive = false;
  function pushToDb() {
    if (dbWin && !dbWin.closed) {
      try { dbWin.postMessage({ aeBases: AEMapIntel.allBases(), aeStatus: dbStatus, aeLive: dbLive }, '*'); } catch (e) {}
    }
  }
  function setDbStatus(msg, live) { dbStatus = msg; dbLive = !!live; pushToDb(); }

  // Poll the cross-window revision so an open DB tab refreshes as bases are written.
  // Also watch a command channel: the DB tab (a separate document that can't write GM
  // storage) posts delete-range requests here; the module executes them against the
  // authoritative store and re-mirrors. Commands carry an id so each runs once.
  const CMD_KEY = 'ae::bases::cmd::' + SERVER;
  const CMD_DONE_KEY = 'ae::bases::cmddone::' + SERVER;   // survives reloads
  let lastRev = -1;
  // Seed from storage so a command already executed in a PRIOR page load is never
  // re-run after a navigation/reload. Without this, lastCmdId reset to null on load
  // and the lingering command re-executed every time — silently re-deleting ranges.
  let lastCmdId = (() => { try { return localStorage.getItem(CMD_DONE_KEY); } catch (e) { return null; } })();
  // ── page-level scan banner: fixed across the top of the WHOLE tab, loud, animated ──
  let BANNER_HOST = null, BANNER_SHADOW = null, bannerEls = null;
  const BANNER_CSS = `
    :host { all: initial; }
    .ban { position: fixed; top: 0; left: 0; right: 0; z-index: 2147483000; font-family: var(--ae-font, system-ui, sans-serif);
           display: none; align-items: center; gap: 14px; padding: 8px 16px; box-sizing: border-box;
           color: #fff; border-bottom: 1px solid rgba(255,255,255,.25); backdrop-filter: blur(2px); }
    .ban.on { display: flex; }
    .ban .dot { width: 12px; height: 12px; border-radius: 50%; flex: 0 0 auto; box-shadow: 0 0 8px currentColor; }
    .ban .txt { font-size: 13px; font-weight: 700; letter-spacing: .02em; white-space: nowrap; }
    .ban .sub { font-size: 11px; opacity: .85; font-weight: 500; }
    .ban .barwrap { flex: 1 1 auto; height: 8px; background: rgba(0,0,0,.35); border-radius: 5px; overflow: hidden; min-width: 80px; }
    .ban .bar { display: block; height: 100%; width: 0; background: #fff; transition: width .3s ease; }
    .ban .close { cursor: pointer; font-size: 16px; opacity: .7; flex: 0 0 auto; }
    .ban .close:hover { opacity: 1; }
    /* RUNNING: loud pulsing red */
    .ban.running { background: rgba(190, 30, 30, .92); }
    .ban.running .dot { color: #ff5a5a; background: #ff5a5a; animation: banPulseRed 1s ease-in-out infinite; }
    @keyframes banPulseRed { 0%,100% { box-shadow: 0 0 6px #ff5a5a; opacity: 1; } 50% { box-shadow: 0 0 18px 4px #ff2a2a; opacity: .55; } }
    /* PAUSED: slow transparent orange */
    .ban.paused { background: rgba(200, 120, 20, .55); }
    .ban.paused .dot { color: #ffb454; background: #ffb454; animation: banPulseOrange 2.6s ease-in-out infinite; }
    @keyframes banPulseOrange { 0%,100% { opacity: .9; } 50% { opacity: .35; } }
    /* DONE: breathing transparent green */
    .ban.done { background: rgba(40, 150, 70, .5); }
    .ban.done .dot { color: #57e08b; background: #57e08b; animation: banBreatheGreen 3.2s ease-in-out infinite; }
    @keyframes banBreatheGreen { 0%,100% { opacity: .5; box-shadow: 0 0 6px #57e08b; } 50% { opacity: 1; box-shadow: 0 0 16px 3px #57e08b; } }
  `;
  function mountBanner() {
    if (BANNER_HOST) return;
    BANNER_HOST = document.createElement('div');
    BANNER_HOST.setAttribute('data-ae-script', 'map-intel-banner');
    BANNER_SHADOW = BANNER_HOST.attachShadow({ mode: 'open' });
    const st = document.createElement('style'); st.textContent = BANNER_CSS; BANNER_SHADOW.appendChild(st);
    const ban = document.createElement('div'); ban.className = 'ban';
    const dot = document.createElement('span'); dot.className = 'dot';
    const txt = document.createElement('span'); txt.className = 'txt'; txt.textContent = 'Base Intel';
    const sub = document.createElement('span'); sub.className = 'sub';
    const barwrap = document.createElement('span'); barwrap.className = 'barwrap';
    const bar = document.createElement('span'); bar.className = 'bar'; barwrap.appendChild(bar);
    const close = document.createElement('span'); close.className = 'close'; close.textContent = '×'; close.title = 'Hide banner';
    close.addEventListener('click', () => { ban.classList.remove('on'); });
    ban.appendChild(dot); ban.appendChild(txt); ban.appendChild(sub); ban.appendChild(barwrap); ban.appendChild(close);
    BANNER_SHADOW.appendChild(ban);
    document.documentElement.appendChild(BANNER_HOST);
    bannerEls = { ban, dot, txt, sub, bar };
  }
  function fmtEtaBanner(sec) { if (sec == null || sec < 0) return ''; const m = Math.floor(sec / 60), s = sec % 60; return (m ? m + 'm ' : '') + s + 's'; }
  let bannerWasScanning = false, bannerHideTimer = null;
  function updateBanner() {
    if (!bannerEls) return;
    const { ban, txt, sub, bar } = bannerEls;
    let scanning = false, paused = false, prog = null;
    try { scanning = localStorage.getItem('ae::bases::scanning::' + SERVER) === '1'; } catch (e) {}
    try { paused = localStorage.getItem('ae::bases::paused::' + SERVER) === '1'; } catch (e) {}
    try { prog = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('ae::bases::progress::' + SERVER)); } catch (e) {}
    function setBar(pct, frozen) { bar.style.transition = frozen ? 'none' : 'width .3s ease'; bar.style.width = pct + '%'; }
    function cls(state) { ban.className = 'ban on ' + state; }

    if (scanning && paused) {
      if (bannerHideTimer) { clearTimeout(bannerHideTimer); bannerHideTimer = null; }
      cls('paused');
      txt.textContent = 'SCAN PAUSED';
      if (prog && prog.total) { sub.textContent = prog.done + '/' + prog.total + ' — paused'; setBar(Math.round(100 * prog.done / prog.total), true); }
      else sub.textContent = 'paused';
      bannerWasScanning = true;
    } else if (scanning) {
      if (bannerHideTimer) { clearTimeout(bannerHideTimer); bannerHideTimer = null; }
      cls('running');
      txt.textContent = 'SCANNING';
      if (prog && (prog.phase === 'base' || prog.phase === 'trade') && prog.total) {
        const eta = prog.etaSec != null ? (' · ETA ' + fmtEtaBanner(prog.etaSec)) : '';
        const step = prog.phase === 'trade' ? ' · trade' : '';
        sub.textContent = prog.done + '/' + prog.total + (prog.resumed ? ' (resumed)' : '') + step + eta;
        setBar(Math.round(100 * prog.done / prog.total), !!prog.frozen);
      } else { sub.textContent = (prog && prog.label) ? prog.label : 'discovering…'; }
      bannerWasScanning = true;
    } else if (bannerWasScanning) {
      cls('done');
      txt.textContent = 'SCAN COMPLETE';
      sub.textContent = ''; setBar(100, false);
      if (!bannerHideTimer) bannerHideTimer = setTimeout(() => { ban.classList.remove('on'); bannerWasScanning = false; bannerHideTimer = null; }, 8000);
    } else {
      ban.classList.remove('on');
    }
    updateTrafficLight(scanning, paused);
  }

  // Reflect scan state on the panel's traffic-light indicator.
  function updateTrafficLight(scanning, paused) {
    const tl = $('#mi-traffic'); if (!tl) return;
    let state = 'idle';
    if (scanning && paused) state = 'paused';
    else if (scanning) state = 'running';
    else if (bannerWasScanning) state = 'done';
    tl.className = 'mi-traffic ' + state;
    tl.title = 'Scan: ' + state;
  }
  function startRevPoll() {
    setInterval(() => {
      const r = AEMapIntel.currentRev();
      if (r !== lastRev) { lastRev = r; refreshStats(); }
      updateBanner();
      // command channel
      let cmd = null;
      try { cmd = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(CMD_KEY)); } catch (e) {}
      if (cmd && cmd.id && cmd.id !== lastCmdId) {
        lastCmdId = cmd.id;
        try { localStorage.setItem(CMD_DONE_KEY, cmd.id); } catch (e) {}   // remember across reloads
        try { localStorage.removeItem(CMD_KEY); } catch (e) {}             // CONSUME: run exactly once
        if (cmd.type === 'deleteRange' && cmd.scope) {
          const res = AEMapIntel.deleteBasesInRange(cmd.scope);
          refreshStats();   // re-mirror happens via bumpRev inside delete
          setStatus('deleted ' + res.deleted + ' base(s) in ' + cmd.scope);
        }
      }
    }, 1000);
  }

  function onCSV() {
    const csv = AEMapIntel.toCSV(AEMapIntel.search({}));
    try { if (typeof GM_setClipboard === 'function') { GM_setClipboard(csv, { type: 'text', mimetype: 'text/csv' }); setStatus('CSV copied'); return; } } catch (e) {}
    const a = document.createElement('a'); a.href = 'data:text/csv;charset=utf-8,' + encodeURIComponent(csv);
    a.download = 'ae_bases_' + SERVER + '.csv'; a.click(); setStatus('CSV downloaded');
  }

    // NOTE: grid script is self-contained; no imports (blank window).
    return [

  /* Self-contained grid: columns, per-column text filters, click-to-sort, CSV.
     Stringified into the tab. Kept dependency-free on purpose. */
  const DB_GRID_JS = `
    // Per-defense abbreviations (from reference tool). Full structure name -> column.
    var DEF_ABBR = {
      'laser turrets':'LT','laser turret':'LT','missile turrets':'MT','missile turret':'MT',
      'plasma turrets':'PT','plasma turret':'PT','ion turrets':'IT','ion turret':'IT',
      'photon turrets':'PhT','photon turret':'PhT','disruptor turrets':'DT','disruptor turret':'DT',
      'deflection shields':'DS','deflection shield':'DS','barracks':'BR',
      'planetary shields':'PS','planetary shield':'PS','planetary ring':'PR','planetary rings':'PR'
    };
    var DEF_ORDER = ['BR','LT','MT','PT','IT','PhT','DT','DS','PS','PR'];
    function defAbbrev(name){ return DEF_ABBR[String(name||'').toLowerCase().trim()] || ''; }
    function defByAbbr(r){ var o={}; if(r.defenses){ Object.keys(r.defenses).forEach(function(n){ var a=defAbbrev(n); if(a) o[a]=r.defenses[n]; }); } return o; }
    function defCell(r,ab){ var m=defByAbbr(r); var d=m[ab]; if(!d) return ''; return (d.raw!=null?d.raw:(d.val+(d.max!=null?('/'+d.max):''))); }
    // Level of a named structure (tries alternate names AE uses); '' if the base lacks it.
    function structLevel(r){ var names=[].slice.call(arguments,1); if(!r.structures) return ''; for(var i=0;i<names.length;i++){ if(r.structures[names[i]]!=null) return String(r.structures[names[i]]); } return ''; }
    // Canonical structure list: key = column id, label = short header, names = the AE
    // structure name(s) to match (some have singular/plural variants), def = shown by default.
    // Strategic ones (jump gate, command centre, orbital base, econ centre) default-visible;
    // the economy/production buildings are parsed too but hidden until toggled on.
    var STRUCT_COLS = [
      { k:'st_jumpgate', label:'JG',    names:['Jump Gate'],                      def:true  },
      { k:'st_command',  label:'CmdC',  names:['Command Centers','Command Center'], def:true },
      { k:'st_orbital',  label:'OrbB',  names:['Orbital Base'],                   def:true  },
      { k:'st_econ',     label:'EconC', names:['Economic Centers','Economic Center'], def:true },
      { k:'st_urban',    label:'Urban', names:['Urban Structures'],               def:false },
      { k:'st_solar',    label:'Solar', names:['Solar Plants'],                   def:false },
      { k:'st_fusion',   label:'Fusion',names:['Fusion Plants'],                  def:false },
      { k:'st_antimatter',label:'AntiM',names:['Antimatter Plants'],             def:false },
      { k:'st_metal',    label:'Metal', names:['Metal Refineries'],               def:false },
      { k:'st_crystal',  label:'Crystal',names:['Crystal Mines'],                def:false },
      { k:'st_robotic',  label:'Robo',  names:['Robotic Factories'],              def:false },
      { k:'st_shipyard', label:'Shipyd',names:['Shipyards'],                      def:false },
      { k:'st_spaceport',label:'Sport', names:['Spaceports'],                     def:false },
      { k:'st_nanite',   label:'Nanite',names:['Nanite Factories'],               def:false },
      { k:'st_android',  label:'Andrd', names:['Android Factories'],              def:false },
      { k:'st_terraform',label:'Terra', names:['Terraform'],                      def:false },
      { k:'st_platform', label:'MLP',   names:['Multi-Level Platforms'],          def:false }
    ];

    // Inlined distance — three mutually-exclusive cases (galaxy | planar | ring).
    function coordP(c){ var m=/^([A-Za-z])(\\d{2}):(\\d{2}):(\\d{2}):(\\d+)$/.exec(String(c||'').trim()); return m?{g:+m[2],region:m[3],system:m[4],planet:+m[5]}:null; }
    function galDist(g1,g2){ if(g1===g2)return 0; var c1=Math.floor(g1/10),c2=Math.floor(g2/10); if(c1===c2)return Math.abs(g1-g2)*200; var lo=Math.min(g1,g2),hi=Math.max(g1,g2); return ((Math.floor(lo/10)*10+9)-lo)*200+(hi-(Math.floor(hi/10)*10))*200+2000; }
    function planar(a,b){ var x1=parseInt(a.region[0]+a.system[0],10),y1=parseInt(a.region[1]+a.system[1],10),x2=parseInt(b.region[0]+b.system[0],10),y2=parseInt(b.region[1]+b.system[1],10); var dx=x1-x2,dy=y1-y2,d=Math.sqrt(dx*dx+dy*dy); if(d>45)d+=0.8; return Math.ceil(d); }
    function dist(a,b){ var x=coordP(a),y=coordP(b); if(!x||!y)return null; if(x.g!==y.g)return galDist(x.g,y.g); if(x.region!==y.region||x.system!==y.system)return planar(x,y); var ra=Math.floor(x.planet/10),rb=Math.floor(y.planet/10); if(ra===rb&&x.planet===y.planet)return 0; return (ra===rb)?0.1:Math.round(0.2*Math.abs(ra-rb)*100)/100; }
    var refCoord = '';

    var ALL_COLS = [
      { k:'baseName', label:'Base', link:function(r){return r.baseId?'/base.aspx?base='+r.baseId:null;} },
      { k:'dist', label:'Dist', num:true, fmt:function(v,r){ if(!refCoord)return ''; var d=dist(refCoord,r.coord); return d==null?'':String(d); } },
      { k:'coord',    label:'Loc', link:function(r){return '/map.aspx?loc='+r.coord;} },
      { k:'player',   label:'Player', link:function(r){return r.playerId?'/profile.aspx?player='+r.playerId:null;} },
      { k:'guild',    label:'Guild' },
      { k:'economy',  label:'Eco', num:true, fmt:function(v,r){return r.economy!=null?String(r.economy):'';} },
      { k:'ownerIncome',label:'Income', num:true, fmt:function(v){return v!=null?v.toLocaleString():'';} },
      { k:'construction',label:'Const', num:true },
      { k:'production',label:'Prod', num:true },
      { k:'research', label:'Rsch', num:true },
      { k:'ownerFleet',label:'Owner Fleet', num:true, fmt:function(v){return v!=null?v.toLocaleString():'';} },
      { k:'guildFleet',label:'Guild Fleet', num:true, fmt:function(v){return v!=null?v.toLocaleString():'';} },
      { k:'otherFleet',label:'Other Fleet', num:true, fmt:function(v){return v!=null?v.toLocaleString():'';} },
      { k:'tradeRoutes',label:'TR Plunder', num:true, fmt:function(v,r){
          if(r.trPlunder!=null) return r.trPlunder.toLocaleString();
          if(r.recordedAge) return '—';           // fogged: TRs not visible
          return v&&v.raw? ('('+v.raw+')') : '';    // have route count but no plunder yet
        } }
    ];
    // one column per defense type
    DEF_ORDER.forEach(function(ab){ ALL_COLS.push({ k:'def_'+ab, label:ab, def:ab, fmt:function(v,r){return defCell(r,ab);} }); });
    // one column per structure (all parsed; visibility handled by default-hidden pref below)
    STRUCT_COLS.forEach(function(sc){ ALL_COLS.push({ k:sc.k, label:sc.label, num:true, structNames:sc.names, fmt:function(v,r){ return structLevel.apply(null,[r].concat(sc.names)); } }); });
    ALL_COLS = ALL_COLS.concat([
      { k:'structures',label:'Struct', fmt:function(v){ return v?Object.keys(v).length:''; } },
      { k:'commander', label:'Commander', fmt:function(v,r){ return r.commander&&r.commander.label?r.commander.label:''; } },
      { k:'population',label:'Pop', fmt:function(v){return v&&v.raw?v.raw:'';} },
      { k:'energy',   label:'Energy', fmt:function(v){return v&&v.raw?v.raw:'';} },
      { k:'recordedAge',label:'Intel Age' },
      { k:'lastSeen', label:'Recorded', num:true, fmt:function(v){return v?new Date(v).toISOString().replace('T',' ').slice(0,16):'';} }
    ]);
    var COLMAP={}; ALL_COLS.forEach(function(c){COLMAP[c.k]=c;});
    var COLKEYS=ALL_COLS.map(function(c){return c.k;});
    var SRV=(MIRROR_KEY.split('::').pop());
    var COLPREF_KEY='ae::bases::cols::'+SRV;
    var VIEWS_KEY='ae::bases::views::'+SRV;

    // Structure columns default to HIDDEN unless flagged def:true (strategic ones).
    var STRUCT_DEFAULT_HIDDEN = {};
    STRUCT_COLS.forEach(function(sc){ if(!sc.def) STRUCT_DEFAULT_HIDDEN[sc.k]=true; });

    function loadColPref(){
      var hadSaved=false;
      var pref = {order:COLKEYS.slice(),hidden:{}};
      try{ var p=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(COLPREF_KEY)); if(p&&p.order){ pref=p; hadSaved=true; } }catch(e){}
      // Merge in columns added since the pref was saved so they never silently disappear.
      // NEW structure columns not flagged def:true come in HIDDEN (all 16 exist; toggle on).
      COLKEYS.forEach(function(k){ if(pref.order.indexOf(k)<0){ pref.order.push(k); if(STRUCT_DEFAULT_HIDDEN[k]) pref.hidden[k]=true; } });
      if(!hadSaved){ Object.keys(STRUCT_DEFAULT_HIDDEN).forEach(function(k){ pref.hidden[k]=true; }); }
      // Drop any stale keys no longer in ALL_COLS.
      pref.order = pref.order.filter(function(k){ return COLMAP[k]; });
      return pref;
    }
    function saveColPref(){ try{localStorage.setItem(COLPREF_KEY,JSON.stringify(colpref));}catch(e){} }
    var colpref=loadColPref();
    function visibleCols(){ return colpref.order.filter(function(k){return COLMAP[k]&&!colpref.hidden[k];}).map(function(k){return COLMAP[k];}); }

    var state={rows:[],filters:{},sort:'economy',desc:true,seen:{}};
    function esc(s){return String(s==null?'':s).replace(/[&<>"]/g,function(c){return{'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;'}[c];});}
    function cellText(c,r){ var v=r[c.k]; return c.fmt?c.fmt(v,r):(v==null?'':String(v)); }

    // Leading numeric value of a cell: for defence cols the current units before '/'
    // (e.g. "12.5/15" -> 12.5); for num cols the number; non-numeric -> 0 so sorting and
    // >/</= filters behave consistently.
    function numOf(c,r){
      var t=cellText(c,r);
      if(c.def) t=String(t).split('/')[0];
      var m=String(t).replace(/,/g,'').match(/-?\\d+(?:\\.\\d+)?/);
      return m?parseFloat(m[0]):0;
    }
    // Parse an operator filter like ">5", "<=10", "=12". Returns a test fn or null.
    function numFilter(f){
      var m=String(f).trim().match(/^(>=|<=|>|<|=)?\\s*(-?\\d+(?:\\.\\d+)?)$/);
      if(!m) return null;
      var op=m[1]||'=', n=parseFloat(m[2]);
      return function(val){
        switch(op){ case '>':return val>n; case '<':return val<n; case '>=':return val>=n; case '<=':return val<=n; default:return val===n; }
      };
    }

    function apply(){
      var cols=visibleCols();
      var out=state.rows.filter(function(r){
        return cols.every(function(c){ var f=(state.filters[c.k]||'').trim(); if(!f) return true;
          if(c.def||c.num){ var nf=numFilter(f); if(nf) return nf(numOf(c,r)); }   // >,<,=,>=,<= on numeric cols
          var hay=cellText(c,r); if(c.def) hay=String(hay).split('/')[0].trim();
          return hay.toLowerCase().indexOf(f.toLowerCase())!==-1; });
      });
      out.sort(function(a,b){
        var x, y, d=state.desc?-1:1;
        if(state.sort==='dist'){ x=refCoord?dist(refCoord,a.coord):0; y=refCoord?dist(refCoord,b.coord):0; return ((x||0)-(y||0))*d; }
        var sc=COLMAP[state.sort];
        if(sc&&(sc.def||sc.num)){ x=numOf(sc,a); y=numOf(sc,b); return ((x||0)-(y||0))*d; }   // numeric sort, missing -> 0
        x=a[state.sort]; y=b[state.sort];
        if(typeof x==='string'||typeof y==='string') return String(x||'').localeCompare(String(y||''))*-d;
        return ((x||0)-(y||0))*d; });
      return out;
    }

    // Build header + filter row ONCE; only the tbody is re-rendered on filter/poll,
    // so a focused filter input is never destroyed mid-typing.
    function buildHead(){
      var cols=visibleCols();
      var thead=document.getElementById('thead');
      var h='<tr>';
      cols.forEach(function(c,i){ var ar=state.sort===c.k?('<span class="ar">'+(state.desc?'▼':'▲')+'</span>'):''; h+='<th data-k="'+c.k+'" class="'+(i===0?'first':'ctr')+'">'+esc(c.label)+' '+ar+'</th>'; });
      h+='</tr><tr class="filters">';
      cols.forEach(function(c,i){ var ph=(c.def||c.num)?' placeholder="&gt;5 &lt;10 =3"':''; h+='<th class="'+(i===0?'first':'ctr')+'"><input data-k="'+c.k+'"'+ph+' value="'+esc(state.filters[c.k]||'')+'"></th>'; });
      h+='</tr>';
      thead.innerHTML=h;
      thead.querySelectorAll('th[data-k]').forEach(function(th){ th.addEventListener('click',function(){ var k=th.getAttribute('data-k'); if(state.sort===k)state.desc=!state.desc; else{state.sort=k;state.desc=true;} buildHead(); renderBody(); }); });
      thead.querySelectorAll('tr.filters input').forEach(function(inp){ inp.addEventListener('input',function(){ state.filters[inp.getAttribute('data-k')]=inp.value; renderBody(); }); });
    }

    function renderBody(flashCoords){
      var cols=visibleCols();
      var data=apply();
      document.getElementById('count').textContent=data.length+' / '+state.rows.length+' bases';
      var tb=document.getElementById('tbody');
      if(!state.rows.length){ tb.innerHTML='<tr><td class="empty">No bases yet. Run a scan or visit base pages to populate.</td></tr>'; return; }
      var h='';
      data.forEach(function(r){
        var fl=flashCoords&&flashCoords[r.coord]?' class="flash"':'';
        h+='<tr'+fl+'>';
        cols.forEach(function(c,i){
          var disp=cellText(c,r); var lnk=c.link&&c.link(r);
          var cls=(i===0?'first':'ctr');
          // Defence under 100% strength (current/max) -> red text. current is the number
          // before '/', max after. e.g. "10/20" = 50% -> under strength.
          if(c.def && disp){ var pm=String(disp).split('/'); if(pm.length===2){ var cur=parseFloat(pm[0]), mx=parseFloat(pm[1]); if(!isNaN(cur)&&!isNaN(mx)&&mx>0&&cur<mx) cls+=' underdef'; } }
          var inner=(lnk?('<a href="'+esc(lnk)+'" target="_blank">'+esc(disp)+'</a>'):esc(disp));
          if(c.k==='player'&&r.playerId&&disp) inner+=' <span class="pico" data-player="'+esc(r.playerId)+'" title="Player profile">\\u24D8</span>';
          h+='<td class="'+cls+'">'+inner+'</td>';
        });
        h+='</tr>';
      });
      tb.innerHTML=h;
    }

    function renderGrid(rows,flashCoords){ if(rows)state.rows=rows; buildHead(); renderBody(flashCoords); }

    // ── live self-poll: mirror + rev + scan flag; flash new rows; complete pill ──
    var lastRev=-1, wasScanning=false;
    function setStatus(txt,cls){ var st=document.getElementById('status'); if(st){ st.textContent=txt; st.className='status'+(cls?(' '+cls):''); } }
    function startDbPolling(){
      setInterval(function(){
        var rev=parseInt(localStorage.getItem(REV_KEY),10)||0;
        var scanning=localStorage.getItem(SCANFLAG_KEY)==='1';
        var prog=null; try{prog=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(PROG_KEY));}catch(e){}
        var pw=document.getElementById('progwrap'), pb=document.getElementById('progbar');
        if(scanning){
          wasScanning=true;
          // base AND trade phases both carry done/total now; trade sets frozen=true so the
          // bar HOLDS (no transition) at the current base's position instead of resetting.
          if(prog&&(prog.phase==='base'||prog.phase==='trade')&&prog.total){
            var pct=Math.round(100*prog.done/prog.total);
            var etaTxt = prog.etaSec!=null ? (' · ETA '+fmtEtaDb(prog.etaSec)) : '';
            var sub = prog.phase==='trade' ? ' · trade' : '';
            setStatus('scanning '+prog.done+'/'+prog.total+(prog.resumed?' (resumed)':'')+sub+etaTxt,'live');
            if(pw&&pb){ pw.className='progwrap on'; pb.style.transition = prog.frozen ? 'none' : 'width .3s ease'; pb.style.width=pct+'%'; }
          } else {
            setStatus('scanning… '+((prog&&prog.label)?prog.label:''),'live');
            if(pw){ pw.className='progwrap on'; if(pb) pb.style.width='0%'; }
          }
        }
        else if(wasScanning){ setStatus('Scan Complete — Idle','done'); if(pw) pw.className='progwrap'; }
        else { setStatus('idle',''); if(pw) pw.className='progwrap'; }
        if(rev!==lastRev){
          lastRev=rev;
          var rows=[]; try{rows=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(MIRROR_KEY))||[];}catch(e){}
          var flash={}; rows.forEach(function(r){ if(r.coord&&!state.seen[r.coord]){ flash[r.coord]=1; state.seen[r.coord]=1; } });
          state.rows=rows; renderBody(flash);   // header preserved -> filter focus safe
        }
      },800);
    }
    function fmtEtaDb(sec){ if(sec==null||sec<0)return ''; var m=Math.floor(sec/60),s=sec%60; return (m?m+'m ':'')+s+'s'; }

    // ── column settings + saved views ──
    function openColSettings(){
      var back=document.createElement('div'); back.className='ae-colset-back';
      var panel=document.createElement('div'); panel.className='ae-colset';
      var title=document.createElement('div'); title.className='ae-colset-title'; title.textContent='Columns — drag to reorder, toggle to show/hide'; panel.appendChild(title);

      // saved views bar
      var vbar=document.createElement('div'); vbar.className='ae-views';
      var vsel=document.createElement('select'); vsel.className='ae-vsel';
      var def=document.createElement('option'); def.value=''; def.textContent='— saved views —'; vsel.appendChild(def);
      var views=loadViews(); Object.keys(views).forEach(function(name){ var o=document.createElement('option'); o.value=name; o.textContent=name; vsel.appendChild(o); });
      vsel.addEventListener('change',function(){ if(vsel.value&&views[vsel.value]){ colpref=JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(views[vsel.value])); saveColPref(); document.body.removeChild(back); renderGrid(); openColSettings(); } });
      var vsave=document.createElement('button'); vsave.textContent='Save view'; vsave.className='ae-vbtn';
      vsave.addEventListener('click',function(){ var n=prompt('Name this view:'); if(!n)return; var vv=loadViews(); vv[n]=JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(colpref)); saveViews(vv); document.body.removeChild(back); openColSettings(); });
      var vdel=document.createElement('button'); vdel.textContent='Delete'; vdel.className='ae-vbtn';
      vdel.addEventListener('click',function(){ if(!vsel.value)return; var vv=loadViews(); delete vv[vsel.value]; saveViews(vv); document.body.removeChild(back); openColSettings(); });
      vbar.appendChild(vsel); vbar.appendChild(vsave); vbar.appendChild(vdel); panel.appendChild(vbar);

      var list=document.createElement('div'); list.className='ae-colset-list';
      colpref.order.forEach(function(k){ if(!COLMAP[k])return;
        var row=document.createElement('div'); row.className='ae-colset-row'; row.setAttribute('draggable','true'); row.dataset.k=k;
        var grip=document.createElement('span'); grip.textContent='⠿'; grip.className='ae-colset-grip';
        var cb=document.createElement('input'); cb.type='checkbox'; cb.checked=!colpref.hidden[k];
        cb.addEventListener('change',function(){ colpref.hidden[k]=!cb.checked; saveColPref(); renderGrid(); });
        var lbl=document.createElement('span'); lbl.textContent=COLMAP[k].label; lbl.className='ae-colset-lbl';
        row.appendChild(grip); row.appendChild(cb); row.appendChild(lbl);
        row.addEventListener('dragstart',function(){ row.classList.add('dragging'); });
        row.addEventListener('dragend',function(){ row.classList.remove('dragging'); colpref.order=[].slice.call(list.children).map(function(el){return el.dataset.k;}); saveColPref(); renderGrid(); });
        row.addEventListener('dragover',function(e){ e.preventDefault(); var d=list.querySelector('.dragging'); if(!d||d===row)return; var rc=row.getBoundingClientRect(); var after=(e.clientY-rc.top)/rc.height>0.5; list.insertBefore(d,after?row.nextSibling:row); });
        list.appendChild(row);
      });
      panel.appendChild(list);
      var close=document.createElement('button'); close.textContent='Done'; close.className='ae-colset-done';
      close.addEventListener('click',function(){ document.body.removeChild(back); });
      panel.appendChild(close);
      back.appendChild(panel);
      back.addEventListener('click',function(e){ if(e.target===back)document.body.removeChild(back); });
      document.body.appendChild(back);
    }
    function loadViews(){ try{return JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(VIEWS_KEY))||{};}catch(e){return {};} }
    function saveViews(v){ try{localStorage.setItem(VIEWS_KEY,JSON.stringify(v));}catch(e){} }

    var refInp=document.getElementById('ref'), refBtn=document.getElementById('refbtn');
    function applyRef(){ refCoord=(refInp?refInp.value.trim():''); if(state.sort!=='dist'&&refCoord){ state.sort='dist'; state.desc=false; } renderGrid(); }
    if(refInp){ refInp.addEventListener('input',function(){ refCoord=refInp.value.trim(); renderBody(); });
      refInp.addEventListener('keydown',function(e){ if(e.key==='Enter') applyRef(); }); }
    if(refBtn){ refBtn.addEventListener('click',applyRef); }
    // ── player profile overlay: bases owned + total eco (from the rows) + techs and
    //    fleet spec (from the tech harvester's localStorage mirror). No commanders.
    function openProfile(pid){
      var box=document.getElementById('profile'); if(!box) return;
      var rows=state.rows.filter(function(r){ return String(r.playerId||'')===String(pid); });
      var name=rows.length?(rows[0].player||('#'+pid)):('#'+pid);
      var guild=rows.length?(rows[0].guild||''):'';
      var totalEco=0; rows.forEach(function(r){ totalEco+=(r.economy||0); });
      var mir=null; try{ mir=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(TECHMIRROR_KEY)); }catch(e){}
      var mp=mir&&mir.players?mir.players[pid]:null;
      var h='<div class="ph">'+esc(name)+(guild?(' <span style="color:var(--ae-secondary)">['+esc(guild)+']</span>'):'')+'<span class="x" id="profile-x">\u00d7</span></div>';
      // bases
      h+='<div class="ps"><h4>Bases \u00b7 '+rows.length+' \u00b7 total eco '+totalEco.toLocaleString()+'</h4><table>';
      rows.slice().sort(function(a,b){return (b.economy||0)-(a.economy||0);}).slice(0,12).forEach(function(r){
        h+='<tr><td>'+esc(r.coord||'')+'</td><td>'+esc(r.baseName||'')+'</td><td class="r">'+(r.economy!=null?r.economy:'')+'</td></tr>';
      });
      if(rows.length>12) h+='<tr><td colspan="3" style="opacity:.6">\u2026 '+(rows.length-12)+' more</td></tr>';
      h+='</table></div>';
      // fleet spec
      h+='<div class="ps"><h4>Fleet spec</h4>';
      // shown from the FIRST battle report; confidence is stated, not withheld
      var exposures=mp&&mp.spec?(mp.spec.obs+mp.spec.drops):0;
      if(mp&&mp.spec){ h+='<div><b>'+esc(mp.spec.dom)+'-led</b> \u00b7 '+esc(mp.spec.label)+'</div><div style="opacity:.6;margin-top:2px">'+mp.spec.obs+' observation(s), '+mp.spec.drops+' drop wave(s) excluded'+(exposures<5?' \u00b7 low confidence ('+exposures+' BR'+(exposures===1?'':'s')+')':'')+'</div>'; }
      else h+='<div style="opacity:.6">no battle-report observations yet</div>';
      h+='</div>';
      // techs
      h+='<div class="ps"><h4>Tech (harvested)</h4>';
      if(mp&&mp.techs&&Object.keys(mp.techs).length){ h+='<table>';
        Object.keys(mp.techs).sort().forEach(function(t){ h+='<tr><td>'+esc(t)+'</td><td class="r">'+mp.techs[t]+'</td></tr>'; });
        h+='</table>'; }
      else h+='<div style="opacity:.6">no harvested tech for this player</div>';
      h+='</div>';
      box.innerHTML=h; box.className='on';
      var x=document.getElementById('profile-x'); if(x) x.addEventListener('click',function(){ box.className=''; });
    }
    document.getElementById('tbody').addEventListener('click',function(e){
      var t=e.target; if(t&&t.classList&&t.classList.contains('pico')){ openProfile(t.getAttribute('data-player')); }
    });

    var clrBtn=document.getElementById('clrbtn'), clrInp=document.getElementById('clr');
    if(clrBtn&&clrInp){
      clrBtn.addEventListener('click',function(){
        var scope=(clrInp.value||'').trim();
        if(!scope){ clrInp.focus(); return; }
        // count what WOULD be deleted so the confirm is honest
        function coordP(c){ return /^[A-Za-z]\\d{2}:\\d{2}:\\d{2}:\\d+$/.test(c); }
        var affected=state.rows.filter(function(r){
          // simple prefix match on the scope's leading segments (galaxy/region/system);
          // ranges are handled authoritatively by the module — this is just for the count
          var s=scope.toUpperCase(), c=(r.coord||'').toUpperCase();
          if(s.indexOf('-')>=0) return true;   // range: let the module report the real count
          return c===s || c.indexOf(s+':')===0;
        }).length;
        var msg='Permanently delete stored intel for '+scope+'?';
        if(affected&&scope.indexOf('-')<0) msg+=' ('+affected+' base'+(affected===1?'':'s')+' in view)';
        if(!window.confirm(msg)) return;
        try{ localStorage.setItem(CMD_KEY, JSON.stringify({ id: Date.now()+'-'+Math.random().toString(36).slice(2,7), type:'deleteRange', scope:scope })); }catch(e){}
        clrInp.value='';
        setStatus('clearing '+scope+'\\u2026', 'live');
        // the module executes + bumps the rev; our poll will pick up the shrunk mirror
      });
    }
    document.getElementById('cols').addEventListener('click',openColSettings);
    document.getElementById('csv').addEventListener('click',function(){
      var cols=visibleCols(),data=apply();
      var head=cols.map(function(c){return c.label;}).join(',');
      var body=data.map(function(r){ return cols.map(function(c){ var v=cellText(c,r); return /[",\\n]/.test(v)?('"'+v.replace(/"/g,'""')+'"'):v; }).join(','); }).join('\\n');
      var blob=new Blob([head+'\\n'+body],{type:'text/csv'}); var url=URL.createObjectURL(blob);
      var a=document.createElement('a'); a.href=url; a.download='ae_bases_'+Date.now()+'.csv'; document.body.appendChild(a); a.click(); document.body.removeChild(a);
      setTimeout(function(){URL.revokeObjectURL(url);},4000);
    });
  `;

  // Restored: opens the database tab and writes the grid document into it.
  const DB_DOC_VERSION = 29;   // bump when the DB document markup/poll changes
  function openDatabase() {
    const rows = AEMapIntel.allBases();
    try { dbWin = window.open('', DB_WIN_NAME); } catch (e) { dbWin = null; }
    if (!dbWin) { setStatus('allow pop-ups to open the database'); return; }
    // window.open with an existing named tab REUSES it without rewriting — so an old tab
    // keeps running stale code (missing new features like the progress bar/ETA). Always
    // rewrite the document so "Open database" guarantees the current version.
    try {
      dbWin.document.open();
      dbWin.document.write(dbDocument(rows));
      dbWin.document.close();
      dbWin.focus();
    } catch (e) { setStatus('could not refresh the database tab: ' + e.message); }
  }

  // Show the actual jitter range for the chosen interval, same model as the engine's
  // pacing: delay = base*(1±0.30) + rand(0..1.5)s, floored.
  function updateJitterRange() {
    const el = $('#mi-jitter'); if (!el) return;
    const base = Math.max(0.6, parseFloat($('#mi-delay').value) || AEMapIntel.getCrawlCfg().baseDelaySec);
    const floor = AEMapIntel.getCrawlCfg().floorSec || 0.6;
    const lo = Math.max(floor, base * 0.7).toFixed(1);
    const hi = (base * 1.3 + 1.5).toFixed(1);
    const mean = AEMapIntel.meanDelaySec(base, floor).toFixed(1);
    el.textContent = 'actual delay ' + lo + '\u2013' + hi + 's per page (\u2248' + mean + 's avg with jitter)';
  }

  let prepped = null;   // { targets, count, etaSec } from the last Prep scan
  async function onPrep() {
    if (AEMapIntel.scanActive()) return;
    const raw = ($('#mi-range').value || '').trim();
    if (!raw) { setStatus('enter a scope first'); return; }
    const interval = Math.max(0.6, parseFloat($('#mi-delay').value) || AEMapIntel.getCrawlCfg().baseDelaySec);
    $('#mi-prep').disabled = true; setStatus('prepping \u2014 running report(s)\u2026');
    try {
      prepped = await AEMapIntel.prepScan(raw, { intervalSec: interval });
      prepped.scope = raw; prepped.interval = interval;
      const m = Math.floor(prepped.etaSec / 60), sec = prepped.etaSec % 60;
      const eta = (m ? m + 'm ' : '') + sec + 's';
      const info = $('#mi-prepinfo');
      info.style.display = ''; info.textContent = prepped.count + ' bases to scan \u00b7 ETA \u2248 ' + eta + ' @ ' + interval + 's';
      setStatus('prepped \u2014 press Scan bases to run');
      refreshStats();
    } catch (e) { setStatus('prep failed: ' + e.message); }
    $('#mi-prep').disabled = false;
  }

  // Show/update the Resume row if an unfinished scan is persisted.
  function refreshResumeRow() {
    const row = $('#mi-resume-row'), btn = $('#mi-resume');
    if (!row || !btn) return;
    const info = AEMapIntel.scanResumeInfo();
    if (info && !AEMapIntel.scanActive()) {
      btn.textContent = 'Resume scan (' + info.left + ' of ' + info.total + ' left)';
      row.style.display = '';
    } else {
      row.style.display = 'none';
    }
  }

  async function onResume() {
    const info = AEMapIntel.scanResumeInfo();
    if (!info) { refreshResumeRow(); return; }
    await driveScan(info.scope, { resume: true });
  }

  function onResumeClear() {
    AEMapIntel.clearScanResume();
    refreshResumeRow();
    setStatus('unfinished scan discarded');
  }

  let watchWin = null;
  async function onScan() {
    if (AEMapIntel.scanActive()) { setStatus('scan already running'); return; }
    const raw = ($('#mi-range').value || '').trim();
    if (!raw) { setStatus('enter a scope, e.g. B37, B37:12:92, or B37:12:92:11'); return; }
    AEMapIntel.clearScanResume();   // a NEW scan discards any stale unfinished one
    await driveScan(raw, {});
  }

  // Shared driver for both fresh scans and resumes.
  async function driveScan(raw, extraOpts) {
    const plan = AEMapIntel.planScan(raw);
    if (!plan || (!plan.astros.length && !plan.galaxies.length)) { setStatus('could not parse scope'); return; }
    const interval = Math.max(0.6, parseFloat($('#mi-delay').value) || AEMapIntel.getCrawlCfg().baseDelaySec);
    const visible = !!($('#mi-visible') && $('#mi-visible').checked);
    if (visible) { try { watchWin = window.open('about:blank', 'ae_watch'); } catch (e) { watchWin = null; } }

    $('#mi-scan').disabled = true; if($('#mi-runctl')) $('#mi-runctl').style.display='';
    if($('#mi-pause')){ $('#mi-pause').textContent='❚❚ Pause'; $('#mi-pause').classList.remove('resuming'); }
    refreshResumeRow();
    try { localStorage.setItem('ae::bases::scanning::' + SERVER, '1'); } catch (e) {}

    const started = Date.now();
    const cfgNow = AEMapIntel.getCrawlCfg();
    const oneDelay = AEMapIntel.meanDelaySec(interval, cfgNow.floorSec) + 0.4;   // one fetch + overhead
    // With TR-plunder on, each base does a SECOND fetch (its own jittered interval), so
    // budget ~2 delays per base. Only unfogged bases actually trade-fetch, but we can't
    // know fog status ahead of time, so this is a conservative upper bound.
    const perPage = cfgNow.tradePlunder ? oneDelay * 2 : oneDelay;
    let lastBaseProg = null;   // module-side: the last base done/total, carried into trade writes
    let lastDone = 0;          // monotonic guard: displayed progress never moves backwards
    const PROG_KEY = 'ae::bases::progress::' + SERVER;
    function writeProgress(o) { try { localStorage.setItem(PROG_KEY, JSON.stringify(o)); } catch (e) {} }
    function fmtEta(sec) { if (sec == null || sec < 0) return ''; const mm = Math.floor(sec / 60), ss = sec % 60; return (mm ? mm + 'm ' : '') + ss + 's'; }
    const r = await AEMapIntel.runScan(raw, Object.assign({ intervalSec: interval }, extraOpts), (p) => {
      const secs = Math.round((Date.now() - started) / 1000);
      if (p.phase === 'report') { setStatus(p.error ? ('report ' + p.current + ': ' + p.error) : ('report ' + p.current + (p.found != null ? ' → ' + p.found + ' bases' : ' …'))); writeProgress({ phase: 'report', current: p.current, label: 'discovering' }); }
      else if (p.phase === 'astro') { setStatus('astro ' + p.current + (p.error ? ' (' + p.error + ')' : '')); writeProgress({ phase: 'astro', current: p.current, label: 'discovering' }); }
      else if (p.phase === 'base') {
        // resume-aware: X = bases done since THIS run started, Y = remaining at (re)start.
        // fresh scan -> startIndex 0 -> normal done/total.
        const start0 = p.startIndex || 0;
        let done = (p.index - start0) + (p.parsed ? 1 : 0);
        const total = p.total - start0;
        // MONOTONIC: two emits per base (pre/post parse) + interleaved trade writes can make
        // the raw count stutter or dip; never let the displayed progress move backwards.
        if (done < lastDone) done = lastDone; else lastDone = done;
        const left = Math.max(0, total - done);
        const etaSec = Math.round(left * perPage);
        const eta = fmtEta(etaSec);
        lastBaseProg = { done: done, total: total, etaSec: etaSec, resumed: !!p.resumed };
        setStatus('base ' + p.current + ' · ' + done + '/' + total + (eta ? ' · ETA ' + eta : ''));
        writeProgress({ phase: 'base', done: done, total: total, etaSec: etaSec, resumed: !!p.resumed, current: p.current, frozen: false });
        if (visible && watchWin && !watchWin.closed && p.current) { try { watchWin.location.href = location.origin + '/map.aspx?loc=' + encodeURIComponent(p.current); } catch (e) {} }
      }
      else if (p.phase === 'trade') {
        // FREEZE the bar at the current base's position during the trade sub-fetch. Carry
        // the last base's done/total so the DB tab doesn't need to have observed the base
        // phase (its 800ms poll can miss fast writes). frozen=true tells it to hold.
        const lp = lastBaseProg || {};
        writeProgress({ phase: 'trade', frozen: true, done: lp.done, total: lp.total, etaSec: lp.etaSec, resumed: lp.resumed, current: p.current, label: 'trade' });
      }
      else if (p.phase === 'done') { setStatus('done · ' + p.bases + ' bases, ' + p.errors + ' errors, ' + secs + 's'); writeProgress({ phase: 'done', done: p.done, total: p.done }); }
      else if (p.phase === 'stopped') { setStatus('stopped — resumable'); writeProgress({ phase: 'stopped' }); }
      else if (p.phase === 'error') { setStatus('error: ' + p.error); writeProgress({ phase: 'error' }); }
      updateBanner();   // same-document panel banner: refresh now (after writeProgress), no poll lag
    });

    try { localStorage.setItem('ae::bases::scanning::' + SERVER, '0'); } catch (e) {}
    try { localStorage.removeItem('ae::bases::progress::' + SERVER); } catch (e) {}
    $('#mi-scan').disabled = false; if($('#mi-runctl')) $('#mi-runctl').style.display='none';
    try { localStorage.removeItem('ae::bases::paused::' + SERVER); } catch (e) {}
    refreshStats();
    refreshResumeRow();
    return r;
  }
  function onStopScan() { AEMapIntel.stopScan(); try { localStorage.setItem('ae::bases::scanning::' + SERVER, '0'); localStorage.removeItem('ae::bases::paused::' + SERVER); } catch (e) {} setStatus('stopping…'); }
  function onPauseScan() {
    if (!AEMapIntel.scanActive()) return;
    if (AEMapIntel.scanPaused()) {
      AEMapIntel.resumeScanRun();
      try { localStorage.setItem('ae::bases::paused::' + SERVER, '0'); } catch (e) {}
      if ($('#mi-pause')) { $('#mi-pause').textContent = '❚❚ Pause'; $('#mi-pause').classList.remove('resuming'); }
      setStatus('resuming…');
    } else {
      AEMapIntel.pauseScan();
      try { localStorage.setItem('ae::bases::paused::' + SERVER, '1'); } catch (e) {}
      if ($('#mi-pause')) { $('#mi-pause').textContent = '▶ Resume'; $('#mi-pause').classList.add('resuming'); }
      setStatus('paused');
    }
    updateBanner();
  }

  /* ---------- lazy harvest: parse map/base pages the user visits normally -- */
  function lazyHarvest() {
    try {
      if (/\/base\.aspx/i.test(location.pathname)) {
        const baseId = new URLSearchParams(location.search).get('base');
        const r = AEMapIntel.ingestLoadedPage(document, { base: baseId });
        if (r.bases) refreshStats();
      } else if (/\/map\.aspx/i.test(location.pathname)) {
        const coord = new URLSearchParams(location.search).get('loc');
        const rows = AEMapIntel.parseBasesTable(document, coord);
        if (rows.length) { rows.forEach(r => AEMapIntel.upsertBase(Object.assign({ via: 'map' }, r))); refreshStats(); }
      }
    } catch (e) {}
  }

  // master hide: abort any running crawl and dismiss every popup
  window.addEventListener('ae-master-hidden', (e) => {
    if (!(e.detail && e.detail.hidden)) return;
    try { AEMapIntel.stopCrawl(); } catch (e2) { /* not crawling */ }
    try { window.AE_DROPTIPS_CLOSE_ALL && window.AE_DROPTIPS_CLOSE_ALL(); } catch (e2) { /* */ }
  });
  window.AE_MAPINTEL_PUSH = function () { refreshStats(); };

  // ── settings overlay (shadow-DOM modal, Trusted-Types safe) ──
  function openSettings() {
    if (SHADOW.querySelector('.mi-settings-back')) return;
    const cfg = AEMapIntel.getCrawlCfg();
    const back = document.createElement('div'); back.className = 'mi-settings-back';
    const panel = document.createElement('div'); panel.className = 'mi-settings';
    const h = document.createElement('div'); h.className = 'mi-settings-h'; h.textContent = 'Base Intel — settings'; panel.appendChild(h);

    function field(label, id, val, step, min, hint) {
      const row = document.createElement('label'); row.className = 'mi-set-row';
      const span = document.createElement('span'); span.textContent = label; row.appendChild(span);
      const inp = document.createElement('input'); inp.type = 'number'; inp.id = id; inp.value = val;
      if (step != null) inp.step = step; if (min != null) inp.min = min;
      row.appendChild(inp); panel.appendChild(row);
      if (hint) { const hc = document.createElement('div'); hc.className = 'mi-set-hint'; hc.textContent = hint; panel.appendChild(hc); }
      return inp;
    }

    const inInterval = field('Parse interval (s)', 'set-interval', cfg.baseDelaySec, '0.5', '0.6', 'Mean delay between page loads.');
    const inJitter = field('Jitter (\u00b1%)', 'set-jitter', Math.round(cfg.jitterPct * 100), '5', '0', 'Randomness applied to each delay.');
    const inFloor = field('Minimum average (s)', 'set-floor', cfg.floorSec, '0.1', '0.6', 'Delay never averages below this (hard min 0.6s).');

    // trade-plunder opt-in (an extra fetch per base -> its own delay interval)
    const tpRow = document.createElement('label'); tpRow.className = 'mi-set-row';
    const tpSpan = document.createElement('span'); tpSpan.textContent = 'Fetch TR plunder'; tpRow.appendChild(tpSpan);
    const tpBox = document.createElement('input'); tpBox.type = 'checkbox'; tpBox.checked = !!cfg.tradePlunder; tpRow.appendChild(tpBox); panel.appendChild(tpRow);
    // rankings: manual top-300 fetch (paced). URL/table shape UNVERIFIED until a real
    // rankings page is captured — errors will surface in the status line below.
    const rkRow = document.createElement('div'); rkRow.className = 'mi-row';
    const rkBtn = document.createElement('button'); rkBtn.className = 'ae-btn'; rkBtn.style.flex = '1';
    rkBtn.textContent = 'Update rankings (top 300)';
    const rkInfo = document.createElement('div'); rkInfo.className = 'ae-muted'; rkInfo.style.fontSize = '10px';
    const rkNow = AEMapIntel.getRankings();
    rkInfo.textContent = rkNow && rkNow.updated ? (Object.keys(rkNow.players || {}).length + ' players \u00b7 ' + Math.round((Date.now() - rkNow.updated) / 3600000) + 'h old') : 'not fetched yet';
    rkBtn.addEventListener('click', async () => {
      rkBtn.disabled = true;
      try {
        const r = await AEMapIntel.fetchRankings({}, (p) => {
          if (p.phase === 'rank') rkInfo.textContent = p.view + ' p' + p.page + ' \u00b7 ' + p.step + '/' + p.total + (p.error ? ' \u2014 ' + p.error : '');
          else if (p.phase === 'rank-done') rkInfo.textContent = p.count + ' players fetched, ' + p.errors + ' error(s)';
        });
        if (!r.count) rkInfo.textContent = 'no players parsed \u2014 rankings page shape likely differs; capture one page for me';
      } catch (e) { rkInfo.textContent = 'failed: ' + e.message; }
      rkBtn.disabled = false;
    });
    rkRow.appendChild(rkBtn); panel.appendChild(rkRow); panel.appendChild(rkInfo);
    const tpHint = document.createElement('div'); tpHint.className = 'mi-set-hint'; tpHint.textContent = 'Also loads each base\u2019s trade page (extra request per base) to sum plunder = 2\u00d7distance.'; panel.appendChild(tpHint);

    const preview = document.createElement('div'); preview.className = 'mi-set-preview'; panel.appendChild(preview);
    function updatePreview() {
      const base = Math.max(0.6, parseFloat(inInterval.value) || 3);
      const pct = Math.max(0, Math.min(90, parseFloat(inJitter.value) || 0)) / 100;
      const floor = Math.max(0.6, parseFloat(inFloor.value) || 0.6);
      const lo = Math.max(floor, base * (1 - pct)).toFixed(1);
      const hi = (base * (1 + pct) + 1.5).toFixed(1);
      const mean = Math.max(floor, base + 0.75).toFixed(1);
      preview.textContent = 'Actual delay ' + lo + '\u2013' + hi + 's per page (\u2248' + mean + 's avg)';
    }
    [inInterval, inJitter, inFloor].forEach(i => i.addEventListener('input', updatePreview));
    updatePreview();

    const btns = document.createElement('div'); btns.className = 'mi-set-btns';
    const save = document.createElement('button'); save.className = 'ae-btn ae-btn--primary'; save.textContent = 'Save';
    save.addEventListener('click', () => {
      AEMapIntel.setCrawlCfg({
        baseDelaySec: Math.max(0.6, parseFloat(inInterval.value) || 3),
        jitterPct: Math.max(0, Math.min(90, parseFloat(inJitter.value) || 30)) / 100,
        floorSec: Math.max(0.6, parseFloat(inFloor.value) || 0.6),
        tradePlunder: tpBox.checked
      });
      if ($('#mi-delay')) { $('#mi-delay').value = AEMapIntel.getCrawlCfg().baseDelaySec; updateJitterRange(); }
      SHADOW.removeChild(back);
    });
    const cancel = document.createElement('button'); cancel.className = 'ae-btn'; cancel.textContent = 'Cancel';
    cancel.addEventListener('click', () => SHADOW.removeChild(back));
    btns.appendChild(cancel); btns.appendChild(save); panel.appendChild(btns);

    back.appendChild(panel);
    back.addEventListener('click', (e) => { if (e.target === back) SHADOW.removeChild(back); });
    SHADOW.appendChild(back);
  }

  // Build advisor: on OWN base pages (identified by the structures sub-nav link), a
  // panel row picks a goal and ranks the cheapest next build INCLUDING its support
  // chain (pop -> Urban, energy -> cheapest plant, area -> Terraform/MLP).
  function installBuildAdvisor() {
    if (!/\/base\.aspx/i.test(location.pathname)) return;
    if (!document.querySelector('a[href*="view=structures"]')) return;   // rival base: no sub-nav
    const panel = document.querySelector('[data-ae-script="map-intel"]');
    const sh = panel && panel.shadowRoot; if (!sh) return;
    const bodyEl = sh.querySelector('.ae-panel__body'); if (!bodyEl || sh.getElementById('mi-badv')) return;
    const row = document.createElement('div'); row.className = 'mi-row'; row.id = 'mi-badv';
