    const sel = document.createElement('select'); sel.className = 'ae-input'; sel.style.flex = '1';
    [['economy', 'Economy'], ['production', 'Production'], ['construction', 'Construction']].forEach(([v, l]) => {
      const o = document.createElement('option'); o.value = v; o.textContent = l; sel.appendChild(o);
    });
    const btn = document.createElement('button'); btn.className = 'ae-btn'; btn.textContent = 'Next build';
    row.appendChild(sel); row.appendChild(btn); bodyEl.appendChild(row);
    const outBox = document.createElement('div'); outBox.className = 'ae-muted';
    outBox.style.cssText = 'font-size:10px;line-height:1.6;white-space:pre-wrap;display:none;margin-top:4px;';
    bodyEl.appendChild(outBox);
    btn.addEventListener('click', () => {
      try {
        const st = AEMapIntel.parseStructures(document);
        const astro = AEMapIntel.parseAstroDetails(document);
        if (!st || !astro.area) { outBox.textContent = 'could not parse this base page'; outBox.style.display = 'block'; return; }
        const adv = AEMapIntel.adviseNextBuild({ structures: st.structures, astro }, sel.value, 5);
        if (!adv.length) { outBox.textContent = 'no candidates for this goal'; outBox.style.display = 'block'; return; }
        const fmt = (n) => n.toLocaleString();
        outBox.textContent = adv.map((o, i) => {
          let line = (i + 1) + '. ' + o.name + ' \u2192L' + o.toLevel + ' \u00b7 ' + fmt(o.totalCost) + ' cr \u00b7 '
            + (Math.round(o.costPer * 10) / 10) + '/' + sel.value.slice(0, 4);
          if (o.chain.length > 1) line += '\n    chain: ' + o.chain.map(c => c.name + ' L' + c.toLevel + ' (' + fmt(c.cost) + ')').join(' + ');
          if (o.reqNote) line += '\n    ' + o.reqNote;
          return line;
        }).join('\n');
        outBox.style.display = 'block';
      } catch (e) { outBox.textContent = 'advisor failed: ' + e.message; outBox.style.display = 'block'; }
    });
  }

  // Drop-calc hover: on fleet rows, show single-round BO + main-spec drop estimates.
  // Gated by the predictor's mirrored setting (ae::predcfg) and the same freshness rules
  // as the % injection (rank data <24h). Tooltip is light-DOM, createElement only.
  function installDropHover() {
    let pred = null; try { pred = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('ae::predcfg::' + SERVER)); } catch (e) {}
    if (pred && pred.dropCalc === false) return;                       // turned off in predictor settings
    const age = AEMapIntel.rankAgeMs();
    if (age == null || age > 24 * 3600 * 1000) return;                 // no/stale rank data
    const rk = AEMapIntel.getRankings(); if (!rk || !rk.players) return;
    let tech = null; try { tech = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('ae::techmirror::' + SERVER)); } catch (e) {}
    if (!tech || !tech.players) return;
    // one calibration per page: guildmates etc. with BOTH harvested tech and a rank
    // score anchor the combat-share factor and the tech-per-fleet extrapolation ratio
    const calib = AEMapIntel.computeTechCalibration(tech.players, rk.players);
    const attacker = { boAttack: (pred && pred.boAttack) || 11.5, mainAbbr: (pred && pred.mainAbbr) || 'BS',
                       mainAttack: (pred && pred.mainAttack) || 264,
                       assumeTech: (pred && pred.assumeTech != null) ? pred.assumeTech : 15 };
    // mark fleet rows with the player id (same tables the % annotator walks)
    for (const tbl of document.querySelectorAll('table')) {
      const first = tbl.querySelector('tr'); if (!first) continue;
      const heads = [...first.querySelectorAll('td,th')].map(c => (c.textContent || '').trim().toLowerCase());
      if (heads.indexOf('size') < 0 || heads.indexOf('player') < 0 || !heads.includes('fleet')) continue;
      const pi = heads.indexOf('player');
      for (const tr of [...tbl.querySelectorAll('tr')].slice(1)) {
        const tds = tr.querySelectorAll('td'); if (tds.length <= pi) continue;
        const link = tds[pi].querySelector('a[href*="player="]'); if (!link) continue;
        const m = (link.getAttribute('href') || '').match(/player=(\d+)/); if (m) tr.setAttribute('data-ae-droppid', m[1]);
        const fl = tr.querySelector('a[href*="fleet.aspx?fleet="]');
        if (fl) { const fm = (fl.getAttribute('href') || '').match(/fleet=(\d+)/); if (fm) tr.setAttribute('data-ae-dropfleet', fm[1]); }
      }
    }
    // `tip` is the TRANSIENT popup that follows the cursor. Pinned popups are moved
    // into pinnedTips and persist independently so several can be compared side by
    // side; each closes only via its own ×.
    let tip = null, dwellTimer = null, pinTimer = null;
    const pinnedTips = [];
    const isPinned = (t) => pinnedTips.indexOf(t) >= 0;
    /* A popup is LIVE while it is either the current transient popup or pinned.
       Async fetch callbacks must test this, NOT `tip === t`: pinning detaches the
       transient reference (tip = null), so an identity check silently discarded every
       live-composition result once pin and dwell both landed at 3s. */
    const isLive = (t) => tip === t || isPinned(t);
    const compCache = {};   // fleetId -> { units|null, t } — 10 min TTL, page-lifetime
    const DWELL_MS = 3000;  // hover time before any network request is made
    const PIN_MS = 3000;    // == DWELL_MS: at 3s the popup both fetches and pins
    const MARGIN = 8;       // keep this clear of every viewport edge
    let ptr = { x: 0, y: 0 };
    document.addEventListener('mousemove', (e) => {
      ptr = { x: e.clientX, y: e.clientY };
      if (tip) positionTip(tip);   // only the transient popup follows the cursor
    }, { passive: true });
    /* Place next to the CURSOR, flipping side/vertical when the preferred placement
       would overflow, then hard-clamping so the popup can never leave the viewport
       (it cannot be scrolled to, and scrolling dismisses it). */
    function positionTip(t) {
      const vw = innerWidth, vh = innerHeight;
      const w = t.offsetWidth || 380, h = t.offsetHeight || 200;
      if (t.hasAttribute('data-ae-moved')) {          // user-placed: clamp, never re-anchor
        t.style.left = Math.max(MARGIN, Math.min(parseFloat(t.style.left) || 0, vw - w - MARGIN)) + 'px';
        t.style.top = Math.max(MARGIN, Math.min(parseFloat(t.style.top) || 0, vh - h - MARGIN)) + 'px';
        t.style.maxHeight = (vh - 2 * MARGIN) + 'px';
        return;
      }
      let x = ptr.x + 16;
      if (x + w > vw - MARGIN) x = ptr.x - 16 - w;          // flip to the left of the cursor
      let y = ptr.y + 16;
      if (y + h > vh - MARGIN) y = ptr.y - 16 - h;          // flip above the cursor
      t.style.left = Math.max(MARGIN, Math.min(x, vw - w - MARGIN)) + 'px';
      t.style.top = Math.max(MARGIN, Math.min(y, vh - h - MARGIN)) + 'px';
      // very tall popups: cap height and let the popup itself scroll
      t.style.maxHeight = (vh - 2 * MARGIN) + 'px';
    }
    // Only ever removes the TRANSIENT popup; pinned ones are independent.
    function hideTip() {
      clearTimeout(dwellTimer); dwellTimer = null;
      clearTimeout(pinTimer); pinTimer = null;
      if (tip) { tip.remove(); tip = null; }
    }
    function closeTip(t) {
      const i = pinnedTips.indexOf(t); if (i >= 0) pinnedTips.splice(i, 1);
      if (tip === t) tip = null;
      t.remove();
    }
    /* The close button lives OUTSIDE the content, but fillTip() rebuilds all children —
       so it must be re-attached after every refill or a pinned popup loses its only
       way to close (certain now that pinning and the live fetch both land at 3s). */
    function addCloseButton(t) {
      if (t.querySelector('[data-ae-tipclose]')) return;
      const x = document.createElement('div');
      x.setAttribute('data-ae-tipclose', '1');
      x.textContent = '\u00d7';
      x.title = 'close';
      x.style.cssText = 'position:absolute;top:2px;right:6px;cursor:pointer;font:14px/1 monospace;padding:2px 4px;';
      x.style.color = AEShared.token('--ae-danger') || 'red';
      x.addEventListener('click', (ev) => { ev.stopPropagation(); closeTip(t); });
      t.appendChild(x);
      t.style.paddingRight = '20px';
    }
    /* Drag handle: a slim strip across the top of a pinned popup. Like the close
       button it sits outside the content, so fillTip()'s rebuild wipes it and it must
       be re-attached idempotently. Dragging marks the popup user-placed, after which
       positionTip() only clamps it. */
    function addDragHandle(t) {
      if (t.querySelector('[data-ae-tipdrag]')) return;
      const h = document.createElement('div');
      h.setAttribute('data-ae-tipdrag', '1');
      h.title = 'drag to move';
      h.style.cssText = 'position:absolute;top:0;left:0;right:24px;height:15px;cursor:move;'
        + 'border-top-left-radius:6px;display:flex;align-items:center;padding-left:7px;'
        + 'font:9px/1 monospace;letter-spacing:2px;user-select:none;';
      h.style.color = AEShared.token('--ae-text-dim') || 'inherit';
      h.textContent = '\u2261';
      h.addEventListener('mousedown', (ev) => {
        ev.preventDefault(); ev.stopPropagation();
        const r = t.getBoundingClientRect();
        const dx = ev.clientX - r.left, dy = ev.clientY - r.top;
        t.setAttribute('data-ae-moved', '1');
        const move = (e2) => {
          t.style.left = Math.max(MARGIN, Math.min(e2.clientX - dx, innerWidth - t.offsetWidth - MARGIN)) + 'px';
          t.style.top = Math.max(MARGIN, Math.min(e2.clientY - dy, innerHeight - t.offsetHeight - MARGIN)) + 'px';
        };
        const up = () => { removeEventListener('mousemove', move); removeEventListener('mouseup', up); };
        addEventListener('mousemove', move); addEventListener('mouseup', up);
      });
      t.appendChild(h);
      t.style.paddingTop = '17px';
    }
    function pinTip(t) {
      if (!tip || tip !== t || isPinned(t)) return;
      pinnedTips.push(t);
      tip = null;                      // detach: the next hover gets a fresh transient popup
      clearTimeout(dwellTimer); dwellTimer = null;
      clearTimeout(pinTimer); pinTimer = null;
      t.style.pointerEvents = 'auto';
      t.style.overflowY = 'auto';
      addCloseButton(t); addDragHandle(t);
      positionTip(t);
    }
    // Entries are { kind, text }: head (dim caption) | title (accent) | stat (data,
    // indented) | note (dim) | gap (blank spacer). Strings are still accepted.
    function fillTip(t, lns, note) {
      while (t.firstChild) t.removeChild(t.firstChild);
      lns.forEach((ln) => {
        const e = (typeof ln === 'string') ? { kind: 'stat', text: ln } : ln;
        const d = document.createElement('div');
        if (e.kind === 'gap') { d.style.height = '7px'; t.appendChild(d); return; }
        // block separator: a DOUBLE rule, visually distinct from the sum row below
        if (e.kind === 'divide') {
          d.style.height = '0'; d.style.margin = '9px 0 8px';
          d.style.borderTop = '3px double ' + (AEShared.token('--ae-border') || 'currentColor');
          t.appendChild(d); return;
        }
        d.textContent = e.text;
        if (e.kind === 'head') { d.style.opacity = '.65'; d.style.fontSize = '10px'; d.style.marginBottom = '6px'; }
        else if (e.kind === 'plan') { d.style.color = AEShared.token('--ae-primary') || 'inherit'; d.style.fontWeight = '700'; d.style.fontSize = '10px'; d.style.letterSpacing = '.6px'; d.style.marginBottom = '2px'; }
        else if (e.kind === 'total') {
          d.style.margin = '5px 0 0 10px'; d.style.padding = '3px 8px';
          d.style.background = AEShared.token('--ae-surface-2') || 'transparent';
          d.style.borderLeft = '3px solid ' + (AEShared.token('--ae-primary') || 'currentColor');
          d.style.borderRadius = '3px';
          d.style.color = AEShared.token('--ae-primary') || 'inherit';
          d.style.fontWeight = '700'; d.style.fontVariantNumeric = 'tabular-nums';
        }
        else if (e.kind === 'title') { d.style.paddingLeft = '10px'; d.style.color = AEShared.token('--ae-secondary') || 'inherit'; d.style.fontSize = '10px'; d.style.letterSpacing = '.4px'; }
        else if (e.kind === 'loss') { d.style.paddingLeft = '20px'; d.style.opacity = '.75'; d.style.fontSize = '10px'; d.style.fontVariantNumeric = 'tabular-nums'; }
        else if (e.kind === 'warn') { d.style.paddingLeft = '20px'; d.style.fontWeight = '700'; d.style.fontSize = '10px'; d.style.color = AEShared.token('--ae-danger') || 'red'; }
        else if (e.kind === 'note') { d.style.opacity = '.55'; d.style.fontSize = '10px'; }
        else { d.style.paddingLeft = '20px'; d.style.fontVariantNumeric = 'tabular-nums'; }
        t.appendChild(d);
      });
      if (note) { const d = document.createElement('div'); d.textContent = note; d.style.opacity = '.55'; d.style.fontSize = '10px'; d.style.marginTop = '5px'; t.appendChild(d); }
      // Content just changed size (the live plan is far taller than the placeholder):
      // restore the close button if pinned, then RE-MEASURE so the popup cannot spill
      // off-screen while the user holds still (no mousemove = no reposition).
      if (isPinned(t)) { addCloseButton(t); addDragHandle(t); }
      positionTip(t);
    }
    async function liveComp(fleetId) {
      const c = compCache[fleetId];
      if (c && (Date.now() - c.t) < 600000) return c.units;
      let comp = null;
      try {
        const res = await fetch(location.origin + '/fleet.aspx?fleet=' + fleetId, { credentials: 'same-origin' });
        if (res.ok) { const dp = new DOMParser().parseFromString(await res.text(), 'text/html');
          comp = AEMapIntel.parseFleetComposition(dp); }
      } catch (e) { /* no vision / network — keep the estimate */ }
      compCache[fleetId] = { comp: comp || null, t: Date.now() };
      return comp;
    }
    document.addEventListener('mouseover', (e) => {
      const tr = e.target && e.target.closest ? e.target.closest('tr[data-ae-droppid]') : null;
      if (!tr) { hideTip(); return; }
      const pid0 = tr.getAttribute('data-ae-droppid');
      if (tip && tip.getAttribute('data-pid') === pid0) return;
      if (pinnedTips.some(p => p.getAttribute('data-pid') === pid0)) { hideTip(); return; }  // already pinned
      hideTip();       // replaces only the transient popup — pinned ones stay
      const pid = tr.getAttribute('data-ae-droppid');
      const fleetId = tr.getAttribute('data-ae-dropfleet');
      const mp = tech.players[pid]; const rp = rk.players[pid];
      const ownU = AEMapIntel.getOwnUnits();
      const out = AEMapIntel.computeDropForPlayer(mp, tech.archetypes, rp && rp.fleet, attacker, null, ownU, null, rp, calib);
      if (!out && !fleetId) return;   // no estimate possible and no live view to try
      tip = document.createElement('div');
      tip.setAttribute('data-pid', pid);
      tip.style.cssText = 'position:fixed;z-index:2147483100;background:rgba(10,16,20,.97);border-radius:6px;padding:9px 12px;font:11px/1.6 monospace;pointer-events:none;max-width:380px;';
      tip.style.border = '1px solid ' + (AEShared.token('--ae-primary') || 'currentColor');
      tip.style.color = AEShared.token('--ae-text') || 'inherit';
      const cached = fleetId && compCache[fleetId] && (Date.now() - compCache[fleetId].t) < 600000;
      fillTip(tip, out ? out.lines : [{ kind: 'head', text: 'Drop plan' }],
        fleetId && !cached ? 'hold 3s for live composition' + String.fromCharCode(8230) : null);
      // Only fetch after a 3s dwell, so scrolling across rows never fires a burst of
      // requests. Cached fleets resolve immediately (no network).
      if (fleetId) {
        const myTip = tip;
        const runFetch = () => {
          if (!isLive(myTip)) return;
          if (!cached) fillTip(myTip, out ? out.lines : [{ kind: 'head', text: 'Drop plan' }], 'fetching live comp' + String.fromCharCode(8230));
          liveComp(fleetId).then((comp) => {
            if (!isLive(myTip)) return;   // popup was dismissed while the fetch was in flight
            const units = comp && comp.units;
            if (units) {
              const lv = AEMapIntel.computeDropForPlayer(mp || {}, tech.archetypes, null, attacker, units, ownU, comp.mismatch, rp, calib);
              if (lv) fillTip(myTip, lv.lines);
              else if (out) fillTip(myTip, out.lines, 'no droppable units in live comp');
              else closeTip(myTip);
            } else if (out) fillTip(myTip, out.lines, 'no live view ' + String.fromCharCode(8212) + ' estimate from spec');
            else closeTip(myTip);
          });
        };
        if (cached) runFetch();
        else { clearTimeout(dwellTimer); dwellTimer = setTimeout(runFetch, DWELL_MS); }
      }
      document.documentElement.appendChild(tip);
      positionTip(tip);                       // needs to be in the DOM to measure
      const pinMe = tip;
      clearTimeout(pinTimer);
      pinTimer = setTimeout(() => pinTip(pinMe), PIN_MS);
    });
    document.addEventListener('mouseout', (e) => {
      if (e.target && e.target.closest && e.target.closest('tr[data-ae-droppid]')) {
        const to = e.relatedTarget;
        if (to && to.closest && (to.closest('tr[data-ae-droppid]') || (tip && tip.contains(to)))) return;
        hideTip();   // pinned popups are untouched
      }
    });
    window.AE_DROPTIPS_CLOSE_ALL = () => { hideTip(); [...pinnedTips].forEach(closeTip); };
    // repositioning on scroll/resize keeps a PINNED popup on screen; unpinned ones are
    // dismissed by scrolling as before
    // scrolling dismisses the transient popup; pinned ones are position:fixed and stay
    // put (they may have been dragged). A resize re-clamps everything on screen.
    addEventListener('scroll', hideTip, { passive: true });
    addEventListener('resize', () => { if (tip) positionTip(tip); pinnedTips.forEach(positionTip); }, { passive: true });
  }

  // 12h reminder to re-parse rankings. Snoozes 1h on dismissal so it doesn't nag on
  // every navigation; disappears entirely once ranks are refreshed. Uses theme tokens
  // (AEShared.adopt) — no hardcoded colours.
  const RANK_REMIND_SNOOZE_KEY = 'ae::rankremind::' + SERVER;
  function checkRankReminder() {
    const age = AEMapIntel.rankAgeMs();
    if (age == null || age < 12 * 3600 * 1000) return;   // no data yet, or fresh enough
    let last = 0; try { last = parseInt(localStorage.getItem(RANK_REMIND_SNOOZE_KEY), 10) || 0; } catch (e) {}
    if (Date.now() - last < 3600 * 1000) return;         // snoozed within the hour
    const host = document.createElement('div');
    host.setAttribute('data-ae-script', 'map-intel-rankremind');
    const sh = host.attachShadow({ mode: 'open' });
    AEShared.adopt(sh);
    const card = document.createElement('div');
    card.className = 'ae-panel';
    card.style.position = 'fixed'; card.style.top = '48px'; card.style.right = '12px';
    card.style.width = '250px'; card.style.zIndex = '2147482000';
    const bar = document.createElement('div'); bar.className = 'ae-panel__bar';
    const tt = document.createElement('span'); tt.className = 'ae-panel__title'; tt.textContent = 'Rank data is stale';
    bar.appendChild(tt); card.appendChild(bar);
    const body = document.createElement('div'); body.className = 'ae-panel__body';
    const msg = document.createElement('div'); msg.className = 'ae-muted'; msg.style.fontSize = '11px';
    msg.textContent = 'Rankings are ' + Math.round(age / 3600000) + 'h old' + (age > 24 * 3600 * 1000 ? ' \u2014 fleet % injection is disabled until refreshed.' : ' \u2014 fleet % may be off.');
    body.appendChild(msg);
    const row = document.createElement('div'); row.style.display = 'flex'; row.style.gap = '6px'; row.style.marginTop = '6px';
    const upd = document.createElement('button'); upd.className = 'ae-btn ae-btn--primary'; upd.style.flex = '1'; upd.textContent = 'Update now';
    const later = document.createElement('button'); later.className = 'ae-btn'; later.textContent = 'Later';
    row.appendChild(upd); row.appendChild(later); body.appendChild(row); card.appendChild(body);
    sh.appendChild(card); document.documentElement.appendChild(host);
    later.addEventListener('click', () => { try { localStorage.setItem(RANK_REMIND_SNOOZE_KEY, String(Date.now())); } catch (e) {} host.remove(); });
    upd.addEventListener('click', async () => {
      upd.disabled = true; later.disabled = true;
      try {
        await AEMapIntel.fetchRankings({}, (p) => {
          if (p.phase === 'rank') msg.textContent = 'updating\u2026 ' + p.view + ' p' + p.page + ' (' + p.step + '/' + p.total + ')';
          else if (p.phase === 'rank-done') msg.textContent = p.count + ' players refreshed.';
        });
        const rk = AEMapIntel.getRankings();
        if (rk && rk.players) AEMapIntel.annotateFleetPercents(document, rk, true);   // replace stale annotations
        setTimeout(() => host.remove(), 1500);
      } catch (e) { msg.textContent = 'update failed: ' + e.message; upd.disabled = false; later.disabled = false; }
    });
  }

  function build() {
    if (HOST) return;
    HOST = document.createElement('div');
    HOST.setAttribute('data-ae-script', 'map-intel');
    SHADOW = HOST.attachShadow({ mode: 'open' });
    AEShared.adopt(SHADOW);
    const st = document.createElement('style'); st.textContent = PANEL_CSS; SHADOW.appendChild(st);
    PANEL = document.createElement('div'); PANEL.className = 'ae-panel'; PANEL.innerHTML = panelMarkup();
    SHADOW.appendChild(PANEL);
    document.documentElement.appendChild(HOST);
    mountBanner();
    AEShared.placeHost(HOST, 'map-intel', SHADOW);
    AEShared.startLiveUpdates();
    // Fleet %-of-total injection: annotate fleet listings on this page with each fleet's
    // share of the player's TOTAL fleet (from the rankings store, if fetched).
    try { const rk = AEMapIntel.getRankings(); if (rk && rk.players) AEMapIntel.annotateFleetPercents(document, rk); } catch (e) {}
    try { checkRankReminder(); } catch (e) {}
    // Own-units capture MUST happen here: harvestCurrentPage() is never called by any
    // module (it is exported but has no caller), which is why visiting Empire -> Units
    // previously did nothing. build() runs on every AE page, so capture directly.
    try { AEMapIntel.captureOwnUnitsIfOnPage(document); } catch (e) {}
    // Build advisor parked for now (Aran) — re-enable by uncommenting:
    // try { installBuildAdvisor(); } catch (e) {}
    try { installDropHover(); } catch (e) {}

    // collapse toggle (shared idiom)
    const ui = loadUi();
    const body = $('#mi-body');
    const bar = SHADOW.querySelector('.ae-panel__bar');
    // right-aligned controls: settings gear + collapse caret
    const rightGroup = document.createElement('span'); rightGroup.className = 'ae-bar-right';
    const gear = document.createElement('button'); gear.className = 'ae-toggle'; gear.textContent = '\u2699'; gear.title = 'Module settings';
    gear.addEventListener('click', (e) => { e.stopPropagation(); openSettings(); });
    const toggle = AEShared.makeToggle({ collapsed: !!ui.collapsed, onToggle: (c) => { body.classList.toggle('ae-hidden', c); saveUi({ collapsed: c }); } });
    rightGroup.appendChild(gear); rightGroup.appendChild(toggle);
    bar.appendChild(rightGroup);
    bar.addEventListener('click', (e) => { if (rightGroup.contains(e.target)) return; toggle.click(); });
    if (ui.collapsed) body.classList.add('ae-hidden');

    $('#mi-delay').value = AEMapIntel.getCrawlCfg().baseDelaySec;
    // One-time cleanup: a command left in storage by the earlier buggy build would
    // re-fire on this first load. Consume any pre-existing command WITHOUT executing it,
    // so upgrading to this build can't trigger one last stray delete.
    try {
      const stale = localStorage.getItem(CMD_KEY);
      if (stale) { const c = JSON.parse(stale); if (c && c.id) { localStorage.setItem(CMD_DONE_KEY, c.id); } localStorage.removeItem(CMD_KEY); }
    } catch (e) {}
    startRevPoll();
    updateJitterRange();
    $('#mi-delay').addEventListener('input', updateJitterRange);
    $('#mi-prep').addEventListener('click', onPrep);
    $('#mi-scan').addEventListener('click', onScan);
    $('#mi-stop').addEventListener('click', onStopScan);
    $('#mi-pause').addEventListener('click', onPauseScan);
    $('#mi-resume').addEventListener('click', onResume);
    $('#mi-resume-clear').addEventListener('click', onResumeClear);
    refreshResumeRow();   // surface any unfinished scan from before a navigation
    $('#mi-open').addEventListener('click', openDatabase);
    $('#mi-csv').addEventListener('click', onCSV);

    lazyHarvest();
  }

  build();
})();

} });


/* ============ MODULE: overseer ============ */
AESuite.register({ id: "overseer", title: "Layout Overseer", pages: null, init: function () {
/* Embeds canonical AEShared v0.3.1 (unchanged — consumers need no update).
 * New in 0.5.0 (reconciliation): REGIONS now includes bookmarks2 + prebattle-predictor (merged from project canonical lineage). Features from 0.4.0:
 *  - Arrange mode: drag ghost outlines; snap to 6 gutter zones (top/mid/bottom
 *    x left/right) or drop free. Corners save as anchors (viewport-responsive);
 *    mid slots and free drops save explicit px (NOT viewport-responsive).
 *  - Cross-server persistence: every save mirrors to GM storage (per-script,
 *    spans subdomains); on load, newest copy wins and is synced into this
 *    server's localStorage so consumers pick it up. Consumers untouched.
 *  - Width clamp 160..500 (gutter 520 - 2x10 border). */




/* ============================ OVERSEER UI ================================= */
(function aeOverseer() {
  'use strict';

  const REGIONS = AEShared.REGIONS;
  const EXPANDED_W_DEFAULT = 460;
  // NOTE: there is no separate collapsed width. The overseer keeps its configured
  // width whether open or collapsed — same as every other panel, which only hides
  // its body. A hardcoded collapsed width silently ignored the user's setting.
  // The overseer's own width is user-adjustable like any other panel (stored under
  // the 'overseer' id in the shared layout store).
  function overseerWidth() {
    const ov = AEShared.getLayoutOverrides('overseer');
    const w = (ov && ov.width) || (AEShared.REGIONS.overseer && AEShared.REGIONS.overseer.width) || EXPANDED_W_DEFAULT;
    return Math.max(W_MIN, Math.min(W_MAX, w));
  }
  const W_MIN = 160, W_MAX = 500;   // gutter 520 - 2*10 border
  let LISTED = [];
  let arrangeOn = false;

  // ── cross-server sync (GM = per-script, spans subdomains; verify on 2nd server)
  const MASTER_KEY = 'ae::overseer::master';
  const gmOK = (typeof GM_setValue === 'function' && typeof GM_getValue === 'function');

  function syncFromMaster() {
    if (!gmOK) return 'GM unavailable — per-server only';
    try {
      const raw = GM_getValue(MASTER_KEY, null);
      const master = raw ? JSON.parse(raw) : null;
      const local = AEShared.store.read();
      const mts = (master && master.ts) || 0, lts = local.ts || 0;
      if (mts > lts) {
        master.rev = (local.rev || 0) + 1;          // trigger consumer watchers
        AEShared.store.write(master);
        return 'pulled newer master (cross-server)';
      }
      if (lts > mts && (local.layout || local.theme)) {
        GM_setValue(MASTER_KEY, JSON.stringify(local));
        return 'pushed local to master';
      }
      return 'in sync';
    } catch (e) { console.warn('[AE Overseer] sync failed', e); return 'sync error'; }
  }

  function mirrorToMaster() {
    const s = AEShared.store.read();
    s.ts = Date.now();
    AEShared.store.write(s);
    if (gmOK) { try { GM_setValue(MASTER_KEY, JSON.stringify(s)); } catch (e) {} }
  }

  const syncMsg = syncFromMaster();

  /* ONE-TIME REPAIR of layouts saved by the pre-fix overseer.
     Those stored an explicit `left` for right-side panels, so their right margin
     drifted (and could overflow the viewport) whenever the width or viewport
     changed. Convert any right-half placement to a `right` offset, and normalise
     a near-edge value to the standard edgeGap so every right-hand element lines
     up on the same offset. Idempotent; runs once per origin. */
  function repairLayouts() {
    try {
      const st = AEShared.store.read();
      if (st.layoutRepaired) return null;
      const map = st.layout || {};
      const vw = window.innerWidth;
      const g = AEShared.layout.edgeGap;
      let fixed = 0;
      Object.keys(map).forEach(id => {
        const r = map[id];
        if (!r || r.anchor || typeof r.left !== 'number') return;   // anchors already fine
        const w = r.width || (AEShared.REGIONS[id] && AEShared.REGIONS[id].width) || 300;
        if (r.left + w / 2 <= vw / 2) return;                        // genuinely left-side
        let right = Math.round(vw - (r.left + w));
        if (Math.abs(right - g) <= 40 || right < 0) right = g;       // standardise the edge
        delete r.left;
        r.right = right;
        fixed++;
      });
      st.layout = map;
      st.layoutRepaired = 1;
      st.rev = (st.rev || 0) + 1;
      st.ts = Date.now();
      AEShared.store.write(st);
      if (fixed) {
        try { if (gmOK) GM_setValue(MASTER_KEY, JSON.stringify(st)); } catch (e) {}
        console.log('[AE Overseer] repaired ' + fixed + ' right-side layout(s): left-px -> right offset ' + g + 'px');
      }
      return fixed;
    } catch (e) { console.warn('[AE Overseer] layout repair failed', e); return null; }
  }
  const repaired = repairLayouts();

  function detectLoaded() {
    const present = new Set();
    document.querySelectorAll('[data-ae-script]').forEach(el => {
      const id = el.getAttribute('data-ae-script');
      if (id && id !== 'overseer' && id !== '__fallback__') present.add(id);
    });
    return Object.keys(REGIONS).filter(id => id !== '__fallback__' && present.has(id));
  }

  // ── host: standard chrome, top-left, collapsible ───────────────────────────
  const host = document.createElement('div');
  host.setAttribute('data-ae-script', 'overseer');
  const shadow = host.attachShadow({ mode: 'open' });
  AEShared.adopt(shadow);
  document.documentElement.appendChild(host);

  const ostyle = document.createElement('style');
  ostyle.textContent = `
    .ov-bar { cursor: pointer; }
    .ov-caret { font-family: var(--ae-font-mono); }
    .ov-row { padding:6px 0; border-bottom:1px solid var(--ae-border-soft); }
    .ov-name { font-size:11px; margin-bottom:4px; }
    .ov-ctrls { display:flex; gap:4px; align-items:center; flex-wrap:wrap; }
    .ov-ctrls .ae-input { width:58px; padding:4px 6px; }
    .ov-ctrls .ae-btn { padding:4px 8px; }
    .ov-ctrls label { font-size:9px; color:var(--ae-text-dim); display:flex; flex-direction:column; align-items:center; gap:1px; }
    .ov-actions { display:flex; gap:4px; flex-wrap:wrap; margin-top:8px; }
    .ov-msg { font-family:var(--ae-font-mono); font-size:10px; color:var(--ae-text-muted); margin-top:6px; min-height:12px; }
    .ov-empty { color:var(--ae-text-dim); padding:6px 0; font-size:11px; }
    .ov-master { display:inline-flex; align-items:center; cursor:pointer; margin-right:2px; }
 .ov-master-cb { display:none; }
 .ov-pill { width:26px; height:13px; border-radius:7px; background:var(--ae-surface-2); border:1px solid var(--ae-border);
   position:relative; transition:background .2s; }
 .ov-knob { position:absolute; top:1px; left:1px; width:9px; height:9px; border-radius:50%;
   background:var(--ae-success); transition:left .2s, background .2s; box-shadow:0 0 4px var(--ae-success); }
 .ov-master-cb:checked + .ov-pill { background:var(--ae-danger); opacity:.85; }
 .ov-master-cb:checked + .ov-pill .ov-knob { left:14px; background:var(--ae-bg); box-shadow:none; }
 .ov-arranging { outline:1px solid var(--ae-secondary); }
    .ov-arranging .ae-panel__sub { color:var(--ae-secondary); }
  `;
  shadow.appendChild(ostyle);

  const root = document.createElement('div');
  root.className = 'ae-panel';
  shadow.appendChild(root);
  root.innerHTML = `
    <div class="ae-panel__bar ov-bar">
      <span class="ae-panel__dot is-ok"></span>
      <span class="ae-panel__titles">
        <span class="ae-panel__title">Layout Overseer</span>
        <span class="ae-panel__sub">drag &amp; snap · cross-server</span>
      </span>
      <label class="ov-master" title="Master switch: hide EVERY suite panel and pause all activity (scans, sessions, loads)">
        <input type="checkbox" class="ov-master-cb"><span class="ov-pill"><span class="ov-knob"></span></span>
      </label>
      <button class="ae-toggle ov-caret" type="button" title="Expand" aria-expanded="false">+</button>
    </div>
    <div class="ae-panel__sweep"></div>
    <div class="ae-panel__body ov-body" style="display:none">
      <div class="ov-rows"></div>
      <div class="ov-actions">
        <button class="ae-btn ov-arrange">Arrange</button>
        <button class="ae-btn ov-settings">Settings</button>
        <button class="ae-btn ov-resetall">Reset all</button>
        <button class="ae-btn ov-export">Export</button>
        <button class="ae-btn ov-import">Import</button>
        <button class="ae-btn ov-close" style="margin-left:auto">Collapse</button>
      </div>
      <textarea class="ae-input ae-input--mono ov-io" rows="4" style="margin-top:6px;display:none" placeholder="exported JSON / paste to import"></textarea>
      <div class="ov-msg"></div>
    </div>`;

  const bar = root.querySelector('.ov-bar');
  const sub = root.querySelector('.ae-panel__sub');
  const SUB_DEFAULT = sub ? sub.textContent : '';
  const body = root.querySelector('.ov-body');
  const caret = root.querySelector('.ov-caret');
  // master hide pill: shared owns the mechanics (host hiding, restore chip, event);
  // this checkbox is just the visible control, kept in sync across tabs
  const masterCb = root.querySelector('.ov-master-cb');
  masterCb.checked = AEShared.isMasterHidden();
  masterCb.addEventListener('change', () => AEShared.setMasterHidden(masterCb.checked));
  window.addEventListener('ae-master-hidden', (e) => { masterCb.checked = !!(e.detail && e.detail.hidden); });
  const rowsWrap = root.querySelector('.ov-rows');
  const io = root.querySelector('.ov-io');
  const msg = root.querySelector('.ov-msg');
  const setMsg = t => { msg.textContent = t || ''; };

  // Overseer occupies its RESERVED zone (AEShared.OVERSEER_ZONE, default left-1).
  // No module can be dropped there, so it never blocks a panel in Arrange mode.
  function place() {   // width/position no longer depend on open state
    const w = overseerWidth();                 // applies open AND collapsed
    const cr = AEShared.computeRect('overseer', { zone: AEShared.OVERSEER_ZONE, width: w });
    const s = host.style;
    s.position = 'fixed';
    s.zIndex = String(AEShared.layout.z + 100);
    s.top = cr.css.top + 'px';
    s.left = cr.css.left + 'px';
    s.right = s.bottom = 'auto';
    s.width = w + 'px';
  }
  place();

  function buildRows(ids) {
    // The overseer is listed too, so its own width is adjustable like any panel.
    LISTED = ids.slice();
    const rows = ['overseer'].concat(ids);
    rowsWrap.innerHTML = rows.map(id => {
      const label = (REGIONS[id] && REGIONS[id].label) || id;
      const self = id === 'overseer' ? ' <span class="ae-badge">this panel</span>' : '';
      return `
        <div class="ov-row" data-sid="${id}">
          <div class="ov-name">${label} <span class="ae-muted">${id}</span>${self}</div>
          <div class="ov-ctrls">
            <label>W (max ${W_MAX})<input class="ae-input w" type="number" min="${W_MIN}" max="${W_MAX}" step="10"></label>
            <label title="Higher wins when panels overlap">Prio<input class="ae-input prio" type="number" min="0" max="99" step="1"></label>
            <span class="ae-muted pos-desc" style="font-size:10px"></span>
            <button class="ae-btn savew">Apply</button>
            <button class="ae-btn reset">Reset</button>
          </div>
        </div>`;
    }).join('');
    rows.forEach(prefill);
    if (!ids.length) {
      const n = document.createElement('div');
      n.className = 'ov-empty';
      n.textContent = 'No other AE panels detected on this page.';
      rowsWrap.appendChild(n);
    }
  }

  function effective(id) { return Object.assign({}, REGIONS[id], AEShared.getLayoutOverrides(id) || {}); }
  const rowOf = id => rowsWrap.querySelector(`[data-sid="${id}"]`);

  function describe(id) {
    const ov = AEShared.getLayoutOverrides(id);
    if (!ov) return 'default (' + (REGIONS[id].anchor || '?') + ')';
    if (ov.zone) return ov.zone;
    if (ov.anchor) return ov.anchor;
    const edge = (ov.right != null) ? 'R' + ov.right : (ov.left != null ? 'L' + ov.left : '');
    return `${edge}${ov.top != null ? ' T' + ov.top : ''}`;
  }

  function prefill(id) {
    const row = rowOf(id); if (!row) return;
    const e = effective(id);
    row.querySelector('.w').value = (id === 'overseer') ? overseerWidth() : e.width;
    row.querySelector('.prio').value = e.priority || 0;
    row.querySelector('.pos-desc').textContent = describe(id);
  }

  function clampW(v, id) {
    v = parseInt(v, 10) || REGIONS[id].width;
    return Math.max(W_MIN, Math.min(W_MAX, v));
  }

  // ── snap zones: 5 slots per gutter (left-1..left-5, right-1..right-5) ──────
  // The overseer's own slot is EXCLUDED as a drop target: its panel sits there and
  // would cover a module dropped underneath it, making that module unreachable in
  // Arrange mode (exactly the top-left blocking bug).
  function zoneRects() {
    const box = AEShared.getContentBox();
    const vw = window.innerWidth, vh = window.innerHeight;
    const g = AEShared.layout.edgeGap;
    const gg = AEShared.layout.gutterGap;
    const y0 = AEShared.layout.bannerHeight + g;
    const H = Math.max(vh - y0 - g, 120);
    const slotH = H / AEShared.getSlots();
    const cols = {
      left:  { x0: g, x1: Math.max(box.left - gg, g + 100) },
      right: { x0: Math.min(box.right + gg, vw - g - 100), x1: vw - g }
    };
    const zones = [];
    AEShared.assignableZones().forEach(name => {
      const z = AEShared.parseZone(name);
      const c = cols[z.side];
      zones.push({
        name, side: z.side, slot: z.slot,
        x0: c.x0, x1: c.x1,
        y0: y0 + (z.slot - 1) * slotH,
        y1: y0 + z.slot * slotH
      });
    });
    return zones;
  }

  // zone -> stored override. Storing the ZONE NAME (not pixels) keeps placement
  // viewport-responsive: AEShared.computeRect() resolves it at render time, so a
  // resize reflows every panel instead of freezing snap-time coordinates.
  function zoneToRegion(z, w) {
    return { zone: z.name, width: w };
  }

  // ── arrange layer: draggable ghosts + zone highlights ─────────────────────
  const ghostLayer = document.createElement('div');
  Object.assign(ghostLayer.style, { position: 'fixed', inset: '0', pointerEvents: 'none', zIndex: String(AEShared.layout.z + 90), display: 'none' });
  document.documentElement.appendChild(ghostLayer);
  const zoneLayer = document.createElement('div');
  Object.assign(zoneLayer.style, { position: 'fixed', inset: '0', pointerEvents: 'none', zIndex: String(AEShared.layout.z + 85), display: 'none' });
  document.documentElement.appendChild(zoneLayer);

  let drag = null; // { id, g, dx, dy }

  function renderZones(hover) {
    zoneLayer.innerHTML = '';
    if (!drag) { zoneLayer.style.display = 'none'; return; }
    zoneLayer.style.display = 'block';
    zoneRects().forEach(z => {
      const d = document.createElement('div');
      const hot = hover && hover.name === z.name;
      Object.assign(d.style, {
        position: 'absolute', left: z.x0 + 'px', top: z.y0 + 'px',
        width: (z.x1 - z.x0) + 'px', height: (z.y1 - z.y0) + 'px',
        border: '1px dashed ' + (hot ? AEShared.token('--ae-secondary') : AEShared.token('--ae-primary')),
        background: hot ? 'rgba(240,181,65,.12)' : 'rgba(63,182,196,.04)',
        boxSizing: 'border-box', borderRadius: '6px',
        font: '10px ' + AEShared.token('--ae-font-mono'), color: hot ? AEShared.token('--ae-secondary') : AEShared.token('--ae-primary'), opacity: hot ? '1' : '.7', padding: '2px 4px'
      });
      d.textContent = z.name;
      zoneLayer.appendChild(d);
    });
  }

  function hitZone(cx, cy) {
    return zoneRects().find(z => cx >= z.x0 && cx <= z.x1 && cy >= z.y0 && cy <= z.y1) || null;
  }

  function renderGhosts() {
    if (!arrangeOn) { ghostLayer.style.display = 'none'; renderZones(null); return; }
    ghostLayer.style.display = 'block';
    ghostLayer.innerHTML = '';
    LISTED.forEach(id => {
      const w = clampW(rowOf(id) ? rowOf(id).querySelector('.w').value : effective(id).width, id);
      const { rect } = AEShared.computeRect(id, Object.assign({}, AEShared.getLayoutOverrides(id) || {}, { width: w }));
      const g = document.createElement('div');
      Object.assign(g.style, {
        position: 'absolute', left: rect.left + 'px', top: rect.top + 'px',
        width: rect.width + 'px', height: rect.height + 'px',
        border: '1px dashed ' + AEShared.token('--ae-primary'), background: 'rgba(63,182,196,.10)',
        borderRadius: '8px', font: '11px ' + AEShared.token('--ae-font-mono'),
        color: AEShared.token('--ae-primary'), padding: '4px', boxSizing: 'border-box', overflow: 'hidden',
        pointerEvents: 'auto', cursor: 'move', userSelect: 'none'
      });
      g.textContent = '⠿ ' + id;
      g.addEventListener('mousedown', e => {
        drag = { id, g, dx: e.clientX - rect.left, dy: e.clientY - rect.top };
        renderZones(null);
        e.preventDefault();
      });
      ghostLayer.appendChild(g);
    });
  }

  window.addEventListener('mousemove', e => {
    if (!drag) return;
    const L = e.clientX - drag.dx, T = e.clientY - drag.dy;
    drag.g.style.left = L + 'px'; drag.g.style.top = T + 'px';
    const cx = L + drag.g.offsetWidth / 2, cy = T + drag.g.offsetHeight / 2;
    renderZones(hitZone(cx, cy));
  });

  window.addEventListener('mouseup', e => {
    if (!drag) return;
    const id = drag.id;
    const L = parseInt(drag.g.style.left, 10), T = parseInt(drag.g.style.top, 10);
    const w = clampW(rowOf(id) ? rowOf(id).querySelector('.w').value : effective(id).width, id);
    const gh = drag.g.offsetHeight;
    const z = hitZone(L + drag.g.offsetWidth / 2, T + gh / 2);
    // Free drop: pin to whichever edge it landed nearest, so the right-hand offset
    // stays consistent with the tiled panels even under free placement.
    let region;
    if (z) {
      region = zoneToRegion(z, w);
    } else {
      const vw = window.innerWidth;
      region = { top: Math.max(0, T), width: w };
      if (L + w / 2 > vw / 2) region.right = Math.max(0, Math.round(vw - (L + w)));
      else                    region.left  = Math.max(0, L);
    }
    AEShared.store.replaceLayout(id, region);
    mirrorToMaster();
    drag = null;
    prefill(id); renderGhosts(); renderZones(null);
    setMsg(z ? `snapped ${id} → ${z.name}`
             : `placed ${id} free (pinned to nearest edge)`);
  });

  // ── open/close ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  /* Open/collapse is now INDEPENDENT of arrange mode.
     Reserving left-1 was not enough: the EXPANDED overseer is ~530px tall, so it
     physically covered the left-2/left-3 ghosts no matter which zone was reserved.
     So entering Arrange collapses the panel to its header, leaving the whole
     gutter free to drag in; reopening (+) exits Arrange. Drops are saved as they
     happen, so nothing is lost by collapsing. */
  function setOpen(open) {
    body.style.display = open ? 'block' : 'none';
    caret.textContent = open ? '\u2212' : '+';       // shared idiom
    caret.title = open ? 'Collapse' : (arrangeOn ? 'Finish arranging' : 'Expand');
    caret.setAttribute('aria-expanded', String(open));
    place();
    if (open) {
      if (arrangeOn) exitArrange();                 // reopening ends the arrange session
      buildRows(detectLoaded());
      renderGhosts();
      setMsg('sync: ' + syncMsg);
    }
    // NOTE: collapsing deliberately does NOT cancel arrange — that is how the
    // gutter is cleared for dragging.
  }
  bar.addEventListener('click', () => setOpen(body.style.display === 'none'));
  root.querySelector('.ov-close').addEventListener('click', e => { e.stopPropagation(); setOpen(false); });

  rowsWrap.addEventListener('input', () => { if (arrangeOn) renderGhosts(); });
  rowsWrap.addEventListener('click', e => {
    const row = e.target.closest('.ov-row'); if (!row) return;
    const id = row.getAttribute('data-sid');
    if (e.target.classList.contains('savew')) {
      const w = clampW(row.querySelector('.w').value, id);
      const prio = Math.max(0, Math.min(99, parseInt(row.querySelector('.prio').value, 10) || 0));
      row.querySelector('.w').value = w;
      row.querySelector('.prio').value = prio;
      const base = REGIONS[id] || {};
      const ov = AEShared.getLayoutOverrides(id) || { zone: base.zone, anchor: base.anchor };
      ov.width = w;
      ov.priority = prio;
      AEShared.store.replaceLayout(id, ov);
      mirrorToMaster();
      if (id === 'overseer') place();   // apply new width immediately, open or collapsed
      prefill(id); renderGhosts();
      setMsg(`${id}: width ${w}px, priority ${prio}`);
    } else if (e.target.classList.contains('reset')) {
      AEShared.store.clearLayout(id);
      mirrorToMaster();
      if (id === 'overseer') place();   // apply new width immediately, open or collapsed
      prefill(id); renderGhosts();
      setMsg(`reset ${id} to default`);
    }
  });

  function enterArrange() {
    arrangeOn = true;
    root.querySelector('.ov-arrange').classList.add('ae-btn--primary');
    setOpen(false);                       // clear the gutter so ghosts are reachable
    sub.textContent = 'ARRANGE \u00B7 drag ghosts \u00B7 press + when done';
    root.classList.add('ov-arranging');
    renderGhosts();
  }
  function exitArrange() {
    arrangeOn = false;
    root.querySelector('.ov-arrange').classList.remove('ae-btn--primary');
    sub.textContent = SUB_DEFAULT;
    root.classList.remove('ov-arranging');
    drag = null;
    renderGhosts(); renderZones(null);
    // Each drop already persisted on mouseup (replaceLayout + GM mirror); this
    // just confirms what is now in effect.
    const n = Object.keys(AEShared.getLayoutOverrides() || {}).length;
    setMsg('arrange finished \u00B7 ' + n + ' saved placement' + (n === 1 ? '' : 's'));
  }
  root.querySelector('.ov-arrange').addEventListener('click', e => {
    e.stopPropagation();
    if (arrangeOn) { exitArrange(); setOpen(true); }
    else enterArrange();
  });
  root.querySelector('.ov-resetall').addEventListener('click', () => {
    AEShared.store.clearAll(); mirrorToMaster();
    LISTED.forEach(prefill); renderGhosts();
    setMsg('all overrides cleared (mirrored)');
  });
  root.querySelector('.ov-export').addEventListener('click', () => {
    const s = AEShared.store.read();
    io.style.display = 'block';
    io.value = JSON.stringify({ layout: s.layout || {}, theme: s.theme || {} }, null, 2);
    io.focus(); io.select();
    setMsg('exported');
  });
  root.querySelector('.ov-import').addEventListener('click', () => {
    if (io.style.display === 'none' || !io.value.trim()) {
      io.style.display = 'block'; io.focus();
      setMsg('paste JSON, click Import again'); return;
    }
    let parsed;
    try { parsed = JSON.parse(io.value); } catch (err) { setMsg('invalid JSON: ' + err.message); return; }
    const s = AEShared.store.read();
    if (parsed.layout) s.layout = parsed.layout;
    if (parsed.theme)  s.theme  = parsed.theme;
    s.rev = (s.rev || 0) + 1;
    AEShared.store.write(s);
    mirrorToMaster();
    LISTED.forEach(prefill); renderGhosts();
    setMsg('imported + mirrored');
  });

  window.addEventListener('resize', () => { if (arrangeOn) { renderGhosts(); renderZones(null); } });

  /* ═══════════════════ MASTER SETTINGS OVERLAY ═══════════════════
     One place for suite-wide settings. Deliberately scoped to what the shared
     layer actually owns — theme tokens, module enable/disable, layout store.
     Module-INTERNAL settings (scan intervals, whitelists, …) stay in each
     module's own settings panel; this does not try to proxy them. */
  const THEME_FIELDS = [
    ['--ae-primary',   'Primary (cyan)'],
    ['--ae-secondary', 'Secondary (amber)'],
    ['--ae-orange',    'Outbound / departed'],
    ['--ae-success',   'Success / whitelist'],
    ['--ae-danger',    'Danger / blacklist'],
    ['--ae-safe',      'Safe drop'],
    ['--ae-bg',        'Background'],
    ['--ae-surface',   'Surface'],
    ['--ae-text',      'Text']
  ];
  const SUITE_CFG_KEY = 'ae::suite::v1';
  const suiteCfg = () => { try { return JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(SUITE_CFG_KEY)) || {}; } catch (e) { return {}; } };
  const saveSuiteCfg = (c) => { try { localStorage.setItem(SUITE_CFG_KEY, JSON.stringify(c)); } catch (e) {} };

  let overlay = null;
  function closeSettings() { if (overlay) { overlay.remove(); overlay = null; } }

  function openSettings() {
    closeSettings();
    overlay = document.createElement('div');
    overlay.className = 'ae-overlay';

    const modal = document.createElement('div');
    modal.className = 'ae-modal';
    overlay.appendChild(modal);

    const bar = document.createElement('div');
    bar.className = 'ae-modal__bar';
    const dot = document.createElement('span'); dot.className = 'ae-panel__dot is-ok';
    const title = document.createElement('span'); title.textContent = 'Master Settings';
    const closeX = document.createElement('button');
    closeX.className = 'ae-toggle'; closeX.type = 'button';
    closeX.textContent = '\u00D7'; closeX.title = 'Close';
    closeX.style.marginLeft = 'auto';
    bar.append(dot, title, closeX);
    modal.appendChild(bar);

    const body = document.createElement('div');
    body.className = 'ae-modal__body';
    modal.appendChild(body);

    // ── modules ──
    const secM = document.createElement('div'); secM.className = 'ae-sec';
    const hM = document.createElement('div'); hM.className = 'ae-sec__h';
    hM.textContent = 'Modules (reload applies)';
    secM.appendChild(hM);
    const cfg = suiteCfg();
    const disabled = cfg.disabled || {};
    const KNOWN = [
      ['reports-monitor', 'Report Scanner + Intercept Solver'],
      ['tech-harvester', 'Tech Harvester'],
      ['bookmarks', 'Quick Bookmarks'], ['bookmarks2', 'Auto-Launch + Destinations'],
      ['prebattle-predictor', 'Pre-Battle Predictor'], ['empire-menu', 'Empire Menu'],
      ['map-intel', 'Base Intel Database'],
      ['overseer', 'Layout Overseer']
    ];
    KNOWN.forEach(([id, label]) => {
      const row = document.createElement('div'); row.className = 'ae-field';
      const lab = document.createElement('label'); lab.textContent = label;
      const cb = document.createElement('input');
      cb.type = 'checkbox'; cb.checked = !disabled[id];
      cb.addEventListener('change', () => {
        const c = suiteCfg(); c.disabled = c.disabled || {};
        if (cb.checked) delete c.disabled[id]; else c.disabled[id] = true;
        saveSuiteCfg(c);
        setMsg('module "' + id + '" ' + (cb.checked ? 'enabled' : 'disabled') + ' — reload to apply');
      });
      row.append(lab, cb);
      secM.appendChild(row);
    });
    body.appendChild(secM);

    // ── theme ──
    const secT = document.createElement('div'); secT.className = 'ae-sec';
    const hT = document.createElement('div'); hT.className = 'ae-sec__h';
    hT.textContent = 'Theme (applies live to every panel)';
    secT.appendChild(hT);
    const ov = AEShared.getOverrides();
    THEME_FIELDS.forEach(([tok, label]) => {
      const row = document.createElement('div'); row.className = 'ae-field';
      const lab = document.createElement('label'); lab.textContent = label;
      const inp = document.createElement('input');
      inp.type = 'color';
      inp.value = ov[tok] || AEShared.TOKENS[tok];   // every listed token is canonical
      inp.addEventListener('change', () => {
        const patch = {}; patch[tok] = inp.value;
        AEShared.store.setTheme(patch);      // bumps rev -> all panels re-adopt
        mirrorToMaster();
        setMsg('theme: ' + tok + ' = ' + inp.value);
      });
      row.append(lab, inp);
      secT.appendChild(row);
    });
    body.appendChild(secT);

    // ── layout ──
    const secL = document.createElement('div'); secL.className = 'ae-sec';
    const hL = document.createElement('div'); hL.className = 'ae-sec__h';
    hL.textContent = 'Layout';
    secL.appendChild(hL);
    // tiles per gutter — rebuilds the snap grid for every module
    const rowSlots = document.createElement('div'); rowSlots.className = 'ae-field';
    const labSlots = document.createElement('label');
    labSlots.textContent = 'Tiles per gutter (' + AEShared.ZONE_SLOTS_MIN + '\u2013' + AEShared.ZONE_SLOTS_MAX + ')';
    const inpSlots = document.createElement('input');
    inpSlots.type = 'number';
    inpSlots.className = 'ae-input';
    inpSlots.style.width = '64px';
    inpSlots.min = String(AEShared.ZONE_SLOTS_MIN);
    inpSlots.max = String(AEShared.ZONE_SLOTS_MAX);
    inpSlots.value = String(AEShared.getSlots());
    inpSlots.addEventListener('change', () => {
      const n = AEShared.setSlots(inpSlots.value);
      inpSlots.value = String(n);
      mirrorToMaster();
      // zones saved beyond the new count are clamped by parseZone, so nothing
      // ends up off-grid; just re-render.
      LISTED.forEach(prefill); renderGhosts(); renderZones(null);
      setMsg('grid: ' + n + ' tiles per gutter (' + (n * 2) + ' zones)');
    });
    rowSlots.append(labSlots, inpSlots);
    secL.appendChild(rowSlots);

    const note = document.createElement('div');
    note.className = 'ae-muted';
    note.style.fontSize = '10px';
    note.textContent = 'Edge offset ' + AEShared.layout.edgeGap + 'px \u00B7 widths ' +
                       W_MIN + '\u2013' + W_MAX + 'px \u00B7 priority: higher = on top \u00B7 ' +
                       'panels flip upward if they would run off the bottom \u00B7 syncs across servers.';
    secL.appendChild(note);
    body.appendChild(secL);

    // ── footer ──
    const foot = document.createElement('div');
    foot.className = 'ae-modal__foot';
    const bReset = document.createElement('button');
    bReset.className = 'ae-btn'; bReset.textContent = 'Reset theme';
    bReset.addEventListener('click', () => {
      const st = AEShared.store.read(); delete st.theme;
      st.rev = (st.rev || 0) + 1; st.ts = Date.now();
      AEShared.store.write(st); mirrorToMaster();
      closeSettings(); openSettings();
      setMsg('theme reset to defaults');
    });
    const bResetAll = document.createElement('button');
    bResetAll.className = 'ae-btn'; bResetAll.textContent = 'Reset layout';
    bResetAll.addEventListener('click', () => {
      AEShared.store.clearAll(); mirrorToMaster();
      LISTED.forEach(prefill); renderGhosts();
      setMsg('all layout overrides cleared');
    });
    const bDone = document.createElement('button');
    bDone.className = 'ae-btn ae-btn--primary'; bDone.textContent = 'Done';
    bDone.style.marginLeft = 'auto';
    bDone.addEventListener('click', closeSettings);
    foot.append(bReset, bResetAll, bDone);
    modal.appendChild(foot);

    closeX.addEventListener('click', closeSettings);
    overlay.addEventListener('click', (e) => { if (e.target === overlay) closeSettings(); });
    shadow.appendChild(overlay);
  }

  root.querySelector('.ov-settings').addEventListener('click', (e) => { e.stopPropagation(); openSettings(); });

  console.log('[AE Overseer] v0.6.0 ready; sync:', syncMsg, '; detected:', detectLoaded().join(', ') || '(none)');
})();

} });


/* ============ MODULE: scout-killer ============ */
AESuite.register({ id: "scout-killer", title: "Scout Killer", pages: null, init: function () {
/* AE Scout Killer — predict autoscout arrival windows.
   Model: scouts hop region->region in ZIG-ZAG grid order, entering each region at the
   lowest system coordinate (guild JG bases override — supported in the shared model,
   not exposed in this UI), dwell ~90 s per astro. Speed 12 x (1+0.05 x Warp).
   Data sources:
   - TRACKED: the report scanner's observation log (scout-sized movements, size 40) —
     shared GM storage in the suite. Last 3 scouts shown with their next predicted
     SYSTEM and a countdown.
   - MANUAL: a sighting coordinate + time, against the galaxy's enumerated astro list
     (paced + 429-backoff; cached per galaxy). */
(function () {
  'use strict';
  if (typeof AEShared === 'undefined' || typeof AEMapIntel === 'undefined') return;
  const SERVER = location.hostname.split('.')[0];
  const CACHE_KEY = 'ae::scoutkiller::astros::' + SERVER;
  const REPLOG_KEY = location.hostname.toLowerCase() + '::aeplus_rep_obs_log';   // reports-monitor log (shared GM scope in the suite)

  function el(tag, attrs, ...kids) {
    const e = document.createElement(tag);
    if (attrs) for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(attrs)) { if (k === 'class') e.className = v; else if (k === 'style') e.style.cssText = v; else e.setAttribute(k, v); }
    for (const k of kids) e.appendChild(typeof k === 'string' ? document.createTextNode(k) : k);
    return e;
  }
  // 24h, built by hand: toLocaleTimeString is locale-dependent and yields "10:21 AM"
  // under en-US regardless of the 2-digit hints.
  const fmtT = (ms) => { const d = new Date(ms);
    return String(d.getHours()).padStart(2, '0') + ':' + String(d.getMinutes()).padStart(2, '0'); };
  // countdown: m:ss under an hour, else h+mm — seconds matter when a scout is imminent
  function fmtCd(ms) {
    const t = Math.max(0, Math.round(ms / 1000));
    const h = Math.floor(t / 3600), m = Math.floor((t % 3600) / 60), sec = t % 60;
    return h ? (h + 'h' + String(m).padStart(2, '0')) : (m + ':' + String(sec).padStart(2, '0'));
  }
  function fmtDur(ms) { const s = Math.max(0, Math.round(ms / 1000)); const h = Math.floor(s / 3600), m = Math.floor((s % 3600) / 60); return h ? h + 'h' + String(m).padStart(2, '0') : m + 'm'; }
  const sysOf = (coord) => coord.split(':').slice(0, 3).join(':');
  function sysLink(coord) {
    const sys = sysOf(coord);
    const a = el('a', { href: '/map.aspx?loc=' + sys }, sys);
    a.style.color = AEShared.token('--ae-primary') || 'inherit'; a.style.textDecoration = 'none';
    return a;
  }

  function cachedAstros(gal) { try { const c = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(CACHE_KEY)) || {}; return c[gal] || null; } catch (e) { return null; } }
  function cacheAstros(gal, rows) { try { const c = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(CACHE_KEY)) || {}; c[gal] = { t: Date.now(), rows }; localStorage.setItem(CACHE_KEY, JSON.stringify(c)); } catch (e) {} }
  function galaxyOfHere() {
    const m = document.body ? document.body.textContent.match(/\b([A-Z]\d{2}):\d{2}:\d{2}/) : null;
    return m ? m[1] : '';
  }
  // Scout-sized observations from the report scanner's log, grouped per player.
  // Returns [{player, playerId, coord, galaxy, t, history:[{coord,t}...]}] newest-first
  // (history = ALL of that player's sightings, oldest-first, for warp inference).
  function scoutSightings(n) {
    let log = [];
    try { const raw = GM_getValue(REPLOG_KEY, ''); if (raw) log = JSON.parse(raw); } catch (e) {}
    if (!Array.isArray(log)) return [];
    const byPlayer = new Map();
    for (const e of log) {
      if (!e || !e.isScout40 || !e.destLoc) continue;
      const t = (e.arrivalEta && Date.parse(e.arrivalEta)) || (e.arrivalClock && Date.parse(e.arrivalClock)) || Date.parse(e.observedAt) || Date.now();
      const key = e.playerId || e.player || '?';
      if (!byPlayer.has(key)) byPlayer.set(key, { player: e.player || 'unknown', playerId: e.playerId || null, sightings: [] });
      byPlayer.get(key).sightings.push({ coord: e.destLoc, t });
    }
    const out = [];
    for (const p of byPlayer.values()) {
      p.sightings.sort((a, b) => a.t - b.t);
      const last = p.sightings[p.sightings.length - 1];
      out.push({ player: p.player, playerId: p.playerId, coord: last.coord,
                 galaxy: last.coord.split(':')[0], t: last.t, history: p.sightings });
    }
    out.sort((a, b) => b.t - a.t);   // recency; renderTracked re-sorts by predicted ETA
    return out;
  }
  // Remove a scout's observations from the shared log once its prediction has run too
  // far ahead of the last real sighting (>5 regions): stale predictions otherwise
  // propagate as phantom rows. Returns true if anything was purged.
  function purgeScout(playerId, coord) {
    try {
      const raw = GM_getValue(REPLOG_KEY, ''); if (!raw) return false;
      let log = JSON.parse(raw); if (!Array.isArray(log)) return false;
      const before = log.length;
      log = log.filter(e => {
        if (!e || !e.isScout40) return true;
        const samePlayer = playerId ? (e.playerId === playerId) : true;
        return !samePlayer;   // drop this scout's sightings
      });
      if (log.length !== before) { GM_setValue(REPLOG_KEY, JSON.stringify(log)); return true; }
    } catch (e) {}
    return false;
  }
  function mirrorWarpFor(playerId) {
    if (!playerId) return null;
    try {
      const mir = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('ae::techmirror::' + SERVER)) || {};
      const rec = mir.players && mir.players[playerId];
      return rec && rec.warpInferred ? rec.warpInferred : null;
    } catch (e) { return null; }
  }
  // Write an inferred Warp Drive level into the tech mirror (the harvester's central
  // store) so every consumer sees it alongside harvested techs. Non-destructive:
  // techs['Warp Drive'] plus a warpInferred provenance marker.
  function publishInferredWarp(playerId, playerName, inf, force) {
    if (!playerId || !inf) return;
    try {
      const key = 'ae::techmirror::' + SERVER;
      const mir = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(key)) || { updated: 0, players: {}, archetypes: {} };
      const rec = mir.players[playerId] || (mir.players[playerId] = { name: playerName, techs: {} });
      rec.techs = rec.techs || {};
      const prev = rec.warpInferred;
      let level = inf.warp, pairs = inf.pairs, rejectedNext = prev && prev.rejectedNext;
      if (force) {
        // user confirmed a real tech increase: adopt outright and clear any prior rejection
        level = inf.warp; pairs = Math.max(inf.pairs, 2); rejectedNext = null;
      } else if (prev) {
        // MERGE, never overwrite: evidence accumulates. Levels agree (±1) -> keep max pairs;
        // a disagreeing estimate replaces only if it has MORE pairs behind it.
        if (Math.abs(prev.level - inf.warp) <= 1) { pairs = Math.max(prev.pairs, inf.pairs); level = inf.pairs >= prev.pairs ? inf.warp : prev.level; }
        else if (prev.pairs > inf.pairs) { level = prev.level; pairs = prev.pairs; }
      }
      rec.techs['Warp Drive'] = level;
      rec.warpInferred = { level, speed: inf.speed, pairs, at: Date.now(), src: 'scout-killer', rejectedNext: rejectedNext };
      mir.updated = Date.now();
      localStorage.setItem(key, JSON.stringify(mir));
    } catch (e) {}
  }
  // ── warp-increase confirmation channel (a JG artefact and a real tech gain look alike) ──
  const WARP_CONFIRM_KEY = 'ae::scoutkiller::warpconfirm::' + SERVER;
  function readWarpConfirms() { try { return JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(WARP_CONFIRM_KEY)) || {}; } catch (e) { return {}; } }
  function writeWarpConfirms(o) { try { localStorage.setItem(WARP_CONFIRM_KEY, JSON.stringify(o)); } catch (e) {} }
  function addWarpConfirm(playerId, name, prev, next, inf) {
    const o = readWarpConfirms();
    const cur = o[playerId];
    if (cur && cur.next >= next) return;   // already pending an equal/greater raise
    o[playerId] = { name: name, prev: prev, next: next, speed: inf.speed, pairs: inf.pairs, jgDropped: inf.jgDropped || 0, at: Date.now() };
    writeWarpConfirms(o);
  }
  function removeWarpConfirm(playerId) { const o = readWarpConfirms(); if (o[playerId]) { delete o[playerId]; writeWarpConfirms(o); } }
  // Gate adoption: a jump of MORE THAN 2 levels above the known warp is held for the user to
  // confirm (real increase) or reject (JG artefact / noise). Within 2 levels -> adopt normally.
  function maybeAdoptWarp(playerId, name, inf) {
    if (!playerId || !inf) return;
    const prev = mirrorWarpFor(playerId);
    if (!prev) { publishInferredWarp(playerId, name, inf); return; }
    if (inf.warp - prev.level > 2) {
      if (prev.rejectedNext != null && inf.warp <= prev.rejectedNext) return;   // user already declined this raise
      addWarpConfirm(playerId, name, prev.level, inf.warp, inf);
      return;   // hold at the known (lower) level until confirmed
    }
    publishInferredWarp(playerId, name, inf);
  }

  function predictLine(rows, coord, tSeen, wMin, wMax) {
    const path = AEMapIntel.buildScoutPath(rows);
    const res = AEMapIntel.predictScoutStops(path, coord, tSeen, wMin, wMax, { count: 1 });
    return res && res.stops[0] ? res.stops[0] : null;
  }

  const CFG_KEY = 'ae::scoutkiller::cfg::' + SERVER;
  function loadCfg() { try { return JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(CFG_KEY)) || {}; } catch (e) { return {}; } }
  function saveCfg(patch) { try { localStorage.setItem(CFG_KEY, JSON.stringify(Object.assign(loadCfg(), patch))); } catch (e) {} }
