  function build() {
    const host = document.createElement('div');
    host.id = 'ae-scoutkiller-host';
    host.setAttribute('data-ae-script', 'scout-killer');
    const root = host.attachShadow({ mode: 'open' });
    AEShared.adopt(root);                                    // theme via textContent (TT-safe)
    const css = document.createElement('style'); css.textContent = SK_CSS; root.append(css);

    // Panel chrome: EXACT house pattern (ae-panel__bar / __titles / __sub / __status /
    // __sweep / __body) so shared CSS and the Overseer treat it like every other module.
    const panel = el('div', { class: 'ae-panel' });
    const dot = el('span', { class: 'ae-panel__dot sk-traffic idle', title: 'idle' });
    const statusEl = el('span', { class: 'ae-panel__status' }, 'idle');
    const collapseBtn = AEShared.makeToggle({ collapsed: false });
    // header toggle for the MANUAL section: hiding it turns the panel into a pure
    // watchlist, so it then shows up to 10 observations instead of 3.
    const manualBtn = el('button', { class: 'ae-toggle', title: 'Show/hide manual prediction' }, 'M');
    const bar = el('div', { class: 'ae-panel__bar' },
      dot,
      el('span', { class: 'ae-panel__titles' },
        el('span', { class: 'ae-panel__title' }, 'SCOUT KILLER'),
        el('span', { class: 'ae-panel__sub' }, SERVER + ' / autoscout predictor')),
      statusEl, manualBtn, collapseBtn);
    const sweep = el('div', { class: 'ae-panel__sweep' });
    const body = el('div', { class: 'ae-panel__body' });
    panel.append(bar, sweep, body);
    root.append(panel);

    // collapse: persisted, CSS-class driven (house pattern)
    const cfg0 = loadCfg();
    const applyCollapsed = (c) => { panel.classList.toggle('sk-collapsed', c); collapseBtn.set(c); };
    let collapsed = !!cfg0.collapsed; applyCollapsed(collapsed);
    let manualHidden = !!cfg0.manualHidden;
    let autoKill = !!cfg0.autoKill;
    let killFleetId = cfg0.killFleetId || null;
    let testMode = cfg0.testMode !== false;   // default ON: never commits until Aran flips it
    let killScouts = cfg0.killScouts != null ? cfg0.killScouts : 3;   // send a TRIO of Scout Ships, not the whole fleet
    let priorityGuilds = Array.isArray(cfg0.priorityGuilds) ? cfg0.priorityGuilds.slice() : [];
    let maxTeams = cfg0.maxTeams != null ? cfg0.maxTeams : 5;
    let paceMinS = cfg0.paceMinS != null ? cfg0.paceMinS : 3;   // global min gap between ANY two AK actions
    let paceMaxS = cfg0.paceMaxS != null ? cfg0.paceMaxS : 5;
    let runMinutes = cfg0.runMinutes != null ? cfg0.runMinutes : 60;   // overall run cap (0 = no limit)
    let softStop = !!cfg0.softStop;    // soft: let in-flight kills finish; hard: abandon them
    let winddown = false;              // soft-stop drain in progress (queue still finishing)
    let killSpeedOverride = cfg0.killSpeedOverride != null ? cfg0.killSpeedOverride : 0;   // ly/h, 0 = auto
    let lookAhead = !!cfg0.lookAhead;
    let lookAheadN = cfg0.lookAheadN != null ? cfg0.lookAheadN : 2;
    let myGuildOverride = cfg0.myGuild || null;   // else AEMapIntel.getSelfGuild() (own-base detection)
    const myGuild = () => (myGuildOverride || AEMapIntel.getSelfGuild() || '').replace(/[\[\]]/g, '').toUpperCase() || null;
    // Same-guild Jump Gate speed boost for a fleet DEPARTING its origin region, from Base
    // Intel's lazily-parsed JG registry: effective speed x (1 + 0.7 x level). Only used for
    // the ESTIMATE fallback — a MEASURED speed already includes the real boost.
    const jgLevelAtOrigin = () => { const kf = killFleet(); return kf && kf.loc ? AEMapIntel.friendlyJgLevel(kf.loc, myGuild()) : 0; };
    const jgBoost = () => 1 + 0.7 * jgLevelAtOrigin();
    // Real kill-fleet speed, measured from the game's own distance/duration on each send
    // (so a strategic-JG / tech speed boost is captured, unit-safe), cached per fleet.
    const FLEETSPEED_KEY = 'ae::scoutkiller::fleetspeed::' + SERVER;
    const readFleetSpeeds = () => { try { return JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(FLEETSPEED_KEY)) || {}; } catch (e) { return {}; } };
    const cacheFleetSpeed = (fleetId, lyh) => { if (!(lyh > 0)) return; try { const m = readFleetSpeeds(); m[fleetId] = { lyh: Math.round(lyh * 10) / 10, at: Date.now() }; localStorage.setItem(FLEETSPEED_KEY, JSON.stringify(m)); } catch (e) {} };
    // measured (real, boost-included) > user override > null (fall back to the warp model)
    const killFleetSpeed = () => { const kf = killFleet(); if (kf) { const m = readFleetSpeeds()[kf.fleetId]; if (m && m.lyh > 0) return m.lyh; } return killSpeedOverride > 0 ? killSpeedOverride : null; };
    const RUN_DEADLINE_KEY = 'ae::scoutkiller::rundeadline::' + SERVER;
    const runDeadline = () => { const v = parseInt(localStorage.getItem(RUN_DEADLINE_KEY), 10); return Number.isFinite(v) ? v : null; };
    const setRunDeadline = (ms) => { try { if (ms) localStorage.setItem(RUN_DEADLINE_KEY, String(ms)); else localStorage.removeItem(RUN_DEADLINE_KEY); } catch (e) {} };
    const guildOf = (name) => { const m = /^\s*\[([^\]]+)\]/.exec(name || ''); return m ? m[1].trim().toUpperCase() : null; };
    const isPriority = (sg) => { const g = guildOf(sg && sg.player); return !!g && priorityGuilds.includes(g); };
    const parsePriorityInput = (txt) => [...new Set(String(txt || '').split(/[\s,]+/).map(t => t.replace(/[\[\]]/g, '').trim().toUpperCase()).filter(Boolean))];
    let empireFleets = [];                     // parsed from the empire fleet page on demand
    const killFleet = () => empireFleets.find(f => f.fleetId === killFleetId) || null;
    collapseBtn.addEventListener('click', () => { collapsed = !collapsed; applyCollapsed(collapsed); saveCfg({ collapsed }); });

    /* Traffic light has two independent sources: the astro LOAD state and an ARRIVAL
       alert (a scout inside its window). The load state wins while a load is running;
       otherwise an in-window scout pulses green rapidly. */
    let loadState = 'idle', alerting = false;
    const paintLight = () => {
      const st = (loadState === 'running' || loadState === 'paused') ? loadState
               : (alerting ? 'alert' : loadState);
      dot.className = 'ae-panel__dot sk-traffic ' + st;
      dot.title = st === 'alert' ? 'scout arriving now' : st;
    };
    const setLight = (state) => { loadState = state; paintLight(); };
    // Rows register their window here; a 1s ticker updates ONLY the countdown text and
    // class, so the expensive path prediction still runs on the 60s cadence.
    let liveRows = [];
    let akLaunchAt = null;
    let akCandidates = [];               // {sg, est, launchAt} for feasible, not-yet-launched scouts
    const SEND_LEAD_MS = 15000;          // processing/network buffer before the fleet departs
    // Conservative launch moment: assume the SLOWEST warp so we depart early enough, minus
    // the 5s pre-land margin and the send lead. Auto-kill fires when now reaches this.
    function launchAtFor(stop) {
      const kf = killFleet();
      const d = (kf && kf.loc && stop) ? AEMapIntel.distance(kf.loc, stop.coord) : null;
      const travelSec = d ? d.ly / (killFleetSpeed() || 12 * (1 + 0.05 * wMinV())) * 3600 : 0;   // conservative: no speculative JG boost in timing (measured speed corrects it)
      return stop.etaEarly - travelSec * 1000 - 5000 - SEND_LEAD_MS;
    }
    // The next N upcoming stops on the scout's predicted path (N=1 unless look-ahead is on).
    function upcomingStops(sg, path) {
      const n = lookAhead ? Math.max(1, lookAheadN) : 1;
      const res = AEMapIntel.predictScoutStops(path, sg.coord, sg.t, wMinV(), wMaxV(), { count: 40, allowLaps: true });
      if (!res || !res.stops) return [];
      const now = Date.now();
      return res.stops.filter(st => st.etaLate >= now).slice(0, n);
    }
    // Choose where to intercept: the NEAREST upcoming stop the kill fleet can still reach in
    // time. With look-ahead off this is just the next stop; on, it lets a JG-fast fleet skip
    // to a stop 2..N regions ahead when the immediate one is out of reach.
    function chooseInterceptStop(sg, path, est) {
      const stops = upcomingStops(sg, path);
      const kf = killFleet();
      if (kf && kf.loc) {
        for (let i = 0; i < stops.length; i++) {
          const st = stops[i];
          const d = AEMapIntel.distance(kf.loc, st.coord);
          if (!d) continue;
          const travelSec = d.ly / killSpeedLyH() * 3600;
          if ((st.etaEarly - Date.now()) / 1000 - travelSec - 5 >= 0) { st.aheadIdx = i; return st; }
        }
      }
      const s0 = stops[0] || (est ? est.stop : null); if (s0) s0.aheadIdx = 0; return s0;
    }
    function tickCountdowns() {
      const now = Date.now();
      let any = false;
      for (const r of liveRows) {
        // No isConnected guard: liveRows is rebuilt by every renderTracked(), so entries
        // are always current — and the first render happens BEFORE the host is attached,
        // which would otherwise leave every countdown blank until the first tick.
        if (!r.el) continue;
        if (now < r.etaEarly) { r.el.textContent = fmtCd(r.etaEarly - now); r.el.classList.remove('now'); }
        else if (now <= r.etaLate) { r.el.textContent = 'NOW'; r.el.classList.add('now'); any = true; }
        else { r.el.textContent = 'passed'; r.el.classList.remove('now'); }
      }
      if (any !== alerting) { alerting = any; paintLight(); }
      if (autoKill) { const dl = runDeadline(); if (dl && Date.now() >= dl) stopKillSession('time up', softStop); }
      if (winddown) { let q = []; try { q = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(ATK_QUEUE_KEY)) || []; } catch (e) {} if (!q.length) { winddown = false; setStatus('run ended \u2014 in-flight kills complete'); } }
      updateAkBanner();
      // AK launch countdown
      if (akTimerEl) {
        if (akLaunchAt == null) { akTimerEl.textContent = 'AK \u2014'; akTimerEl.classList.remove('due'); }
        else {
          const left = akLaunchAt - now;
          if (left <= 0) { akTimerEl.textContent = 'AK now'; akTimerEl.classList.add('due'); }
          else { akTimerEl.textContent = 'AK ' + fmtCd(left); akTimerEl.classList.remove('due'); }
        }
      }
    }
    let lastStatus = 'idle';
    const setStatus = (t) => { statusEl.textContent = t; lastStatus = t; updateAkBanner(); };

    // ── progress bar + load controls ──
    const pbar = el('div', { class: 'sk-bar' }, el('div', { class: 'sk-bar__fill' }));
    const barFill = pbar.firstChild;
    const ctlRow = el('div', { style: 'display:none;gap:4px;margin:2px 0;' });
    const pauseBtn = el('button', { class: 'ae-btn', style: 'font-size:10px;padding:1px 8px;' }, '\u23f8 pause');
    const stopBtn = el('button', { class: 'ae-btn', style: 'font-size:10px;padding:1px 8px;' }, '\u23f9 stop');
    ctlRow.append(pauseBtn, stopBtn);
    body.append(pbar, ctlRow);
    const loadCtl = { paused: false, stopped: false };
    pauseBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
      loadCtl.paused = !loadCtl.paused;
      pauseBtn.textContent = loadCtl.paused ? '\u23f5 resume' : '\u23f8 pause';
      setLight(loadCtl.paused ? 'paused' : 'running');
    });
    stopBtn.addEventListener('click', () => { loadCtl.stopped = true; });

    /* Export the cached galaxy's path in the AE-SCOUTPATH v1 format for the Discord
       bot. Clipboard write is attempted; the block is ALSO shown in a copyable overlay
       because navigator.clipboard can be denied without user gesture context. */
    function exportPath(gal) {
      const cached = cachedAstros(gal);
      if (!cached) { setStatus('load ' + gal + ' astros first'); return; }
      const text = AEMapIntel.exportScoutPath(gal, cached.rows);
      if (!text) { setStatus('export failed \u2014 cached rows are not from ' + gal + '?'); return; }
      const over = el('div', { style: 'position:fixed;inset:0;z-index:2147483300;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;background:rgba(0,0,0,.5);' });
      const card = el('div', { class: 'ae-panel', style: 'width:min(520px,90vw);padding:10px;' });
      card.append(el('div', { class: 'ae-muted', style: 'font-size:10px;margin-bottom:4px;' },
        'AE-SCOUTPATH v1 \u00b7 ' + gal + ' \u00b7 paste into Discord: -scoutpath set'));
      const ta = el('textarea', { class: 'ae-input', style: 'width:100%;height:110px;font:10px/1.4 var(--ae-font-mono);resize:vertical;' });
      ta.value = text;
      card.append(ta);
      if (text.length > 1900) card.append(el('div', { class: 'ae-muted', style: 'font-size:10px;color:var(--ae-warn);' },
        text.length + ' chars \u2014 exceeds one Discord message (2000); split before sending'));
      const rowB = el('div', { style: 'display:flex;gap:6px;margin-top:6px;' });
      const copyB = el('button', { class: 'ae-btn ae-btn--primary' }, 'Copy');
      const closeB = el('button', { class: 'ae-btn' }, 'Close');
      rowB.append(copyB, closeB); card.append(rowB);
      copyB.addEventListener('click', () => {
        ta.select();
        const done = () => { copyB.textContent = 'Copied \u2713'; setTimeout(() => { copyB.textContent = 'Copy'; }, 1500); };
        if (navigator.clipboard && navigator.clipboard.writeText) navigator.clipboard.writeText(text).then(done, done);
        else { try { document.execCommand('copy'); } catch (e) {} done(); }
      });
      closeB.addEventListener('click', () => over.remove());
      over.addEventListener('click', (e) => { if (e.target === over) over.remove(); });
      over.append(card); root.append(over);
      ta.select();
      setStatus(gal + ' path: ' + text.split('\n')[1].split(',').length + ' stops, ' + text.length + ' chars');
    }

    /* Idle header note: whether the manual galaxy's astros are already parsed.
       Never overwrites an active load's status. */
    function updateCachedNote() {
      if (loading) return;
      const gal = galIn.value.toUpperCase().trim();
      if (!gal) { setStatus('idle'); return; }
      const c = cachedAstros(gal);
      setStatus(c ? gal + ' \u2713 ' + c.rows.length + ' astros \u00b7 ' + fmtDur(Date.now() - c.t) + ' old'
                  : gal + ' not parsed');
    }

    let loading = false;
    async function runLoad(gal) {
      if (loading) { setStatus('a load is already running'); return null; }
      const cached = cachedAstros(gal);
      if (cached) return cached.rows;
      loading = true; loadCtl.paused = false; loadCtl.stopped = false;
      pauseBtn.textContent = '\u23f8 pause';
      pbar.style.display = 'block'; barFill.style.width = '0%'; ctlRow.style.display = 'flex';
      setLight('running'); setStatus('enumerating ' + gal + '\u2026');
      const cfg = loadCfg();
      try {
        const rows = await AEMapIntel.enumerateGalaxyAstros(gal, (p) => {
          if (!p) return;
          if (p.frac != null) barFill.style.width = Math.round(p.frac * 100) + '%';
          if (p.rateLimited) setStatus(gal + ': rate-limited \u2014 backing off ' + Math.round(p.waitMs / 1000) + 's\u2026');
          else if (p.paused) setStatus(gal + ': paused');
          else if (p.total != null) setStatus(gal + ': ' + p.total + ' astros \u00b7 ' + Math.round((p.frac || 0) * 100) + '%');
        }, { isPaused: () => loadCtl.paused, isStopped: () => loadCtl.stopped,
             paceSec: parseFloat(cfg.paceSec) || 1.1, jitterPct: parseFloat(cfg.jitterPct) || 30 });
        const mapped = rows.map(a => ({ coord: a.coord, hasBase: !!a.hasBase }));
        cacheAstros(gal, mapped);
        barFill.style.width = '100%'; setLight('done');
        setStatus(mapped.length + ' astros \u00b7 ' + gal);
        return mapped;
      } catch (e) {
        setLight('idle');
        setStatus(/stopped by user/.test(e.message) ? gal + ': stopped \u2014 partial discarded' : gal + ' failed: ' + e.message);
        return null;
      } finally {
        loading = false;
        setTimeout(() => { pbar.style.display = 'none'; ctlRow.style.display = 'none'; }, 1200);
      }
    }

    // ── auto-kill setup ──
    // Priority-guilds settings overlay (opened from the gear in the KILL header)
    function openPriorityOverlay() {
      const over = el('div', { style: 'position:fixed;inset:0;z-index:2147483300;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;background:rgba(0,0,0,.5);' });

      // max concurrent teams
      const teamsIn = el('input', { class: 'ae-input sk-num', type: 'number', min: '1', max: '20', title: 'how many kill teams may be out at once (returning teams do not count)' });
      teamsIn.value = String(maxTeams);
      card.append(el('div', { class: 'sk-row', style: 'font-size:11px;margin-bottom:6px;' }, el('span', { class: 'ae-muted', style: 'min-width:120px;' }, 'max AK teams'), teamsIn));
      // global pace (min/max seconds between ANY two actions, across all teams)
      const pMinIn = el('input', { class: 'ae-input sk-num', type: 'number', min: '1', step: '0.5', title: 'shortest gap between any two actions' });
      const pMaxIn = el('input', { class: 'ae-input sk-num', type: 'number', min: '1', step: '0.5', title: 'longest gap between any two actions' });
      pMinIn.value = String(paceMinS); pMaxIn.value = String(paceMaxS);
      card.append(el('div', { class: 'sk-row', style: 'font-size:11px;margin-bottom:6px;' },

      card.append(el('div', { class: 'ae-muted', style: 'font-size:10px;margin-bottom:8px;' },
        'One shared pace across ALL teams \u2014 the script acts like a single player, never bursting.'));
      // overall run cap
      const runIn = el('input', { class: 'ae-input sk-num', type: 'number', min: '0', title: 'stop the whole scout killer after this many minutes (0 = no limit); stopping clears every in-flight kill too' });

      card.append(el('div', { class: 'sk-row', style: 'font-size:11px;margin-bottom:2px;' }, el('span', { class: 'ae-muted', style: 'min-width:120px;' }, 'run for (min)'), runIn, el('span', { class: 'ae-muted' }, '0 = no limit')));
      // kill-fleet speed override (0 = auto-measure from the game). Accounts for a strategic-JG boost.

      spdIn.value = String(killSpeedOverride);

      card.append(el('div', { class: 'sk-row', style: 'font-size:11px;margin-bottom:2px;' }, el('span', { class: 'ae-muted', style: 'min-width:120px;' }, 'kill speed (ly/h)'), spdIn, el('span', { class: 'ae-muted', style: 'font-size:10px;' }, measured ? ('measured ' + measured) : '0 = auto')));
      // my guild (for same-guild JG boost) + a live readout of the boost that applies now
      const guildIn = el('input', { class: 'ae-input', style: 'width:90px;', title: 'your guild tag \u2014 a same-guild Jump Gate in the kill fleet\'s region boosts its speed. Blank = auto-detect from your own base pages.' });

      card.append(el('div', { class: 'sk-row', style: 'font-size:11px;margin-bottom:2px;' }, el('span', { class: 'ae-muted', style: 'min-width:120px;' }, 'my guild'), guildIn, el('span', { class: 'ae-muted', style: 'font-size:10px;' }, jgNote)));

      card.append(el('div', { class: 'ae-muted', style: 'font-size:11px;margin-bottom:2px;' }, 'priority guilds \u2014 killed first'));
      card.append(el('div', { class: 'ae-muted', style: 'font-size:10px;margin-bottom:4px;' }, 'one per line or comma-separated (brackets optional)'));

      ta.value = priorityGuilds.join('\n');
      card.append(ta);
      const rowB = el('div', { style: 'display:flex;gap:6px;margin-top:8px;' });

      saveB.addEventListener('click', () => {

        let pmin = parseFloat(pMinIn.value), pmax = parseFloat(pMaxIn.value);
        if (Number.isFinite(pmin) && pmin >= 0) paceMinS = pmin;
        if (Number.isFinite(pmax) && pmax >= paceMinS) paceMaxS = pmax; else paceMaxS = Math.max(paceMinS, paceMaxS);
        const rm = parseInt(runIn.value, 10); if (Number.isFinite(rm) && rm >= 0) runMinutes = rm;
        const sp = parseFloat(spdIn.value); if (Number.isFinite(sp) && sp >= 0) killSpeedOverride = sp;
        const la = parseInt(laN.value, 10); if (Number.isFinite(la) && la >= 1) lookAheadN = la;

        priorityGuilds = parsePriorityInput(ta.value);
        saveCfg({ maxTeams, paceMinS, paceMaxS, runMinutes, softStop, killSpeedOverride, lookAhead, lookAheadN, myGuild: myGuildOverride, priorityGuilds });
        if (autoKill) startKillRun();   // apply the new run length to the active session
        over.remove();
        setStatus('settings saved \u00b7 ' + maxTeams + ' teams \u00b7 pace ' + paceMinS + '\u2013' + paceMaxS + 's' + (runMinutes > 0 ? ' \u00b7 ' + runMinutes + 'm run' : ''));
        renderTracked();

      closeB.addEventListener('click', () => over.remove());
      over.addEventListener('click', (e) => { if (e.target === over) over.remove(); });
      over.append(card); root.append(over); teamsIn.focus();
    }
    const prioGear = el('button', { class: 'ae-toggle', title: 'Kill settings \u2014 teams, pace, priority guilds', style: 'margin-left:auto;' }, '\u2699');

    body.append(el('div', { class: 'ae-muted sk-sect', style: 'display:flex;align-items:center;' }, 'KILL', prioGear));
    const fleetSel = el('select', { class: 'ae-input', style: 'flex:1;min-width:0;', title: 'kill fleet (from your empire fleet list)' });
    const refreshFleets = el('button', { class: 'ae-btn', style: 'font-size:10px;padding:1px 7px;', title: 'reload your fleet list' }, '\u21bb');

      fleetSel.append(el('option', { value: '' }, empireFleets.length ? '\u2014 select kill fleet \u2014' : '\u21bb load fleets'));
      for (const f of empireFleets) {
