      }
    }
    fleetSel.addEventListener('change', () => { killFleetId = fleetSel.value || null; saveCfg({ killFleetId }); renderTracked(); });
    refreshFleets.addEventListener('click', async () => { refreshFleets.disabled = true; await loadEmpireFleets(true); paintFleetOptions(); refreshFleets.disabled = false; renderTracked(); });
    paintFleetOptions();
    const scoutsIn = el('input', { class: 'ae-input sk-num', type: 'number', min: '1', max: '20', title: 'how many Scout Ships to send per kill (a trio is plenty)' });
    scoutsIn.value = String(killScouts);
    scoutsIn.addEventListener('change', () => { const v = parseInt(scoutsIn.value, 10); if (Number.isFinite(v) && v >= 1) { killScouts = v; saveCfg({ killScouts }); } });
    body.append(el('div', { class: 'sk-row' }, fleetSel, refreshFleets));
    body.append(el('div', { class: 'sk-row', style: 'font-size:10px;' }, el('span', { class: 'ae-muted' }, 'send'), scoutsIn, el('span', { class: 'ae-muted' }, 'Scout Ships per kill')));

    const autoCb = el('input', { type: 'checkbox' }); autoCb.checked = autoKill;
    const testCb = el('input', { type: 'checkbox' }); testCb.checked = testMode;
    const requestScanner = () => { try { window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('ae-start-report-scan')); } catch (e) {} };
    autoCb.addEventListener('change', () => {
      autoKill = autoCb.checked; saveCfg({ autoKill });
      if (autoKill) { akBannerDismissed = false; startKillRun(); requestScanner(); }   // start run clock + observations
      else { setRunDeadline(null); }
      setStatus(autoKill ? ('auto-kill ARMED' + (testMode ? ' (test)' : '') + (runMinutes > 0 ? ' \u00b7 ' + runMinutes + 'm run' : '')) : 'auto-kill off');
      renderTracked();
    });
    testCb.addEventListener('change', () => { testMode = testCb.checked; saveCfg({ testMode }); });
    const akTimerEl = el('span', { class: 'sk-ak', title: 'time until the next auto-kill fleet would launch (latest departure that still lands before the scout)' }, 'AK \u2014');
    body.append(el('div', { class: 'sk-row', style: 'gap:12px;font-size:10px;align-items:center;' },
      el('label', { class: 'sk-chk', title: 'automatically kill every feasible tracked scout' }, autoCb, ' auto-kill'),
      el('label', { class: 'sk-chk', title: 'test mode: do everything EXCEPT the final battle-confirm \u2014 beeps for a manual confirm' }, testCb, ' test mode'),
      akTimerEl));

    // ── tracked scouts ──
    body.append(el('div', { class: 'ae-muted sk-sect' }, 'TRACKED (report scanner, size-40)'));
    const trackBox = el('div', {});
    body.append(trackBox);

    // ── AUTO-KILL BANNER (top of page; mirrors the Base Intel scan banner) ──
    let AK_BANNER_HOST = null, akBannerEls = null, akBannerDismissed = false;
    const AK_BANNER_CSS = `
:host { all: initial; }
.ban { position: fixed; top: 0; left: 0; right: 0; z-index: 2147483001; font-family: var(--ae-font, system-ui, sans-serif);
       display: none; align-items: center; gap: 14px; padding: 8px 16px; box-sizing: border-box;
       color: #fff; border-bottom: 1px solid rgba(255,255,255,.25); backdrop-filter: blur(2px); }
.ban.on { display: flex; }
.ban .dot { width: 12px; height: 12px; border-radius: 50%; flex: 0 0 auto; box-shadow: 0 0 8px currentColor; }
.ban .txt { font-size: 13px; font-weight: 700; letter-spacing: .02em; white-space: nowrap; }
.ban .ak { font-size: 13px; font-weight: 700; font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums; white-space: nowrap; }
.ban .sub { font-size: 11px; opacity: .85; font-weight: 500; flex: 1 1 auto; overflow: hidden; text-overflow: ellipsis; white-space: nowrap; }
.ban .teams { font-size: 11px; opacity: .9; font-weight: 600; white-space: nowrap; }
.ban .close { cursor: pointer; font-size: 16px; opacity: .7; flex: 0 0 auto; }
.ban .close:hover { opacity: 1; }
.ban.armed { background: rgba(190, 30, 30, .92); }
.ban.armed .dot { color: #ff5a5a; background: #ff5a5a; animation: akPulseRed 1s ease-in-out infinite; }
@keyframes akPulseRed { 0%,100% { box-shadow: 0 0 6px #ff5a5a; opacity: 1; } 50% { box-shadow: 0 0 18px 4px #ff2a2a; opacity: .55; } }
.ban.due { background: rgba(30, 150, 70, .9); }
.ban.due .dot { color: #57e08b; background: #57e08b; animation: akPulseGreen .5s ease-in-out infinite; }
@keyframes akPulseGreen { 0%,100% { box-shadow: 0 0 8px #57e08b; opacity: 1; } 50% { box-shadow: 0 0 20px 5px #12d06a; opacity: .5; } }
.ban.test { filter: saturate(.8); }
    `;
    function mountAkBanner() {
      if (AK_BANNER_HOST) return;
      AK_BANNER_HOST = document.createElement('div');
      AK_BANNER_HOST.setAttribute('data-ae-script', 'scoutkiller-banner');
      const sh = AK_BANNER_HOST.attachShadow({ mode: 'open' });
      const st = document.createElement('style'); st.textContent = AK_BANNER_CSS; sh.appendChild(st);
      const ban = document.createElement('div'); ban.className = 'ban';
      const dot = document.createElement('span'); dot.className = 'dot';
      const txt = document.createElement('span'); txt.className = 'txt'; txt.textContent = 'AUTO-KILL';
      const ak = document.createElement('span'); ak.className = 'ak';
      const sub = document.createElement('span'); sub.className = 'sub';
      const teams = document.createElement('span'); teams.className = 'teams';
      const run = document.createElement('span'); run.className = 'teams';
      const close = document.createElement('span'); close.className = 'close'; close.textContent = '\u00d7'; close.title = 'Hide banner (stays hidden until re-armed)';
      close.addEventListener('click', () => { akBannerDismissed = true; ban.classList.remove('on'); });
      ban.append(dot, txt, ak, sub, teams, run, close);
      sh.appendChild(ban);
      document.documentElement.appendChild(AK_BANNER_HOST);
      akBannerEls = { ban, txt, ak, sub, teams, run };
    }
    function updateAkBanner() {
      if (!akBannerEls) return;
      const { ban, txt, ak, sub, teams, run } = akBannerEls;
      const hidden = AEShared.isMasterHidden && AEShared.isMasterHidden();
      if ((!autoKill && !winddown) || hidden || akBannerDismissed) { ban.classList.remove('on'); return; }
      const now = Date.now();
      if (winddown && !autoKill) {
        ban.className = 'ban on armed test';   // muted red, no pulse emphasis needed
        txt.textContent = 'WINDING DOWN';
        ak.textContent = '';
        teams.textContent = activeTeamCount() + ' finishing';
        run.textContent = '';
        sub.textContent = lastStatus || '';
        return;
      }
      const due = alerting || (akLaunchAt != null && akLaunchAt - now <= 0);
      ban.className = 'ban on ' + (due ? 'due' : 'armed') + (testMode ? ' test' : '');
      txt.textContent = 'AUTO-KILL' + (testMode ? ' (TEST)' : '');
      ak.textContent = akLaunchAt == null ? 'AK \u2014' : (akLaunchAt - now <= 0 ? 'LAUNCH NOW' : 'AK ' + fmtCd(akLaunchAt - now));
      teams.textContent = activeTeamCount() + '/' + maxTeams + ' teams';
      const dl = runDeadline();
      run.textContent = dl ? ('run ' + fmtCd(Math.max(0, dl - now)) + ' left') : '';
      sub.textContent = lastStatus || '';
    }

    // ── KILL ENGINE ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    // SAFETY: testMode (default ON) performs every step EXCEPT the final battle-confirm
    // POST — it lands the fleet, opens the attack, parses the target, fetches the
    // pre-battle page, then BEEPS and waits for a manual confirm. Flip testMode off only
    // after validating targeting + timing live. The move send itself is real in both
    // modes (a scout trio in flight is low-stakes and the attack phase needs it landed).
    // ── global action pacer (one shared rate across every team) ──
    let _paceChain = Promise.resolve();
    let _lastActionAt = 0;
    const paceGapMs = () => {
      const lo = Math.max(0, paceMinS) * 1000, hi = Math.max(paceMinS, paceMaxS) * 1000;
      return lo + Math.random() * Math.max(0, hi - lo);
    };
    // Serialises through a promise chain; each action waits until >= pace since the last one.
    function pacedFetch(url, opts) {
      const job = _paceChain.then(async () => {
        const wait = Math.max(0, _lastActionAt + paceGapMs() - Date.now());
        if (wait) await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, wait));
        _lastActionAt = Date.now();
        return fetch(url, opts);
      });
      _paceChain = job.then(() => {}, () => {});
      return job;
    }
    const KILL_CMD_KEY = 'ae::scoutkiller::killcmd::' + SERVER;
    const KILL_DONE_KEY = 'ae::scoutkiller::killdone::' + SERVER;
    const ATK_QUEUE_KEY = 'ae::scoutkiller::atkq::' + SERVER;
    const DWELL_S = 90;

    // Optimistic kill-fleet speed for the FEASIBILITY estimate only (assumes scout-class
    // base 12; the actual send re-gates on the game's own computed duration). ly/hour.
    function killSpeedLyH() { return killFleetSpeed() || 12 * (1 + 0.05 * wMaxV()) * jgBoost(); }   // measured/override, else optimistic warp x friendly-JG boost

    // Can the selected fleet land at the predicted system >=5s before the EARLIEST
    // arrival? Uses etaEarly (safe bound) and the target astro (all astros in a system
    // are ~0.1-0.2 ly apart, so inter-system distance dominates the estimate).
    function killFeasibility(stop) {
      const kf = killFleet();
      if (!kf) return { ok: false, reason: 'select a kill fleet' };
      if (!kf.loc) return { ok: false, reason: 'fleet location unknown' };
      if (kf.moving) return { ok: false, reason: 'kill fleet is in transit' };
      if (!stop) return { ok: false, reason: 'no predicted stop' };
      const d = AEMapIntel.distance(kf.loc, stop.coord);
      if (!d) return { ok: false, reason: 'distance unresolved' };
      const travelSec = d.ly / killSpeedLyH() * 3600;
      const slackSec = (stop.etaEarly - Date.now()) / 1000 - travelSec - 5;
      return { ok: slackSec >= 0, reason: slackSec >= 0 ? ('lands ~' + Math.round(slackSec) + 's early (est.)') : 'too far / too late', travelSec, slackSec, ly: d.ly };
    }

    const FLEETS_KEY = 'ae::scoutkiller::fleets::' + SERVER;
    const FLEETS_TTL = 30 * 60 * 1000;   // fleet list changes rarely (build/disband/arrival)
    function readFleetCache() {
      try { const c = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(FLEETS_KEY)); if (c && Array.isArray(c.fleets)) return c; } catch (e) {}
      return null;
    }
    // Only hits the network when the cache is missing/stale or force===true. The ↻ button
    // and the pre-kill refresh force it; ordinary page loads reuse the cache.
    async function loadEmpireFleets(force) {
      const c = readFleetCache();
      if (!force && c && (Date.now() - c.t) < FLEETS_TTL) { empireFleets = c.fleets; return empireFleets; }
      try {
        const res = await pacedFetch(location.origin + '/fleet.aspx', { credentials: 'same-origin' });
        if (res.ok) {
          empireFleets = AEMapIntel.parseEmpireFleets(new DOMParser().parseFromString(await res.text(), 'text/html'));
          try { localStorage.setItem(FLEETS_KEY, JSON.stringify({ t: Date.now(), fleets: empireFleets })); } catch (e) {}
        } else if (c) { empireFleets = c.fleets; }
      } catch (e) { if (c) empireFleets = c.fleets; }
      return empireFleets;
    }

    // Phase A — send: hand off to the move page (verified pattern) and open it.
    const isMovePage = () => !!document.getElementById('move_fleet_form');
    const STALE_CMD_MS = 20 * 60 * 1000;   // a command older than this is abandoned
    function readPendingCmd() {
      let cmd = null;
      try { cmd = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(KILL_CMD_KEY)); } catch (e) { return null; }
      if (!cmd) return null;
      const abandoned = (Date.now() - (cmd.id || 0)) > STALE_CMD_MS;
      const windowPassed = cmd.etaLate != null && Date.now() > (cmd.etaLate + DWELL_S * 1000 + 120000);
      if (abandoned || windowPassed) { try { localStorage.removeItem(KILL_CMD_KEY); } catch (e) {} return null; }
      return cmd;
    }
    const hasPendingCmd = () => !!readPendingCmd();
    const enemyInQueue = (sg) => {
      try { const q = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(ATK_QUEUE_KEY)) || []; return q.some(r => r.enemyPlayerId && r.enemyPlayerId === sg.playerId); } catch (e) { return false; }
    };
    // A target may be armed only when nothing else is in flight — one move tab at a time,
    // and never re-arm an enemy already being handled.
    // Active teams = queued kills (all pre-confirm; a confirmed kill is dropped from the
    // queue and flies home uncounted) + any command currently being armed.
    const activeTeamCount = () => {
      let q = []; try { q = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(ATK_QUEUE_KEY)) || []; } catch (e) {}
      return q.length + (hasPendingCmd() ? 1 : 0);
    };
    const atCapacity = () => activeTeamCount() >= maxTeams;
    const canArm = (sg) => !isMovePage() && !hasPendingCmd() && !enemyInQueue(sg) && !atCapacity();

    async function refreshOriginJg(originCoord) {
      try {
        const rk = (originCoord || '').split(':').slice(0, 2).join(':');
        const entry = AEMapIntel.getJgRegistry()[rk];
        if (!entry || !entry.coord) return;
        const mir = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('ae::bases::mirror::' + SERVER)) || {};
        const baseId = mir[entry.coord] && mir[entry.coord].baseId;
        if (!baseId) return;   // can't re-fetch without the base id; the lazy base-view path keeps it fresh
        const res = await pacedFetch(location.origin + '/base.aspx?base=' + baseId, { credentials: 'same-origin' });
        if (!res.ok) return;
        const st = AEMapIntel.parseStructures(new DOMParser().parseFromString(await res.text(), 'text/html'));
        const lvl = st && st.structures && st.structures['Jump Gate'];
        if (lvl != null && lvl !== entry.level) {
          AEMapIntel.upsertBase({ coord: entry.coord, structures: st.structures, guild: entry.guild });   // feeds recordJumpGate
          setStatus('JG at ' + entry.coord + ' changed L' + entry.level + '\u2192L' + lvl + ' \u2014 updated Base Intel');
        }
      } catch (e) {}
    }
    async function startKill(sg, stop) {
      if (!killFleetId) { setStatus('select a kill fleet first'); return; }
      if (hasPendingCmd()) { setStatus('a kill is already armed \u2014 finish or cancel it first'); return; }
      if (enemyInQueue(sg)) { setStatus('already killing ' + sg.player); return; }
      if (atCapacity()) { setStatus('at capacity (' + maxTeams + ' kill teams active)'); return; }
      await loadEmpireFleets(true);   // refresh so origin (return target) + fleet state are current
      const kf = killFleet();
      if (!kf) { setStatus('kill fleet no longer in the empire list'); return; }
      if (kf.moving) { setStatus('kill fleet is already in transit'); return; }
      try {
        localStorage.setItem(KILL_CMD_KEY, JSON.stringify({
          id: Date.now(), fleetId: kf.fleetId, target: stop.coord, origin: kf.loc,
          enemyPlayerId: sg.playerId, enemyPlayer: sg.player, priority: isPriority(sg), scouts: killScouts,
          etaEarly: stop.etaEarly, etaLate: stop.etaLate, testMode
        }));
      } catch (e) {}
      refreshOriginJg(kf.loc);   // re-parse the friendly JG level + feed any change back to Base Intel
      window.open(location.origin + '/fleet.aspx?fleet=' + kf.fleetId + '&view=move', '_blank');
      setStatus('kill armed \u2192 ' + stop.coord + (testMode ? ' (TEST)' : '') + ' \u2014 move tab opened');
    }

    // Phase B — attack: pure background fetch, runs from any page for each queued kill.
    // Polls the fleet's attack view (paced 5-15s) until the autoscout appears as a
    // target, POSTs the attack, parses the pre-battle page, then confirms (live) or
    // beeps for manual confirm (test).
    const enqueueAttack = (rec) => { try { const q = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(ATK_QUEUE_KEY)) || []; if (!q.some(x => x.fleetId === rec.fleetId)) { q.push(rec); localStorage.setItem(ATK_QUEUE_KEY, JSON.stringify(q)); } } catch (e) {} };
    // The trio is a SUBSET move from the source fleet, so it arrives as its own fleet at
    // the destination — resolve that fleet id (once) rather than trusting the source id.
    // Doubles as an "has the trio landed here yet?" gate.
    async function resolveKillFleetId(rec) {
      if (rec.killFleetId) return rec.killFleetId;
      await loadEmpireFleets(true);
      const here = empireFleets.filter(f => f.loc === rec.atAstro && !f.moving);
      if (!here.length) return null;
      const want = (rec.scouts || 3) * 40;   // scout value ~40 each
      here.sort((a, b) => Math.abs((a.size || 0) - want) - Math.abs((b.size || 0) - want));
      rec.killFleetId = here[0].fleetId;
      return rec.killFleetId;
    }
    async function pollOnce(rec) {
      // give up shortly after the latest possible arrival + one dwell
      if (Date.now() > rec.etaLate + DWELL_S * 1000 + 60000) { setStatus('kill window passed for ' + rec.target); return false; }
      if (Date.now() < (rec.hopUntil || 0)) return true;    // mid-hop, wait for arrival

      // 1) locate the scout via the SYSTEM view (handles the randomized landing astro)
      const system = rec.target.split(':').slice(0, 3).join(':');
      let sysDoc;
      try {
        const r = await pacedFetch(location.origin + '/map.aspx?loc=' + system, { credentials: 'same-origin' });
        if (!r.ok) return;
        sysDoc = new DOMParser().parseFromString(await r.text(), 'text/html');
      } catch (e) { return true; }
      const astros = AEMapIntel.parseSystemScoutAstros(sysDoc);
      const landed = astros.find(a => a.present > 0);       // a fleet is sitting on an astro
      const inbound = astros.find(a => a.incoming > 0);     // one is arriving
      const kfid = await resolveKillFleetId(rec);
      if (!kfid) return true;   // our trio has not landed at our astro yet — wait

      // 2) position: if the scout landed on a DIFFERENT astro, hop there; if it is only
      //    inbound to a different astro and we are idle, pre-hop to meet it
      const wantAstro = (landed && landed.coord) || (inbound && inbound.coord) || null;
      if (wantAstro && wantAstro !== rec.atAstro) {
        setStatus('scout at ' + wantAstro + ' \u2014 hopping');
        const ok = await moveFleetBg(kfid, wantAstro);
        if (ok) { rec.hopUntil = Date.now() + hopSecs(rec.atAstro, wantAstro) * 1000 + 2000; rec.atAstro = wantAstro; }
        return true;   // resume after the hop lands
      }
      if (!landed) return true;   // not landed yet — keep polling

      // 3) attack from our fleet's current astro
      let atkDoc;
      try {
        const res = await pacedFetch(location.origin + '/fleet.aspx?fleet=' + kfid + '&view=attack', { credentials: 'same-origin' });
        if (!res.ok) return;
        atkDoc = new DOMParser().parseFromString(await res.text(), 'text/html');
      } catch (e) { return true; }
      const targets = AEMapIntel.parseAttackTargets(atkDoc);
      const tgt = targets.find(t => t.isAutoScout && rec.enemyPlayerId && t.playerId === rec.enemyPlayerId)
               || targets.find(t => t.isAutoScout);
      if (!tgt) return true;   // present in-system but not attackable from here yet — keep polling
      try {
        const pre = await pacedFetch(location.origin + '/' + tgt.formAction, { method: 'POST', credentials: 'same-origin', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/x-www-form-urlencoded' }, body: 'form=true' });
        const cf = AEMapIntel.parsePreBattleConfirm(new DOMParser().parseFromString(await pre.text(), 'text/html'));
        if (!cf) { setStatus('pre-battle not confirmable for ' + (tgt.name || tgt.enemyFleetId)); return true; }
        if (rec.testMode) {
          try { new (window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext)(); } catch (e) {}
          setStatus('TEST: target ' + (tgt.name || tgt.enemyFleetId) + ' ready \u2014 confirm manually');
          return false;   // done: stop before the committing confirm
        }
        const body = 'confirm=true&confirm_code=' + encodeURIComponent(cf.confirmCode) + '&form=true';
        const done = await pacedFetch(location.origin + '/' + cf.action, { method: 'POST', credentials: 'same-origin', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/x-www-form-urlencoded' }, body });
        const result = AEMapIntel.parseBattleResult(new DOMParser().parseFromString(await done.text(), 'text/html'));
        if (result.ok && result.defenderLost > 0) {
          setStatus('KILLED ' + (tgt.name || tgt.enemyFleetId) + ' (\u2212' + result.defenderLost + 'c)');
          // 4) send the scouts back home
          if (rec.origin) { await moveFleetBg(kfid, rec.origin); setStatus('KILLED ' + (tgt.name || tgt.enemyFleetId) + ' \u00b7 returning to ' + rec.origin); }
        } else {
          setStatus('attack finished, unclear result on ' + tgt.enemyFleetId);
        }
        return false;   // done
      } catch (e) { setStatus('attack error on ' + (tgt ? tgt.enemyFleetId : rec.fleetId)); return true; }
    }
    // Background fleet move (used for the intra-system HOP and the RETURN home). The move
    // form is a plain POST (destination + one field per unit + the 'units' csv, no token),
    // so this needs no page navigation. NOTE: field-verified against the move DOM but not
    // yet live-verified like the tab-based outbound send — flagged for Aran.
    async function moveFleetBg(fleetId, dest) {
      try {
        const res = await pacedFetch(location.origin + '/fleet.aspx?fleet=' + fleetId + '&view=move', { credentials: 'same-origin' });
        if (!res.ok) return false;
        const mv = AEMapIntel.parseMovePage(new DOMParser().parseFromString(await res.text(), 'text/html'));
        if (!mv.formAction) return false;
        const body = new URLSearchParams();
        body.set('destination', dest);
        for (const [u, q] of Object.entries(mv.avail || {})) if (q > 0) body.set(u, String(q));
        if (mv.unitsCsv) body.set('units', mv.unitsCsv);
        await pacedFetch(location.origin + '/' + mv.formAction, { method: 'POST', credentials: 'same-origin', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/x-www-form-urlencoded' }, body: body.toString() });
        return true;
      } catch (e) { return false; }
    }
    // intra-system hop time estimate (astro-ring distance / kill speed), for the wait gate
    function hopSecs(fromAstro, toAstro) {
      const d = AEMapIntel.distance(fromAstro, toAstro);
      return d ? Math.max(3, d.ly / killSpeedLyH() * 3600) : 5;
    }
    // Auto-launch runs on a SHORT cadence (not the 60s render): fires a feasible target the
    // moment its conservative launch time arrives. One at a time — startKill sets a pending
    // command that blocks the rest until it is consumed.
    const runExpired = () => { const dl = runDeadline(); return dl != null && Date.now() >= dl; };
    function startKillRun() { setRunDeadline(runMinutes > 0 ? Date.now() + runMinutes * 60000 : null); }
    // Stops EVERYTHING in flight, not just new arming: empties the attack queue, drops any
    // pending move command + candidates, and disengages auto-kill. In-flight fleets already
    // sent are abandoned at the target (no further hop/attack/return) — an intentional hard
    // stop per Aran's spec.
    function stopKillSession(reason, soft) {
      autoKill = false;
      try { if (autoCb) autoCb.checked = false; } catch (e) {}
      saveCfg({ autoKill: false });
      try { localStorage.removeItem(KILL_CMD_KEY); } catch (e) {}   // drop the un-launched command either way
      akCandidates = []; akLaunchAt = null;
      setRunDeadline(null);
      let q = []; try { q = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(ATK_QUEUE_KEY)) || []; } catch (e) {}
      if (soft && q.length) {
        // SOFT: stop new launches, let the in-flight kills finish their attack + return
        winddown = true;
        setStatus('run ended \u2014 ' + (reason || 'stopped') + ' \u00b7 finishing ' + q.length + ' in-flight kill' + (q.length === 1 ? '' : 's'));
      } else {
        // HARD: abandon everything in flight too
        try { localStorage.removeItem(ATK_QUEUE_KEY); } catch (e) {}
        winddown = false;
        setStatus('run ended \u2014 ' + (reason || 'stopped') + ' \u00b7 all kills cleared');
      }
      updateAkBanner();
      renderTracked();
    }
    function maybeAutoLaunch() {
      if (!autoKill || !killFleetId || runExpired()) return;
      if (AEShared.isMasterHidden && AEShared.isMasterHidden()) return;
      if (hasPendingCmd() || atCapacity()) return;
      const now = Date.now();
      const due = akCandidates.filter(c => now >= c.launchAt && canArm(c.sg))
        .sort((a, b) => (isPriority(b.sg) - isPriority(a.sg)) || (a.launchAt - b.launchAt));
      if (due.length) startKill(due[0].sg, due[0].stop);
    }
    let atkTimer = null;
    function scheduleAttackPoll() {
      if (atkTimer) return;
      const tick = async () => {
        if (!(AEShared.isMasterHidden && AEShared.isMasterHidden()) && !runExpired()) {
          let q = [];
          try { q = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(ATK_QUEUE_KEY)) || []; } catch (e) {}
          q.sort((a, b) => ((b.prio ? 1 : 0) - (a.prio ? 1 : 0)) || (a.etaLate - b.etaLate));
          const keep = [];
          for (const rec of q) { const alive = await pollOnce(rec); if (alive !== false) keep.push(rec); }
          try { localStorage.setItem(ATK_QUEUE_KEY, JSON.stringify(keep)); } catch (e) {}   // persist hop state + removals
        }
        atkTimer = setTimeout(tick, paceGapMs());   // inter-pass gap uses the shared pace
      };
      atkTimer = setTimeout(tick, 3000);
    }

    // Move-page handler: fill destination, max all units, read the game's duration, gate,
    // then submit (live) or show an arm bar (always, so the user sees the gate). On send,
    // enqueue the attack phase.
    function tryKillMovePage() {
      const form = document.getElementById('move_fleet_form'); if (!form) return;
      let cmd = null; try { cmd = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(KILL_CMD_KEY)); } catch (e) {}
      if (!cmd) return;
      const fm = (form.getAttribute('action') || '').match(/fleet=(\d+)/);
      if (!fm || fm[1] !== cmd.fleetId) return;
      if (localStorage.getItem(KILL_DONE_KEY) === String(cmd.id)) return;
      const dest = document.getElementById('destination');
      if (dest) { dest.value = cmd.target; dest.dispatchEvent(new Event('change', { bubbles: true })); }
      if (typeof window.fastloc === 'function') { try { window.fastloc(cmd.target); } catch (e) {} }
      if (typeof window.calc_distance === 'function') { try { window.calc_distance(); } catch (e) {} }
      // send ONLY a trio of Scout Ships (enough to kill an autoscout) — never the whole
      // fleet. Zero every other unit; set the Scout Ship field to min(killScouts, avail).
      const mv = AEMapIntel.parseMovePage(document);
      const availScouts = (mv.avail && mv.avail['Scout Ship']) || 0;
      const toSend = Math.min(cmd.scouts || 3, availScouts);
      for (const unit of Object.keys(mv.avail || {})) {
        const q = document.getElementById('quant' + unit);
        if (!q) continue;
        q.value = unit === 'Scout Ship' ? String(toSend) : '';
        try { if (typeof window.update === 'function') window.update(unit); } catch (e) {}
      }
      if (toSend < 1) {
        const warn = document.createElement('div');
        warn.setAttribute('data-ae-script', 'scoutkiller-killbar');
        warn.style.cssText = 'position:fixed;top:8px;left:50%;transform:translateX(-50%);z-index:2147483341;background:' + (AEShared.token('--ae-bg') || '#0c1018') + ';color:' + (AEShared.token('--ae-danger') || '#e05555') + ';border:1px solid ' + (AEShared.token('--ae-danger') || '#e05555') + ';border-radius:6px;padding:8px 12px;font:11px/1.6 monospace;';
        warn.textContent = 'No Scout Ships available in this fleet \u2014 pick a fleet that has scouts.';
        document.documentElement.appendChild(warn);
        try { localStorage.setItem(KILL_DONE_KEY, String(cmd.id)); localStorage.removeItem(KILL_CMD_KEY); } catch (e) {}
        return;
      }
      const consume = () => { try { localStorage.setItem(KILL_DONE_KEY, String(cmd.id)); localStorage.removeItem(KILL_CMD_KEY); } catch (e) {} };
      const bar = document.createElement('div');
      bar.setAttribute('data-ae-script', 'scoutkiller-killbar');
      bar.style.cssText = 'position:fixed;top:8px;left:50%;transform:translateX(-50%);z-index:2147483341;background:' + (AEShared.token('--ae-bg') || '#0c1018') + ';color:' + (AEShared.token('--ae-text') || '#d6deea') + ';border:1px solid ' + (AEShared.token('--ae-primary') || '#3fb6c4') + ';border-radius:6px;padding:8px 12px;font:11px/1.6 monospace;box-shadow:0 4px 18px rgba(0,0,0,.5);';
      const line = document.createElement('div');
      bar.appendChild(line);
      const send = document.createElement('button'); send.textContent = 'Send + arm attack'; send.style.cssText = 'cursor:pointer;padding:3px 10px;margin-right:6px;';
      const cancel = document.createElement('button'); cancel.textContent = 'Cancel'; cancel.style.cssText = 'cursor:pointer;padding:3px 10px;';
      bar.appendChild(send); bar.appendChild(cancel);
      const fireSend = () => {
        enqueueAttack({ fleetId: cmd.fleetId, target: cmd.target, atAstro: cmd.target, origin: cmd.origin, enemyPlayerId: cmd.enemyPlayerId, prio: !!cmd.priority, etaEarly: cmd.etaEarly, etaLate: cmd.etaLate, testMode: cmd.testMode, hopUntil: 0 });
        consume();
        const btn = form.querySelector('input[type="submit"]');
        if (btn) btn.click(); else form.submit();
      };
      send.addEventListener('click', fireSend);
      cancel.addEventListener('click', () => { consume(); bar.remove(); });
      document.documentElement.appendChild(bar);

      // calc_distance() populates #duration ASYNCHRONOUSLY, so poll for it (up to ~3s)
      // before deciding the gate. If it never appears, fall back to our OWN travel
      // estimate so live mode still auto-sends rather than silently stalling (the
      // "game ETA unknown" stall). estimated flag is shown in the bar.
      let armed = false;
      function finalize(durSec, estimated) {
        if (armed) return;
        const landMs = durSec != null ? Date.now() + durSec * 1000 : null;
        const gateOk = landMs == null ? null : (landMs <= cmd.etaEarly - 5000);
        line.textContent = 'SCOUT KILL \u2192 ' + cmd.target + ' \u00b7 ETA '
          + (durSec != null ? Math.round(durSec) + 's' + (estimated ? ' (est.)' : '') : 'unknown')
          + (gateOk === null ? '' : gateOk ? ' \u00b7 lands before scout \u2713' : ' \u00b7 WOULD LAND LATE \u26a0');
        line.style.color = gateOk === false ? (AEShared.token('--ae-danger') || '#e05555') : '';
        if (!estimated && durSec > 0) { const dd = AEMapIntel.parseMovePage(document).distance; if (dd > 0) cacheFleetSpeed(cmd.fleetId, dd / (durSec / 3600)); }
        if (!cmd.testMode && gateOk === true) { armed = true; fireSend(); }
      }
      let tries = 0;
      (function pollDuration() {
        const d = AEMapIntel.parseMovePage(document).durationSec;
        if (d != null) { finalize(d, false); return; }
        if (tries++ >= 10) {
          // fallback: our own estimate (slowest sent unit = Scout Ship base 12 x warp)
          const start = AEMapIntel.parseMovePage(document).start;
          const dist = start ? AEMapIntel.distance(start, cmd.target) : null;
          const est = dist ? dist.ly / (12 * (1 + 0.05 * wMaxV())) * 3600 : null;
          finalize(est, true);
          return;
        }
        line.textContent = 'SCOUT KILL \u2192 ' + cmd.target + ' \u00b7 computing ETA\u2026';
        setTimeout(pollDuration, 300);
      })();
    }

    async function renderTracked() {
      while (trackBox.firstChild) trackBox.removeChild(trackBox.firstChild);
      liveRows = [];
      akLaunchAt = null;   // soonest upcoming auto-kill launch, recomputed each render
      akCandidates = [];
      let blockedByPending = false;
      const all = scoutSightings();
      if (!all.length) {
        trackBox.append(el('div', { class: 'ae-muted', style: 'font-size:10px;' }, 'no scout-sized movements logged yet'));
        return;
      }
      /* Enrich each scout with its current estimate up front so we can (a) PRUNE scouts
         whose prediction has run >5 regions past the last real sighting — stale data
         that would otherwise propagate as phantom rows — and (b) SORT by soonest
         arrival rather than recency. */
      // pending warp-increase confirmations (a >2-level jump could be a real tech gain or a
      // JG artefact) — the user accepts or keeps the known level.
      const wc = readWarpConfirms();
      for (const pid of Object.keys(wc)) {
        const c = wc[pid];
        const wrow = el('div', { class: 'sk-warpconf' });
        wrow.append(el('span', {}, '\u26a0 ' + (c.name || 'player') + ' warp W' + c.prev + '\u2192W' + c.next + '?'
          + (c.jgDropped ? '' : ' (no JG leg seen)')));
        const yes = el('button', { class: 'ae-btn', style: 'font-size:9px;padding:0 6px;', title: 'real tech increase \u2014 adopt W' + c.next }, 'Yes');
        const keep = el('button', { class: 'ae-btn', style: 'font-size:9px;padding:0 6px;', title: 'keep W' + c.prev + ' (treat as a JG artefact / noise)' }, 'Keep W' + c.prev);
        yes.addEventListener('click', () => { publishInferredWarp(pid, c.name, { warp: c.next, speed: c.speed, pairs: Math.max(2, c.pairs) }, true); removeWarpConfirm(pid); setStatus(c.name + ' warp updated to W' + c.next); renderTracked(); });
        keep.addEventListener('click', () => {
          // record the rejection so we don't re-prompt for this level, keep the lower warp
          const m = mirrorWarpFor(pid); if (m) publishInferredWarp(pid, c.name, { warp: m.level, speed: m.speed, pairs: m.pairs });
          try { const key = 'ae::techmirror::' + SERVER; const mir = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(key)) || { players: {} }; if (mir.players[pid] && mir.players[pid].warpInferred) { mir.players[pid].warpInferred.rejectedNext = c.next; localStorage.setItem(key, JSON.stringify(mir)); } } catch (e) {}
          removeWarpConfirm(pid); setStatus('kept W' + c.prev + ' for ' + c.name); renderTracked();
        });
        wrow.append(yes, keep);
        trackBox.append(wrow);
      }
      let purged = false;
      const enriched = [];
      for (const sg of all) {
        const cached = cachedAstros(sg.galaxy);
        let est = null;
        if (cached) {
          const path = AEMapIntel.buildScoutPath(cached.rows);
          est = AEMapIntel.currentScoutEstimate(path, sg.coord, sg.t, wMinV(), wMaxV(), Date.now(), {});
          if (est && est.hopsPassed > 5) { purgeScout(sg.playerId, sg.coord); purged = true; continue; }
        }
        enriched.push({ sg, est, hasCache: !!cached });
      }
      // soonest earliest-arrival first; unknown/lost estimates sink to the bottom
      const etaKey = (r) => (r.est && r.est.stop) ? r.est.stop.etaEarly : Infinity;
      enriched.sort((a, b) => etaKey(a) - etaKey(b));
      const shown = enriched.slice(0, manualHidden ? 10 : 3);
      const list = el('div', { class: 'sk-obs-list' });
      trackBox.append(list);
      for (const rec of shown) {
        const sg = rec.sg;
        // One dense line per scout, matching the report-scanner observation style:
        // player (guild+name) | next system | +hops passed | earliest-latest | warp
        const row = el('div', { class: 'sk-obs' });
        if (isPriority(sg)) row.append(el('span', { class: 'sk-star', title: 'priority guild' }, '\u2605'));
        row.append(el('span', { class: 'p' }, sg.player));
        list.append(row);
        const cached = cachedAstros(sg.galaxy);
        if (!cached) {
          const b = el('button', { class: 'ae-btn', style: 'font-size:9px;padding:0 5px;' }, 'load ' + sg.galaxy);
          b.addEventListener('click', async () => {
            b.disabled = true;
            const rows = await runLoad(sg.galaxy);
            if (rows) renderTracked(); else b.disabled = false;
          });
          row.append(b);
          continue;
        }
        const path = AEMapIntel.buildScoutPath(cached.rows);
        // Warp evidence: fresh inference corroborated by the tech-harvester mirror.
        // >=2 agreeing pairs => confirmed (plain "W16"); otherwise an estimate ("W16~").
        let wLo = wMinV(), wHi = wMaxV(), warpTxt = null, warpTip = null;
        const inf = sg.history.length >= 2 ? AEMapIntel.inferScoutWarp(path, sg.history, { jgFilter: true }) : null;
        if (inf) maybeAdoptWarp(sg.playerId, sg.player, inf);      // JG-gated publish
        // PREDICT from the tech-harvester mirror (gated + aggregated across the owner's
        // scouts); fall back to this scout's own filtered inference only if none is stored.
        const mw = mirrorWarpFor(sg.playerId);
        const best = mw ? { warp: mw.level, pairs: mw.pairs } : (inf ? { warp: inf.warp, pairs: inf.pairs } : null);
        if (best) {
          wLo = Math.max(0, best.warp - 1); wHi = best.warp + 1;
          const confirmed = best.pairs >= 2;
          warpTxt = 'W' + best.warp + (confirmed ? '' : '~');
          const jgNote = inf && inf.jgDropped ? ' \u00b7 ' + inf.jgDropped + ' JG leg' + (inf.jgDropped === 1 ? '' : 's') + ' filtered' : '';
          warpTip = (confirmed ? 'Warp ' + best.warp + ' (' + best.pairs + ' pairs, from tech harvester)'
                               : 'estimated from ' + best.pairs + ' sighting pair' + (best.pairs === 1 ? '' : 's')) + jgNote;
        }
        const est = AEMapIntel.currentScoutEstimate(path, sg.coord, sg.t, wLo, wHi, Date.now(), {});
        if (!est) { row.append(el('span', { class: 'note' }, 'off-path')); continue; }
        if (est.status === 'lost') {
          row.append(el('span', { class: 'note' }, 'needs fresh sighting'));
        } else {
          const link = sysLink(est.stop.coord); link.className = 'l';
          row.append(link);
          // systems already passed since the sighting, in red
          if (est.hopsPassed > 0) {
            row.append(el('span', { class: 'x', title: est.hopsPassed + ' system(s) passed since the sighting' },
              '+' + est.hopsPassed));
          }
          row.append(el('span', { class: 't', title: 'earliest \u2013 latest arrival' },
            fmtT(est.stop.etaEarly) + '\u2013' + fmtT(est.stop.etaLate)));
          // live countdown to the EARLIEST arrival; turns green + pulses inside the window
          const cd = el('span', { class: 'cd', title: 'time until the earliest arrival' });
          row.append(cd);
          liveRows.push({ el: cd, etaEarly: est.stop.etaEarly, etaLate: est.stop.etaLate });
        }
        if (warpTxt) row.append(el('span', { class: 'w', title: warpTip }, warpTxt));
        // KILL affordance. Auto-kill on: fire feasible targets automatically. Off: a per-row
        // Kill button, greyed (with reason) when the selected fleet can't make it in time.
        if (est.status !== 'lost') {
          // where to intercept: nearest reachable stop within the look-ahead window
          const tgt = chooseInterceptStop(sg, path, est);
          const feas = killFeasibility(tgt);
          const aheadIdx = tgt && tgt.aheadIdx ? tgt.aheadIdx : 0;
          if (feas.ok && killFleetId && tgt && !enemyInQueue(sg)) {
            const laShared = launchAtFor(tgt);
            akCandidates.push({ sg, stop: tgt, launchAt: laShared });
            if (akLaunchAt == null || laShared < akLaunchAt) akLaunchAt = laShared;
          }
          if (autoKill && feas.ok && killFleetId && !canArm(sg) && hasPendingCmd()) blockedByPending = true;
          // show the intercept target when it is beyond the scout's next stop
          if (feas.ok && aheadIdx > 0) row.append(el('span', { class: 'note', title: 'intercept ' + (aheadIdx + 1) + ' stops ahead at ' + tgt.coord }, '\u21d2' + sysOf(tgt.coord) + ' (+' + aheadIdx + ')'));
          if (!autoKill) {
            const kb = el('button', { class: 'ae-btn sk-kill', style: 'font-size:9px;padding:0 6px;' }, 'Kill');
            kb.disabled = !feas.ok;
            kb.title = feas.ok ? ('send ' + (killFleet() ? killFleet().label : 'fleet') + ' \u2192 ' + (tgt ? tgt.coord : '?') + ' \u2014 ' + feas.reason)
                               : ('cannot kill: ' + feas.reason);
            if (feas.ok) kb.addEventListener('click', () => startKill(sg, tgt));
            row.append(kb);
          }
        }
        row.setAttribute('title', sg.player + ' \u00b7 last seen ' + sysOf(sg.coord) + ' at ' + fmtT(sg.t)
          + ' (' + fmtDur(Date.now() - sg.t) + ' ago)');
      }
      /* With the manual section hidden there is no other way to fetch a galaxy, so
         surface a loader for exactly the galaxies these observations still need. */
      const missing = [...new Set(shown.filter(r => !r.hasCache).map(r => r.sg.galaxy))];
      if (manualHidden && missing.length) {
        const sel = el('select', { class: 'ae-input sk-num', title: 'galaxies these observations need' });
        missing.forEach(g => { const o = el('option', {}, g); o.value = g; sel.appendChild(o); });
        const btn = el('button', { class: 'ae-btn', style: 'font-size:10px;padding:1px 7px;' }, 'Load astros');
        btn.addEventListener('click', async () => {
          btn.disabled = true;
          const rows = await runLoad(sel.value);
          if (rows) renderTracked(); else btn.disabled = false;
        });
        const exp = el('button', { class: 'ae-btn', style: 'font-size:10px;padding:1px 7px;', title: 'Export the scout path for the Discord bot' }, 'Export');
        exp.addEventListener('click', () => exportPath(sel.value));
        trackBox.append(el('div', { class: 'sk-row' }, sel, btn, exp));
      }
      if (autoKill && blockedByPending && !hasPendingCmd.__silent) {
        // a feasible target is waiting on an unfinished move tab — tell the user
        if (/idle|off|armed|not parsed|B\d/.test(lastStatus) || lastStatus === '') setStatus('auto-kill waiting \u2014 finish/close the open move tab');
      }
      tickCountdowns();
    }

    // ── manual prediction (collapsible via the header M toggle) ──
    const manualBox = el('div', {});
    body.append(manualBox);
    const bodyAppend = (n) => manualBox.append(n);
    bodyAppend(el('div', { class: 'ae-muted sk-sect' }, 'MANUAL'));
    const galIn = el('input', { class: 'ae-input sk-num', title: 'Galaxy' }); galIn.value = galaxyOfHere() || '';
    const loadBtn = el('button', { class: 'ae-btn' }, 'Load astros');
    const exportBtn = el('button', { class: 'ae-btn', title: 'Export the scout path for the Discord bot (AE-SCOUTPATH v1)' }, 'Export');
    exportBtn.addEventListener('click', () => { const g = galIn.value.toUpperCase().trim(); if (g) exportPath(g); else setStatus('enter a galaxy'); });
    galIn.addEventListener('input', updateCachedNote);
    bodyAppend(el('div', { class: 'sk-row' }, el('span', { class: 'ae-muted' }, 'galaxy'), galIn, loadBtn, exportBtn));
    const seenIn = el('input', { class: 'ae-input', style: 'flex:1;min-width:130px;', placeholder: 'B35:10:22:31' });
    const agoIn = el('input', { class: 'ae-input sk-num', type: 'number' }); agoIn.value = '0';
    bodyAppend(el('div', { class: 'sk-row' }, el('span', { class: 'ae-muted' }, 'seen at'), seenIn, agoIn, el('span', { class: 'ae-muted' }, 'min ago')));
    // Warp range defaults 12-14, persisted per server (survives page loads). Used for
    // scouts with no inferred warp; inferred ones override with their own narrow band.
    const wMin = el('input', { class: 'ae-input sk-num', type: 'number', title: 'minimum Warp Drive assumed' });
    wMin.value = String(cfg0.warpMin != null ? cfg0.warpMin : 12);
    const wMax = el('input', { class: 'ae-input sk-num', type: 'number', title: 'maximum Warp Drive assumed' });
    wMax.value = String(cfg0.warpMax != null ? cfg0.warpMax : 14);
    bodyAppend(el('div', { class: 'sk-row' }, el('span', { class: 'ae-muted' }, 'warp'), wMin, el('span', { class: 'ae-muted' }, '\u2013'), wMax));
    // typed warp range live-updates the tracked windows (scouts WITHOUT an inferred
    // warp use this range; inferred/confirmed scouts keep their narrowed band)
    const wMinV = () => { const v = parseInt(wMin.value, 10); return Number.isFinite(v) && v >= 0 ? v : 12; };
    const wMaxV = () => { const v = parseInt(wMax.value, 10); return Number.isFinite(v) && v >= 0 ? v : 14; };
    // typed range live-updates the tracked windows and persists
    const onWarpEdit = () => { saveCfg({ warpMin: wMinV(), warpMax: wMaxV() }); renderTracked(); };
    wMin.addEventListener('input', onWarpEdit);
    wMax.addEventListener('input', onWarpEdit);
    const goBtn = el('button', { class: 'ae-btn ae-btn--primary', style: 'margin-top:5px;' }, 'Predict');
    bodyAppend(goBtn);
    const outBox = el('div', { style: 'font-size:10px;line-height:1.6;margin-top:4px;display:none;font-variant-numeric:tabular-nums;' });
    bodyAppend(outBox);

    // ── settings: crawl pace + jitter for the astro parse ──
    body.append(el('div', { class: 'ae-muted sk-sect' }, 'SETTINGS'));
    const paceIn = el('input', { class: 'ae-input sk-num', type: 'number', step: '0.1', min: '0.6', title: 'seconds between report requests (min 0.6)' });
    paceIn.value = String(cfg0.paceSec != null ? cfg0.paceSec : 1.1);
    const jitIn = el('input', { class: 'ae-input sk-num', type: 'number', step: '5', min: '0', max: '60', title: 'random \u00b1% around the pace (0\u201360)' });
    jitIn.value = String(cfg0.jitterPct != null ? cfg0.jitterPct : 30);
    paceIn.addEventListener('change', () => saveCfg({ paceSec: Math.max(0.6, parseFloat(paceIn.value) || 1.1) }));
    jitIn.addEventListener('change', () => saveCfg({ jitterPct: Math.max(0, Math.min(60, parseFloat(jitIn.value) || 30)) }));
    body.append(el('div', { class: 'sk-row' },
      el('span', { class: 'ae-muted' }, 'pace'), paceIn, el('span', { class: 'ae-muted' }, 's \u00b7 jitter \u00b1'), jitIn, el('span', { class: 'ae-muted' }, '%')));

    loadBtn.addEventListener('click', async () => {
      const gal = galIn.value.toUpperCase().trim();
      if (!gal) { setStatus('enter a galaxy'); return; }
      loadBtn.disabled = true;
      const rows = await runLoad(gal);
      if (rows) renderTracked();
      loadBtn.disabled = false;
    });
    goBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
      const gal = galIn.value.toUpperCase().trim();
      const cached = cachedAstros(gal);
      if (!cached) { setStatus('load ' + (gal || 'galaxy') + ' astros first'); return; }
      const seen = seenIn.value.trim().toUpperCase();
      if (!AEMapIntel.isCoord(seen)) { setStatus('sighting coordinate invalid'); return; }
      const tSeen = Date.now() - (parseFloat(agoIn.value) || 0) * 60000;
      const path = AEMapIntel.buildScoutPath(cached.rows);
      const res = AEMapIntel.predictScoutStops(path, seen, tSeen, wMinV(), wMaxV(), { count: 8 });
      while (outBox.firstChild) outBox.removeChild(outBox.firstChild);
      if (!res) { setStatus('sighting region not on the path \u2014 wrong galaxy?'); return; }
      setStatus(path.length + ' region stops');
      const seenSys = new Set();
      res.stops.forEach((st) => {
        const sys = sysOf(st.coord);
        if (seenSys.has(sys)) return; seenSys.add(sys);
        const now = Date.now();
        const line = el('div', { style: 'display:flex;gap:6px;align-items:baseline;' });
        line.append(sysLink(st.coord),
          el('span', { class: 'ae-muted' }, Math.round(st.distLy) + ' ly'),
          el('span', {}, st.etaEarly > now
            ? 'in ' + fmtDur(st.etaEarly - now) + '\u2013' + fmtDur(st.etaLate - now)
            : fmtT(st.etaEarly) + '\u2013' + fmtT(st.etaLate)));
        outBox.append(line);
      });
      if (res.wrapped) outBox.append(el('div', { class: 'ae-muted' }, 'wrapped past the last region'));
      outBox.style.display = 'block';
    });

    // manual visibility: persisted; hiding it widens the watchlist to 10 rows
    const applyManual = () => {
      manualBox.classList.toggle('sk-hidden', manualHidden);
      manualBtn.style.opacity = manualHidden ? '.45' : '';
      manualBtn.setAttribute('aria-pressed', String(!manualHidden));
    };
    manualBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
      manualHidden = !manualHidden;
      saveCfg({ manualHidden });
      applyManual();
      renderTracked();          // row budget changed (3 <-> 10)
    });
    applyManual();

    // master hide: abort any running load; periodic work no-ops while hidden
    window.addEventListener('ae-master-hidden', (e) => { if (e.detail && e.detail.hidden) loadCtl.stopped = true; updateAkBanner(); });
    const guarded = (fn) => () => { if (AEShared.isMasterHidden && AEShared.isMasterHidden()) return; fn(); };
    renderTracked();
    updateCachedNote();
    readPendingCmd();   // drop any abandoned command from a prior session (unblocks auto-kill)
    mountAkBanner();
    if (autoKill) {
      const dl = runDeadline();
      if (dl && Date.now() >= dl) { stopKillSession('time up', softStop); }   // run already elapsed
      else { if (!dl) startKillRun(); try { window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('ae-start-report-scan')); } catch (e) {} }
    }
    tryKillMovePage();          // if this IS a move page with a pending kill, take over
    scheduleAttackPoll();       // background attack loop for any queued kills
    setInterval(maybeAutoLaunch, 2000);   // fire auto-kills promptly at their launch moment
    // Seed the dropdown from cache immediately (no network). Only fetch on boot if the
    // cache is missing/stale — not on every page load.
    { const c = readFleetCache(); if (c) { empireFleets = c.fleets; paintFleetOptions(); } }
    loadEmpireFleets(false).then(() => { paintFleetOptions(); renderTracked(); });
    setInterval(guarded(renderTracked), 60000);   // re-predict (rolling estimate advances)
    setInterval(guarded(tickCountdowns), 1000);   // cheap: text + class only
    document.documentElement.appendChild(host);
    AEShared.placeHost(host, 'scout-killer', root);   // fixed-region tiling (respects overrides)
    AEShared.startLiveUpdates();                       // Overseer edits re-apply WITHOUT reload
  }

  if (document.readyState === 'loading') document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', build);
  else build();
})();

} });


/* ============ MODULE: player-dashboard ============ */
AESuite.register({ id: "player-dashboard", title: "Player Dashboard", pages: null, init: function () {
/* AE Player Dashboard — full-page joined view of everything the suite knows about one
   player, served by the AEHub virtual-centralization layer. Opens in its own AE tab via
   the #ae-player-dashboard[=pid] hash (same takeover pattern as the tech browser — the
   about:blank route inherits CSP, a real AE page does not). Panel-less: no REGIONS slot.
   Visuals are hand-drawn SVG (no libraries): fleet-spec pie by credit share, 24-bin
   activity histogram in server time. */
(function () {
  'use strict';
  if (typeof AEShared === 'undefined' || typeof AEHub === 'undefined') return;
  const HASH_RE = /(?:^|#|&)ae-player-dashboard(?:=(\d+))?/;
  const m = HASH_RE.exec(location.hash);
  if (!m) {
    // not in dashboard mode: just expose the opener for other modules
    window.AE_OPEN_DASHBOARD = (pid) => window.open(location.href.split('#')[0] + '#ae-player-dashboard' + (pid ? '=' + pid : ''), '_blank');
    return;
  }

  function el(tag, attrs, ...kids) {
    const ns = (tag === 'svg' || tag === 'path' || tag === 'rect' || tag === 'text' || tag === 'circle' || tag === 'line' || tag === 'g');
    const e = ns ? document.createElementNS('http://www.w3.org/2000/svg', tag) : document.createElement(tag);
    if (attrs) for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(attrs)) { if (k === 'class') e.setAttribute('class', v); else if (k === 'style' && !ns) e.style.cssText = v; else e.setAttribute(k, v); }
    for (const k of kids) e.append(typeof k === 'string' ? document.createTextNode(k) : k);
    return e;
  }
  const fmt = (n) => n == null ? '\u2014' : Number(n).toLocaleString('en-US');
  const ago = (ms) => { if (!ms) return '\u2014'; const h = Math.round((Date.now() - ms) / 3600000); return h < 1 ? '<1h' : h < 48 ? h + 'h' : Math.round(h / 24) + 'd'; };

  const PIE_COLORS = ['var(--ae-primary)', 'var(--ae-secondary)', 'var(--ae-success)', 'var(--ae-danger)', 'var(--ae-warn)', 'var(--ae-text-dim)'];

  function pieSvg(pie) {
    const svg = el('svg', { viewBox: '0 0 240 130', width: '240', height: '130' });
    const entries = Object.entries(pie.shares).sort((a, b) => b[1] - a[1]);
    const cx = 60, cy = 62, r = 52;
    let a0 = -Math.PI / 2, i = 0;
    for (const [ab, share] of entries) {
      const a1 = a0 + share * 2 * Math.PI;
      const large = (a1 - a0) > Math.PI ? 1 : 0;
      const x0 = cx + r * Math.cos(a0), y0 = cy + r * Math.sin(a0);
      const x1 = cx + r * Math.cos(a1), y1 = cy + r * Math.sin(a1);
      const d = share >= 0.999
        ? `M ${cx} ${cy - r} A ${r} ${r} 0 1 1 ${cx - 0.01} ${cy - r} Z`
        : `M ${cx} ${cy} L ${x0} ${y0} A ${r} ${r} 0 ${large} 1 ${x1} ${y1} Z`;
      const path = el('path', { d, fill: PIE_COLORS[i % PIE_COLORS.length], stroke: 'var(--ae-bg)', 'stroke-width': '1' });
      path.append(el('title', {}, ab + ' ' + Math.round(share * 100) + '%'));
      svg.append(path);
      const ly = 14 + i * 15;
      if (i < 7) {
        svg.append(el('rect', { x: 128, y: ly - 8, width: 9, height: 9, fill: PIE_COLORS[i % PIE_COLORS.length] }));
        svg.append(el('text', { x: 142, y: ly, fill: 'var(--ae-text)', 'font-size': '10', 'font-family': 'monospace' },
          ab + ' ' + Math.round(share * 100) + '%'));
      }
      a0 = a1; i++;
    }
    return svg;
  }

  function histogramSvg(act) {
    const W = 340, H = 110, pad = 16, bw = (W - pad * 2) / 24;
    const svg = el('svg', { viewBox: `0 0 ${W} ${H}`, width: String(W), height: String(H) });
    const max = Math.max(1, ...act.bins);
    act.bins.forEach((v, h) => {
      const bh = v / max * (H - 36);
      const x = pad + h * bw;
      const rect = el('rect', { x: String(x + 1), y: String(H - 22 - bh), width: String(bw - 2), height: String(Math.max(bh, v ? 2 : 0)),
        fill: v === max && v > 0 ? 'var(--ae-secondary)' : 'var(--ae-primary)', opacity: v ? '0.9' : '0' });
      rect.append(el('title', {}, String(h).padStart(2, '0') + ':00 \u2014 ' + v + ' event(s)'));
      svg.append(rect);
      if (h % 4 === 0) svg.append(el('text', { x: String(x + bw / 2), y: String(H - 8), fill: 'var(--ae-text-dim)', 'font-size': '8', 'text-anchor': 'middle', 'font-family': 'monospace' }, String(h).padStart(2, '0')));
    });
    return svg;
  }

  function techGrid(p) {
    const wrap = el('div', { class: 'pd-grid' });
    const order = ['Laser','Missiles','Plasma','Ion','Photon','Disruptor','Armour','Shielding','Warp Drive'];
    for (const t of order) {
      const raw = p.techs ? p.techs[t] : null;
      let lvl = raw != null ? (typeof raw === 'number' ? raw : raw.level) : null;
      let note = '';
      if (t === 'Warp Drive' && lvl == null && p.warpInferred) { lvl = p.warpInferred.level; note = '~'; }
      wrap.append(el('div', { class: 'pd-cell' + (lvl == null ? ' empty' : '') },
        el('div', { class: 'pd-tname' }, t),
        el('div', { class: 'pd-tval' }, lvl == null ? '\u2014' : String(lvl) + note)));
    }
    return wrap;
  }

  function section(title, node, sub) {
    const s = el('div', { class: 'pd-sect' });
    s.append(el('div', { class: 'pd-sect-title' }, title + (sub ? '  ' : '')));
    if (sub) s.firstChild.append(el('span', { class: 'pd-sub' }, sub));
    s.append(node);
    return s;
  }

  function renderPlayer(root, pid) {
    const p = AEHub.player(pid);
    const box = el('div', {});
    // identity + ranks
    const head = el('div', { class: 'pd-head' },
      el('div', { class: 'pd-name' }, (p.guild ? '[' + p.guild + '] ' : '') + (p.name || '#' + pid)),
      el('div', { class: 'pd-meta' }, '#' + pid + ' \u00b7 sources: ' + (p.sources.join(', ') || 'none')));
    box.append(head);
    if (p.ranks) {
      const r = el('div', { class: 'pd-ranks' });
      for (const [k, label] of [['level', 'Level'], ['economy', 'Economy'], ['fleet', 'Fleet'], ['tech', 'Technology']])
        if (p.ranks[k] != null) r.append(el('div', { class: 'pd-rank' }, el('b', {}, label), ' ' + fmt(p.ranks[k])));
      box.append(section('Rankings', r));
    }
    box.append(section('Techs', techGrid(p), p.warpInferred ? 'Warp ~' + p.warpInferred.level + ' from ' + p.warpInferred.pairs + ' sighting pair(s)' : ''));
    if (p.techCheck) {
      const tc = p.techCheck;
      const cls = tc.verdict === 'impossible' ? 'pd-bad' : tc.verdict === 'hidden' ? 'pd-warn' : 'pd-ok';
      box.append(section('Tech-rank check', el('div', { class: 'pd-check ' + cls },
        `known combat spend ${fmt(tc.spend)} of rank ${fmt(tc.rankTech)} (expected combat ~${fmt(tc.expectedCombat)}) \u2192 ${tc.verdict.toUpperCase()}`),
        'rank Technology = cumulative research credits (verified); \u03b1=0.47 uncalibrated'));
    }
    if (p.fleetPie) box.append(section('Fleet composition', pieSvg(p.fleetPie),
      'by credit share \u00b7 richest observed force ' + fmt(Math.round(p.fleetPie.total)) + 'c \u00b7 ' + (p.fleetPie.side || '?') + ' \u00b7 ' + ago(p.fleetPie.t)));
    if (p.activity && p.activity.samples > 0) box.append(section('Activity by hour', histogramSvg(p.activity),
      p.activity.samples + ' event(s) \u00b7 ' + p.activity.tz + ' \u00b7 autoscout sightings excluded'));
    if (p.bases.length) {
      const tb = el('table', { class: 'pd-tbl' });
      tb.append(el('tr', {}, ...['Coord', 'Name', 'Level', 'Economy', 'Seen'].map(h => el('th', {}, h))));
      for (const b of p.bases.slice(0, 40))
        tb.append(el('tr', {}, el('td', {}, b.coord || ''), el('td', {}, b.baseName || ''), el('td', {}, String(b.level ?? '\u2014')), el('td', {}, String(b.economy ?? '\u2014')), el('td', {}, ago(b.lastSeen || b.firstSeen))));
      box.append(section('Bases (' + p.bases.length + ')', tb));
    }
    if (p.observations.length) {
      const tb = el('table', { class: 'pd-tbl' });
      tb.append(el('tr', {}, ...['Seen', 'Kind', 'Size', 'Target'].map(h => el('th', {}, h))));
      for (const o of p.observations.slice(0, 15))
        tb.append(el('tr', {}, el('td', {}, new Date(o.observedAt).toISOString().slice(5, 16).replace('T', ' ')), el('td', {}, o.isScout40 ? 'SCOUT' : 'FLEET'), el('td', {}, fmt(o.size)), el('td', {}, o.destLoc || o.destText || '?')));
      box.append(section('Movement observations (' + p.observations.length + ')', tb));
    }
    root.append(box);
  }

  function build() {
    document.title = 'AE Player Dashboard';
    while (document.body.firstChild) document.body.removeChild(document.body.firstChild);
    const host = document.createElement('div');
    host.setAttribute('data-ae-script', 'player-dashboard');
    const sh = host.attachShadow({ mode: 'open' });
    AEShared.adopt(sh);
    const css = document.createElement('style');
    css.textContent = [
      ':host { display:block; }',
      '.pd-wrap { max-width: 780px; margin: 20px auto; padding: 14px; background: var(--ae-bg); color: var(--ae-text); font: 12px/1.5 var(--ae-font); border: 1px solid var(--ae-border); border-radius: var(--ae-radius); }',
      '.pd-title { font-size: 15px; letter-spacing: .06em; margin-bottom: 8px; color: var(--ae-primary); }',
      '.pd-search { display: flex; gap: 6px; margin-bottom: 12px; }',
      '.pd-list { display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 2px; }',
      '.pd-prow { padding: 3px 6px; border-radius: 3px; cursor: pointer; font-family: var(--ae-font-mono); font-size: 11px; }',
      '.pd-prow:hover { background: var(--ae-surface-2); }',
      '.pd-head { margin: 6px 0 10px; } .pd-name { font-size: 14px; color: var(--ae-primary); } .pd-meta { color: var(--ae-text-dim); font-size: 10px; }',
      '.pd-sect { margin: 12px 0; } .pd-sect-title { font-size: 10px; letter-spacing: .08em; text-transform: uppercase; color: var(--ae-text-dim); margin-bottom: 4px; }',
      '.pd-sub { text-transform: none; letter-spacing: 0; margin-left: 6px; }',
      '.pd-ranks { display: flex; gap: 14px; flex-wrap: wrap; font-family: var(--ae-font-mono); font-size: 11px; }',
      '.pd-grid { display: grid; grid-template-columns: repeat(3, 1fr); gap: 3px; max-width: 420px; }',
      '.pd-cell { display: flex; justify-content: space-between; padding: 3px 7px; background: var(--ae-surface); border: 1px solid var(--ae-border-soft); border-radius: 3px; font-size: 11px; }',
      '.pd-cell.empty { opacity: .45; } .pd-tval { font-family: var(--ae-font-mono); color: var(--ae-secondary); }',
      '.pd-check { padding: 5px 8px; border-radius: 3px; font-family: var(--ae-font-mono); font-size: 11px; }',
      '.pd-ok { color: var(--ae-success); } .pd-warn { color: var(--ae-warn); } .pd-bad { color: var(--ae-danger); }',
      '.pd-tbl { border-collapse: collapse; width: 100%; font-family: var(--ae-font-mono); font-size: 10px; }',
      '.pd-tbl th { text-align: left; color: var(--ae-text-dim); padding: 2px 6px; border-bottom: 1px solid var(--ae-border); text-transform: uppercase; font-size: 9px; }',
      '.pd-tbl td { padding: 2px 6px; border-bottom: 1px solid var(--ae-border-soft); }'
    ].join('\n');
    sh.append(css);
    const wrap = el('div', { class: 'pd-wrap' });
    wrap.append(el('div', { class: 'pd-title' }, 'AE PLAYER DASHBOARD \u00b7 ' + location.hostname.split('.')[0]));
    const searchIn = el('input', { class: 'ae-input', placeholder: 'search player name / guild / id\u2026', style: 'flex:1;' });
    wrap.append(el('div', { class: 'pd-search' }, searchIn));
    const content = el('div', {});
    wrap.append(content);
    sh.append(wrap);
    document.body.appendChild(host);

    const idx = AEHub.index();
    function showList(filter) {
      while (content.firstChild) content.removeChild(content.firstChild);
      const f = (filter || '').toLowerCase();
      const rows = Object.values(idx)
        .filter(r => !f || (r.name || '').toLowerCase().includes(f) || (r.guild || '').toLowerCase().includes(f) || r.id.includes(f))
        .sort((a, b) => (a.name || '').localeCompare(b.name || ''))
        .slice(0, 60);
      const list = el('div', { class: 'pd-list' });
      for (const r of rows) {
        const row = el('div', { class: 'pd-prow' }, (r.guild ? '[' + r.guild + '] ' : '') + (r.name || '#' + r.id) + '  \u00b7 ' + r.sources.join(','));
        row.addEventListener('click', () => { location.hash = 'ae-player-dashboard=' + r.id; showPlayer(r.id); });
        list.append(row);
      }
      if (!rows.length) list.append(el('div', { class: 'ae-muted' }, 'no players known yet \u2014 run the scanners first'));
      content.append(list);
    }
    function showPlayer(pid) {
      while (content.firstChild) content.removeChild(content.firstChild);
      const back = el('button', { class: 'ae-btn', style: 'margin-bottom:8px;' }, '\u2190 all players');
      back.addEventListener('click', () => { location.hash = 'ae-player-dashboard'; showList(searchIn.value); });
      content.append(back);
      renderPlayer(content, pid);
    }
    searchIn.addEventListener('input', () => showList(searchIn.value));
    if (m[1]) showPlayer(m[1]); else showList('');
  }

  if (document.readyState === 'loading') document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', build);
  else build();
})();

} });


/* ============ MODULE: fleet-scanner ============ */
AESuite.register({ id: "fleet-scanner", title: "Fleet Scanner", pages: null, init: function () {
/* AE Fleet Scanner — exact composition of every fleet visible on the current map or
   base page. Finds fleet.aspx links (with their owning player), fetches each fleet page
   paced, parses via the shared parseFleetComposition (the same verified parser the drop
   popup uses), shows a results table, and RECORDS each composition into the tech
   harvester's fleetObs (same GM store, exact record shape incl. dedup key and the
   60-cap) so fleet-spec confidence accumulates from map scans, not only battles.
   rt:false on these records — a fleet SEEN parked is not evidence the player is online,
   so the activity histogram correctly ignores them. */
(function () {
  'use strict';
  if (typeof AEShared === 'undefined' || typeof AEMapIntel === 'undefined') return;
  const SERVER = location.hostname.split('.')[0];
  const HARVEST_KEY = 'aeTechHarvester::' + SERVER;

  function el(tag, attrs, ...kids) {
    const e = document.createElement(tag);
    if (attrs) for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(attrs)) { if (k === 'class') e.className = v; else if (k === 'style') e.style.cssText = v; else e.setAttribute(k, v); }
    for (const k of kids) e.append(typeof k === 'string' ? document.createTextNode(k) : k);
    return e;
  }
  const fmt = (n) => n == null ? '\u2014' : Number(n).toLocaleString('en-US');

  /* Fleets on this page: dedup by fleetId; owner resolved from the closest row's
     player link when present. */
  function fleetsOnPage() {
    const out = new Map();
    for (const a of document.querySelectorAll("a[href*='fleet.aspx?fleet=']")) {
      const m = (a.getAttribute('href') || '').match(/fleet=(\d+)/);
      if (!m) continue;
      const id = m[1];
      if (out.has(id)) continue;
      let pid = null, pname = null;
      const tr = a.closest('tr');
      const scope = tr || a.parentElement || document;
      const pl = scope.querySelector ? scope.querySelector("a[href*='player=']") : null;
      if (pl) {
        const pm = (pl.getAttribute('href') || '').match(/player=(\d+)/);
        if (pm) { pid = pm[1]; pname = (pl.textContent || '').trim(); }
      }
      out.set(id, { fleetId: id, playerId: pid, playerName: pname });
    }
    return [...out.values()];
  }

  /* Record into the harvester store with ITS semantics: dedup key, value from
     DROP_STATS costs, sort by t, cap 60. side:'map' distinguishes scans from battles
     (deriveSpec counts them as observations either way — that is the confidence gain). */
  function recordObservation(f, comp) {
    if (!f.playerId || !comp || !comp.units) return false;
    try {
      const raw = (typeof GM_getValue === 'function') ? GM_getValue(HARVEST_KEY, null) : null;
      const db = raw ? JSON.parse(raw) : { players: {} };
      const now = Date.now();
      const p = db.players[f.playerId] || { id: f.playerId, name: f.playerName, server: SERVER, techs: {}, history: [], anomalies: [], firstSeen: now, lastReportTime: null };
      if (f.playerName && !p.name) p.name = f.playerName;
      p.fleetObs = p.fleetObs || [];
      // one map observation per fleet per day: composition changes slowly while parked
      const day = new Date(now).toISOString().slice(0, 10);
      const k = 'map|' + day + '|' + f.fleetId + '|' + f.playerId;
      if (p.fleetObs.some(o => o.k === k)) return false;
      let v = 0; for (const [ab, q] of Object.entries(comp.units)) { const st = AEMapIntel.DROP_STATS[ab]; if (st) v += q * st.c; }
      p.fleetObs.push({ k, t: now, rt: false, side: 'map', units: comp.units, v });
      p.fleetObs.sort((a, b) => a.t - b.t);
      if (p.fleetObs.length > 60) p.fleetObs = p.fleetObs.slice(-60);
      p.lastSeen = now;
      db.players[f.playerId] = p;
      if (typeof GM_setValue === 'function') GM_setValue(HARVEST_KEY, JSON.stringify(db));
      return true;
    } catch (e) { return false; }
  }

  function build() {
    const host = document.createElement('div');
    host.id = 'ae-fleetscanner-host';
    host.setAttribute('data-ae-script', 'fleet-scanner');
    const root = host.attachShadow({ mode: 'open' });
    AEShared.adopt(root);
    const css = document.createElement('style');
    css.textContent = [
      '.fs-collapsed .ae-panel__sweep, .fs-collapsed .ae-panel__body { display: none; }',
      '.fs-bar { height: 3px; background: var(--ae-surface-2); border-radius: 2px; overflow: hidden; margin: 4px 0 2px; display: none; }',
      '.fs-bar__fill { height: 100%; width: 0; background: linear-gradient(90deg, var(--ae-primary), var(--ae-secondary)); transition: width .3s ease; }',
      '.fs-row { display: flex; align-items: baseline; gap: 6px; font: 10px/1.5 var(--ae-font-mono); padding: 1px 4px; border-radius: 3px; color: var(--ae-text-muted); }',
      '.fs-row:hover { background: var(--ae-surface-2); }',
      '.fs-row .p { flex: 1 1 auto; min-width: 0; overflow: hidden; text-overflow: ellipsis; white-space: nowrap; color: var(--ae-text); }',
      '.fs-row .u { flex: 3 1 auto; min-width: 0; overflow: hidden; text-overflow: ellipsis; white-space: nowrap; }',
      '.fs-row .v { flex: 0 0 auto; color: var(--ae-secondary); font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums; }',
      '.fs-row.rec .p::after { content: " \u2713"; color: var(--ae-success); }'
    ].join('\n');
    root.append(css);
    const panel = el('div', { class: 'ae-panel' });
    const dot = el('span', { class: 'ae-panel__dot', title: 'idle' });
    const statusEl = el('span', { class: 'ae-panel__status' }, 'idle');
    const collapseBtn = AEShared.makeToggle({ collapsed: false, onToggle: (c) => panel.classList.toggle('fs-collapsed', c) });
    const bar = el('div', { class: 'ae-panel__bar' },
      dot,
      el('span', { class: 'ae-panel__titles' },
        el('span', { class: 'ae-panel__title' }, 'FLEET SCANNER'),
        el('span', { class: 'ae-panel__sub' }, SERVER + ' / exact compositions')),
      statusEl, collapseBtn);
    const body = el('div', { class: 'ae-panel__body' });
    panel.append(bar, el('div', { class: 'ae-panel__sweep' }), body);
    root.append(panel);

    const scanBtn = el('button', { class: 'ae-btn ae-btn--primary' }, 'Scan fleets here');
    const stopBtn = el('button', { class: 'ae-btn', style: 'display:none;' }, '\u23f9 stop');
    body.append(el('div', { style: 'display:flex;gap:6px;align-items:center;' }, scanBtn, stopBtn));
    const pbar = el('div', { class: 'fs-bar' }, el('div', { class: 'fs-bar__fill' }));
    body.append(pbar);
    const out = el('div', { style: 'margin-top:4px;' });
    body.append(out);

    let stopped = false, running = false;
    stopBtn.addEventListener('click', () => { stopped = true; });
    window.addEventListener('ae-master-hidden', (e) => { if (e.detail && e.detail.hidden) stopped = true; });

    const sleep = (ms) => new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, ms));
    async function scan() {
      if (running) return;
      if (AEShared.isMasterHidden && AEShared.isMasterHidden()) { statusEl.textContent = 'suite hidden'; return; }
      const fleets = fleetsOnPage();
      if (!fleets.length) { statusEl.textContent = 'no fleet links on this page'; return; }
      running = true; stopped = false;
      while (out.firstChild) out.removeChild(out.firstChild);
      pbar.style.display = 'block'; stopBtn.style.display = '';
      let done = 0, recorded = 0;
      for (const f of fleets) {
        if (stopped) break;
        statusEl.textContent = 'fleet ' + (done + 1) + '/' + fleets.length + '\u2026';
        let comp = null;
        try {
          const res = await fetch(location.origin + '/fleet.aspx?fleet=' + f.fleetId, { credentials: 'same-origin' });
          if (res.ok) comp = AEMapIntel.parseFleetComposition(new DOMParser().parseFromString(await res.text(), 'text/html'));
        } catch (e) { /* no vision / network */ }
        done++;
        pbar.firstChild.style.width = Math.round(done / fleets.length * 100) + '%';
        const row = el('div', { class: 'fs-row' });
        row.append(el('span', { class: 'p' }, (f.playerName || '#' + (f.playerId || '?')) ));
        if (comp && comp.units) {
          const parts = Object.entries(comp.units).sort((a, b) => (AEMapIntel.DROP_STATS[b[0]].c * b[1]) - (AEMapIntel.DROP_STATS[a[0]].c * a[1]))
            .map(([ab, q]) => ab + ':' + fmt(q));
          row.append(el('span', { class: 'u', title: parts.join(' ') }, parts.join(' ')));
          row.append(el('span', { class: 'v' }, fmt(comp.value) + 'c'));
          row.title = 'fleet ' + f.fleetId + (comp.stated != null ? ' \u00b7 page states ' + fmt(comp.stated) : '');
          if (recordObservation(f, comp)) { row.classList.add('rec'); recorded++; }
        } else {
          row.append(el('span', { class: 'u ae-muted' }, 'no vision (fleet page unreadable)'));
        }
        out.append(row);
        if (done < fleets.length) await sleep(1100 * (0.7 + Math.random() * 0.6));   // crawl pacing
      }
      statusEl.textContent = (stopped ? 'stopped \u00b7 ' : '') + done + ' scanned \u00b7 ' + recorded + ' recorded to observations';
      pbar.style.display = 'none'; stopBtn.style.display = 'none';
      running = false;
    }
    scanBtn.addEventListener('click', scan);

    const n = fleetsOnPage().length;
    statusEl.textContent = n ? n + ' fleet(s) on this page' : 'no fleets here';
    document.documentElement.appendChild(host);
    AEShared.placeHost(host, 'fleet-scanner', root);
    AEShared.startLiveUpdates();
  }

  if (document.readyState === 'loading') document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', build);
  else build();
})();

} });


AESuite.boot();
